# Chapter ki script · 11. Photosynthesis in Higher Plants

21 headings · 221 gyan-bindu · 2274 lines · lagbhag 309 minute bolna · padhai ki NCERT lines 156/156 · summary 11/11 · exercises ka gyan 13/13 · ruko-socho 21 · sawaal 9% · poori kahani dobara 16 · tukde 0

## Shuruaat Opening

_Maqsad: Ek chhota sa beej badhkar bada ped ban jaata hai; uski lakdi, pattiyan aur phal, yeh saara wazan aakhir aata kahan se hai?_

_Misaal: rasoi: patti paudhe ki rasoi, carbon dioxide-paani-minerals kachcha saamaan, sooraj ki roshni aag, sugar pakwaan; autotroph apni rasoi wala, heterotroph doosron ki rasoi ka khana khaane wala; Calvin ka C¹⁴ kachche saamaan par lagi haldi_

Kitab ki baatein (93):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Unit ki pehli tasveer: do mude hue, rajma jaise cells beech ke ek chhote, lens jaise khule raaste ko gherte hain; un par aar-paar dhaariyan hain; chaaron taraf bade, tedhe-medhe cells hain; tasveer par koi label ya teer nahi.
- f2 · shabd ka matlab · guard cells · Guard cells: stomatal pore ke dono taraf ke do rajma jaise cells, jo paani se phool kar pore kholte aur pichak kar band karte hain; guard yaani pehredaar.

**0-k1** · yaad · Pichhle chapter mein humne dekha ki ek cell bant kar do cell ban jaati hai. Yaad karo, bantne se pehle cell badhti hai. Woh apne DNA ki copy banati hai, aur naye protein aur doosre molecules jodti hai. Isi tarah baar-baar bant kar beej ke andar ka nanha sa paudha karodon cells wala ped ban jaata hai. Yaad rakhein: har nayi cell ko banne ke liye naya material chahiye, aur kaam karne ke liye energy. Aage hum dekhenge ki paudhe ko yeh material aur energy milti kahan se hai. Toh socho, ek chhota sa beej badhkar bada ped ban jaata hai. Uski lakdi, pattiyan aur phal, yeh saara wazan aakhir aata kahan se hai?
  - screen: Ek cell do mein bant-ti hui; bantne se pehle cell badhti, DNA ki copy aur naye molecules; beej ke andar nanha paudha, phir cells badhte-badhte bada ped; neeche 'Yaad rakhein: nayi cell = naya material + energy'; aakhir mein bada ped, uski lakdi, pattiyan aur phal par sawaal: yeh saara wazan kahan se?
  - kitaab se bahar: Pichhle chapter (cell cycle aur cell division) ki yaad: bantne se pehle cell badhti hai.
**0-k2** · figure · Is sawaal ki khoj hum ek naye hisse se shuru karte hain, jiska naam hai Plant Physiology. Is hisse ki pehli tasveer dhyaan se dekho. Is par koi naam ya teer nahi likha hai. Beech mein do mude hue cells hain, bilkul rajma ke daane jaise. Dekho, dono milkar ek chhote se khule raaste ko gherte hain. Anatomy wale chapter mein aap inse mil chuke ho. Yeh do cells beech ke chhed ke pehredaar hain. Isliye hum inhe guard cells kehte hain; guard yaani pehredaar. Inke beech ka lens jaisa chhota chhed stomatal pore hai; samjho yeh patti ka darwaza hai. Guard cells paani se phool jaate hain toh pore khul jaata hai. Paani kam hone par woh pichak kar use band kar dete hain. Guard cells par jo aar-paar dhaariyan dikhti hain, woh cellulose ke patle reshon ki hain. Yahi reshe phoolte cell ko bahar ki taraf modte hain, aur pore khulta hai. Chaaron taraf ke bade, tedhe-medhe cells patti ki upari parat ke cells hain. Har cell ke chaaron taraf cell wall hoti hai. Yaad karo, patti apne aas-paas ki hawa se gases ka len-den karti hai. Carbon dioxide andar aati hai, aur oxygen bahar jaati hai. Is chhote se darwaze ko yaad rakhna. Aaj ke sawaal ke jawab mein yeh bahut kaam aayega.
  - terms: guard cells = Stomatal pore ke dono taraf ke do rajma jaise cells jo use kholte aur band karte hain; guard yaani pehredaar.; stomatal pore = Patti ki sateh par do guard cells ke beech ka chhota chhed, jisse gases aati-jaati hain.
  - screen: Unit ki pehli tasveer: do rajma jaise mude cells aur unke beech lens jaisa chhed, koi label nahi; naam tabhi ubhrein jab line le: guard cells (pehredaar), stomatal pore (patti ka darwaza); guard cells paani se phoolte to chhed khulta, pichakte to band; un par aar-paar dhaariyan, cellulose ke reshe; chaaron taraf upari parat ke tedhe-medhe cells, har ek ki cell wall; chhed se carbon dioxide andar aur oxygen bahar ke chhote teer.
  - NCERT: p001-i1 Unlabelled botanical illustration: two curved, kidney-shaped cells surround a ce
  - kitaab se bahar: Tasveer par naam nahi: guard cells, stomatal pore aur upari parat ke cells; guard cells ki dhaariyan cellulose ke reshe hain jo pore kholne mein madad karte hain.
**0-k3** · example · Aksar log sochte hain ki paudha apna khana mitti se kheenchta hai, jaise hum thaali se kha lete hain. Lagbhag 1640 ke aas-paas Belgium ke ek vaigyanik, van Helmont, ne isi baat ko parakhne ka socha. Unhone lagbhag 90 kilo sookhi mitti tauli. Usme unhone willow naam ke ped ka lagbhag 2 kilo ka paudha laga diya. Paanch saal tak unhone use sirf paani diya, aur kuch nahi diya. Paanch saal baad woh paudha lagbhag 77 kilo ka ped ban chuka tha. Ab socho, is beech mitti kitni kam hui hogi? Mitti kam hui sirf lagbhag 57 gram, yaani aapki ek mutthi se bhi kam. Ped badha lagbhag 75 kilo, aur mitti ghati sirf 57 gram. Toh ped ka wazan mitti se nahi aaya, aur mitti wali baat galat nikli. Van Helmont ne socha ki saara wazan paani se aaya, kyunki unhone paudhe ko sirf paani hi diya tha. Par kya sirf paani se itna bada ped ban sakta hai? Sirf paani se itna bada ped bana ya nahi, iska jawab thodi der mein milega. Woh jawab ek doosre vaigyanik ki khoj se aayega.
  - screen: Galat soch: jad mitti se 'khana' kheenchti hui, us par sawaal ka nishaan; van Helmont, lagbhag 1640; tarazu par lagbhag 90 kilo sookhi mitti ka gamla aur 2 kilo ka willow paudha; paanch saal ka calendar, sirf paani ki baalti; ped 77 kilo; mitti ka tarazu: sirf 57 gram kam, ek mutthi se bhi kam; sawaal: sirf paani se itna bada ped?
  - kitaab se bahar: Van Helmont ka willow prayog (lagbhag 1640): 2 kilo ka paudha, 90 kilo sookhi mitti, paanch saal sirf paani; ped 77 kilo, mitti sirf 57 gram kam.
**0-k4** · samjhana · Ab tak Class 11 mein aapne jeevon ko do tarah se dekha hai. Shuru ke chapters mein jeevon ki vividhta aur banawat dekhi. Aapne dekha ki kaun sa paudha kaisa dikhta hai, aur kaun sa jaanwar kis samooh ka hai. Phir cell aur biomolecules wale chapters mein hum andar gaye, cell ke hisson aur unke molecules tak. Yaad karo, organism yaani ek poora jeevit praani ya paudha. Cells uski banawat aur kaam ki ikai hain, aur molecules cells ke chemical hisse hain. Toh hum ek hi jeevan ko alag-alag staron par dekh sakte hain: molecule, cell, poora jeev, aur jeevon ka samooh. Jeevan ko is tarah chhote se bade staron par dekhne ko levels of organisation kehte hain. Ek ghadi socho: bahar se dekho toh sui chalti dikhti hai, aur khol kar dekho toh andar purze ghoomte dikhte hain. Dono nazar ek hi ghadi ki hain. Ek batati hai ghadi kya karti hai, aur doosri batati hai kaise karti hai. Kisi cheez ko dekhne ke aise alag nazariyon ko perspectives kehte hain. Biology mein bhi jeevon ki banawat aur vividhta padhte-padhte aise hi do nazariye ban gaye. Ek nazariya poore jeev aur usse upar ke star par dekhta hai, jise hum organismic star kehte hain. Isse ecology aur usse judi shaakhaayein bani. Ecology mein eco yaani ghar, yaani jeevon aur unke aas-paas ke rishton ki padhai. Doosra nazariya cell aur molecule ke star par dekhta hai, yaani cellular and molecular star par. Isse physiology aur biochemistry bani. Physiology mein padhte hain ki jeev ke andar kaam kaise chalte hain. Biochemistry mein hum jeevon ke molecules aur unki reactions padhte hain. Upar se dekhne par dono nazariye aise lagte hain jaise inka mel hi na baithe. Isi ko apparently irreconcilable kehte hain. Apparently yaani upar se dekhne par, aur irreconcilable yaani jinka aapas mein mel na baith sake. Par yeh mel na baithna sirf upar se hai, kyunki dono ek hi jeevan ke do star hain. Bilkul ghadi ke bahar aur andar ki tarah. Van Helmont ko yaad karo: unhone poore ped ko taula, yaani organismic star par dekha. Wazan badha, yeh toh dikh gaya, par kahan se aaya, yeh nahi samjha. Yeh jawab tab milega jab hum cell aur molecule ke star par jhaankenge. Dono nazariyon ko jodna hi is naye hisse ka kaam hai.
  - terms: levels of organisation = Jeevan ko molecule, cell, poore jeev aur jeevon ke samooh jaise chhote se bade staron par dekhna.; perspectives = Ek hi cheez ko dekhne ke alag-alag nazariye.; organismic = Poore jeev ke star par.; ecology = Jeevon ke aapas mein aur aas-paas se rishton ki padhai; eco yaani ghar.; cellular and molecular = Cell aur uske andar ke molecules ke star par.; physiology = Jeev ke andar kaam kaise chalte hain, iski padhai.; biochemistry = Jeevon ke molecules aur unki chemical reactions ki padhai.; apparently irreconcilable = Upar se aisa lagna ki dono ka mel nahi baith sakta.
  - screen: Class 11 ka safar: jeevon ki vividhta aur banawat (poore jeev), phir cell aur molecules; seedhi jaise levels of organisation: molecule, cell, poora jeev, jeevon ka samooh; ghadi bahar se (sui) aur khul kar (purze), dono ek hi ghadi (apni tasveer); do nazariye do raaston jaise: organismic star se ecology (eco = ghar), cellular and molecular star se physiology aur biochemistry; beech mein 'mel nahi?' ka nishaan jo aakhir ek pul ban jaata; van Helmont ka taula hua ped, us par '?'.
  - NCERT: p001-i6 The description of structure and variation of living organisms over a period of 
  - kitaab se bahar: Ghadi ki misaal; Class 11 ke pehle hisson (vividhta, banawat, cell, biomolecules) se jod.
**0-k5** · samjhana · Is naye hisse mein hum paudhon ke andar chalne wale kaam padhenge, aur misaal ke liye phool wale paudhe lenge. Yaad karo, cells ke andar chemical reactions chalti rehti hain. Poora paudha apni cells ke kaam par hi tika hai. Jeev ko zinda rakhne aur badhane wale aise andar ke kaamon ko hum physiological processes kehte hain. Aur jin paudhon mein phool aate hain, jaise aam, gulab aur gehun, unhe flowering plants kehte hain. Is hisse mein teen kaam aayenge, ek ke baad ek. Pehla, photosynthesis, jiska naam aap pehle bhi sun chuke ho. Is chapter mein hum ise poori tarah kholenge. Doosra, respiration, yaani khane ko tod kar usme se kaam ki energy nikaalna. Teesra, growth and development, yaani paudhe ka badhna, aur zindagi bhar uske roop aur kaam ka badalna. Yeh kram isliye hai, kyunki pehle khana banta hai, phir usse energy nikalti hai, aur tab jaakar paudha badhta hai. Har kaam ko molecules ki bhasha mein samjhaya jaayega. Saath mein cell ke andar ki halchal bhi dikhegi, aur poore paudhe ka star bhi. Yaani wahi star jo abhi dekhe: molecule, cell aur poora paudha, teeno saath. Paudhe ke aas-paas roshni, paani, hawa aur garmi hoti hai. Yeh sab uske kaam par asar daalte hain. Aas-paas ki in sab haalaton ko hum environment kehte hain. Jahan zaroori ho, wahan yeh bhi dekhenge ki environment badalne par paudhe ke yeh kaam kaise badalte hain.
  - terms: physiological processes = Jeev ko zinda rakhne aur badhane wale andar ke kaam.; flowering plants = Jin paudhon mein phool aate hain.; respiration = Khane ko tod kar kaam ki energy nikaalna.; growth and development = Paudhe ka badhna, aur zindagi bhar uske roop aur kaam ka badalna.; environment = Paudhe ke aas-paas ki haalatein: roshni, paani, hawa, garmi.
  - screen: Phool wale paudhe: aam, gulab, gehun = flowering plants; paudhe ke andar chalte kaam = physiological processes; teen dabbe kram se: 1 photosynthesis, 2 respiration (khane se energy), 3 growth and development (badhna, badalna), beech mein teer: khana → energy → badhna; har dabbe ke neeche teen star: molecule, cell, poora paudha; paudhe ke chaaron taraf roshni, paani, hawa, garmi = environment.
  - NCERT: p001-i7 Description of physiological processes, in flowering plants as an example, is wh
**0-k6** · kahani · Ab ek aise vaigyanik ki baat, jinhone paudhe ke khane ka raaz molecule ke star par khola. Yeh tasveer Melvin Calvin ki hai. Unka janm April 1911 mein America ke Minnesota mein hua. Kisi vishay mein naya sawaal lekar kai saal research karne par sabse oonchi degree milti hai. Is degree ko Ph.D. kehte hain, yaani Doctor of Philosophy. Yahan philosophy ka matlab hai gyaan. Calvin ne University of Minnesota se Ph.D. in Chemistry ki, yaani unki research Chemistry mein thi. Baad mein woh University of California, Berkeley mein Professor of Chemistry rahe. Par ek chemist paudhon ka sawaal kyun uthayega? Chemist ne paudhon ka sawaal isliye uthaya, kyunki paudhe ke andar khana banna asal mein chemical reactions ka kaam hai. Socho, chemical reactions ek substance ko doosre substance mein badalti hain, aur hare paudhe isi tarah organic substances banate hain.
  - terms: Ph.D. in Chemistry = Ph.D. yaani Doctor of Philosophy: Chemistry mein kai saal research ke baad milne wali sabse oonchi degree.
  - screen: Melvin Calvin ki black-and-white tasveer; neeche timeline: April 1911, Minnesota (America) mein janm → University of Minnesota se Ph.D. in Chemistry (Ph.D. = Doctor of Philosophy, research ki sabse oonchi degree) → University of California, Berkeley mein Professor of Chemistry; sawaal 'chemist aur paudha?'; patti ke andar ek molecule ka doosre mein badalna = chemical reaction.
  - NCERT: p002-i1 MELVIN CALVIN born in Minnesota in April, 1911, received his Ph.D. in Chemistry ; p002-i7 Black-and-white photographic portrait of Melvin Calvin, shown from the upper tor
  - kitaab se bahar: Ph.D. ka poora naam Doctor of Philosophy; Minnesota America mein hai.
**0-k7** · kahani · Calvin ki khoj ki kahani ek dukh bhare samay se shuru hoti hai. 1945 mein World War II ke aakhir mein Japan ke do shehron par atom bomb giraaye gaye. Woh shehar the Hiroshima aur Nagasaki. Lakhon log maare gaye, aur bache hue logon mein radiation se bimariyan failin. Poori duniya sadme mein thi. Class 9 mein aapne padha tha ki har atom ke beech mein nucleus hota hai, jisme proton aur neutron hote hain. Ek hi element ke atomon mein proton barabar hote hain, par neutron kam-zyada ho sakte hain. Aise atomon ko hum isotopes kehte hain. Kuch isotopes asthir hote hain, aur apne-aap ek dikhai na dene wali radiation chhodte rehte hain. Atomon ke is apne-aap radiation chhodne ko hum radio-activity kehte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki radio-activity sirf tabaahi laati hai. Par Calvin aur unke saathiyon ne isi radio-activity ko achhe kaam mein lagaya. Jo radiation nuksaan karti hai, woh research mein kaise kaam aa sakti hai? Radiation research mein isliye kaam aati hai, kyunki use machine se pakda ja sakta hai. Geiger counter naam ka yantra iske milte hi tik-tik ki awaaz karta hai, chahe woh kan aankh se dikhe ya na dikhe. Jaise gale mein ghanti bandhi gaay khet mein dikhe na dikhe, uski ghanti ki awaaz batati hai ki woh kahan hai. Isi tarah radioactive atom apni radiation se batata rehta hai ki woh kahan pahuncha. Prayog mein yeh itni kam maatra mein hota hai ki nuksaan nahi karta.
  - terms: radio-activity = Kuch asthir atomon ka apne-aap radiation chhodna.
  - screen: 1945: World War II ka ant, Hiroshima aur Nagasaki par atom bomb, dhuan aur ujda shehar; atom ka nucleus (proton, neutron); ek element ke do atom, proton barabar, neutron alag = isotopes; asthir atom se nikalti radiation ki lehren = radio-activity; 'sirf tabaahi?' par kaata nishaan, phir prayogshala; Geiger counter ki suii aur awaaz; khet mein ghanti bandhi gaay (apni tasveer), aankh se ojhal par ghanti ki awaaz.
  - NCERT: p002-i2 Just after world war II, when the world was under shock after the Hiroshima-Naga
  - kitaab se bahar: 1945 mein Hiroshima aur Nagasaki par atom bomb; isotopes aur Geiger counter (Class 9 ki atom ki banawat se).
**0-k8** · pehle poori jhalak · Toh Calvin ne radio-activity ke sahaare kya dekha? Calvin ne radio-activity ke sahaare yeh dekha ki paudhe ke andar kachche saamaan ka carbon kahan-kahan jaata hai. Unka prayog teen kadamon mein samjho. Pehla kadam: carbon dioxide ke carbon par aisa nishaan lagao, jise machine pakad sake. Doosra kadam: yeh nishaan wali carbon dioxide ek hare paudhe ko roshni mein do. Teesra kadam: kuch der baad paudhe mein bani cheezon ko alag-alag karke dekho ki nishaan kis-kis mein pahuncha. Ab ek-ek kadam kholte hain.
  - screen: Teen dabbe ek kataar mein: 1 carbon dioxide ke carbon par chamakta nishaan, 2 nishaan wali carbon dioxide roshni mein rakhe hare paudhe tak, 3 paudhe ki bani cheezein alag-alag, kis mein nishaan chamakta.
  - kitaab se bahar: Calvin ke prayog ke teen kadam (label lagana, paudhe ko dena, bani cheezon mein dhoondhna).
**0-k9** · samjhana · Pehle dekho ki Calvin aur J.A. Bassham samajhna kya chahte the. Woh hare paudhon ki un reactions ko samajh rahe the, jinse sugar aur doosre substances bante hain. Koi bhi nayi cheez kuch shuruaati cheezon se banti hai, jaise roti aate aur paani se. Jin shuruaati cheezon se nayi cheez banti hai, unhe raw materials, yaani kachcha saamaan, kehte hain. Toh dekho, paudhe ke raw materials hain carbon dioxide, paani aur minerals. Carbon dioxide hawa mein milne wali gas hai. Iske ek molecule mein ek carbon aur do oxygen atom hote hain, isliye ise CO₂ likhte hain. Minerals mitti se jadon ke raaste aate hain, jaise nitrogen, phosphorus, potassium aur magnesium. Inki zaroorat thodi hi hoti hai. Ab van Helmont ki mitti ke woh 57 gram samajh aate hain. Paudhe ne mitti se zyadatar yahi thode minerals liye the. Sugar bhi carbon wale molecules hain. Uske har molecule mein kai carbon atom hote hain. Toh sugar ka carbon aaya kahan se, hawa ki carbon dioxide se ya kahin aur se? Sugar ka carbon kahan se aaya, yeh dekhne ke liye unhe carbon par nishaan lagana tha. Socho, aate mein thodi haldi mila do. Jo roti peeli nikle, samjho usme wahi aata gaya. Kisi cheez par pehchaan ka aisa nishaan lagane ko hum labelling kehte hain, taaki aage uska raasta pata chale. Aam carbon atom mein 6 proton aur 6 neutron hote hain, isliye uska wazan 12 hai. Carbon ke ek isotope mein 6 proton aur 8 neutron hote hain. Iska wazan 14 hai, aur ise C¹⁴ kehte hain. C¹⁴ radioactive hai, yaani yeh lagaataar radiation chhodta rehta hai, aur yantra ise pakad leta hai. Sabse kaam ki baat yeh hai ki paudha C¹⁴ ko aam carbon jaisa hi istemal karta hai. Kyunki reactions proton aur electron se tay hoti hain, neutron se nahi. Isliye Calvin aur Bassham ne carbon dioxide ko C¹⁴ se label kiya, yaani uske carbon ki jagah C¹⁴ rakha. Yeh label wali carbon dioxide paudhe ko roshni mein di gayi. Kuch der baad wahi C¹⁴ paudhe ki bani sugar mein mila. Ek exam ki baat: ise ¹⁴C bhi likhte hain, jisme 14 carbon ke naam se pehle upar likha jaata hai. Aage aapko yahi roop milega.
  - terms: raw materials = Kachcha saamaan: jin shuruaati cheezon se nayi cheez banti hai.; CO₂ = Carbon dioxide: ek carbon aur do oxygen atom wali hawa ki gas.; minerals = Mitti se jadon ke raaste aane wale tatva, jaise nitrogen, phosphorus, potassium, magnesium.; labelling = Kisi cheez par pehchaan ka nishaan lagana, taaki aage uska raasta pata chale.; C¹⁴ = Carbon ka radioactive isotope: 6 proton aur 8 neutron, wazan 14; ise ¹⁴C bhi likhte hain.
  - screen: Calvin aur J.A. Bassham; hare paudhe mein reactions se sugar banti; kachcha saamaan = raw materials: hawa se carbon dioxide (CO₂: ek carbon, do oxygen), jad se paani aur minerals (nitrogen, phosphorus, potassium, magnesium); van Helmont ki mitti ke 57 gram = minerals; aate mein haldi aur peeli roti (apni tasveer) = labelling; carbon atom: 6 proton + 6 neutron = 12, aur C¹⁴: 6 proton + 8 neutron = 14, radiation ki lehren; C¹⁴ wali carbon dioxide paudhe ko, phir sugar mein chamakta C¹⁴; kone mein dono likhaawat: C¹⁴ aur ¹⁴C.
  - NCERT: p002-i3 He along with J.A. Bassham studied reactions in green plants forming sugar and o
  - kitaab se bahar: C¹⁴ aam carbon jaisa hi reactions mein chalta hai (neutron rasayanik vyavhaar nahi badalte); prayog mein C¹⁴ sugar mein mila; van Helmont ki mitti ke 57 gram minerals the; C¹⁴ ko ¹⁴C bhi likhte hain.
**0-k10** · ruko, socho · Ab do baatein saath rakho: van Helmont ka ped lagbhag 75 kilo badha, aur mitti sirf 57 gram ghati. Aur Calvin ne dekha ki hawa ki carbon dioxide ka carbon paudhe ki sugar mein pahunch jaata hai. Ek clue aur lo: lakdi, pattiyan aur phal bhi aakhir carbon wale molecules se hi bane hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: van Helmont ke ped ka itna saara wazan aakhir aaya kahan se? Aapne socha hoga ki hawa se, aur bilkul sahi socha. Ped ka zyadatar wazan hawa ki carbon dioxide aur paani se bana. Carbon dioxide se carbon aaya, paani bhi juda, aur mitti ne bas thode minerals diye. Van Helmont aadhe sahi the: paani juda tha, par woh hawa ko bhool gaye. Kyunki hawa na dikhti hai, aur unhone use taula bhi nahi tha. Ab yaad karo stomatal pore. Hawa ki carbon dioxide isi chhote darwaze se patti ke andar jaati hai. Toh ek bada ped asal mein kaafi had tak hawa se bana hai. Sunne mein ajeeb lagta hai, par yahi sach hai.
  - screen: Do tarazu saath: ped +75 kilo, mitti −57 gram; C¹⁴ wala carbon sugar mein chamakta; lakdi, patti aur phal ke andar carbon ke molecule; bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk); phir hawa se carbon dioxide ke teer stomatal pore se patti mein, jad se paani, mitti se thode minerals; ped ka wazan teen hisson mein: hawa ka carbon, paani, aur bahut chhota minerals ka hissa.
  - kitaab se bahar: Ped ka zyadatar wazan hawa ki carbon dioxide aur paani se banta hai, mitti se sirf thode minerals.
  - jawab: Ped ka zyadatar wazan hawa ki carbon dioxide (uska carbon) aur paani se aaya; mitti ne sirf thode minerals diye (57 gram); carbon dioxide stomatal pore se patti mein jaati hai, aur C¹⁴ ne dikhaya ki uska carbon sugar mein pahunchta hai.
  - aam galti: Do galat jawab: mitti se (mitti sirf 57 gram ghati) aur sirf paani se (van Helmont ki soch); hawa dikhti nahi aur tauli nahi gayi, isliye log use bhool jaate hain.
**0-k11** · analogy · Ab is poori baat ko ek rasoi ki tarah socho, jahan kachche saamaan se khana pakta hai. Patti paudhe ki rasoi hai, aur carbon dioxide, paani aur minerals iska kachcha saamaan hain. Rasoi mein khana pakane ke liye aag chahiye. Paudhe ki rasoi mein yeh kaam sooraj ki roshni karti hai. Aur jo pakwaan banta hai, woh hai sugar, jisse aage lakdi, pattiyan aur phal bante hain. Calvin ka C¹⁴ isi rasoi ke kachche saamaan par lagi haldi tha, jo pakwaan tak pahunch kar dikh gayi. Par is rasoi mein ek baat bahut alag hai; kya aap bata sakte ho, kaun si? Alag baat yeh hai ki yahan koi rasoiya bahar se nahi aata. Patti ki cells khud hi khana banati hain. Aur yahan aag sirf garmi nahi deti. Roshni ki energy khud pakwaan ke andar band ho jaati hai.
  - screen: Rasoi (apni tasveer): ek patti jo rasoi ban jaati; kachche saamaan ke teen dabbe: carbon dioxide, paani, minerals; choolhe ki aag ki jagah sooraj ki roshni; thaali mein pakwaan = sugar, usse lakdi, patti, phal; kachche saamaan par haldi ka nishaan jo pakwaan mein dikhta = C¹⁴; rasoiya ki jagah patti ki cells; roshni ki chamak pakwaan ke andar band hoti hui.
  - kitaab se bahar: Rasoi ki misaal.
  - kya kis jaisa: Patti = rasoi; carbon dioxide, paani aur minerals = kachcha saamaan; sooraj ki roshni = aag; sugar = pakwaan; C¹⁴ = kachche saamaan par lagi haldi ka nishaan.
  - kahan tootti hai: Rasoi mein khana koi rasoiya pakata hai aur aag sirf garam karti hai; patti mein cells khud khana banati hain, roshni ki energy khane ke molecules mein band ho jaati hai, aur kachche saamaan ke atom hi jud kar naye molecule bante hain.
**0-k12** · samjhana · Ab doosra sawaal: roshni ki energy khane ke andar band kaise hoti hai? Roshni ki energy khane mein kaise band hoti hai, iska bhi ek vichaar Calvin ne diya. Pehle yaad karo, energy ek roop se doosre roop mein badal sakti hai. Jaise bulb mein bijli roshni ban jaati hai. Khane ke molecules ke andar bhi energy band rehti hai, jo zaroorat padne par reaction mein bahar aati hai. Molecules ke andar band is energy ko hum chemical energy kehte hain. Calvin ne kaha ki paudhe light energy ko chemical energy mein badalte hain. Har atom ke andar bahut chhote, negative charge wale kan hote hain. In kano ko hum electron kehte hain, aur yeh ek molecule se doosre molecule tak ja sakte hain. Patti mein kuch rang wale molecules hote hain, jo roshni ka kuch hissa sokh lete hain. Patti ka hara rang inhi se hai. Inhe pigment molecules kehte hain; pigment yaani rang dene wala padarth. Calvin ke vichaar mein roshni milne par ek electron aage badhta hai. Yeh electron pigment molecules aur doosre substances ke beech chalta hai, aur isi se energy ka roop badalta hai. Par yeh molecules bikhre hue nahi hote. Har ek apni sahi jagah aur sahi kram mein laga hota hai. Kaam ke hisaab se sahi jagah aur kram mein lage aise samooh ko organised array kehte hain; array yaani kataar. Jaise rasoi mein masale aur bartan sahi kram mein rakhe hon, tabhi khana jaldi aur theek banta hai. Kram isliye zaroori hai, kyunki electron ko ek molecule se agle tak sahi disha mein hi jaana hota hai. Electron ka yeh safar kahan se shuru hokar kahan jaata hai, yeh hum aage light reaction mein kholenge.
  - terms: chemical energy = Molecules ke andar band energy, jo reaction mein bahar aati hai.; electron = Atom ke andar ka bahut chhota, negative charge wala kan.; pigment molecules = Rang wale molecules jo roshni ka kuch hissa sokh lete hain.; organised array = Kaam ke hisaab se sahi jagah aur sahi kram mein laga samooh; array yaani kataar.
  - screen: Bulb mein bijli se roshni (energy ka roop badalna); khane ke molecule mein band energy = chemical energy; atom ke andar chhote negative kan = electron; patti ke hare pigment molecules roshni sokhte; pigment molecules aur doosre substances ek kram mein, unke beech ek electron aage badhta, light energy se chemical energy; rasoi mein kram se rakhe masale aur bartan (apni tasveer); electron ke safar par '?', aage light reaction.
  - NCERT: p002-i4 Calvin proposed that plants change light energy to chemical energy by transferri
  - kitaab se bahar: Bulb mein bijli se roshni (energy ka roop badalna).
**0-k13** · samjhana · Calvin ki sabse badi khoj yeh thi ki carbon dioxide ka carbon sugar tak kis raaste se pahunchta hai. Yaad karo, jo khana hum kha lete hain, woh pach kar hamare shareer ka hi hissa ban jaata hai. Socho, paudha bhi hawa ke carbon ko andar le kar apne molecules ka hissa bana leta hai. Carbon dioxide ke carbon ko is tarah apne organic molecules mein mila lene ko carbon assimilation kehte hain. Assimilation yaani apne mein mila lena. Par carbon dioxide se sugar ek jhatke mein nahi banti. Beech mein kai padaav aate hain. Jaise ghar se school jaate hue raaste mein kai mod aur chauraahe aate hain. Ek substance se agle substance tak judi reactions ke aise raaste ko pathway kehte hain; path yaani raasta. Calvin ne C¹⁴ ke sahaare is raaste ka har padaav pehchaana, aur uska poora naksha bana diya. Naksha banane ko hi mapping kehte hain. Photosynthesis mein carbon assimilation ke pathway ki isi mapping par unhe 1961 mein Nobel Prize mila. Yeh Nobel Chemistry mein tha. Yaani ek chemist ko paudhe ke raaz ke liye duniya ka sabse bada inaam mila. Is raaste ke padaav kaun-se hain, yeh hum aage is chapter mein dekhenge.
  - terms: carbon assimilation = Carbon dioxide ke carbon ko apne organic molecules ka hissa bana lena; assimilation yaani apne mein mila lena.; pathway = Ek substance se agle substance tak judi reactions ka raasta, beech ke padaavon samet.
  - screen: Carbon dioxide ka carbon (chamakta C¹⁴) ek raaste par chalta, beech mein kai padaav, aakhir sugar; khana pach kar shareer ka hissa banta = assimilation; ghar se school ka raasta, mod aur chauraahe (apni tasveer); padaavon ka naksha banta hua = mapping; 'Nobel Prize, 1961, Chemistry'; padaavon par '?', aage.
  - NCERT: p002-i5 The mapping of the pathway of carbon assimilation in photosynthesis earned him N
  - kitaab se bahar: Mapping yaani naksha banana; Calvin ka Nobel Chemistry mein tha.
**0-k14** · asli duniya · Calvin ne photosynthesis ke jo niyam pakke kiye, woh aaj bhi bahut kaam aa rahe hain. Aap jaante ho ki petrol, diesel aur koyla dharti ke andar karodon saalon mein bane. Ek baar jal kar woh khatam ho jaate hain. Par aap dekhte ho ki fasal, ghaas aur lakdi har saal phir se ug aati hain. Aise saadhan, jo prakriti mein phir se ban jaate hain, renewable resource kehlate hain; re yaani phir se. Photosynthesis ke niyamon se vaigyanik renewable resource se energy aur naye materials banane ki khoj kar rahe hain. Jaise aaj petrol mein ganne se bana ethanol milaya jaata hai, aur ganna har saal phir ugta hai. Sooraj ki roshni ko pakad kar kaam mein laane ki khoj bhi chal rahi hai, jaise chhaton par lage solar panel. Is khoj ko solar energy research kehte hain; sol yaani sooraj. Calvin ke niyam solar energy research ki neev mein bhi hain. Vaigyanik aisi banawati patti bhi bana rahe hain, jo asli patti ki tarah kaam kare. Yeh sooraj ki roshni se paani aur carbon dioxide ko fuel mein badalne ki koshish hai.
  - terms: renewable resource = Aisa saadhan jo prakriti mein phir se ban jaata hai, jaise fasal aur lakdi; re yaani phir se.; solar energy research = Sooraj ki roshni ki energy ko pakad kar kaam mein laane ki khoj; sol yaani sooraj.
  - screen: Petrol pump aur koyle ki khaan, khatam hote dabbe; har saal phir ugti fasal, ghaas, ped = renewable resource (re = phir se); ganne ke khet se ethanol, petrol mein milta hua; chhaton par solar panel; prayogshala mein banawati patti: roshni, paani, carbon dioxide andar, fuel bahar.
  - NCERT: p002-i6 The principles of photosynthesis as established by Calvin are, at present, being
  - kitaab se bahar: Ganne se bana ethanol petrol mein milaya jaata hai; chhaton par solar panel; banawati patti (artificial leaf) ki khoj.
**0-k15** · samjhana · Ab apni thaali yaad karo. Roti gehun se, daal chane se, aur doodh us gaay se jo ghaas khaati hai. Insaan samet sabhi jaanwar apne khane ke liye aakhir paudhon par hi nirbhar hain. Khana humein do cheezein deta hai: shareer banane ka material, aur kaam karne ki energy. Toh phir paudhe apna khana kahan se laate hain? Paudhe apna khana kahin se nahi laate; hare paudhe use khud banate hain. Woh carbon dioxide aur paani jaise chhote molecules ko jodkar sugar jaisa bada molecule banate hain. Jaise eenton ko jodkar deewar banti hai. Chhoti cheezon ko jodkar nayi, badi cheez banane ko hum synthesise karna kehte hain. Hare paudhe apna khana photosynthesis se synthesise karte hain. Baaki sab jeev apni zarooraton ke liye unhi par nirbhar hain. Baaki jeev isliye unpar nirbhar hain, kyunki woh khud apna khana nahi bana sakte. Isliye hare paudhon ko autotrophs kehte hain: auto yaani khud, aur troph yaani poshan. Yaani autotroph woh hai jiski apni rasoi hai, aur jo apna khana khud pakata hai. Apna khana khud banakar poshan paane ke is tareeke ko autotrophic nutrition kehte hain; nutrition yaani poshan. Aap pehle padh chuke ho ki autotrophic nutrition sirf paudhon mein milta hai. Paudhon aur jaanwaron ki baat karein, toh yahi sahi hai. Baaki sab jeev, jo khane ke liye hare paudhon par nirbhar hain, doosron ki rasoi ke khane par jeete hain. Aise jeevon ko heterotrophs kehte hain: hetero yaani doosra, yaani doosre se khana lene wale. Food chain yaad karo: ghaas ko hiran kha leta hai, aur hiran ko sher. Sher ka khana bhi aakhir ghaas se hi shuru hua. Par ek baareek baat bhi yaad rakho: kuch bacteria, jaise cyanobacteria, bhi apna khana khud banate hain.
  - terms: synthesise = Chhoti cheezon ko jodkar nayi, badi cheez banana.; autotrophs = Apna khana khud banane wale; auto yaani khud, troph yaani poshan.; autotrophic nutrition = Apna khana khud banakar poshan paane ka tareeka.; heterotrophs = Jo apna khana doosron se lete hain; hetero yaani doosra.
  - screen: Thaali: roti (gehun), daal (chana), doodh (ghaas khaati gaay), sab teer peeche paudhon tak; khana = material + energy; sawaal 'paudha khana kahan se?'; chhote molecule (carbon dioxide, paani) jud kar bada sugar molecule, eenton se deewar jaisa = synthesise; hara paudha apni rasoi ke saath: auto = khud, troph = poshan = autotrophs; doosron ki rasoi se khaate jaanwar: hetero = doosra = heterotrophs; ghaas → hiran → sher; kone mein cyanobacteria par 'khud banate'.
  - NCERT: p003-i3 All animals including human beings depend on plants for their food. Have you eve; p003-i4 You have already learnt that the autotrophic nutrition is found only in plants a
  - kitaab se bahar: Cyanobacteria jaise kuch bacteria bhi apna khana khud banate hain (Biological Classification).
**0-k16** · samjhana · Ab hare paudhe ke is khana banane ke kaam ko thoda aur kholte hain. Is kaam ka naam hai photosynthesis: photo yaani roshni, aur synthesis yaani jodkar banana. Socho, chhote molecules ko jodkar bada molecule banane mein energy lagti hai. Yeh energy roshni deti hai, isliye roshni hi is poore kaam ko aage dhakelti hai. Biomolecules wale chapter mein aapne dekha tha ki jeevon ke molecules mein carbon hota hai. Jaise sugar, starch, protein aur fat. Jeevon mein banne wale aise carbon wale compounds ko organic compounds kehte hain. Hare paudhe roshni ki energy se organic compounds ka synthesis karte hain. Yahan ek exam ki baareek baat hai. Carbon dioxide mein bhi carbon hai, phir bhi use organic compound nahi gina jaata. Kyunki organic compounds mein carbon hydrogen se juda hota hai, aksar carbon ki kadi bana kar. Carbon dioxide mein carbon ke saath ek bhi hydrogen nahi, isliye ise carbonates ki tarah inorganic gina jaata hai. Is kaam mein do tarah ke badlaav saath-saath chalte hain. Pehla, rang wale molecule roshni ko pakadte hain. Isme koi naya padarth nahi banta, jaise dhoop mein kaala kapda bas garam ho jaata hai. Aisa badlaav physical kehlata hai. Doosre badlaav mein carbon dioxide aur paani jud kar sugar jaisa naya padarth banate hain. Aisa badlaav chemical kehlata hai. Dono milkar is kaam ko physico-chemical process banate hain: physico yaani physical, aur chemical yaani rasayanik.
  - terms: photosynthesis = Roshni ki energy se chhote molecules jodkar khana banana; photo yaani roshni, synthesis yaani jodkar banana.; organic compounds = Carbon wale compounds jo jeevon mein bante hain, jaise sugar, starch, protein, fat.; physico-chemical = Physical (roshni ka pakda jaana) aur chemical (naye padarth banna) dono badlaav saath.
  - screen: Shabd todna: photo = roshni, synthesis = jodna; chhote molecule jud kar bada molecule, roshni ka teer energy deta; organic compounds: sugar, starch, protein, fat, har ek mein carbon; carbon dioxide par 'organic nahi' ka chhota tag, saath mein sugar ki carbon kadi par jude hydrogen aur CO₂ mein carbon ke saath sirf do oxygen (carbonates bhi inorganic); do khaane: physical (pigment roshni pakadta, dhoop mein kaala kapda garam) aur chemical (carbon dioxide + paani se naya padarth); dono milkar physico-chemical.
  - NCERT: p003-i5 Green plants carry out ‘photosynthesis’, a physico-chemical process by which the
  - kitaab se bahar: Carbon dioxide mein carbon hai par use organic compound nahi gina jaata, kyunki uske carbon se koi hydrogen nahi juda (carbonates ki tarah inorganic); dhoop mein kaala kapda (physical badlaav).
**0-k17** · samjhana · Ab zara socho: sher, hiran, aap aur main, sabki energy aakhir aati kahan se hai? Sabki energy aakhir sooraj ki roshni se aati hai, jo paudhon ke raaste hum tak pahunchti hai. Paudhe sooraj ki energy ko khane mein band karte hain. Yahi dharti par jeevan ki neev hai. Paudhon ke bina jeevan nahi chal sakta. Kyunki sooraj ki energy ko khane mein badalne ka kaam hare paudhe aur unke jaise kuch jeev hi karte hain. Isliye photosynthesis do kaaranon se bahut zaroori hai. Pehla kaaran: dharti ka saara khana yahin se shuru hota hai, chahe hum paudha khaayein ya paudha khaane wala jaanwar. Jahan se kisi cheez ki shuruaat hoti hai, use uska primary source kehte hain. Primary yaani sabse pehla. Photosynthesis dharti par saare khane ka primary source hai. Doosra kaaran: hum saans mein jo oxygen lete hain, woh hawa mein hoti hai. Dharti ko chaaron taraf se gherne wali hawa ki is parat ko hum atmosphere kehte hain. Hare paudhe photosynthesis mein oxygen bana kar atmosphere mein chhodte hain. Yaani paudhe ki is rasoi se dhuan nahi nikalta. Isse oxygen nikalti hai, jisse hum saans lete hain.
  - terms: primary source = Sabse pehla, mool srot; primary yaani pehla.; atmosphere = Dharti ko chaaron taraf se gherne wali hawa ki parat.
  - screen: Sher, hiran, ek bachcha: sabke peeche teer paudhe tak, aur paudhe ke peeche sooraj; do dabbe: 1 saara khana = primary source (seedha paudha, ya paudha khaane wala jaanwar), 2 oxygen; dharti ke chaaron taraf hawa ki parat = atmosphere, paudhe se oxygen usme jaati; paudhe ki rasoi (apni tasveer) se dhuan nahi, oxygen nikalti.
  - NCERT: p003-i6 Ultimately, all living forms on earth depend on sunlight for energy. The use of 
**0-k18** · sawaal · Ab ek sawaal par ruko: agar saans lene ke liye oxygen hi na ho, toh kya hoga? Saans lene ke liye oxygen na ho, toh hum kuch hi minute mein mar jaayenge. Yaad karo, hamari cells khane se kaam ki energy nikaalti hain, aur is kaam mein oxygen lagti hai. Oxygen na mile toh cells ko energy nahi milti, aur sabse pehle dimaag ki cells kaam karna band kar deti hain. Shuru mein dharti ki hawa mein oxygen na ke barabar thi. Aaj ki oxygen arabon saalon ke photosynthesis ki den hai. Matlab hamari har saans ke peeche hare paudhon ka kaam hai.
  - screen: Sawaal ka dabba: oxygen na ho toh?; saans lete insaan se oxygen cells tak, khane se energy; oxygen kat-te hi cells ki energy ki batti bujhti, dimaag sabse pehle; dharti ki purani hawa (oxygen nahi) se aaj ki hawa tak, beech mein hare jeev oxygen bharte hue; har saans ke peeche hari patti.
  - NCERT: p003-i7 Have you ever thought what would happen if there were no oxygen to breath [breat
  - kitaab se bahar: Oxygen ke bina dimaag ki cells kuch hi minute mein kaam band karti hain; dharti ki shuruaati hawa mein oxygen na ke barabar thi.
**0-k19** · samjhana · Toh is chapter mein hum is kaam ko andar se kholenge, do hisson mein. Pehla hissa: kaun-se hisse yeh kaam karte hain, aur patti ki cell mein woh kaise jamaaye gaye hain. Photosynthesis chalane wale in hisson aur unki banawat ko photosynthetic machinery kehte hain. Machinery yaani mashinon ka poora set. Rasoi ki zubaan mein samjho, toh yeh rasoi ke bartan, choolha aur unhe rakhne ka dhang hai. Doosra hissa: woh alag-alag reactions, jo light energy ko chemical energy mein badalti hain. Rasoi mein yeh pakane ke kadam hain: kya pehle hota hai, aur kya baad mein. Dono hisse saath isliye padhne hain, kyunki machinery ki banawat hi batati hai ki reactions kaise chalti hain.
  - terms: photosynthetic machinery = Photosynthesis chalane wale hisse aur unki banawat; machinery yaani mashinon ka set.
  - screen: Do khaane: 1 photosynthetic machinery (patti → cell → chloroplast, pigments aur unki jagah; saath mein rasoi ke bartan aur choolha), 2 reactions (roshni ki energy se chemical energy, kadam-dar-kadam; rasoi mein pakane ke kadam); dono ke beech teer: banawat batati hai kaam kaise chalta hai.
  - NCERT: p003-i8 This chapter focusses on the structure of the photosynthetic machinery and the v
**0-k20** · poori kahani dobara · Ab aaj ki poori baat ek saath jodte hain. Hamara khana aur hamari saans, dono hare paudhon ki den hain. Van Helmont ke willow ne dikhaya ki ped ka wazan mitti se nahi aata. Calvin ke C¹⁴ wale prayog ne dikhaya ki sugar ka carbon hawa ki carbon dioxide se aata hai. Hare paudhe roshni ki energy se carbon dioxide aur paani jodkar khana banate hain. Yahi photosynthesis hai, aur isi wajah se woh autotrophs hain. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: chhota sa beej bada ped banta hai, toh uska saara wazan aata kahan se hai? Ped ka saara wazan zyadatar hawa ki carbon dioxide aur paani se aata hai. Patti inhe roshni ki madad se khane mein badal deti hai. Bilkul wahi rasoi: kachcha saamaan hawa aur paani se, aag sooraj ki, aur pakwaan se banta hai poora ped. Mitti se toh bas thode minerals aate hain, jaise khane mein namak. Par yeh sab hum pakka kaise jaante hain? Paudha sach mein khana banata hai, aur iske liye use kya-kya chahiye, yeh kaise pata chale? Paudhe ko khana banane ke liye kya-kya chahiye, yeh hum pakka kaise jaante hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Ek line mein poori kahani: thaali aur saans → hare paudhe; van Helmont ka willow (mitti −57 gram); C¹⁴ wala carbon sugar mein; patti mein carbon dioxide + paani + roshni → khana = photosynthesis, autotrophs; shuru ka sawaal phir: bada ped, uska wazan hawa aur paani se, rasoi (apni tasveer) ke pakwaan se banta ped; mitti se bas thode minerals, khane mein namak jaise; aakhir mein sawaal khula: paudha sach mein khana banata hai, use kya-kya chahiye?

## 11.1 WHAT DO WE KNOW?

_Maqsad: Patti ke andar khana bante koi nahi dekh sakta; toh hum pakka kaise jaante hain ki patti khana banati hai, aur use kya-kya chahiye?_

_Misaal: rasoi: roshni choolhe ki aag, carbon dioxide aata, chlorophyll tawa jo aag ki garmi pakad kar roti sekta hai; starch dabbe mein rakhi roti; destarching kal ki bachi roti nikal kar dabba khaali karna; har prayog mein ek hi cheez hatana: aag bujhana, tawa hatana ya aata hatana_

Kitab ki baatein (35):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Is hisse mein dekhte hain ki photosynthesis ke baare mein hum pehle se kya jaante hain (What do we know).
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · Pichhli classes ke kuch saral prayogon ne dikhaya hai ki photosynthesis hone ke liye chlorophyll, light (roshni) aur CO₂ zaroori hain.
- f3 · kitab kehti hai · chlorophyll · Chlorophyll patti ka hara pigment hai.
- f4 · shabd ka matlab · chlorophyll · Chlorophyll: patti ko hara rang dene wala pigment, jo roshni pakadta hai; chloro = hara, phyll = patti.
- f5 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Pigment: rang dene wala padarth, jo roshni (light) ka kuch hissa absorb karta, yaani sokh leta hai.
- f6 · pehle ki zaroori baat · CO₂ carbon dioxide ka chemical formula hai; formula mein neeche likha chhota ank (subscript) atoms ginta hai: ek carbon, do oxygen.
- f7 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Photosynthesis mein hara paudha roshni ki energy se carbon dioxide aur paani jodkar organic food banata hai; yeh khana carbohydrates hai, jaise sugar aur starch.
- f8 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Light energy ka srot hai: photosynthesis ko chalane wali energy roshni deti hai, aur carbon dioxide khane ka carbon deti hai.
- f9 · teacher ki extra baat · Patti mein pehle sugar banti hai; bachi sugar jama hone ke liye starch ban jaati hai; isliye naya starch milna is baat ki nishaani hai ki patti ne khana banaya.
- f10 · shabd ka matlab · starch · Starch: bahut saare glucose ek lambi zanjeer mein jud kar bana bada carbohydrate; paudhe ka jama kiya hua khana, jo aaloo, chawal aur gehun mein bhara hota hai.
- f11 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Starch ek stored, yaani jama kiya hua, carbohydrate hai (Biomolecules chapter).
- f12 · teacher ki extra baat · iodine · Iodine test: iodine ka ghol bhoora-peela hota hai; starch se milte hi rang neela-kaala (blue-black) ho jaata hai.
- f13 · teacher ki extra baat · Test se pehle patti ko paani mein ubaalte hain, phir garam paani ke bartan mein rakhe alcohol mein garam karte hain; alcohol chlorophyll ghol leta hai aur patti ka rang halka ho jaata hai; alcohol jaldi aag pakadta hai, isliye use seedhe aag par nahi rakhte; phir patti dhokar iodine daalte hain.
- f14 · teacher ki extra baat · destarching · Destarching: prayog se pehle paudhe ko do-teen din andhere mein rakhna, taaki pattiyan apna purana starch kharch kar dein; tab jo starch milega, woh prayog ke dauraan bana hoga; de = hatana.
- f15 · kitab kehti hai · Starch banne ka prayog do pattiyon par kiya jaata hai: ek variegated leaf, aur ek patti jiska kuch hissa black paper se dhaka ho; dono ko roshni mein rakha jaata hai.
- f16 · kitab kehti hai · In pattiyon mein starch dhoondhne par saaf hua ki photosynthesis sirf patti ke hare hisson mein aur roshni hone par hi hui.
- f17 · shabd ka matlab · variegated leaf · Variegated leaf: aisi patti jisme hare hisson ke beech safed ya peele hisse hon; variegated = chitkabri, kai rangon wali; jaise croton aur money plant ki kuch kismein.
- f18 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chlorophyll patti ke hare hisson mein hi hota hai; variegated patti ke safed ya peele hisson mein chlorophyll nahi hota.
- f19 · shabd ka matlab · black paper · Black paper: kaala kaagaz jo roshni ko aar-paar nahi jaane deta; patti ke ek hisse par dono taraf lagakar us hisse ki roshni rok di jaati hai.
- f20 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Controlled comparison: ek baar mein sirf ek cheez (one factor) badlo aur baaki sab ek jaisa rakho; tab nateeje ka farq usi cheez ki wajah se maana ja sakta hai.
- f21 · teacher ki extra baat · Variegated patti ke safed hisse ko roshni aur hawa mili, par chlorophyll nahi tha; black paper ke neeche ke hisse mein chlorophyll tha, par roshni nahi thi.
- f22 · kitab kehti hai · Ek prayog mein patti ka ek hissa test tube mein band kiya jaata hai, jisme KOH mein bhigi rui hoti hai jo CO₂ sokhti hai; patti ka doosra aadha hissa khuli hawa mein rehta hai.
- f23 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh setup kuch der roshni mein rakha jaata hai.
- f24 · kitab kehti hai · Baad mein dono hisson mein starch dhoondhne par khula hissa starch ke liye tested positive aata hai, aur tube wala hissa tested negative.
- f25 · kitab kehti hai · Isse dikha ki photosynthesis ke liye CO₂ zaroori hai.
- f26 · shabd ka matlab · KOH · KOH: potassium hydroxide; K = potassium, O = oxygen, H = hydrogen; hawa ki carbon dioxide ko sokh leta hai.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · tested positive · Tested positive: test mein jo cheez dhoondh rahe the, woh mil gayi; na mile toh tested negative.
- f28 · shabd ka matlab · test tube · Test tube: kaanch ki patli nali jo ek taraf se band hoti hai; kaanch hone se roshni andar tak jaati hai.
- f29 · teacher ki extra baat · Nali ke munh par cork lagate hain, jiski daraar se patti andar jaati hai, taaki bahar ki hawa andar na aaye; exam mein is prayog ko Moll ka half-leaf experiment bhi kehte hain.
- f30 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: is prayog se yeh nateeja (conclusion) kaise nikala ja sakta hai ki CO₂ zaroori hai?
- f31 · teacher ki extra baat · Nateeja aise nikalta hai: dono hisse ek hi patti ke, ek hi paudhe par, ek jaisi roshni mein, dono mein chlorophyll; farq sirf CO₂ ka (KOH ne nali ki CO₂ sokh li); starch sirf CO₂ wale hisse mein bana, isliye CO₂ zaroori hai.
- f32 · teacher ki extra baat · Bahar wala hissa tulna ka kaam karta hai: woh dikhata hai ki isi patti mein, inhi halaat mein starch ban sakta tha; uske bina akela negative nateeja kuch saabit nahi karta.
- f33 · teacher ki extra baat · Nali kaanch ki hoti hai, isliye roshni andar wale hisse tak pahunchti hai; KOH sirf CO₂ sokhta hai, oxygen nahi.
- f34 · teacher ki extra baat · Paani bhi photosynthesis ke liye zaroori hai, par use is tarah hata kar parakhna mushkil hai: paani ke bina patti murjha jaati hai, aur farq ki wajah alag nahi ho paati.
- f35 · teacher ki extra baat · Exam ki jodi: variegated leaf se chlorophyll, black paper se light, aur KOH se CO₂ ki zaroorat parakhi jaati hai.

**11.1-k1** · yaad · Pichhli baar humne van Helmont ka willow dekha: ped paanch saal mein lagbhag 75 kilo badha, par mitti sirf 57 gram ghati. Phir Calvin ne C¹⁴ wale carbon dioxide ka peechha kiya, aur woh carbon seedha paudhe ki sugar tak pahuncha. Yaad rakhein: hara paudha roshni ki energy se carbon dioxide aur paani jodkar apna khana khud banata hai, aur isi kaam ka naam photosynthesis hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki is baat par hum itna pakka yakeen kyun kar sakte hain. Ab ek ajeeb baat socho: patti ke andar jhaank kar koi bhi khana bante hue nahi dekh sakta. Sugar ke molecule itne chhote hote hain ki sabse achhe microscope mein bhi nahi dikhte. Toh hum pakka kaise jaante hain ki patti sach mein khana banati hai, aur use iske liye kya-kya chahiye? Patti khana banati hai ya nahi, aur use kya-kya chahiye, iska pakka jawab kuch saral prayog dete hain.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: tarazu par willow ka ped (+75 kilo) aur mitti ka gamla (sirf 57 gram kam); C¹⁴ ka chamakta carbon hawa se patti ki sugar tak; neeche 'Yaad rakhein: roshni + carbon dioxide + paani se khana = photosynthesis'; phir ek hari patti par magnifier, andar kuch dikhta nahi, sugar ke nanhe molecule microscope mein bhi gaayab; sawaal ka dabba: patti khana banati hai, pakka kaise? use kya-kya chahiye?
  - kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (opening) ki yaad: van Helmont ka willow aur Calvin ka C¹⁴; sugar ke molecule microscope mein nahi dikhte.
**11.1-k2** · samjhana · Is hisse ka naam hai What do we know, yaani is baare mein hum pehle se kya jaante hain. Class 7 aur Class 10 mein aapne pattiyon par kuch saral prayog kiye honge. Un prayogon ne dikhaya tha ki khana banane ke liye patti ko teen cheezein chahiye: apna hara rang, roshni aur carbon dioxide. Pigment ka matlab aap jaante ho: rang dene wala padarth, jo roshni ka kuch hissa sokh leta hai. Patti ki cells ke andar ek hara pigment bhara hota hai. Yahi patti ko hara rang deta hai, aur yahi roshni ko pakadta hai. Isi hare pigment ko chlorophyll kehte hain: chloro yaani hara, aur phyll yaani patti. CO₂ wahi carbon dioxide hai jisse hum mil chuke hain. Neeche likha chhota 2 batata hai ki ek carbon ke saath oxygen ke do atom jude hain. Roshni is kaam ki energy deti hai, aur carbon dioxide woh carbon deti hai jisse khana banta hai. Toh pichhli classes ka nichod yeh hai: photosynthesis ke liye chlorophyll, light aur CO₂, teeno zaroori hain. Rasoi wali misaal se samjho: roti banane ke liye aag, tawa aur aata, teeno chahiye. Roshni aag hai, carbon dioxide aata hai, aur chlorophyll tawa hai, jo aag ki garmi pakad kar roti sekta hai. Inme se ek bhi na ho, toh roti nahi banegi. Par yeh baat pakki kaise hui ki teeno zaroori hain? Teeno zaroori hain, yeh pakka hua ek seedhe tareeke se: ek baar mein ek cheez hata kar dekha gaya ki khana bana ya nahi. Par jab khana patti ke andar dikhta hi nahi, toh woh bana ya nahi, yeh pata kaise chalega?
  - terms: chlorophyll = Patti ko hara rang dene wala pigment, jo roshni pakadta hai; chloro yaani hara, phyll yaani patti.
  - screen: Shabd 'What do we know?'; Class 7 aur Class 10 ki copy mein patti ke prayog; teen dabbe: hara rang, roshni, carbon dioxide; patti ki cell ke andar hare daane jo roshni ki kirnein pakadte; naam todna: chloro = hara, phyll = patti, chlorophyll; CO₂ molecule: ek carbon, do oxygen, chhote 2 par nishaan; roshni se energy ka teer, carbon dioxide se carbon ka teer; nichod: chlorophyll + light + CO₂; rasoi: choolhe ki aag (= roshni), tawa (= chlorophyll) garmi pakadta, aata (= carbon dioxide), aur roti; ek cheez hatate hi roti gaayab; aakhir mein patti par sawaal: andar ka khana dikhega kaise?
  - NCERT: p003-i10 Let us try to find out what we already know about photosynthesis. Some simple ex
  - kitaab se bahar: Class 7 aur Class 10 ke prayogon ki yaad; chloro = hara, phyll = patti; rasoi ki misaal (aag = roshni, tawa = chlorophyll, aata = carbon dioxide).
**11.1-k3** · activity · Khana bana ya nahi, iska pata hum patti ke jama khane se lagate hain. Yaad karo, patti mein sabse pehle sugar banti hai, jo paani mein ghul jaati hai. Par paudha saari sugar usi waqt kharch nahi karta. Bachi hui sugar ko woh jama karke rakh leta hai. Biomolecules wale chapter mein aapne dekha tha ki bahut saare glucose jud kar ek lambi zanjeer banate hain. Glucose ki isi lambi zanjeer ko starch kehte hain. Yahi paudhe ka jama kiya hua khana hai, yaani ek stored carbohydrate. Aaloo, chawal aur gehun mein jo safed sa bhara hota hai, woh zyadatar starch hi hai. Rasoi ki zubaan mein, starch woh roti hai jo bana kar dabbe mein rakh di gayi. Isliye patti mein naya starch mile, toh samajh lo ki patti ne khana banaya tha. Ab starch dhoondhne ka ek aasaan tareeka hai, jo aapne lab mein dekha hoga. Iodine ka ghol bhoora-peela hota hai. Starch se milte hi uska rang gehra neela-kaala ho jaata hai. Par hari patti par iodine daalo, toh neela rang dikhega kaise? Hari patti par neela rang isliye nahi dikhta, kyunki patti ka gehra hara rang use chhupa leta hai. Isliye hum pehle patti ko kuch minute paani mein ubaalte hain, taaki woh naram ho jaaye. Phir hum use alcohol mein daalte hain, aur alcohol wale bartan ko garam paani mein rakh kar garam karte hain. Alcohol chlorophyll ko ghol leta hai, aur patti ka rang halka ho jaata hai. Alcohol jaldi aag pakadta hai, isliye use kabhi seedhe aag par nahi rakhte. Phir hum patti ko paani se dhokar us par iodine daalte hain. Jahan starch hoga, wahan neela-kaala rang ubhar aayega. Par yahan ek dhokha ho sakta hai: agar patti mein kal ka bana starch pehle se pada ho, toh? Kal ka bana starch pehle se pada ho, toh hum keh hi nahi paayenge ki aaj naya starch bana ya nahi. Bilkul jaise rasoi ke dabbe mein kal ki roti padi ho, toh aaj roti bani ya nahi, pata hi nahi chalega. Isliye prayog se pehle hum gamle ke paudhe ko do-teen din andhere mein rakhte hain. Andhere mein naya khana nahi banta, aur pattiyan apna jama starch kharch kar deti hain. Patti se purane starch ko is tarah hata dene ko destarching kehte hain: de yaani hatana. Ab prayog mein jo bhi starch milega, woh prayog ke dauraan bana naya starch hi hoga.
  - terms: starch = Bahut saare glucose ki lambi zanjeer se bana carbohydrate; paudhe ka jama kiya hua khana.; destarching = Prayog se pehle paudhe ko do-teen din andhere mein rakh kar pattiyon ka purana starch kharch karwa dena; de yaani hatana.
  - screen: Patti ki cell mein sugar ke chhote daane bante, kuch kaam mein jaate, baaki jud kar starch ke daane; glucose ke motiyon ki lambi zanjeer = starch; aaloo, chawal, gehun ke daanon mein starch; rasoi ka dabba jisme roti rakhi; iodine ka bhoora-peela ghol starch par girte hi neela-kaala; hari patti par neela rang chhupa hua; kadam: paani mein ubalti patti, garam paani ke bartan mein alcohol ki katori, patti ka rang utarta, seedhi aag par laal kaata nishaan, phir dhokar iodine aur neela-kaala dhabba; dabbe mein kal ki roti par sawaal ka nishaan; gamla andhere kamre mein, calendar par 2-3 din, pattiyon ka starch ghat-ta; shabd: de = hatana, destarching.
  - kitaab se bahar: Iodine test (iodine bhoora-peela, starch se neela-kaala), patti ubaalna aur garam paani ke bartan mein alcohol se chlorophyll nikaalna, aur destarching (do-teen din andhera) prayog samajhne ke liye jode gaye hain; starch glucose ki zanjeer hai (Biomolecules).
**11.1-k4** · example · Ab pehla prayog dekho, jo patti ke hare rang ki zaroorat parakhta hai. Aapne kuch paudhon ki pattiyon par hare hisson ke beech safed ya peele dhabbe dekhe honge, jaise croton ya money plant ki kuch kismon mein. Aisi chitkabri patti ko variegated leaf kehte hain; variegated yaani kai rangon wali. Safed ya peele hisson mein chlorophyll nahi hota. Chlorophyll sirf hare hisson mein hota hai. Hum destarch kiye gaye paudhe ki ek variegated leaf lete hain, aur uske hare aur safed hisson ka naksha kaagaz par bana lete hain. Phir hum paudhe ko kuch ghante tez dhoop mein rakh dete hain. Rasoi ki zubaan mein, is patti ke kuch hisson mein tawa hai hi nahi, jabki aag aur aata sabko mile hain. Ab socho, iodine daalne par patti ke kaun-se hisse neele-kaale honge? Iodine daalne par sirf hare hisse neele-kaale hote hain. Safed hisse iodine ka bhoora-peela rang hi rakhte hain. Naksha milaao, toh neela-kaala rang theek wahin aata hai jahan pehle hara rang tha. Safed hisson ko bhi utni hi roshni aur hawa mili thi. Toh wahan starch kyun nahi bana? Safed hisson mein starch isliye nahi bana, kyunki wahan chlorophyll nahi tha jo roshni pakad sake. Toh is prayog se pata chala ki khana sirf patti ke hare hisson mein banta hai, yaani chlorophyll zaroori hai. Rasoi mein bhi bina tawe ke aag kitni bhi tez ho, roti nahi sikti.
  - terms: variegated leaf = Hare hisson ke beech safed ya peele hisson wali patti; variegated yaani kai rangon wali, chitkabri.
  - screen: Croton aur money plant ki chitkabri pattiyan; shabd: variegated = kai rangon wali; safed hisson par 'chlorophyll nahi'; patti ka hara-safed naksha kaagaz par; gamla tez dhoop mein, ghadi par kuch ghante; rasoi ka chhota chitra: aag aur aata hain, kuch jagah tawa gaayab; sawaal: kaun-se hisse neele-kaale?; iodine ke baad patti: hare hisse neele-kaale, safed hisse bhoore-peele; naksha upar rakh kar milaana; safed hisson par roshni aur hawa ke teer, phir bhi starch nahi; nateeja: chlorophyll zaroori; bina tawe ke aag par kachchi roti.
  - NCERT: p003-i11 You may have carried out the experiment to look for starch formation in two leav
  - kitaab se bahar: Variegated patti ke udaharan croton aur money plant; prayog se pehle patti ka naksha banana; safed hisse ko bhi roshni aur hawa mili thi.
**11.1-k5** · example · Ab doosra prayog dekho, jo roshni ki zaroorat parakhta hai. Is baar hum destarch kiye paudhe ki ek poori hari patti lete hain, jisme har jagah chlorophyll hai. Uske ek hisse ko kaale kaagaz ki ek patti se dhak dete hain, upar se bhi aur neeche se bhi. Dono taraf isliye dhakte hain, taaki roshni kisi bhi taraf se us hisse tak na pahunche. Roshni ko aar-paar na jaane dene wala yahi kaala kaagaz black paper hai. Phir paudhe ko kuch ghante roshni mein rakhte hain, aur kaagaz hata kar patti par iodine wala test karte hain. Ab batao, is baar patti ka kaun-sa hissa neela-kaala nahi hoga? Is baar wahi hissa neela-kaala nahi hota jo kaagaz ke neeche dhaka tha. Baaki poori patti neeli-kaali ho jaati hai. Dhake hisse mein bhi wahi chlorophyll tha, wahi hara rang. Use bas roshni nahi mili. Toh is prayog se pata chala ki roshni ke bina khana nahi banta, yaani light zaroori hai. Rasoi mein yeh aag bujha dene jaisa hai: tawa aur aata wahin hain, par aag ke bina roti nahi banti. Ab dono prayog saath rakho. Photosynthesis sirf patti ke hare hisson mein hui, aur wahan bhi tabhi hui jab roshni mili. Exam ke liye yeh jodi pakki kar lo: variegated leaf wala prayog chlorophyll parakhta hai, aur black paper wala prayog roshni.
  - terms: black paper = Kaala kaagaz jo roshni ko aar-paar nahi jaane deta; patti ke ek hisse par dono taraf lagakar us hisse ki roshni roki jaati hai.
  - screen: Ek poori hari patti, har jagah chlorophyll; beech mein kaale kaagaz ki patti upar aur neeche dono taraf clip; dhoop ke teer kaagaz par ruk jaate; label: black paper; ghadi par kuch ghante, phir kaagaz hatana aur iodine; nateeja: poori patti neeli-kaali, sirf dhaka patta bhoora-peela; dhake hisse par 'chlorophyll hai, roshni nahi'; rasoi: tawa aur aata, choolhe ki aag bujhi, kachchi roti; dono pattiyan saath: hare hisse + roshni = starch; neeche jodi: variegated leaf se chlorophyll, black paper se roshni.
  - kitaab se bahar: Kaala kaagaz patti ke dono taraf lagaya jaata hai taaki kisi taraf se roshni na pahunche.
**11.1-k6** · example · Ab teesra prayog dekho, jo carbon dioxide ki zaroorat parakhta hai. Par patti ko carbon dioxide se kaise bachaayein, jab hawa mein har jagah carbon dioxide hai? Patti ko carbon dioxide se bachaane ka tareeka hai: uske aas-paas ki hawa se carbon dioxide sokh lo. Jaise sookhi rui paani sokh leti hai, waise hi kuch rasayan hawa se carbon dioxide sokh lete hain. Aisa hi ek rasayan hai KOH, yaani potassium hydroxide: K potassium ka nishaan hai, O oxygen ka, aur H hydrogen ka. KOH hawa ki carbon dioxide ko pakad kar apne saath baandh leta hai, aur woh carbon dioxide hawa mein nahi bachti. Ab hum ek test tube lete hain, yaani kaanch ki ek patli nali, jo ek taraf se band hoti hai. Nali mein hum KOH mein bhigi thodi rui daal dete hain. Phir destarch kiye paudhe ki ek patti ka aadha hissa is nali ke andar daalte hain. Patti paudhe se judi rehti hai, aur uska baaki aadha hissa bahar khuli hawa mein rehta hai. Nali ke munh par ek cork lagate hain, jiski daraar se patti andar jaati hai, taaki bahar ki hawa andar na aaye. Phir is poore setup ko kuch der roshni mein rakh dete hain. Nali kaanch ki hai, isliye roshni andar wale hisse tak bhi barabar pahunchti hai. Rasoi ki zubaan mein, is baar aata hata diya gaya hai, jabki aag aur tawa dono maujood hain. Kuch der baad hum dono hisson par starch wala test karte hain. Aapne suna hoga ki doctor ka koi test positive aaya; matlab jo cheez dhoondhi ja rahi thi, woh mil gayi. Isi tarah bahar wala hissa neela-kaala hota hai, yaani usne starch ke liye tested positive kiya, starch mila. Nali ke andar wala hissa bhoora-peela rehta hai. Woh tested negative hai, yaani wahan starch nahi mila. Isse dikha ki photosynthesis ke liye CO₂ zaroori hai. Exam mein is prayog ko Moll ka half-leaf experiment bhi kehte hain, kyunki isme aadhi patti andar aur aadhi bahar hoti hai.
  - terms: KOH = Potassium hydroxide; K = potassium, O = oxygen, H = hydrogen; hawa ki carbon dioxide sokh leta hai.; test tube = Kaanch ki patli nali jo ek taraf se band hoti hai.; tested positive = Test mein jo cheez dhoondh rahe the, woh mil gayi; na mile toh tested negative.
  - screen: Teesre prayog ka dabba: carbon dioxide; hawa mein har jagah CO₂ ke chhote molecule; sookhi rui paani sokhti; KOH ki bottle, label: K = potassium, O = oxygen, H = hydrogen; KOH CO₂ molecule pakad kar baandh leta; kaanch ki test tube, neeche KOH mein bhigi rui; destarch kiya paudha, patti ka aadha hissa cork ki daraar se nali mein, aadha bahar hawa mein; poora setup dhoop mein, roshni kaanch ke aar-paar; rasoi: aag aur tawa, aata gaayab; iodine ke baad: bahar ka aadha neela-kaala (tested positive), andar ka aadha bhoora-peela (tested negative); doctor ki report par 'positive'; nateeja: CO₂ zaroori; label: Moll ka half-leaf experiment.
  - NCERT: p004-i1 Another experiment you may have carried out where a part of a leaf is enclosed i
  - kitaab se bahar: Nali ke munh par cork; doctor ke test positive ki misaal; exam mein is prayog ka naam: Moll ka half-leaf experiment.
**11.1-k7** · ruko, socho · Ab woh sawaal, jo is prayog ke saath aapse poocha jaata hai. Is prayog se yeh nateeja, yaani conclusion, kaise nikala ja sakta hai ki CO₂ zaroori hai? Yeh nateeja nikala ja sakta hai ek tulna se, aur uska clue yeh hai: dono hisson ki har cheez ek-ek karke milaao. Ruko, pehle khud socho: dono hisson mein kya-kya ek jaisa tha, aur kya alag tha? Aapne socha hoga ki dono hisse ek hi patti ke the, ek hi paudhe par the, aur dono ko ek jaisi roshni mili. Dono mein chlorophyll bhi tha, kyunki patti poori hari thi. Alag sirf ek cheez thi: nali ke andar KOH ne carbon dioxide sokh li thi, aur bahar carbon dioxide maujood thi. Aur starch sirf usi hisse mein bana jise carbon dioxide mili. Jahan carbon dioxide nahi thi, wahan khana nahi bana. Jab sirf ek cheez badli ho aur nateeja badal jaaye, toh nateeje ki wajah wahi cheez hoti hai. Isliye hum kehte hain ki CO₂ zaroori hai. Par bahar wale hisse ki zaroorat hi kya thi? Sirf nali wala hissa test kar lete toh? Sirf nali wala hissa dekhte, toh starch na milne par yeh shak reh jaata ki shaayad patti mein hi koi gadbad thi. Bahar wala hissa dikhata hai ki isi patti mein, inhi halaat mein starch ban sakta tha. Isliye usse tulna karke hi nateeja pakka hota hai. Aksar bachche sochte hain ki nali ke andar starch isliye nahi bana kyunki nali ne roshni rok di. Par aisa nahi hai: nali kaanch ki thi, aur roshni aar-paar gayi. Aur yaad rakho, KOH ne sirf carbon dioxide sokhi thi, oxygen nahi. Isliye yeh prayog carbon dioxide ki hi baat karta hai.
  - screen: Sawaal ka dabba: 'yeh nateeja (conclusion) kaise nikla?'; clue: dono hisson ki tulna ki table; 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath bada ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk); table bharti hai: ek hi patti (dono), ek hi paudha (dono), roshni (dono), chlorophyll (dono), CO₂ (bahar haan, andar nahi, KOH ki wajah se), starch (bahar haan, andar nahi); ek hi khaana badla to nateeja badla; akeli nali wali patti par 'shak'; bahar wali patti par 'tulna' ka label; galat soch 'nali ne roshni roki' par kaata nishaan, kaanch ke aar-paar roshni ke teer; KOH sirf CO₂ pakadta, O₂ ko chhod deta.
  - NCERT: p004-i2 Can you explain how this conclusion could be drawn?
  - kitaab se bahar: Bahar wale hisse se tulna kyun zaroori hai; kaanch ki nali se roshni pahunchti hai; KOH oxygen nahi sokhta.
  - jawab: Dono hisse ek hi patti ke, ek hi paudhe par, ek jaisi roshni mein the, aur dono mein chlorophyll tha; farq sirf CO₂ ka tha, kyunki nali ke andar KOH ne CO₂ sokh li. Starch sirf CO₂ wale hisse mein bana, isliye CO₂ zaroori hai. Bahar wala hissa tulna ka kaam karta hai: woh dikhata hai ki isi patti mein starch ban sakta tha.
  - aam galti: Galat soch: nali ne roshni rok di, ya KOH ne oxygen hatayi. Nali kaanch ki thi, roshni pahunchi; KOH sirf CO₂ sokhta hai. Bahar wale hisse ke bina akela negative nateeja kuch saabit nahi karta.
**11.1-k8** · sawaal · Ab teeno prayog ek saath rakh kar dekho. Variegated leaf ke safed hisse ko roshni aur carbon dioxide mili, par chlorophyll nahi tha. Black paper ke neeche wale hisse mein chlorophyll tha, par roshni nahi thi. Aur nali ke andar wale hisse mein chlorophyll aur roshni dono the, par carbon dioxide nahi thi. Yaani har prayog mein sirf ek cheez hatayi gayi, aur baaki sab waisa hi rakha gaya. Ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badal kar baaki sab barabar rakhne wali is tulna ko controlled comparison kehte hain. Rasoi mein bhi hum yahi karte: kabhi aag bujhao, kabhi tawa hatao, kabhi aata, aur har baar dekho ki roti bani ya nahi. Agar ek saath aag bhi bujha do aur aata bhi hata do, aur roti na bane, toh kya bataoge? Aag aur aata ek saath hataye, toh aap bata hi nahi paoge ki roti kis kami se nahi bani. Aksar log sochte hain ki photosynthesis ke liye bas yahi teen cheezein chahiye. Par aisa nahi hai: paani bhi utna hi zaroori hai. Toh paani ko bhi aise hata kar kyun nahi parakhte? Paani ko aise hata kar parakhna mushkil hai, kyunki paani ke bina patti khud murjha jaati hai. Phir starch na bane, toh pata nahi chalega ki starch paani ki kami se nahi bana, ya patti ke murjhaane se. Paani ka asli kaam kya hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - terms: controlled comparison = Ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badal kar baaki sab barabar rakhne wali tulna.
  - screen: Teen pattiyan ek line mein: variegated (safed hissa: roshni haan, CO₂ haan, chlorophyll nahi), black paper (chlorophyll haan, roshni nahi), nali (chlorophyll haan, roshni haan, CO₂ nahi); har ek mein sirf ek khaana laal; label: controlled comparison; rasoi ke teen chitra: aag bujhi, tawa gaayab, aata gaayab; phir ek saath aag aur aata gaayab par sawaal ka nishaan; galat soch 'bas teen cheezein' par kaata nishaan, saath mein paani ki boond; murjhaayi patti par '?'; paani ki boond par 'aage'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Rasoi mein ek saath do cheezein hatane ki misaal; paani ko is tarah kyun nahi parakhte (patti murjha jaati hai).
**11.1-k9** · poori kahani dobara · Ab aaj ki poori baat ek saath jodte hain. Patti mein bana khana starch bankar jama hota hai, aur iodine use neela-kaala karke dikha deta hai. Purana starch hatane ke liye paudhe ko pehle andhere mein rakhte hain, aur patti ka chlorophyll alcohol se nikaal kar test karte hain. Variegated leaf ne dikhaya ki chlorophyll zaroori hai, black paper ne dikhaya ki roshni, aur KOH wali nali ne dikhaya ki CO₂. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: jo khana dikhta hi nahi, uske baare mein hum itne pakke kaise hain, aur patti ko kya-kya chahiye? Jo khana dikhta nahi, uske baare mein hum pakke isliye hain, kyunki uski nishaani dikhti hai: roshni mein rakhi patti mein naya starch milta hai. Aur patti ko kya-kya chahiye, yeh humne ek-ek cheez hata kar jaana. Chlorophyll, light aur CO₂ mein se ek bhi na ho, toh starch nahi banta. Bilkul rasoi ki tarah: aag, tawa aur aata mein se ek bhi na ho, toh dabbe mein nayi roti nahi milti. Par ek samay aisa bhi tha, jab kisi ko pata hi nahi tha ki hawa mein alag-alag gases hoti hain. Tab log dekhte the ki band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai. Usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga? Jar mein hara paudha rakhne par kya hoga, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Ek line mein kahani: patti mein sugar se starch ke daane, iodine se neela-kaala; andhera kamra (destarching), alcohol mein patti ka rang utarna; teen pattiyan: variegated se chlorophyll, black paper se roshni, KOH nali se CO₂; shuru ka sawaal phir: khana dikhta nahi, par neela-kaala nishaan dikhta hai; rasoi: aag + tawa + aata se dabbe mein nayi roti, ek bhi gaayab to dabba khaali; phir purane zamaane ki prayogshala: band kaanch ka jar, jalti mombatti bujhti; jar mein hara paudha rakhte hi bada sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.

## 11.2 EARLY EXPERIMENTS

_Maqsad: Band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai; usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun?_

_Misaal: jasoos ki file: photosynthesis ek raaz, har vaigyanik ek suraag jodta hai (suraag = prayog ka nateeja); Priestley: hara paudha band hawa theek karta hai; Ingenhousz: sirf roshni mein, aur oxygen ke bulbule sirf hare hisson se; Sachs: glucose chloroplast wale hare hisson mein; Engelmann: neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada kaam ki; oxygen par do shaq, CO₂ aur paani; van Niel ka saancha (A ki jagah oxygen ya sulphur) se faisla: oxygen paani ki; isotope ka nishaan ungliyon ke nishaan jaisa pakka saboot_

Kitab ki baatein (103):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Saral prayogon ki ek lambi kadi ne dheere-dheere photosynthesis ki samajh banayi; in prayogon ke baare mein jaanna dilchasp hai.
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · Joseph Priestley (1733-1804) ne 1770 mein prayogon ki ek series ki, jisne hare paudhon ke badhne mein hawa ki zaroori bhoomika dikhayi.
- f3 · kitab kehti hai · Priestley ne 1774 mein oxygen khoji.
- f4 · shabd ka matlab · oxygen · Oxygen: hawa ki woh gas jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai; formula O₂.
- f5 · teacher ki extra baat · Prayog 1770 mein hue aur oxygen 1774 mein khoji gayi; yaani prayog ke waqt Priestley ko us gas ka naam bhi pata nahi tha.
- f6 · kitab kehti hai · bell jar · Band jagah, yaani bell jar, mein jalti mombatti jaldi bujh jaati hai; band jagah mein chooha bhi jaldi suffocate ho jaata hai.
- f7 · kitab kehti hai · Priestley ka nateeja: jalti mombatti aur saans lene wala jaanwar, dono kisi tarah hawa ko kharab karte hain.
- f8 · kitab kehti hai · mint · Usi bell jar mein mint (pudina) ka paudha rakhne par chooha zinda raha aur mombatti jalti rahi.
- f9 · kitab kehti hai · Priestley ki hypothesis: saans lene wale jaanwar aur jalti mombatti hawa se jo hatate hain, paudhe use hawa mein restore karte hain, yaani lauta dete hain.
- f10 · shabd ka matlab · bell jar · Bell jar: ghanti jaisa ulta kaanch ka bartan jo andar ki hawa ko bahar ki hawa se alag, band rakhta hai; bell = ghanti, jar = bartan.
- f11 · shabd ka matlab · suffocate · Suffocate: saans ke liye zaroori hawa na milne se dam ghutna.
- f12 · shabd ka matlab · hypothesis · Hypothesis: dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye diya gaya vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake; hypothesised = aisa vichaar saamne rakha.
- f13 · shabd ka matlab · restore · Restore: jo ghat gaya ya hat gaya, use phir se bhar dena, lauta dena.
- f14 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Mombatti ka jalna aur jaanwar ki saans, dono ke liye hawa chahiye; dono hawa ki oxygen kharch karte hain.
- f15 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.1 mein Priestley ka prayog chaar chitron mein hai: upar ki line bina paudhe ke, neeche ki line paudhe ke saath; dono lines ko jodne wala koi teer nahi.
- f16 · figure ka nishaan · (a) · Chitra (a): band bell jar mein chooha aur jalti mombatti, paudha nahi; teer daayi taraf (b) tak.
- f17 · figure ka nishaan · (b) · Chitra (b): bina paudhe ke jar mein mombatti bujh gayi aur chooha gira pada.
- f18 · figure ka nishaan · (c) · Chitra (c): patton wala gamle ka paudha, chooha aur jalti mombatti ek saath band; teer daayi taraf (d) tak.
- f19 · figure ka nishaan · (d) · Chitra (d): paudha wahin, chooha seedha khada, mombatti ab bhi jal rahi.
- f20 · teacher ki extra baat · Upar aur neeche ki line ek ke baad ek hone wali ghatna nahi, do alag jar hain jinki tulna hai; dono mein farq sirf paudhe ka; chitra ka naam Priestley's experiment.
- f21 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: Priestley ne mombatti aur paudhe se prayog kaise kiya hoga? Kuch din baad mombatti jalti hai ya nahi, yeh dekhne ke liye use phir jalana (rekindle) padta; set-up ko chhede bina mombatti kitne tareekon se jalayi ja sakti hai?
- f22 · shabd ka matlab · rekindle · Rekindle: bujhi cheez ko phir se jalana; re = phir se, kindle = jalana.
- f23 · teacher ki extra baat · Jar khole bina mombatti jalane ke tareeke: lens se dhoop ko batti par ek bindu mein jama karna; andar lage patle taar mein bahar se bijli bhej kar use laal-garam karna; andar do taaron ke beech bijli ki chingari.
- f24 · teacher ki extra baat · Jar khola toh bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaayegi, aur pata nahi chalega ki mombatti paudhe ki wajah se jali ya taazi hawa se.
- f25 · kitab kehti hai · Jan Ingenhousz (1730-1799) ne Priestley jaisa set-up ek baar andhere mein aur ek baar dhoop (sunlight) mein rakha.
- f26 · kitab kehti hai · Unhone dikhaya ki jalti mombatti ya saans lete jaanwar se kharab hui hawa ko paudha jis kaam se saaf karta hai, us kaam ke liye sunlight zaroori hai.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · purifies the air · Purifies the air: hawa ko phir se aisa bana dena ki mombatti jal sake aur jaanwar saans le sake; purify = saaf karna.
- f28 · teacher ki extra baat · Priestley ke prayog kabhi chalte, kabhi nahi; Ingenhousz ke prayog ne bataya ki farq roshni ka tha.
- f29 · teacher ki extra baat · Paudhe din-raat saans lete hain aur oxygen kharch karte hain; roshni mein unka oxygen banana kharch se zyada hota hai, andhere mein sirf kharch hota hai.
- f30 · kitab kehti hai · aquatic plant · Ingenhousz ne ek aquatic plant se prayog kiya: tez dhoop mein hare hisson ke aas-paas chhote bulbule (bubbles) bane, andhere mein nahi.
- f31 · kitab kehti hai · green parts · Baad mein unhone pehchana ki yeh bulbule oxygen ke the; isse dikha ki sirf paudhe ke hare hisse (green parts) oxygen chhod sakte hain.
- f32 · shabd ka matlab · aquatic plant · Aquatic plant: paani mein ugne aur rehne wala paudha; aqua = paani; jaise Hydrilla.
- f33 · shabd ka matlab · green parts · Green parts: paudhe ke hare hisse, jinme chlorophyll hota hai.
- f34 · shabd ka matlab · bubbles · Bubbles: paani ke andar gas ke chhote gole, bulbule.
- f35 · teacher ki extra baat · Oxygen ki ek aasaan pehchaan: sulagti hui teeli oxygen mein bhabhak kar jal uthti hai.
- f36 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chlorophyll patti ke hare hisson mein hota hai; variegated patti mein starch sirf hare hisson mein bana tha.
- f37 · kitab kehti hai · Lagbhag 1854 mein Julius von Sachs ne saboot diya ki paudhe badhte waqt glucose banate hain; glucose aksar starch bankar jama hota hai.
- f38 · kitab kehti hai · chloroplast · Sachs ke baad ke prayogon ne dikhaya ki hara padarth (chlorophyll) cells ke andar khaas daanon mein hota hai, jinhe baad mein chloroplast kaha gaya.
- f39 · kitab kehti hai · Sachs ne paaya ki glucose paudhe ke hare hisson mein banta hai, aur aksar starch bankar jama hota hai.
- f40 · shabd ka matlab · chloroplast · Chloroplast: plant cell ke andar chlorophyll rakhne wale chhote hisse; chloro = hara, plast = cell ke andar bana chhota hissa.
- f41 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Glucose ek meethi sugar hai jisse cells ko kaam ki energy milti hai; bahut saare glucose judkar starch bante hain.
- f42 · kitab kehti hai · T.W. Engelmann (1843-1909) ne prism se roshni ko uske spectral components mein toda, aur woh rang green alga Cladophora par daale, jo aerobic bacteria ke suspension mein rakhi thi.
- f43 · kitab kehti hai · Bacteria se pata chalaya gaya ki O₂ evolution, yaani oxygen ka nikalna, kahan ho raha hai.
- f44 · kitab kehti hai · Bacteria sabse zyada neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson mein jama hue; is tarah photosynthesis ka pehla action spectrum bana.
- f45 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh action spectrum mota-mota chlorophyll a aur b ke absorption spectra jaisa hai; inhe aage pigments wale hisse mein dekhenge.
- f46 · shabd ka matlab · spectral components · Spectral components: safed roshni ke alag-alag rang; spectrum = rangon ki patti.
- f47 · shabd ka matlab · suspension · Suspension: aisa mishran jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas paani mein tairte rehte hain.
- f48 · shabd ka matlab · aerobic bacteria · Aerobic bacteria: aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye; aero = hawa; isliye woh wahin jama hote hain jahan oxygen zyada ho.
- f49 · shabd ka matlab · O₂ evolution · O₂ evolution: oxygen ka banna aur bahar nikalna; yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi.
- f50 · shabd ka matlab · action spectrum · Action spectrum: kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, uska naqsha; action = kaam.
- f51 · shabd ka matlab · absorption spectra · Absorption spectra: kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; absorb = sokhna, spectra = spectrum ka bahuvachan.
- f52 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai; prism, yaani kaanch ka tikona tukda, use indradhanush jaise rangon mein tod deta hai (Class 10).
- f53 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Alga: paani mein rehne wale saral hare jeev, jo photosynthesis karte hain; Cladophora dhaage jaisi lambi hari alga hai.
- f54 · teacher ki extra baat · Hari roshni wale hisse ke paas bahut kam bacteria jama hue.
- f55 · kitab kehti hai · 19th century ke beech tak paudhe ke photosynthesis ki khaas baatein pata thi: paudhe roshni ki energy se CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate banate hain.
- f56 · kitab kehti hai · Oxygen chhodne wale jeevon ke poore kaam ko ek empirical equation se dikhaya gaya.
- f57 · shabd ka matlab · empirical equation · Empirical equation: dekh kar aur naap kar likhi equation, jo sirf yeh batati hai ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, beech ke kadam nahi.
- f58 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Carbohydrate: carbon, hydrogen aur oxygen se bane khane ke padarth, jaise glucose aur starch.
- f59 · kitab kehti hai · Equation: CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂; [CH₂O] carbohydrate ko dikhata hai, jaise glucose, jo chhe carbon wali sugar hai.
- f60 · shabd ka matlab · [CH₂O] · [CH₂O]: carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket, koi asli alag molecule nahi; aisi chhe ikaiyan milkar glucose C₆H₁₂O₆.
- f61 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chemical equation mein baayi taraf shuru ki cheezein, daayi taraf bani cheezein; teer ke upar Light batata hai ki kaam roshni se chalta hai.
- f62 · kitab kehti hai · Microbiologist Cornelius van Niel (1897-1985) ne purple aur green bacteria par apne kaam se dikhaya ki photosynthesis asal mein ek light-dependent reaction hai.
- f63 · kitab kehti hai · Is reaction mein kisi suitable oxidisable compound ka hydrogen CO₂ ko reduce karke carbohydrate bana deta hai.
- f64 · shabd ka matlab · microbiologist · Microbiologist: bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik; micro = bahut chhota, bio = jeevan.
- f65 · shabd ka matlab · oxidisable compound · Oxidisable compound: aisa yaugik jo apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake.
- f66 · shabd ka matlab · reduce · Reduce: kisi molecule mein hydrogen judna; yahan hydrogen judne se CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai.
- f67 · shabd ka matlab · light-dependent · Light-dependent: jo roshni ke bina na chale.
- f68 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Oxidation: kisi cheez se hydrogen nikalna; reduction: kisi cheez mein hydrogen judna (Class 10).
- f69 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Purple aur green bacteria roshni se photosynthesis karke apna khana banate hain.
- f70 · kitab kehti hai · Van Niel ki aam equation: 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O.
- f71 · shabd ka matlab · H₂A · H₂A: koi bhi hydrogen dene wala yaugik; A ki jagah alag-alag cheez aa sakti hai.
- f72 · shabd ka matlab · A · A: hydrogen dene ke baad donor ka jo hissa bachta hai, uska sanket; paudhon mein oxygen, sulphur bacteria mein sulphur.
- f73 · shabd ka matlab · hydrogen donor · Hydrogen donor: CO₂ ko reduce karne ke liye hydrogen dene wali cheez; donor = dene wala.
- f74 · kitab kehti hai · Hare paudhon mein H₂O hydrogen donor hai, aur woh oxidise hokar O₂ banta hai.
- f75 · kitab kehti hai · H₂S · Kuch jeev photosynthesis mein O₂ nahi chhodte: purple aur green sulphur bacteria mein H₂S hydrogen donor hai, aur oxidation ka nateeja jeev ke hisaab se sulphur ya sulphate hota hai.
- f76 · shabd ka matlab · H₂S · H₂S: hydrogen sulphide, sade ande jaisi badboo wali gas; do hydrogen aur ek sulphur.
- f77 · teacher ki extra baat · Sulphate: sulphur aur oxygen ke atomon se bana ek kan.
