- f78 · kitab kehti hai · Bacteria mein nateeja sulphur ya sulphate hai, O₂ nahi; isliye van Niel ne infer kiya ki hare paudhe ki O₂ H₂O se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi.
- f79 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh baad mein radioisotopic techniques se saabit hua; isliye photosynthesis ki sahi equation alag likhi gayi.
- f80 · shabd ka matlab · infer · Infer: seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar nateeja nikalna; inferred = aisa nateeja nikala.
- f81 · teacher ki extra baat · Aksar log sochte hain ki paudhe ki O₂ CO₂ se aati hai, kyunki CO₂ ka carbon khane mein chala jaata hai; par O₂ paani se aati hai.
- f82 · teacher ki extra baat · Agar O₂ CO₂ se aati, toh CO₂ lene wale sulphur bacteria bhi O₂ chhodte; woh nahi chhodte, isliye oxygen ka srot paani hai.
- f83 · kitab kehti hai · Sahi equation: 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂.
- f84 · shabd ka matlab · C₆H₁₂O₆ · C₆H₁₂O₆: glucose ka formula; ek molecule mein 6 carbon, 12 hydrogen aur 6 oxygen atom.
- f85 · kitab kehti hai · C₆H₁₂O₆ glucose hai; nikalne wali O₂ paani se aati hai, yeh radio isotope techniques se saabit hua.
- f86 · shabd ka matlab · radioisotopic · Radioisotopic techniques: nishaan wale isotope ka peechha karke dekhna ki atom kahan gaya; isotope = ek hi element ka alag wazan wala atom.
- f87 · shabd ka matlab · isotopic tracing · Isotopic tracing: isotope ke nishaan se atomon ka raasta pata karna.
- f88 · teacher ki extra baat · 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ne hari alga ko oxygen-18 wala paani diya; nishaan wala oxygen chhodi gayi O₂ mein mila, CO₂ par nishaan lagane par nahi; oxygen-18 bhaari, sthir isotope hai, radioactive nahi.
- f89 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chapter ki shuruaat mein Calvin ne C¹⁴ ko nishaan bana kar carbon ka peechha kiya tha; ek hi element ke atom neutron ki ginti mein alag ho sakte hain (isotope).
- f90 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh equation ek akeli reaction nahi, balki photosynthesis naam ke kai kadamon wale kaam (multistep process) ka saar hai.
- f91 · shabd ka matlab · multistep process · Multistep process: kai jude kadamon mein poora hone wala kaam; multi = kai, step = kadam.
- f92 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: is equation mein baarah paani ke molecule kyun liye gaye?
- f93 · teacher ki extra baat · 6O₂ mein 12 oxygen atom hain aur sab paani se aate hain; ek H₂O mein ek oxygen atom, isliye 12H₂O.
- f94 · teacher ki extra baat · 12H₂O ke 24 hydrogen mein se 12 glucose mein jaate hain, baaki 12 CO₂ ke 6 oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani banate hain; CO₂ ke baaki 6 oxygen glucose mein jaate hain.
- f95 · teacher ki extra baat · Net kharch 6 paani ka (12 gaye, 6 naye bane); par dono taraf likhne se dikhta hai ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai aur daayi taraf ka paani naya hai.
- f96 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Experiments evidence dete hain: prayog ka nateeja kisi explanation ke paksh ya khilaaf saboot banta hai.
- f97 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Hawa (air) mein different gases hoti hain, jaise oxygen, nitrogen aur carbon dioxide.
- f98 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Observation pehle ho sakta hai aur uske cause ki identification baad mein: Priestley ne 1770 mein asar dekha, aur gas 1774 mein pehchani gayi.
- f99 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Closed jar bahar ki air se freely exchange nahi karta: andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se nahi milti.
- f100 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Figure mein sequence arrow, yaani teer, ka meaning hai ki ek haalat ke baad doosri haalat aati hai.
- f101 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Burning (mombatti ka jalna) aur respiration (saans) dono surrounding air par depend karte hain.
- f102 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Jar ki opening se bahar ki air andar aa jaati hai, yaani air exchange ho jaata hai.
- f103 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Fair comparison mein baaki conditions similar rakhi jaati hain aur sirf ek cheez badli jaati hai (11.1).

**11.2-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli baar humne dekha ki patti ka bana khana starch bankar jama hota hai, aur iodine use neela-kaala kar deta hai. Teen prayogon se pata chala ki khana banane ke liye patti ko chlorophyll, roshni aur CO₂, teeno chahiye. Yaad rakhein: baaki sab ek jaisa rakh kar sirf ek cheez badlo, tabhi nateeja usi cheez ke naam likha ja sakta hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki dhai sau saal pehle ke vaigyanikon ne bhi isi chaal se ek bada raaz khola. Pichhli baar humne ek sawaal khula chhoda tha: band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai. Ab usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun? Jar mein hara paudha rakhne par kya hota hai, yeh ek prayog ne dikhaya. Par iska kyun poora samajhne mein vaigyanikon ko dedh sau saal se zyada lage. Yeh samajh kisi ek din mein nahi bani. Chhote, saral prayogon ki ek lambi kadi ne dheere-dheere photosynthesis ki tasveer poori ki. Isliye aaj ki kahani ek jasoos ki file jaisi hai: raaz ek hai, aur har vaigyanik usme ek naya suraag jodta hai. Suraag yaani kisi prayog ka nateeja, jo raaz ka ek tukda khol deta hai. Is file mein hum paanch vaigyanikon ke suraag jodenge: Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann aur van Niel. Aur aakhir mein in suraagon se photosynthesis ka poora hisaab ek equation mein likhenge.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: patti par iodine girte hi starch neela-kaala; teen pattiyan ek line mein (variegated, black paper, KOH wali nali) aur unke neeche chlorophyll, roshni, CO₂; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badlo'. Phir band kaanch ka jar, jalti mombatti dheere-dheere bujhti; jar ke bagal mein ek hara paudha aur sawaal ka dabba 'kya hoga, aur kyun?'. Phir ek purani jasoos ki file khulti hai: upar 'Raaz', andar paanch panne jin par naam Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann, van Niel; aakhri panne par khaali equation; kone mein 'suraag = prayog ka nateeja'.
  - NCERT: p004-i4 It is interesting to learn about those simple experiments that led to a gradual 
  - kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.1) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; jasoos ki file wali misaal jo poori heading ko jodti hai.
**11.2-k2** · samjhana · Pehla suraag 1770 ka hai, England ke ek vaigyanik Joseph Priestley ke prayogon se. Priestley ka jeevan 1733 se 1804 tak raha, aur 1770 mein unhone prayogon ki ek poori series ki. In prayogon ne dikhaya ki hare paudhon ke badhne mein hawa ka bahut zaroori kaam hai. Aapne shayad yeh naam pehle suna ho: yahi Priestley 1774 mein oxygen gas ke khojkarta bane. Oxygen yaani hawa ki woh gas, jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai. Iska formula O₂ hai, yaani oxygen ke do jude atom. Dhyaan do, prayog 1770 mein hue aur oxygen 1774 mein mili. Yaani prayog karte waqt Priestley ko us gas ka naam bhi nahi pata tha. Ab prayog dekho: unhone kaanch ka ek ulta bartan liya, jo ghanti jaisa dikhta hai. Yeh bartan neeche se band hota hai, isliye andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se mil nahi paati. Aise bartan ko bell jar kehte hain: bell yaani ghanti, aur jar yaani bartan. Dekho, pehle unhone bell jar mein ek jalti mombatti rakhi. Mombatti thodi der jali, phir bujh gayi. Phir unhone band jar mein ek zinda chooha rakha. Woh bhi jaldi hi saans ke liye tadapne laga. Saans ke liye hawa na milne se dam ghutne ko suffocate hona kehte hain. Socho, dono mein kya milta hai: mombatti ko jalne ke liye hawa chahiye, aur chooha ko saans ke liye. Isliye Priestley ne nateeja nikala ki jalti mombatti aur saans lene wala jaanwar, dono kisi tarah hawa ko kharab kar dete hain. Ab asli kamaal dekho: unhone usi bell jar mein pudine ka ek paudha, yaani mint plant, bhi rakh diya. Aur dekho, is baar chooha zinda raha, aur mombatti bhi jalti rahi. Hara paudha andar aisa kya kar raha tha, jisse hawa phir se kaam ki ho gayi? Hara paudha andar kya kar raha tha, iske liye Priestley ne ek vichaar rakha. Saans lete jaanwar aur jalti mombatti hawa se kuch hata dete hain. Paudhe use hawa mein restore kar dete hain, yaani lauta dete hain. Dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye aisa vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake, hypothesis kehlata hai. Priestley ne yahi hypothesis rakha. Aap shayad turant keh do ki paudha oxygen lauta raha tha. Par Priestley ke paas tab us gas ka naam tak nahi tha. Toh jasoos ki file mein pehla suraag likho: band hawa ko jo cheez kharab karti hai, hara paudha use phir theek kar deta hai.
  - terms: oxygen = Hawa ki woh gas jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai; formula O₂.; O₂ = Oxygen gas ka formula: ek molecule mein oxygen ke do atom jude hote hain.; bell jar = Ghanti jaisa ulta kaanch ka bartan jo andar ki hawa ko bahar ki hawa se alag band rakhta hai; bell = ghanti, jar = bartan.; suffocate = Saans ke liye hawa na milne se dam ghutna.; hypothesis = Dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye diya gaya vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake; hypothesised = aisa vichaar rakha.; restore = Jo hat gaya, use phir se bhar dena, lauta dena.
  - screen: Saal 1770 aur Joseph Priestley ka naam (1733-1804); hare paudhe ke paas hawa ke teer. Tareekhon ki line: pehle 1770 (prayog), phir 1774 (oxygen), saath mein O₂ ke do jude atom. Ulta, ghanti jaisa kaanch ka bartan, neeche se band; label: bell = ghanti, jar = bartan. Bell jar mein jalti mombatti bujhti; phir zinda chooha tadapta, label 'suffocate = dam ghutna'; dono ke upar 'hawa kharab'. Phir usi jar mein pudine (mint) ka paudha: chooha chalta-phirta, mombatti jalti; vichaar ka bulbula 'paudha hawa ko restore karta hai (lauta deta hai)', label 'hypothesis'. Jasoos ki file ke pehle panne par suraag likha jaata: 'kharab hawa ko hara paudha theek karta hai'.
  - NCERT: p004-i5 Joseph Priestley (1733-1804) in 1770 performed a series of experiments that reve; p004-i6 Priestley observed that a candle burning in a closed space – a bell jar, soon ge
  - kitaab se bahar: Priestley England ke the; prayog (1770) oxygen ki khoj (1774) se pehle hue, isliye tab us gas ka naam pata nahi tha; mombatti aur saans dono ko hawa chahiye.
**11.2-k3** · figure · Ab Figure 11.1 dekho; isme Priestley ka yahi prayog chaar chitron mein bana hai. Pehle upar wali line dekho, usme paudha nahi hai. Chitra (a) mein band bell jar ke andar ek chooha aur ek jalti mombatti hai. Chitra (a) se ek teer daayi taraf chitra (b) tak jaata hai. Wahan mombatti bujh chuki hai, aur chooha gira pada hai. Neeche wali line mein, chitra (c) mein, wahi chooha aur jalti mombatti hain. Par saath mein patton wala ek gamle ka paudha bhi band hai. Yahan bhi teer daayi taraf chitra (d) tak jaata hai. Wahan paudha wahin hai, chooha seedha khada hai, aur mombatti ab bhi jal rahi hai. Ab ek baarik baat dekho: upar aur neeche ki line ko jodne wala koi teer nahi hai. Upar aur neeche ki line ke beech teer kyun nahi hai? Upar aur neeche ki line ke beech teer isliye nahi hai, kyunki yeh ek ke baad ek hone wali ghatna nahi hai. Yeh do alag jar hain, jinki tulna ho rahi hai. Yaad karo pichhli baar ki chaal: dono jar mein sab kuch ek jaisa hai, farq sirf paudhe ka hai. Isliye jo bhi farq dikha, mombatti ka jalte rehna aur chooha ka bach jaana, woh paudhe ki wajah se hai. Chitra ke neeche iska naam likha hai: Priestley's experiment, yaani Priestley ka prayog.
  - screen: Figure 11.1 ke chaar chitra do line mein. Upar: (a) bell jar mein chooha aur jalti mombatti, paudha nahi; teer daayi taraf (b): mombatti bujhi, chooha gira. Neeche: (c) gamle ka patton wala paudha, chooha aur jalti mombatti; teer daayi taraf (d): paudha, chooha seedha khada, mombatti jalti. Dono line ke beech ki khaali jagah par ungli, aur label 'teer nahi: do alag jar ki tulna'. Dono jar saath-saath, farq wale paudhe par gola. Neeche caption: Priestley's experiment.
  - NCERT: p004-i7 Figure 11.1 shows four bell-jar panels. Upper row: (a) a mouse and a burning can
  - kitaab se bahar: Dono line ke beech teer na hone ka matlab: do alag jar ki tulna, ek ke baad ek ghatna nahi.
**11.2-k4** · ruko, socho · Ab is prayog par ek mazedaar sawaal socho. Priestley ko kuch din baad dekhna tha ki band jar ki hawa mein mombatti phir se jal sakti hai ya nahi. Iske liye bujhi mombatti ko dobara jalana padta, yaani rekindle karna: re yaani phir se, aur kindle yaani jalana. Par jar kholte hi bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaati, aur poora prayog bigad jaata. Tab pata hi nahi chalta ki mombatti paudhe ki wajah se jali ya taazi hawa ki wajah se. Clue yeh hai: batti tak sirf garmi pahunchani hai, bahar ki hawa nahi. Ruko, pehle khud socho: jar ko chhede bina mombatti ko kitne tareekon se jala sakte ho? Aapne socha hoga ki dhoop ko lens se ek chhote bindu par jama karke batti par daala ja sakta hai. Kaanch ke aar-paar dhoop aaram se aa jaati hai. Aapne dekha hoga ki lens se jama ki gayi dhoop kaagaz tak ko jala deti hai. Doosra tareeka: jar ke andar batti ke paas pehle se ek patla taar laga do, aur bahar se usme bijli bhejo. Bijli se taar laal hokar garam ho jaata hai, aur batti jal uthti hai. Teesra tareeka: andar do taaron ke sire paas-paas rakho, aur unke beech bijli ki chingari chhodo. Teeno tareekon mein ek hi baat hai: garmi andar pahunchti hai, par andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se nahi milti.
  - terms: rekindle = Bujhi cheez ko phir se jalana; re = phir se, kindle = jalana.
  - screen: Band bell jar mein bujhi mombatti, paudha aur chooha; calendar par kuch din. Shabd: re = phir se, kindle = jalana, rekindle. Jar ka dhakkan khulta aur bahar ki hawa andar ghusti, us par laal kaata nishaan. Clue ka dabba: 'sirf garmi andar, hawa nahi'. 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath bada ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir teen tareeke: lens se dhoop ka chamakta bindu batti par; andar laga patla taar bahar ki battery se laal hota; do taaron ke beech chingari; teeno mein jar band rehta hai.
  - NCERT: p004-i13 Can you imagine how Priestley would have conducted the experiment using a candle
  - kitaab se bahar: Jar khole bina mombatti jalane ke teen tareeke: lens se dhoop, bijli se garam taar, bijli ki chingari.
  - jawab: Jar khole bina batti tak sirf garmi pahunchani hai: lens se dhoop ko batti par ek bindu mein jama karna, andar lage patle taar mein bahar se bijli bhej kar use laal-garam karna, ya andar do taaron ke beech bijli ki chingari. Jar khola toh taazi hawa andar aa jaayegi aur paudhe ka asar pata nahi chalega.
  - aam galti: Galat soch: jar khol kar machis se mombatti jala lo. Isse bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaati hai, aur phir mombatti ka jalna paudhe ka saboot nahi rehta.
**11.2-k5** · samjhana · Ab doosre suraag ki baari hai, kyunki Priestley ke prayog mein ek ulajh thi. Kabhi paudha band hawa ko theek kar deta tha, aur kabhi wahi prayog dohraane par kuch nahi hota tha. Ek hi prayog kabhi chale aur kabhi na chale, toh ek jasoos kya sochega? Ek jasoos sochega ki koi ek cheez har baar chupke se badal rahi hai, aur abhi tak kisi ki nazar usme nahi gayi. Yeh cheez pakdi Jan Ingenhousz ne, jinka jeevan 1730 se 1799 tak raha. Unhone Priestley jaisa hi set-up banaya, par use ek baar andhere mein rakha aur ek baar dhoop mein. Yahan bhi wahi chaal hai: sab kuch ek jaisa, sirf roshni badli gayi. Ab nateeja dekho: dhoop wale jar mein paudhe ke saath mombatti jalti rahi. Andhere wale jar mein paudha hote hue bhi mombatti bujh gayi. Toh Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki hawa ko saaf karne ka paudhe ka yeh kaam sunlight ke bina nahi chalta. Hawa ko phir se mombatti aur saans ke laayak bana dene ko angrezi mein purifies the air kehte hain. Purify yaani saaf karna. Ab Priestley wali ulajh bhi sulajh gayi: jab jar ko achhi roshni mili, tab paudhe ne hawa theek ki, aur kam roshni mein nahi. Par andhere wale jar mein paudha tha, phir bhi mombatti kyun bujh gayi? Andhere wale jar mein mombatti isliye bujh gayi, kyunki andhere mein paudha hawa ko theek nahi karta. Ulta woh khud bhi saans leta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki paudhe saans nahi lete, ya sirf raat mein lete hain. Par aisa nahi hai: paudhe din-raat saans lete hain aur oxygen kharch karte hain. Bas roshni mein unka hawa ko theek karne ka kaam saans ke kharch se kahin zyada hota hai. Isliye dhoop mein hawa sudharti hai. Yahi baat aapne black paper wale prayog mein bhi dekhi thi: roshni nahi, toh khana nahi. Jasoos ki file mein doosra suraag likho: hawa ko saaf karne ka yeh kaam paudha sirf roshni mein karta hai.
  - terms: purifies the air = Hawa ko phir se aisa bana dena ki mombatti jal sake aur jaanwar saans le sake; purify = saaf karna.
  - screen: Priestley ka prayog do baar: ek baar mombatti jalti (sahi ka nishaan), ek baar bujhti (?); jasoos ka magnifier '?' par. Jan Ingenhousz ka naam (1730-1799). Do ek jaise jar, dono mein paudha aur mombatti: ek andhere kamre mein, ek dhoop mein. Dhoop wale jar mein mombatti jalti, andhere wale mein bujhti. Label: purifies the air = hawa saaf karna. Andhere wale jar mein paudha bhi saans leta, oxygen ke kan uski taraf jaate; galat soch 'paudhe saans nahi lete' par kaata nishaan; dhoop mein paudhe se nikalti oxygen ka bada teer aur saans ka chhota teer. Kone mein black paper wali patti. File par doosra suraag: 'sirf roshni mein'.
  - NCERT: p004-i14 Using a similar setup as the one used by Priestley, but by placing it once in th
  - kitaab se bahar: Priestley ke prayog kabhi chalte kabhi nahi, aur farq roshni ka tha; paudhe din-raat saans lete hain, roshni mein unka oxygen banana kharch se zyada hota hai.
**11.2-k6** · example · Ingenhousz yahin nahi ruke. Ab unhe woh gas dekhni thi jo paudha hawa mein chhod raha tha. Par hawa mein ghuli gas toh dikhti nahi; use aankhon se dekhein kaise? Hawa mein ghuli gas ko aankhon se dekhne ka tareeka unhone paani mein dhoondha. Unhone ek aisa paudha liya jo paani mein hi ugta aur rehta hai. Aise paudhe ko aquatic plant kehte hain, kyunki aqua yaani paani. Hamare taalabon mein milne wala Hydrilla aisa hi ek paudha hai. Paani mein doobe paudhe se jo gas niklegi, woh chhote bulbule bankar upar uthegi, aur hum use dekh payenge. Paani ke andar gas ke in chhote golon ko bubbles kehte hain, yaani bulbule. Ab dekho, tez dhoop mein paudhe ke hare hisson ke aas-paas chhote-chhote bubbles bante dikhe. Andhere mein ek bhi bulbula nahi bana. Kya yeh bulbule paudhe ke har hisse se nikle? Bulbule paudhe ke har hisse se nahi nikle; woh sirf hare hisson ke paas bane. Jo hisse hare nahi the, unke paas kuch nahi bana. Paudhe ke in hare hisson ko green parts kehte hain, aur inme chlorophyll hota hai. Baad mein Ingenhousz ne in bulbulon ki gas pehchaani: woh oxygen thi. Oxygen ki pehchaan ka ek aasaan tareeka aapne lab mein dekha hoga: sulagti hui teeli oxygen mein bhabhak kar jal uthti hai. Toh Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki oxygen chhodne ka kaam paudhe ke sirf hare hisse karte hain. Yaad karo, variegated patti mein bhi starch sirf hare hisson mein bana tha. Jasoos ki file mein teesra suraag: hawa ko theek karne wali gas oxygen hai. Woh sirf roshni mein, sirf hare hisson se nikalti hai.
  - terms: aquatic plant = Paani mein ugne aur rehne wala paudha; aqua = paani; jaise Hydrilla.; bubbles = Paani ke andar gas ke chhote gole, bulbule.; green parts = Paudhe ke hare hisse, jinme chlorophyll hota hai.
  - screen: Paani ka bartan, andar Hydrilla jaisa aquatic plant (aqua = paani). Tez dhoop: hare hisson ke paas chhote bubbles upar uthte; andhera: ek bhi bulbula nahi. Paudhe ke hare hisson par gola, label 'green parts'; jo hisse hare nahi, wahan kuch nahi. Bulbulon ki gas ek ulti nali mein jama hoti; sulagti teeli andar daalte hi bhabhak kar jalti; label 'oxygen'. Kone mein variegated patti, sirf hare hisse neele-kaale. File par teesra suraag: 'oxygen, roshni mein, hare hisson se'.
  - NCERT: p004-i15 Ingenhousz in an elegant experiment with an aquatic plant showed that in bright 
  - kitaab se bahar: Hydrilla ka udaharan; sulagti teeli se oxygen ki pehchaan; variegated patti (11.1) se jod.
**11.2-k7** · samjhana · Ab tak saare suraag hawa ke the. Par paudha jo khana banata hai, uska kya? Paudha kaun-sa khana banata hai, iska saboot lagbhag 1854 mein Julius von Sachs ne diya. Unhone dikhaya ki paudha badhte waqt glucose banata hai. Aap jaante ho, glucose ek meethi sugar hai, jisse cells ko kaam ki energy milti hai. Yeh glucose aksar starch bankar jama ho jaata hai. Yaad karo, bahut saare glucose judkar hi starch bante hain. Baad ke prayogon mein Sachs ne paudhe ki cell ke andar jhaanka. Unhone dekha ki paudhe ka hara padarth, jise aaj hum chlorophyll kehte hain, poori cell mein faila nahi hota. Woh cell ke andar kuch khaas, chhote daanon mein bhara hota hai. In daanon ko baad mein chloroplast naam diya gaya. Chloro yaani hara, aur plast yaani cell ke andar ka ek chhota bana hua hissa. Sachs ne yeh bhi paaya ki glucose paudhe ke hare hisson mein hi banta hai, aur wahi aksar starch bankar rakha jaata hai. Socho, Ingenhousz ki oxygen bhi hare hisson se nikli, aur Sachs ka glucose bhi hare hisson mein bana. Toh isse kya pata chalta hai? Isse pata chalta hai ki oxygen chhodna aur glucose banana do alag kaam nahi hain. Yeh ek hi kaam ke do hisse hain, jo chloroplast wale hare hisson mein hota hai. Jasoos ki file mein agla suraag: khana glucose hai, woh chloroplast wale hare hisson mein banta hai, aur starch bankar jama hota hai.
  - terms: chloroplast = Plant cell ke andar chlorophyll rakhne wale chhote hisse; chloro = hara, plast = cell ke andar bana chhota hissa.
  - screen: Saal lagbhag 1854, Julius von Sachs. Badhta paudha, uske andar glucose ke chhote kan; glucose ke motiyon ki zanjeer starch ban kar jama. Patti se cell tak zoom: cell mein hare daane, chlorophyll sirf unke andar; label: chloroplast = chloro (hara) + plast (cell ka chhota hissa). Hare hisse par 'glucose yahan banta hai'. Do teer ek hi hare hisse par: Ingenhousz ki oxygen bahar, Sachs ka glucose andar. File par agla suraag.
  - NCERT: p005-i1 It was not until about 1854 that Julius von Sachs provided evidence for producti
  - kitaab se bahar: Glucose meethi sugar hai jo cells ko energy deti hai; oxygen chhodna aur glucose banana ek hi kaam ke do hisse hain.
**11.2-k8** · samjhana · Roshni toh chahiye, yeh pakka ho gaya. Par ab agla sawaal uthta hai. Kya roshni ka har rang photosynthesis mein barabar kaam karta hai? Roshni ka har rang barabar kaam karta hai ya nahi, yeh T.W. Engelmann ne dekha. Unka jeevan 1843 se 1909 tak raha. Class 10 mein aapne padha tha ki safed roshni asal mein kai rangon ka mel hai. Kaanch ka ek tikona tukda, yaani prism, is roshni ko indradhanush jaise alag-alag rangon mein tod deta hai. Rangon ki is patti ko spectrum kehte hain, aur uske alag-alag rangon ko spectral components. Engelmann ne prism se roshni ko isi tarah uske spectral components mein toda. Phir unhone ek alga li. Alga paani mein rehne wale saral, hare jeev hain, jo paudhon ki tarah photosynthesis karte hain. Yeh ek green alga thi, jiska naam Cladophora hai. Yeh dhaage jaisi lambi hoti hai, isliye uske alag-alag hisson par alag rang daale ja sakte the. Yeh alga ek aise paani mein rakhi gayi, jisme bahut saare bacteria tair rahe the. Aise mishran ko, jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas tairte rehte hain, suspension kehte hain. Yeh aerobic bacteria the: aero yaani hawa, aur aerobic bacteria yaani aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye. Par Engelmann ne bacteria daale hi kyun? Engelmann ne bacteria isliye daale, kyunki aerobic bacteria wahin jama hote hain jahan oxygen zyada ho. Jaise gud ke paas cheentiyan jama ho jaati hain, waise hi yeh bacteria oxygen ke paas jama ho jaate hain. Toh bacteria batate the ki O₂ evolution kahan ho raha hai, yaani oxygen kahan ban kar bahar nikal rahi hai. Dhyaan do, yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi, sirf bahar nikalna hai. Ab nateeja dekho: bacteria sabse zyada neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson par jama hue. Hari roshni wale hisse ke paas bahut kam bacteria the. Matlab neeli aur laal roshni mein alga ne sabse zyada oxygen chhodi, yaani wahan photosynthesis sabse tez chali. Kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, iske naqshe ko action spectrum kehte hain: action yaani kaam. Is tarah photosynthesis ka pehla action spectrum bana. Yeh naqsha mota-mota chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b ke absorption spectra jaisa dikhta hai. Absorption spectra yaani kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; inhe hum aage pigments wale hisse mein dekhenge. Jasoos ki file mein agla suraag: har rang barabar kaam nahi karta, neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada kaam aati hai.
  - terms: spectral components = Safed roshni ke alag-alag rang; spectrum = rangon ki patti.; suspension = Aisa mishran jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas tairte rehte hain.; aerobic bacteria = Aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye; aero = hawa.; O₂ evolution = Oxygen ka banna aur bahar nikalna; yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi.; action spectrum = Kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, uska naqsha; action = kaam.; absorption spectra = Kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; absorb = sokhna.
  - screen: Safed roshni prism (kaanch ka tikona tukda) se guzar kar indradhanush jaisi rangon ki patti; label: spectrum, spectral components; T.W. Engelmann (1843-1909). Dhaage jaisi hari alga Cladophora paani mein leti, uske alag hisson par alag rang padte; paani mein tairte nanhe bacteria (suspension), label 'aerobic bacteria: aero = hawa'. Kone mein gud par cheentiyan; bacteria oxygen ki taraf khisakte. Label 'O₂ evolution = oxygen ka nikalna (vikaas nahi)'. Bacteria neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson par jhund mein, hari roshni par bahut kam. Neeche ek graph: har rang par photosynthesis ki oonchai, neele aur laal par chotiyan, naam action spectrum; uske peeche halka doosra graph 'absorption spectra: aage'. File par agla suraag.
  - NCERT: p005-i2 Now consider the interesting experiments done by T.W Engelmann (1843 – 1909). Us
  - kitaab se bahar: Prism aur rangon ka mel (Class 10); alga kya hai aur Cladophora dhaage jaisi kyun kaam aayi; hari roshni par bahut kam bacteria; gud aur cheentiyon ki misaal; 'evolution' yahan vikaas nahi.
**11.2-k9** · samjhana · Ab tak ki file padho: 19th century ke beech tak, yaani lagbhag 1850 ke aas-paas, kaafi baatein saaf ho chuki thi. Paudhe roshni ki energy lekar CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate banate hain. Carbohydrate yaani carbon, hydrogen aur oxygen se bane khane ke padarth, jaise glucose aur starch. Oxygen chhodne wale jeevon, jaise hare paudhon, ke is poore kaam ko ek seedhi equation mein likha gaya. Equation mein baayi taraf shuru ki cheezein hoti hain, aur daayi taraf bani hui cheezein. Woh equation thi: CO₂ aur H₂O, yaani paani, roshni ki madad se banate hain [CH₂O] aur O₂. Teer ke upar Light likha hai, kyunki yeh kaam roshni ke bina nahi chalta. Aisi equation, jo bas dekh kar aur naap kar likhi gayi ho ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, empirical equation kehlati hai. Isme beech ke kadam nahi hote; empirical yaani dekhne aur naapne se bana, andar ke kaam ko jaane bina. Ab [CH₂O] ko bracket mein kyun likha? [CH₂O] ko bracket mein isliye likha, kyunki yeh koi asli alag molecule nahi, carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket hai. Aisi chhe ikaiyan jodo, toh chhe carbon wala glucose banta hai, C₆H₁₂O₆. Exam ke liye yeh baarik baat pakki kar lo: [CH₂O] sirf ek ikai hai, glucose ka poora formula nahi. Ab is equation ko jasoos ki nazar se dekho. Oxygen do jagah hai: CO₂ mein bhi, aur H₂O mein bhi. Toh paudhe ne jo O₂ chhodi, uske oxygen atom kahan se aaye? Paudhe ki O₂ ke oxygen atom kahan se aaye, is par do cheezon par shaq hai: CO₂ aur paani. Aksar log sochte hain ki yeh O₂ CO₂ se aati hai. Unka tark hai ki CO₂ ka carbon khane mein chala jaata hai, aur uske do oxygen bach kar bahar nikal jaate hain. Baat seedhi lagti hai, par jasoos kabhi bina saboot ke faisla nahi karta. Is shaq ka faisla bacteria par hue ek kaam se hua, jise hum ab dekhte hain.
  - terms: empirical equation = Dekh kar aur naap kar likhi equation, jo sirf batati hai ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, beech ke kadam nahi.; [CH₂O] = Carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket, koi asli alag molecule nahi; aisi chhe ikaiyan milkar glucose C₆H₁₂O₆.; H₂O = Paani ka formula: ek molecule mein hydrogen ke do atom aur oxygen ka ek atom.
  - screen: Calendar par 19th century, 1850 ke aas-paas. Patti mein roshni, CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate (glucose, starch). Equation likhi jaati: CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂; baayi taraf 'shuru', daayi taraf 'bana', teer par Light. Label: empirical equation = dekh kar likhi, beech ke kadam chhupe. [CH₂O] ke bracket chamakte; chhe [CH₂O] jud kar C₆H₁₂O₆. Jasoos ka magnifier O₂ par: CO₂ ke oxygen aur H₂O ke oxygen, dono par '?' (do shaq). CO₂ se O₂ tak ek teer, us par bada sawaal ka nishaan.
  - NCERT: p005-i3 By the middle of the nineteenth century the key features of plant photosynthesis; p005-i4 CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂
where [CH₂O] represented a carbohydrate (e.g., glu
  - kitaab se bahar: Equation kaise padhte hain (baayi taraf shuru, daayi taraf bana); [CH₂O] asli molecule nahi, ek ikai (exam ki baareek baat); oxygen par do shaq aur aam galat soch.
**11.2-k10** · samjhana · Is shaq ka faisla ek vaigyanik ne kiya, jinka naam tha Cornelius van Niel; unka jeevan 1897 se 1985 tak raha. Woh ek microbiologist the: micro yaani bahut chhota, bio yaani jeevan, aur microbiologist yaani bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik. Van Niel ne purple aur green bacteria par kaam kiya, jo paudhon ki tarah roshni se apna khana banate hain. Class 10 mein aapne padha tha: kisi cheez se hydrogen nikal jaaye, toh use oxidation kehte hain. Aur kisi cheez mein hydrogen jud jaaye, toh use reduction kehte hain. Jo yaugik apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake, use oxidisable compound kehte hain. Van Niel ne dikhaya ki photosynthesis asal mein roshni par tiki ek reaction hai, yaani light-dependent reaction. Is reaction mein kisi oxidisable compound ka hydrogen CO₂ ko reduce karta hai, aur CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai. Seedhe shabdon mein: koi cheez apna hydrogen deti hai, aur roshni is kaam ki energy deti hai. Phir woh hydrogen CO₂ se judkar khana bana deta hai. Hydrogen dene wali is cheez ko hydrogen donor kehte hain: donor yaani dene wala. Isse van Niel ne ek aam equation likhi: 2H₂A aur CO₂, Light yaani roshni ki madad se, banate hain 2A, CH₂O aur H₂O. Yahan H₂A koi bhi hydrogen donor ho sakta hai, aur A uska woh hissa hai jo hydrogen dene ke baad bachta hai. Dekho, is equation ki khoobi yeh hai ki yeh har tarah ke photosynthesis par chalti hai. Bas A ki jagah sahi cheez rakhni hai. Jasoos ke haath mein ab ek saancha hai: A ki jagah alag-alag cheez rakho, aur dekho ki bahar kya nikalta hai.
  - terms: microbiologist = Bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik; micro = bahut chhota, bio = jeevan.; oxidisable compound = Aisa yaugik jo apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake.; reduce = Kisi molecule mein hydrogen judna; yahan hydrogen judne se CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai.; light-dependent = Jo roshni ke bina na chale.; hydrogen donor = CO₂ ko reduce karne ke liye hydrogen dene wali cheez; donor = dene wala.; H₂A = Koi bhi hydrogen dene wala yaugik; A ki jagah alag-alag cheez aa sakti hai.; A = Hydrogen dene ke baad donor ka bacha hissa; paudhon mein oxygen, sulphur bacteria mein sulphur.
  - screen: Cornelius van Niel (1897-1985); label: micro = chhota, bio = jeevan, microbiologist. Roshni mein bangni (purple) aur hare bacteria. Do chhote chitra: molecule se hydrogen nikla = oxidation, molecule mein hydrogen juda = reduction. Ek molecule apna hydrogen deta (oxidisable compound, hydrogen donor), roshni ki kirnein energy deti, hydrogen CO₂ se judkar carbohydrate banata (reduce). Aam equation: 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O; H₂A aur A par khaali khaane jinme alag cheez rakhi ja sakti hai; jasoos ke haath mein yeh saancha.
  - NCERT: p005-i5 A milestone contribution to the understanding of photosynthesis was that made by; p005-i6 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O
  - kitaab se bahar: Oxidation aur reduction ki Class 10 wali paribhasha (hydrogen nikalna aur judna); purple aur green bacteria bhi roshni se khana banate hain; equation ek saancha hai jisme A badalta hai.
**11.2-k11** · samjhana · Ab saanche mein hare paudhe rakho. Hare paudhon mein hydrogen dene wala H₂O hai, yaani A ki jagah oxygen hai. Toh equation banti hai: 2H₂O aur CO₂ se 2O, CH₂O aur H₂O. Do oxygen atom milkar ek O₂ ban jaate hain. Yaani paani apna hydrogen deta hai, aur khud oxidise hokar O₂ ban jaata hai. Ab un bacteria ko dekho jo photosynthesis toh karte hain, par O₂ bilkul nahi chhodte. Purple aur green sulphur bacteria mein hydrogen dene wala H₂S hai, yaani hydrogen sulphide. H₂S ek gas hai jisme do hydrogen aur ek sulphur hota hai, aur isi se sade ande jaisi badboo aati hai. Ishaara yeh hai: is baar hamare saanche mein A ki jagah sulphur aa gaya hai, yaani S. Ruko, pehle khud socho: yeh bacteria hawa mein kya chhodenge, aur isse paudhe ki oxygen ke baare mein kya pata chalta hai? Aapne socha hoga ki yeh bacteria oxygen nahi, sulphur chhodenge, aur yahi sach hai. Jeev ke hisaab se yeh oxidation ka nateeja sulphur hota hai ya sulphate. Sulphate sulphur aur oxygen ke atomon se bana ek kan hai. Par in bacteria se O₂ kabhi nahi nikalti. Ab gaur karo: dono mein CO₂ ek hi hai. Badla sirf hydrogen dene wala, aur uske saath badal gaya jo bahar nikla. Socho, agar O₂ CO₂ se aati, toh in bacteria ko bhi O₂ chhodni chahiye thi, kyunki CO₂ toh unke paas bhi hai. Isliye van Niel ne nateeja nikala: hare paudhe jo O₂ chhodte hain, woh H₂O se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi. Toh asal mein O₂ ka srot CO₂ nahi, paani nikla. Seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar aisa nateeja nikalne ko infer karna kehte hain; van Niel ne yahi inferred kiya. Bilkul jasoos ki tarah: chor ko kisi ne dekha nahi, par saboot jod kar pata chal gaya ki woh kaun hai. Par jasoos ki file tab band hoti hai jab pakka saboot mil jaaye, aur woh saboot baad mein radioisotopic techniques se mila.
  - terms: H₂S = Hydrogen sulphide, sade ande jaisi badboo wali gas; do hydrogen aur ek sulphur.; infer = Seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar nateeja nikalna; inferred = aisa nateeja nikala.
  - screen: Saanche mein A ki jagah O: 2H₂O + CO₂ → 2O + CH₂O + H₂O, do O mil kar O₂. Phir A ki jagah S: purple aur green sulphur bacteria, H₂S (do H, ek S; sade ande ki badboo ka chhota nishaan); clue ka dabba. 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir bacteria se peele sulphur ke kan ya sulphate nikalte, O₂ par kaata nishaan. Dono saanche saath: CO₂ dono mein ek jaisi, sirf donor badla, aur bahar nikli cheez badli. CO₂ wale shaq par kaata nishaan, paani par 'O₂ ka srot'. Label: infer = saboot jod kar nateeja. Jasoos chor ke pairon ke nishaan jodta; file par 'pakka saboot: baad mein'.
  - NCERT: p005-i7 In green plants H₂O is the hydrogen donor and is oxidised to O₂. Some organisms ; p005-i8 and not O₂. Hence, he inferred that the O₂ evolved by the green plant comes from
  - kitaab se bahar: Saanche mein O aur S rakh kar dono equation; H₂S ki badboo; sulphate kya hai; agar O₂ CO₂ se aati toh bacteria bhi chhodte, yeh tark; CO₂ wali galat soch yahan toot-ti hai.
**11.2-k12** · samjhana · Yaad karo, chapter ki shuruaat mein Calvin ne C¹⁴ ko haldi jaisa nishaan bana kar carbon ka peechha kiya tha. Wahan humne dekha tha ki ek hi element ke atom neutron ki ginti mein alag ho sakte hain. Inhe isotope kehte hain, aur kuch isotope radiation chhodte hain. Aise nishaan wale isotope se atom ka peechha karke dekhna ki woh kahan pahuncha, radioisotopic techniques kehlata hai. Ise isotopic tracing bhi kehte hain. Isi tarah 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ne ek hari alga ko aisa paani diya, jiske oxygen par nishaan tha, yaani oxygen-18. Aur dekho, alga ne jo O₂ chhodi, usme wahi nishaan wala oxygen mila. Jab nishaan CO₂ ke oxygen par lagaya, tab woh O₂ mein nahi aaya. Exam ke liye yaad rakho: oxygen-18 oxygen ka bas bhaari roop hai, aur yeh radioactive nahi hota. Jasoos ki zubaan mein yeh ungliyon ke nishaan jaisa pakka saboot hai: nikalne wali O₂ paani se hi aati hai. Ab photosynthesis ki sahi equation likho: 6CO₂ aur 12H₂O, Light yaani roshni ki madad se, banate hain C₆H₁₂O₆, 6H₂O aur 6O₂. Yahan C₆H₁₂O₆ glucose hai: iske ek molecule mein chhe carbon, baarah hydrogen aur chhe oxygen atom hote hain. Aur yaad rakho, is equation ki 6O₂ ki saari oxygen paani se aati hai. Par dhyaan rahe, yeh ek akeli reaction nahi hai. Equation sirf shuru aur ant dikhati hai, jabki beech mein kai chhote kadam hote hain. In sab kadamon ke poore kaam ka naam hi photosynthesis hai. Jaise 'aata, paani aur aag se roti' likhne mein gundhna, belna aur sekna, teeno kadam chhup jaate hain. Kai kadamon wale aise kaam ko multistep process kehte hain: multi yaani kai, aur step yaani kadam. Yeh kadam kaun-se hain, yeh aage ke hisson mein ek-ek karke khulenge.
  - terms: radioisotopic techniques = Nishaan wale isotope ka peechha karke dekhna ki atom kahan gaya; isotope = ek hi element ka alag wazan wala atom.; isotopic tracing = Isotope ke nishaan se atomon ka raasta pata karna.; C₆H₁₂O₆ = Glucose ka formula; ek molecule mein 6 carbon, 12 hydrogen aur 6 oxygen atom.; multistep process = Kai jude kadamon mein poora hone wala kaam; multi = kai, step = kadam.
  - screen: Opening ki yaad: C¹⁴ par haldi jaisa nishaan, carbon sugar tak. Ek hi element ke do atom, ek bhaari; label isotope. Paani ke oxygen par chamakta nishaan (oxygen-18), hari alga ke paas: nikalti O₂ mein wahi nishaan; doosre jar mein CO₂ ke oxygen par nishaan, O₂ mein nahi; '1941: Ruben aur Kamen'; neeche 'oxygen-18: bhaari, radioactive nahi'. Ungliyon ke nishaan wala pakka saboot file par. Sahi equation: 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂; C₆H₁₂O₆ ke neeche 6 C, 12 H, 6 O; 6O₂ se paani tak teer. Phir ek seedha teer kai chhote kadamon mein tootta (multistep process: multi = kai, step = kadam); kone mein rasoi: aata, paani, aag se roti, beech mein gundhna, belna, sekna.
  - NCERT: p005-i9 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂; p005-i10 where C₆H₁₂O₆ represents glucose. The O₂ released is from water; this was proved; p006-i1 reaction but description of a multistep process called photosynthesis.
  - kitaab se bahar: 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ka oxygen-18 wala prayog (dono taraf nishaan); oxygen-18 radioactive nahi (exam ki baareek baat); rasoi wali roti se multistep ki misaal.
**11.2-k13** · ruko, socho · Ab is equation mein ek ajeeb baat dekho: paani dono taraf hai. Baayi taraf 12H₂O hai, aur daayi taraf 6H₂O. Toh shuru mein baarah paani ke molecule kyun likhe, seedhe chhe kyun nahi? Baarah paani ke molecule likhne ki wajah oxygen ki ginti mein chhupi hai, toh chalo gin kar dekhte hain. Pehle daayi taraf ki 6O₂ gino. Har O₂ mein do oxygen atom hain, toh chhe O₂ mein baarah oxygen atom hue. Aur van Niel ke suraag se hum jaante hain ki yeh saare oxygen atom paani se aate hain. Socho, ek H₂O mein kitne oxygen atom hote hain, aur baarah oxygen atom ke liye kitne paani chahiye? Ek H₂O mein oxygen ka sirf ek atom hai, isliye baarah atom ke liye baarah paani lagenge. Yahi wajah hai ki baayi taraf 12H₂O likha hai. Toh daayi taraf ke 6H₂O kahan se aaye? Daayi taraf ke 6H₂O naya paani hai, jo is kaam ke dauraan banta hai. Chalo, ab hydrogen bhi gin lete hain: baarah paani ke molecule kul chaubees hydrogen dete hain. Inme se baarah hydrogen glucose mein chale jaate hain. Baaki baarah hydrogen CO₂ ke chhe oxygen ke saath judkar chhe naye paani bana dete hain. Aur CO₂ ke baaki chhe oxygen glucose mein chale jaate hain. Toh dono taraf ka paani alag paani hai. Baayi taraf ke paani ki oxygen O₂ ban jaati hai, aur daayi taraf ke paani ki oxygen CO₂ se aati hai. Agar dono taraf se chhe-chhe paani kaat kar sirf chhe likh dete, toh ginti barabar rehti. Par yeh baat chhup jaati ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai. Exam mein net hisaab bhi poocha jaata hai: baarah paani gaye aur chhe naye bane, toh kul kharch chhe paani ka hua.
  - screen: Equation mein dono taraf paani chamakta: baayi taraf 12H₂O, daayi taraf 6H₂O, aur sawaal ka nishaan. 6O₂ ke moti gine jaate: chhe jode, baarah laal oxygen moti. Har H₂O mein ek laal oxygen moti; baarah paani se baarah laal moti O₂ mein jaate. Phir hydrogen ke 24 safed moti: 12 glucose mein, 12 CO₂ ke 6 neele oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani; CO₂ ke baaki 6 neele moti glucose mein. Daayi taraf ke paani par 'naya'. Kaat kar 6H₂O likhne par chhupi baat par nishaan. Neeche net: 12 - 6 = 6.
  - NCERT: p006-i2 Can you explain why twelve molecules of water as substrate are used in the equat
  - kitaab se bahar: Oxygen aur hydrogen ke atom gin kar 12H₂O ki wajah; daayi taraf ka paani naya hai jiski oxygen CO₂ se aati hai; net kharch 6 paani ka.
  - jawab: 6O₂ mein baarah oxygen atom hain, aur saare paani se aate hain; ek H₂O mein ek hi oxygen hota hai, isliye 12H₂O chahiye. 12H₂O ke 24 hydrogen mein se 12 glucose mein jaate hain, baaki 12 CO₂ ke 6 oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani banate hain; CO₂ ke baaki 6 oxygen glucose mein. Isliye daayi taraf ka 6H₂O naya paani hai.
  - aam galti: Galat soch: dono taraf ka paani ek hi hai, isliye 6 kaat kar sirf 6H₂O likh do. Ginti barabar rehti hai, par yeh chhup jaata hai ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai aur daayi taraf ka paani naya hai, jiski oxygen CO₂ se aayi.
**11.2-k14** · poori kahani dobara · Ab jasoos ki poori file ek saath padhte hain. Yaad karo, Priestley ne dikhaya ki hara paudha band jar ki kharab hawa ko phir theek kar deta hai. Ingenhousz ke prayogon se pata chala ki yeh kaam roshni maangta hai, aur oxygen ke bulbule hare hisson se uthte hain. Sachs ne dikhaya ki hare hisson ke chloroplast mein glucose banta hai, jo starch bankar jama hota hai. Engelmann ne dikhaya ki neeli aur laal roshni is kaam mein sabse zyada kaam aati hai. Aur van Niel ne dikhaya ki nikalne wali O₂ paani se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: band jar mein jalti mombatti ke saath ek hara paudha rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun? Band jar mein hara paudha rakhne par, agar roshni mile, toh mombatti jalti rahegi aur chooha bhi saans leta rahega. Kyunki roshni mein paudhe ke hare hisse paani ke molecule tod kar oxygen chhodte hain, aur CO₂ se glucose banate hain. Mombatti aur chooha jo oxygen kharch karte hain, paudha use paani se nikaal kar hawa mein lauta deta hai. Aur andhere mein paudha oxygen nahi chhodta, sirf saans mein kharch karta hai, isliye tab mombatti bujh jaati hai. Sachs ne bataya tha ki chlorophyll chloroplast naam ke chhote daanon mein rehta hai. Par paudhe mein yeh kaam theek-theek kahan-kahan hota hai, sirf patti mein ya kisi aur hisse mein bhi, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Jasoos ki file ke paanch panne ek saath: Priestley (paudha hawa theek karta), Ingenhousz (roshni, hare hisse, oxygen ke bulbule), Sachs (chloroplast mein glucose, starch), Engelmann (neeli aur laal roshni), van Niel (O₂ paani se). Shuru wala jar phir: roshni mein paudha, mombatti jalti, chooha chalta; paudhe mein paani tootkar oxygen bahar, CO₂ se glucose. Andhere mein wahi jar, mombatti bujhti. Aakhir mein poora paudha, jad, tana aur patti, us par sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.

## 11.3 WHERE DOES PHOTOSYNTHESIS TAKE PLACE?

_Maqsad: Photosynthesis paudhe ke kin-kin hisson mein hota hai, aur un hisson ke andar bhi theek kis jagah?_

_Misaal: cheeni mill: chloroplast ek mill; do chaardiwari = outer aur inner membrane; dhoop se chalne wala bijlighar = grana ki membranes (light reactions); bijlighar ke kamron ko jodne wale galiyare = stroma lamellae; bijlighar se floor tak jaane wali do cheezein = ATP (kaam ki energy) aur NADPH (hydrogen aur electron); mill ka bada floor = stroma, uski machines = enzymes jo CO₂ se sugar banati hain (dark reactions); godown ki boriyaan = starch granule; purze banane wali workshop = ribosomes_

Kitab ki baatein (73):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Photosynthesis kahan hota hai, iska jawab aap cell wale padhe hue ke aadhar par denge: hari patti mein, ya chloroplast mein; yeh jawab sahi hai.
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · Photosynthesis hari pattiyon mein hota hai, par paudhe ke doosre hare hisson mein bhi hota hai.
- f3 · pehle ki zaroori baat · plant organs · Plant organs: paudhe ke hisse (organs) jad, tana, patti, phool aur phal; patti ek organ hai.
- f4 · pehle ki zaroori baat · organelles · Cells contain organelles: cell ke andar apna-apna kaam karne wale chhote hisse (organelles) hote hain; chloroplast ek organelle hai.
- f5 · pehle ki zaroori baat · chlorophyll · Chlorophyll absorbs light: chloroplast ke andar ka hara chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai.
- f6 · teacher ki extra baat · Patti aur chloroplast, dono jawab sahi hain, bas alag paimaane par: patti organ hai, chloroplast organelle. Galat soch: photosynthesis sirf patti mein hota hai.
- f7 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: paudhe ke aur kaun-se hisse hain jahan photosynthesis ho sakta hai? Jawab: hare tane, phool ke hare sepals, kachche hare phal, yaani har hara hissa jisme chloroplast hon aur roshni pahunche.
- f8 · pehle ki zaroori baat · sepals · Stem, sepal and fruit: tana; phool ke neeche ki chhoti hari pattiyan (sepals) jo khilne se pehle kali ko dhakti hain; phal. Jo hissa hara hai, usme chloroplast hote hain.
- f9 · teacher ki extra baat · Opuntia · Opuntia (nagphani) mein pattiyan kaante ban jaati hain, aur tana chapta, mota aur hara ho jaata hai; usme chlorophyll hota hai aur wahi photosynthesis karta hai. Registan mein chaudi pattiyan bahut paani uda deti.
- f10 · teacher ki extra baat · Phir bhi zyadatar paudhon mein sabse zyada khana patti banati hai, kyunki woh chapti aur chaudi hokar sabse zyada roshni pakadti hai.
- f11 · kitab kehti hai · Patti ki mesophyll cells mein bahut saare (large number) chloroplast hote hain.
- f12 · kitab kehti hai · Chloroplast aam taur par mesophyll cells ki deewaron ke saath line mein lagte hain, taaki unhe incident light ki optimum matra mile.
- f13 · shabd ka matlab · mesophyll · Mesophyll: patti ki upar aur neeche ki epidermis ke beech ka hara bhaag; meso = beech, phyll = patti.
- f14 · shabd ka matlab · incident light · Incident light: bahar se aakar kisi satah par girne wali roshni; incident = aakar padne wala.
- f15 · shabd ka matlab · optimum · Optimum: kaam ke liye sabse theek matra, na kam na zyada; optimum maximum (sabse zyada) nahi hai.
- f16 · pehle ki zaroori baat · cell wall · Leaf mesophyll aur cell wall: plant cell ke chaaron taraf mazboot cell wall hoti hai; mesophyll cell ka zyadatar beech ka hissa paani bhara bada vacuole ghere rehta hai, isliye chloroplast kinaaron par rehte hain.
- f17 · pehle ki zaroori baat · light absorption by chlorophyll · Light absorption by chlorophyll: chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai; bahut kam roshni mein kaam dheema hota hai, aur bahut tez roshni chlorophyll aur chloroplast ko nuksaan pahunchati hai.
- f18 · teacher ki extra baat · Mesophyll ki ek cell mein aksar 20 se 40 chloroplast hote hain.
- f19 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: chloroplast kab apni chapti satah deewaron ke parallel rakhenge, aur kab incident light ke perpendicular?
- f20 · pehle ki zaroori baat · parallel and perpendicular · Parallel and perpendicular directions: parallel = saath-saath chalne wali, kabhi na milne wali rekhaayein (rail ki patriyan); perpendicular = seedha 90 degree par kaatne wali (farsh aur deewar).
- f21 · pehle ki zaroori baat · chloroplast flat surface · Chloroplast flat surface: chloroplast masoor ke daane (lens) jaisa chapta hota hai; ek chauda chapta chehra aur ek patla kinaara.
- f22 · teacher ki extra baat · Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par jama hote hain; chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur incident light ke perpendicular, isliye zyada roshni pakdi jaati hai. Tez roshni mein woh bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain; chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur roshni ke bhi parallel, sirf kinaara roshni ki taraf, isliye kam roshni aur kam nuksaan.
- f23 · kitab kehti hai · Chloroplast ke andar ek membrane system hai, jisme grana, stroma lamellae aur matrix stroma hain.
- f24 · shabd ka matlab · grana · Grana: thylakoid ki chapti thailiyon ke sikkon jaise dher; ek dher granum, kai dher grana; granum = daana.
- f25 · shabd ka matlab · stroma lamellae · Stroma lamellae: alag-alag grana ko jodne wali patli, chapti, bina dher wali membranes; lamella = patli parat, bahuvachan lamellae.
- f26 · shabd ka matlab · stroma · Stroma: chloroplast ke andar membranes ke chaaron taraf bhara gaadha dravya, yaani matrix.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · thylakoid · Thylakoid: chloroplast ke andar ki chapti, band membrane wali thaili; inke dher grana bante hain.
- f28 · pehle ki zaroori baat · chloroplast envelope · Chloroplast: cell ka organelle jo do membranes, bahar wali aur andar wali, ke gher (envelope) se ghira hota hai.
- f29 · pehle ki zaroori baat · membrane versus matrix · Membrane versus fluid matrix: membrane = patli jhilli jo ek jagah ko doosri se alag rakhe; matrix = jagah ko bharne wala gaadha dravya.
- f30 · teacher ki extra baat · Stroma aur stroma lamellae alag cheezein hain: stroma dravya hai, stroma lamellae membrane; granum ekvachan hai, grana bahuvachan.
- f31 · kitab kehti hai · Chloroplast mein kaam ka saaf bantwara (clear division of labour) hai.
- f32 · kitab kehti hai · Membrane system roshni ki energy trap karta hai, yaani pakadta hai, aur ATP aur NADPH banata hai.
- f33 · kitab kehti hai · Stroma mein enzymatic reactions sugar banati hain, aur yeh sugar aage starch banati hai.
- f34 · shabd ka matlab · division of labour · Division of labour: ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baantna, jisme har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale; kaam ka bantwara.
- f35 · shabd ka matlab · ATP · ATP: ek molecule jo energy (chemical energy) apne andar lekar chalta hai aur jahan kaam ho wahan de deta hai; poora naam aur banawat aage.
- f36 · shabd ka matlab · NADPH · NADPH: electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule, jo inhe dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai.
- f37 · shabd ka matlab · enzymatic reactions · Enzymatic reactions: enzymes ki madad se chalne wali reactions.
- f38 · pehle ki zaroori baat · enzymes · Enzymes catalyse reactions: enzyme ek protein hai jo reaction ko tez karata hai aur khud nahi badalta.
- f39 · pehle ki zaroori baat · chemical energy · Chemical energy: molecules ke andar band energy; ATP yahi energy lekar chalta hai.
- f40 · pehle ki zaroori baat · membranes · Chloroplast internal membranes and stroma: andar ki membranes (grana aur stroma lamellae) aur unke chaaron taraf ka stroma.
- f41 · teacher ki extra baat · Roshni pakadne wale molecules membrane par ek tay kram mein jade rehte hain, jabki sugar banane wale enzymes stroma ke dravya mein ghule rehte hain; isliye dono kaam alag jagah hote hain.
- f42 · teacher ki extra baat · Stroma mein bani sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai; ATP aur NADPH sugar nahi bante, sirf energy aur hydrogen dekar khaali hote hain aur membrane par phir bhar jaate hain.
- f43 · kitab kehti hai · Pehla set, yaani membranes wali reactions, seedhe roshni se chalti hain (directly light driven), isliye inhe light reactions (photochemical reactions) kehte hain.
- f44 · shabd ka matlab · light reactions · Light reactions: chloroplast ki membranes mein seedhe roshni se chalne wali reactions; roshni band hote hi yeh ruk jaati hain.
- f45 · shabd ka matlab · photochemical reactions · Photochemical reactions: photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik; roshni se seedhe chalne wali rasayanik reactions, light reactions ka doosra naam.
- f46 · pehle ki zaroori baat · membrane system · Light absorption, chloroplast membrane system, ATP and NADPH: membrane system mein chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai, aur wahin ATP aur NADPH bante hain.
- f47 · kitab kehti hai · Doosra set seedhe roshni se nahi chalta, par light reactions ke products, ATP aur NADPH, par tika hai.
- f48 · kitab kehti hai · Isliye, alag pehchaan ke liye, by convention inhe dark reactions (carbon reactions) kehte hain.
- f49 · kitab kehti hai · Iska yeh matlab nahi ki yeh reactions andhere mein hoti hain, ya light-dependent nahi hain.
- f50 · shabd ka matlab · dark reactions · Dark reactions: stroma ki sugar banane wali reactions, jo seedhe roshni se nahi chalti; naam sunkar andhera mat samjho.
- f51 · shabd ka matlab · carbon reactions · Carbon reactions: dark reactions ka doosra naam; inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai.
- f52 · shabd ka matlab · by convention · By convention: bahut pehle se chala aa raha, sabka maana hua naam ya tareeka, chahe woh poori baat na bataye.
- f53 · pehle ki zaroori baat · light reactions · Light reactions produce ATP and NADPH; stroma synthesises sugar: membranes ATP aur NADPH banati hain, aur stroma unse sugar banata hai.
- f54 · pehle ki zaroori baat · direct versus indirect dependence · Direct versus indirect dependence: direct = seedha kisi par tikna; indirect = kisi doosre ke zariye tikna.
- f55 · teacher ki extra baat · Andhere mein ATP aur NADPH banna rukte hi thodi der mein dark reactions bhi ruk jaati hain; yeh zyadatar din mein, light reactions ke saath-saath chalti hain.
- f56 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.2: chloroplast ke section ki electron micrograph ka diagrammatic representation. Andaakaar chloroplast outer aur inner membrane se ghira; stroma mein several (kai) grana ke dher, stromal lamellae se jude; chhote bindu ribosomes, naarangi gole-dar-gole starch granules, bhoori boond lipid droplet; aath leader lines, koi process teer nahi.
- f57 · shabd ka matlab · section · Section: andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kisi cheez ka kaata gaya patla tukda.
- f58 · shabd ka matlab · Diagrammatic representation · Diagrammatic representation: kisi photo ya cheez ko saaf rekhaon wale chitra (diagram) ke roop mein dikhana.
- f59 · shabd ka matlab · electron micrograph · Electron micrograph: electron microscope se li gayi bahut badi photo; micro = chhota, graph = chitra.
- f60 · figure ka nishaan · Outer membrane · Sabse bahar ki rekha: chloroplast ke gher ki bahar wali jhilli.
- f61 · figure ka nishaan · Inner membrane · Uske andar ki rekha: gher ki andar wali jhilli; dono milkar do parat ka gher.
- f62 · figure ka nishaan · Grana · Sikkon jaise chapti membranes ke dher; figure mein kai dher.
- f63 · figure ka nishaan · Stromal lamella · Grana ko jodti bina dher wali patli membrane; figure ek patti dikhata hai, isliye ekvachan lamella.
- f64 · figure ka nishaan · Stroma · Membranes ke chaaron taraf ki jagah; chitra mein khaali jaisi, asal mein gaadhe dravya se bhari.
- f65 · figure ka nishaan · Ribosomes · Stroma ke chhote bindu; yeh protein banate hain.
- f66 · figure ka nishaan · Starch granule · Stroma mein naarangi, andaakaar, gole ke andar gole wala daana; jama kiya hua starch.
- f67 · figure ka nishaan · Lipid droplet · Neeche bhoori gol boond; charbi aur tel jaisi cheez, starch nahi.
- f68 · pehle ki zaroori baat · leader lines · Chloroplast compartments, reading diagram leader lines: har label se ek leader line theek us hisse tak jaati hai; uske sire se hissa pehchaano.
- f69 · pehle ki zaroori baat · ribosomes and lipids · Ribosomes synthesise proteins; lipids versus carbohydrates: ribosomes protein banate hain; lipid charbi aur tel hai, starch carbohydrate hai, dono alag.
- f70 · pehle ki zaroori baat · microscopy · Microscopy, section reveals internal structure: microscope chhoti cheez ko bada dikhata hai; kisi cheez ko kaatne par uski andar ki banawat dikhti hai.
- f71 · teacher ki extra baat · Chloroplast ke ribosomes chhote (70S) hote hain, aur chloroplast apne kuch protein khud banata hai.
- f72 · teacher ki extra baat · Kachcha tamatar, hari mirch aur matar ki phali hari hoti hain; inke chloroplast dhoop mein thoda khana banate hain. Pakne par tamatar ke chloroplast laal rang wale chromoplast ban jaate hain aur chlorophyll toot jaata hai.
- f73 · teacher ki extra baat · Money plant jaise paudhon ke hare tane bhi photosynthesis karte hain.

**11.3-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli baar humne jasoos ki file poori ki. Paanch vaigyanikon ke suraagon se photosynthesis ki tasveer bani. Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki oxygen paudhe ke hare hisson se nikalti hai. Sachs ne dikhaya ki glucose chloroplast mein banta hai. Yaad rakhein: yeh kaam paudhe ke hare hisson mein, chloroplast naam ke chhote hare daanon ke andar hota hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki sirf 'hare hisse' kehna kaafi nahi hai. Humein jagah ekdum theek-theek dhoondhni hai. Pichhli film ke aakhir mein humne yahi poochha tha: kya yeh kaam sirf patti mein hota hai? Photosynthesis paudhe ke kin-kin hisson mein hota hai, aur un hisson ke andar bhi theek kis jagah? Photosynthesis ki jagah poochho toh aap turant kahoge: hari patti mein, ya chloroplast mein. Yeh aapne cell wale chapter mein padha tha, aur aapka jawab bilkul sahi hai. Dekho, photosynthesis sach mein paudhon ki hari pattiyon mein hota hai. Par sirf pattiyon mein nahi. Hum dekhenge ki doosre hare hisse bhi yeh kaam karte hain. Aksar hum sochte hain ki khana sirf patti banati hai, par aisa nahi hai. Yaad karo, paudhe ke alag-alag hisson ko organs kehte hain. Jaise jad, tana, patti, phool aur phal. Cell ke andar bhi apna-apna kaam karne wale chhote hisse hote hain, jinhe organelles kehte hain. Chloroplast aisa hi ek organelle hai, aur uske andar hara chlorophyll hota hai jo roshni sokh leta hai. Isliye jahan bhi chloroplast hain aur roshni pahunchti hai, wahan photosynthesis ho sakta hai. Matlab aapke dono jawab sahi hain. Patti ek organ hai, aur chloroplast cell ke andar ka organelle. Aaj hum yeh jagah kadam-dar-kadam dhoondhenge. Pehle paudhe ka hissa, phir uski cell, phir chloroplast, aur aakhir mein chloroplast ke andar ki jagah. Chalo, pehle dekhte hain ki patti ke alawa aur kaun-se hisse hare hote hain.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: jasoos ki file ke paanch panne (Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann, van Niel); hare tane se oxygen ke bulbule, chloroplast ke daane mein glucose; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: kaam chloroplast wale hare hisson mein'. Phir ek poora paudha (jad, tana, patti, phool, phal) aur sawaal ka dabba 'Photosynthesis kahan-kahan? Sirf patti?'. Patti par sahi ka nishaan, phir doosre hare hisson par chamak; 'sirf patti' par kaata hua nishaan. Patti se cell, cell se chloroplast, chloroplast ke andar tak zoom ke chaar padaav, har padaav par label: organ, cell, organelle, chloroplast ke andar.
  - NCERT: p006-i4 You would of course answer: in ‘the green leaf’ or ‘in the chloroplasts’, based 
  - kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.2) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; organ aur organelle, do paimaane ki jagah.
**11.3-k2** · example · Toh batao, paudhe ke aur kaun-se hisse aapko hare dikhte hain? Hare hisse aapne roz dekhe hain. Naye paudhon ke hare tane, phool ke neeche ki hari pattiyan, aur kachche hare phal. Phool ke neeche ki in chhoti hari pattiyon ko sepals kehte hain. Khilne se pehle yahi kali ko dhak kar rakhti hain. In sab mein photosynthesis ho sakta hai. Inki cells mein bhi chloroplast hote hain, aur in tak roshni pahunchti hai. Ab ek mazedaar misaal dekho: registan ka nagphani, jise Opuntia bhi kehte hain. Nagphani par hari pattiyan dikhti hi nahi. Uske nukile kaante hi uski pattiyan hain, jo badal kar kaante ban gayi hain. Toh jab hari pattiyan hi nahi, toh nagphani apna khana kahan banata hai? Nagphani apna khana apne chapte, mote aur hare tane mein banata hai. Paudhon ki banawat wale chapter mein aapne padha tha ki Opuntia ka tana chapta aur hara ho jaata hai. Usme chlorophyll hota hai. Registan mein chaudi pattiyan bahut paani uda deti hain. Isliye wahan pattiyan kaante ban gayi, aur khana banane ka kaam hare tane ne sambhal liya. Phir bhi zyadatar paudhon mein sabse zyada khana patti hi banati hai. Woh chapti aur chaudi hoti hai, isliye sabse zyada roshni pakadti hai. Toh niyam yaad rakho: jo hissa hara hai aur jis par roshni padti hai, woh khana bana sakta hai.
  - screen: Ek phoolon wala paudha: naya hara tana, phool ke neeche hari sepals (label: sepals), ek kachcha hara phal; har hare hisse ke andar chhote hare chloroplast ke daane aur upar se aati roshni. Phir registan mein nagphani (Opuntia): nukile kaante (label: badli hui pattiyan) aur chapta, mota, hara tana (label: khana yahan banta hai); registan ki dhoop mein chaudi patti se udta paani. Aakhir mein ek chaudi patti par sabse zyada roshni ke teer.
  - NCERT: p006-i5 Can you name some other parts where you think photosynthesis may occur?
  - kitaab se bahar: Opuntia (nagphani) ki misaal, paudhon ki banawat wale chapter se; registan mein pattiyon ka kaante banna.
**11.3-k3** · samjhana · Chalo, ab patti ke andar chalte hain, kyunki sabse zyada khana wahi banta hai. Patti ki upar aur neeche, dono taraf, cells ki ek patli bahari parat hoti hai. Ise epidermis kehte hain. In dono paraton ke beech jo hari cells bhari hoti hain, unhe mesophyll kehte hain. Naam todo toh matlab khul jaata hai: meso yaani beech, aur phyll yaani patti. Yaani patti ke beech ka hissa. Mesophyll ki cells mein bahut saare chloroplast hote hain. Ek cell mein aksar 20 se 40 tak. Isliye samjho ki patti ka asli khana banane wala hissa yahi mesophyll hai. Yaad karo, plant cell ke chaaron taraf ek mazboot deewar hoti hai, jise cell wall kehte hain. Mesophyll cell ka zyadatar beech ka hissa ek bada vacuole ghere rehta hai. Yeh paani se bhari ek thaili hai. Isliye chloroplast cell mein bikhre nahi rehte. Aksar woh deewaron ke saath-saath ek line mein lag jaate hain. Par chloroplast deewaron ke saath hi kyun lagte hain? Deewaron ke saath woh isliye lagte hain, taaki unhe aane wali roshni theek matra mein mile. Bahar se aakar cell par girne wali roshni ko incident light kehte hain. Incident yaani aakar padne wala. Ab ek shabd par gaur karo: chloroplast ko roshni sabse zyada nahi, sabse theek matra mein chahiye. Kaam ke liye sabse theek matra ko optimum kehte hain. Na itni kam ki kaam dheema pade, na itni zyada ki nuksaan ho. Kyunki bahut tez roshni chlorophyll aur chloroplast ke hisson ko nuksaan pahuncha sakti hai. Exam mein yeh baareek baat pakdi jaati hai: optimum ka matlab maximum, yaani sabse zyada, nahi hai. Socho, sardi ki subah hum dhoop ki taraf munh karke baithte hain. Par garmi ki dopahar mein dhoop se bachkar side ho jaate hain. Toh kya chloroplast bhi roshni ke hisaab se apni jagah badalte hain? Haan, chloroplast bhi jagah badalte hain; ab yahi dekhte hain.
  - terms: mesophyll = Patti ki upar aur neeche ki epidermis ke beech ka hara bhaag; meso = beech, phyll = patti.; incident light = Bahar se aakar kisi satah par girne wali roshni; incident = aakar padne wala.; optimum = Kaam ke liye sabse theek matra, na kam na zyada; sabse zyada (maximum) nahi.
  - screen: Ek patti ka cross-section: upar aur neeche patli epidermis ki parat (label), beech mein hari cells (label: mesophyll = meso 'beech' + phyll 'patti'). Ek mesophyll cell paas se: mazboot cell wall, beech mein bada paani bhara vacuole, aur kinaaron par deewar ke saath line mein lage 20-40 hare chloroplast; upar se aati roshni ka teer (label: incident light). Ek paimaana: kam, optimum (beech mein hara), bahut zyada (laal); neeche 'optimum ≠ maximum'. Kone mein do chhote chitra: sardi ki subah dhoop ki taraf munh karke baitha bachcha, garmi ki dopahar mein dhoop se bachkar side baitha bachcha.
  - NCERT: p006-i6 You would recollect from previous unit that the mesophyll cells in the leaves, h
  - kitaab se bahar: Ek mesophyll cell mein 20 se 40 chloroplast (cell wale chapter se); bada vacuole; sardi-garmi ki dhoop ki misaal; optimum aur maximum ka farq.
**11.3-k4** · ruko, socho · Pehle chloroplast ki shakal pakki kar lo. Woh gend jaisa gol nahi, masoor ke daane jaisa chapta hota hai. Isliye uska ek chauda, chapta chehra hota hai, aur ek patla kinaara. Ab do shabd yaad karo. Parallel yaani saath-saath chalne wali do rekhaayein, jo kabhi nahi miltin, jaise rail ki do patriyan. Aur perpendicular yaani ek-doosre ko seedha, nabbe degree par kaatne wali, jaise farsh aur deewar. Ab ek mesophyll cell socho, jis par roshni seedhe upar se neeche aa rahi hai. Is cell ki upar aur neeche ki deewarein roshni ko seedha kaat-ti hain. Yaani yeh roshni ke perpendicular hain. Aur bagal ki deewarein roshni ke saath-saath upar se neeche chalti hain. Yaani yeh roshni ke parallel hain. Ek clue lo: chapta chehra roshni ki taraf ho toh zyada roshni pakdi jaati hai. Sirf kinaara ho toh kam. Ruko, pehle khud socho: kam roshni mein chloroplast apna chapta chehra kis taraf rakhenge, aur tez roshni mein kis taraf? Aapne socha hoga ki kam roshni mein chehra roshni ki taraf, aur tez roshni mein kinaara; aap bilkul sahi ho. Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar aur neeche ki deewaron par jama ho jaate hain. Woh apna chapta chehra un deewaron ke parallel rakhte hain. Tab unka chehra roshni ke perpendicular hota hai. Isliye woh girti hui roshni ka zyada se zyada hissa pakad lete hain. Tez roshni mein yahi chloroplast khisak kar bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain. Chapta chehra ab un deewaron ke parallel rehta hai. Bagal ki deewarein roshni ke parallel hain. Isliye ab sirf patla kinaara roshni ki taraf rehta hai, aur kam roshni pakdi jaati hai. Is tarah tez roshni ka nuksaan bach jaata hai. Yeh wahi garmi ki dopahar mein side ho jaane wali baat hai. Gaur karo, dono haalaton mein chloroplast kisi na kisi deewar ke parallel hi lete hain. Farq bas itna hai ki kaun-si deewar. Exam mein aisa sawaal aaye toh pehle roshni ka teer aur deewar bana lo. Phir chehra lagao, uljhan khatam ho jaayegi. Paudha khud hil nahi sakta. Par uske chloroplast cell ke andar khisak kar roshni ke hisaab se jagah badal lete hain.
  - screen: Masoor ka daana aur uske paas chapta chloroplast: chauda chehra aur patla kinaara. Rail ki do patriyan (label: parallel) aur farsh-deewar ka 90° kona (label: perpendicular). Ek mesophyll cell, upar se neeche aati roshni ke teer; upar-neeche ki deewarein 'roshni ke perpendicular', bagal ki deewarein 'roshni ke parallel'. Sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir do cell saath-saath: 'kam roshni' mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par, chehra roshni ki taraf; 'tez roshni' mein bagal ki deewaron par, kinaara roshni ki taraf. Aakhir mein kaagaz par roshni ka teer aur deewar banata haath.
  - NCERT: p006-i7 When do you think the chloroplasts will be aligned with their flat surfaces para
  - kitaab se bahar: Kam aur tez roshni mein chloroplast ka khisakna, deewar aur roshni ki disha ke saath; masoor ke daane ki misaal.
  - jawab: Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par lagte hain: chapta chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur incident light ke perpendicular, taaki zyada roshni pakdein. Tez roshni mein woh bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain: chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur roshni ke bhi parallel, sirf kinaara roshni ki taraf, taaki kam roshni pakdein aur nuksaan na ho.
  - aam galti: Galat soch: tez roshni mein bhi chloroplast zyada roshni pakadne ke liye chehra roshni ki taraf karenge; ya 'deewar ke parallel' ka matlab hamesha roshni ke perpendicular. Asal mein dekhna padta hai ki kaun-si deewar hai: upar-neeche ki deewar roshni ke perpendicular hai, bagal ki deewar roshni ke parallel.
**11.3-k5** · samjhana · Ab chloroplast ke andar jhaanko: aapko wahan kya-kya dikhta hai? Chloroplast ke andar ka naksha aapne cell wale chapter mein dekha tha. Chalo, use phir se taaza karte hain. Membrane yaani ek patli jhilli, jo ek jagah ko doosri jagah se alag rakhti hai. Jaise ubale ande ke chhilke ke andar ki patli jhilli. Chloroplast ke chaaron taraf aisi do membranes ka gher hota hai, ek bahar wali aur ek andar wali. Is gher ke andar ek gaadha, jelly jaisa dravya bhara rehta hai. Kisi jagah ko bharne wale aise gaadhe dravya ko matrix kehte hain. Chloroplast ke is matrix ka naam stroma hai. Stroma ke beech membranes ka ek poora jaal faila hota hai. Ise chloroplast ka membrane system kehte hain. Is jaal mein chapti, band thailiyan hoti hain, jinhe thylakoid kehte hain. Dekho, yeh thailiyan ek ke upar ek aise rakhi hoti hain, jaise sikkon ka dher. Thylakoid ke aise ek dher ko granum kehte hain, aur kai dheron ko grana. Granum yaani daana, kyunki aam microscope mein yeh hare daanon jaise dikhte hain. Ek chloroplast mein aise kai grana hote hain, jo stroma mein yahan-wahan khade rehte hain. Ab ek dher se doosre dher tak patli, chapti membranes judi rehti hain. Jaise do imaaraton ko jodne wala pul. In jodne wali membranes ko stroma lamellae kehte hain. Lamella yaani patli parat, aur kai hon toh lamellae. Naam mein stroma isliye juda hai, kyunki yeh patli paratein dheron ke beech, stroma mein se hokar guzarti hain. Yahan ek baareek baat dhyan se samjho: stroma aur stroma lamellae ek cheez nahi hain. Stroma ek dravya hai jo sabko ghere hai. Stroma lamellae membranes hain, jo grana ko jodti hain. Toh chloroplast ke andar do tarah ki jagah hui. Ek, membranes, yaani grana aur stroma lamellae; doosri, unke chaaron taraf ka dravya, stroma.
  - terms: stroma = Chloroplast ke andar membranes ke chaaron taraf bhara gaadha dravya (matrix).; thylakoid = Chloroplast ke andar ki chapti, band membrane wali thaili.; grana = Thylakoid ke sikkon jaise dher; ek dher granum, kai dher grana; granum = daana.; stroma lamellae = Grana ko jodne wali patli, chapti, bina dher wali membranes; lamella = patli parat.
  - screen: Ubale ande ke chhilke ke andar ki patli jhilli (membrane ki misaal). Chloroplast ka bada chitra: bahar do parat ka gher (bahar wali, andar wali membrane); andar gaadha dravya (label: matrix = stroma). Stroma mein chapti band thailiyan (label: thylakoid) sikkon ke dher ki tarah (label: ek dher = granum, kai = grana); halke microscope mein wahi dher hare daanon jaise. Dheron ko jodti patli membranes pul ki tarah (label: stroma lamellae, lamella = patli parat). Do alag rang: membranes hari, stroma halka peela; neeche 'stroma ≠ stroma lamellae'.
  - NCERT: p006-i8 You have studied the structure of chloroplast in Chapter 8. Within the chloropla
  - kitaab se bahar: Thylakoid, granum ka arth (daana) aur lamella ka arth; ande ki jhilli ki misaal; chloroplast ka do membrane wala gher (cell wale chapter se).
**11.3-k6** · figure · Ab Figure 11.2 dekho; isme chloroplast ke andar ka yahi naksha bana hai. Yeh chitra ek khaas microscope ki photo se banaya gaya hai, jise electron microscope kehte hain. Microscope chhoti cheezon ko bada karke dikhata hai. Electron microscope itna bada dikhata hai ki chloroplast ki membranes bhi saaf dikh jaati hain. Electron microscope se li gayi photo ko electron micrograph kehte hain. Micro yaani chhota, aur graph yaani chitra. Yeh photo poore chloroplast ki nahi, uske ek bahut patle kate hue tukde ki hai. Jaise aam ko beech se kaatne par andar ka hissa dikhta hai. Andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kaate gaye aise patle tukde ko section kehte hain. Photo mein bahut kuch ek saath dikhta hai, isliye usse saaf rekhaon wala chitra banaya gaya. Aise chitra ko Diagrammatic representation kehte hain, yaani diagram ke roop mein dikhana. Isliye figure ke neeche likha hai: chloroplast ke section ki electron micrograph ka diagrammatic representation. Har label se ek patli rekha nikalti hai, jo theek us hisse tak jaati hai. Is rekha ke sire ko dekh kar hissa pehchaano. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo: sabse bahar ki rekha par likha hai Outer membrane, yaani bahar ki jhilli. Uske theek andar likha hai Inner membrane, yaani andar ki jhilli. Dono milkar chloroplast ka do parat wala gher banati hain. Ab andar sikkon jaise dher dekho; unpar likha hai Grana. Figure mein aise kai dher bane hain. In dheron ko jodti patli rekha par likha hai Stromal lamella. Yahan ek hi patti dikhayi gayi hai, isliye ekvachan lamella likha hai. Inke chaaron taraf ki jagah par likha hai Stroma. Chitra mein yeh khaali dikhti hai, par asal mein gaadhe dravya se bhari hai. Stroma mein chhote-chhote bindu dikhte hain, aur unpar likha hai Ribosomes. Ribosomes woh chhote kan hain jo protein banate hain. Chloroplast ke ribosomes chhote, 70S, hote hain, aur apne kuch protein yahin banate hain. Ek naarangi, andaakaar daana dikhta hai, jisme gole ke andar gole bane hain. Uspar likha hai Starch granule, yaani starch ka daana. Neeche ki taraf ek bhoori, gol boond par likha hai Lipid droplet. Lipid yaani charbi aur tel jaisi cheezein. Yeh starch nahi hai, isliye chitra ki har gol cheez ko starch mat samajhna. Gaur karo, is figure mein kul aath label hain, par koi teer nahi. Yeh sirf banawat dikhata hai, kaam kaise chalta hai yeh nahi.
  - terms: electron micrograph = Electron microscope se li gayi bahut badi photo; micro = chhota, graph = chitra.; section = Andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kisi cheez ka kaata gaya patla tukda.; Diagrammatic representation = Kisi photo ya cheez ko saaf rekhaon wale chitra (diagram) ke roop mein dikhana.
  - screen: Figure 11.2 ka saaf, bada chitra. Pehle electron microscope aur uski dhundhli kaali-safed photo; phir aam ko beech se kaatne ka chhota chitra (label: section); phir photo se bana saaf rekhaon wala diagram (label: diagrammatic representation) aur figure ke neeche ka naam. Phir ungli har leader line ke sire par baari-baari: Outer membrane, Inner membrane (do parat ka gher), Grana (kai sikkon jaise dher), Stromal lamella (dheron ko jodti ek patti), Stroma (gaadhe dravya se bhara), Ribosomes (chhote bindu, label 70S), Starch granule (naarangi, gole ke andar gole), Lipid droplet (bhoori boond, saath mein 'starch nahi'). Aakhir mein 'aath label, koi teer nahi'.
  - NCERT: p006-i12 Section of a chloroplast shown as an oval bounded by outer and inner membranes. 
  - kitaab se bahar: Electron microscope aur section ka matlab; chloroplast ke 70S ribosomes; lipid aur starch ka farq; figure mein koi teer nahi.
**11.3-k7** · samjhana · Ab asli sawaal: chloroplast ke andar photosynthesis ka kaam hota kahan hai, membranes par ya stroma mein? Photosynthesis ka kaam chloroplast mein dono jagah hota hai, membranes par bhi aur stroma mein bhi. Par dono jagah ka kaam alag hai. Ise ek cheeni mill ki tarah samjho. Mill ka ek hissa bijli banata hai, aur doosra hissa us bijli se cheeni banata hai. Jab ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baant kar har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale, toh ise division of labour kehte hain. Yaani kaam ka bantwara. Ab dekho, chloroplast mein bhi kaam ka ekdum saaf bantwara hai. Membranes ka kaam hai roshni ki energy ko pakad lena, jaise jaal mein machhli pakdi jaati hai. Roshni pakadne wala chlorophyll inhi membranes mein jada hota hai. Isliye roshni wala kaam yahin hota hai. Pakdi hui is energy se membranes do khaas molecule banati hain: ATP aur NADPH. Dekho, ATP ek molecule hai jo energy ko apne andar band karke chalta hai. Jahan kaam ho, wahan woh yeh energy de deta hai. Yaad karo, molecules ke andar band energy ko humne chemical energy kaha tha. ATP yahi chemical energy lekar chalta hai. Aur NADPH ek molecule hai jo electron aur hydrogen lekar chalta hai. Yaad karo, kisi molecule mein hydrogen judne ko humne reduce hona kaha tha. NADPH yahi hydrogen aur electron dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai. Inke poore naam, aur yeh dono bante kaise hain, yeh aage light reaction mein dekhenge. Abhi ke liye bas itna jaan lo. Ab stroma ka kaam dekho: stroma mein sugar banti hai. Yeh kaam enzymes karte hain. Enzyme ek tarah ka protein hai, jo reaction ko tez kar deta hai aur khud nahi badalta. Enzymes ki madad se chalne wali aisi reactions ko enzymatic reactions kehte hain. Stroma ki enzymatic reactions hawa se aayi CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banati hain. Aur yahi sugar aage starch banati hai, jo starch granule ban kar stroma mein jama rehti hai. Par sugar banane mein energy aur hydrogen, dono lagte hain. Yeh dono stroma ko membranes ke banaye ATP aur NADPH se milte hain. Par yeh dono kaam ek hi jagah kyun nahi hote? Dono kaam ek jagah isliye nahi hote, kyunki roshni pakadne wale molecules membrane par jade rehte hain. Yaad karo, opening mein humne dekha tha ki yeh molecules bikhre nahi, sahi kram mein lage hote hain. Jabki sugar banane wale enzymes stroma ke dravya mein ghule rehte hain. Wahin CO₂ aur baaki saamaan unke paas pahunchta rehta hai. Toh membranes is cheeni mill ka bijlighar hain, aur stroma woh floor jahan cheeni banti hai.
  - terms: division of labour = Ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baantna, jisme har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale; kaam ka bantwara.; ATP = Ek molecule jo chemical energy apne andar lekar chalta hai aur jahan kaam ho wahan de deta hai.; NADPH = Electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule, jo inhe dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai.; enzymatic reactions = Enzymes ki madad se chalne wali reactions; enzyme = reaction tez karne wala protein.
  - screen: Chloroplast ka cutaway, do hisse do rang mein: hari membranes (grana aur stroma lamellae) aur unke chaaron taraf halka peela stroma. Kone mein cheeni mill ka chhota chitra: ek taraf bijlighar, doosri taraf cheeni banane wala floor; label 'division of labour = kaam ka bantwara'. Roshni ke teer membranes par girte, jaal mein phansi energy; membranes se do moti nikalte (labels: ATP = energy lekar chalne wala; NADPH = electron + hydrogen lekar chalne wala) aur stroma mein jaate. Stroma mein enzymes (chhote protein) CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banate, sugar se starch granule. Membrane par pigment molecules ek kram mein jade, aur stroma mein enzymes tairte hue. Aakhir mein jod: membranes = bijlighar, stroma = cheeni wala floor.
  - NCERT: p006-i9 There is a clear division of labour within the chloroplast. The membrane system 
  - kitaab se bahar: Cheeni mill ki misaal; ATP aur NADPH ka abhi ke liye matlab (poora naam aage); enzyme kya hai; sugar ka carbon CO₂ se; dono kaam alag jagah kyun.
**11.3-k8** · analogy · Ab is cheeni mill ko poora khol kar dekho, taaki chloroplast ka har hissa ek saath yaad rahe. Mill ke chaaron taraf do chaardiwari hain; yeh chloroplast ki outer aur inner membrane hain. Mill ka bijlighar koyle se nahi, dhoop se chalta hai. Yeh grana ki membranes hain, jahan roshni pakdi jaati hai. Bijlighar ke alag-alag kamre galiyaron se jude hain; yeh galiyare stroma lamellae hain. Bijlighar se do cheezein floor tak jaati hain. ATP kaam ki energy deta hai, aur NADPH hydrogen aur electron deta hai. Mill ka bada floor stroma hai. Wahan ki machines enzymes hain, jo CO₂ se sugar banati hain. Jo cheeni turant kaam nahi aati, woh boriyon mein bhar kar godown mein rakh di jaati hai. Yeh boriyaan starch granule hain. Mill ki apni chhoti workshop bhi hai, jo machines ke purze banati hai. Yeh ribosomes hain, jo protein banate hain. Dekho, is mill mein har hissa ek hi kaam karta hai, isliye poora kaam tez aur bina gadbad chalta hai. Par yaad rakho, asli mill mein cheeni ganne se banti hai. Chloroplast ki sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai. Aur ATP aur NADPH khud sugar nahi bante. Woh energy aur hydrogen dekar khaali ho jaate hain, aur membrane par laut kar phir bhar jaate hain.
  - screen: Ek cheeni mill ka bada chitra, har hissa chloroplast ke hisse se juda: do chaardiwari = outer aur inner membrane; dhoop se chalta bijlighar = grana ki membranes; bijlighar ke kamron ke beech galiyare = stroma lamellae; bijlighar se floor tak do tokriyan = ATP (energy) aur NADPH (hydrogen + electron); bada floor = stroma, machines = enzymes, CO₂ andar aati, sugar banti; godown ki boriyaan = starch granule; chhoti workshop = ribosomes (protein). Kone mein 'mill: ganna → cheeni' aur 'chloroplast: CO₂ → sugar'; khaali tokriyan wapas bijlighar jaati hain.
  - kitaab se bahar: Cheeni mill ki poori misaal, aur woh kahan tak sahi hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: Outer aur inner membrane = mill ki do chaardiwari; grana ki membranes = dhoop se chalne wala bijlighar; stroma lamellae = bijlighar ke kamron ko jodne wale galiyare; ATP aur NADPH = bijlighar se floor tak jaane wali do cheezein (kaam ki energy, aur hydrogen-electron); stroma = mill ka floor; enzymes = floor ki machines; starch granule = godown ki boriyaan; ribosomes = purze banane wali workshop.
  - kahan tootti hai: Mill ki cheeni ganne se banti hai, par chloroplast ki sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai. ATP aur NADPH sugar ka kachcha maal nahi hain; woh sirf energy aur hydrogen-electron dekar khaali hote hain aur membrane par phir bhare jaate hain. Mill ki bijli taar se bahti hai, jabki ATP aur NADPH molecule hain jo stroma mein ghoom kar pahunchte hain.
**11.3-k9** · samjhana · Ab in dono kaamon ke apne-apne naam bhi hain, aur yeh naam aapko pakke yaad rakhne hain. Socho, membranes wali reactions roshni padte hi chal padti hain.
