Roshni hi unhe seedhe dhakelti hai. Jaise cheeni mill ka bijlighar tabhi chalta hai, jab us par dhoop padti hai. Jo reactions seedhe roshni se chalti hain, unhe light reactions kehte hain. Inka doosra naam photochemical reactions hai. Photo yaani roshni, aur chemical yaani rasayanik; yaani roshni se chalne wali rasayanik reactions. Yeh wahi membrane system hai jahan chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai, aur jahan ATP aur NADPH bante hain. Inhe light reactions isliye kehte hain, kyunki roshni band hote hi yeh pal bhar mein ruk jaati hain. Light reactions mein electron ka safar kaise chalta hai, yeh hum aage kholenge.
  - terms: light reactions = Chloroplast ki membranes mein seedhe roshni se chalne wali reactions.; photochemical reactions = Light reactions ka doosra naam; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
  - screen: Thylakoid ki membrane par roshni girte hi chamak aur kaam shuru; roshni hatate hi sab ruk jaata. Kone mein cheeni mill ka bijlighar: dhoop aate hi chalta, dhoop jaate hi band. Label: light reactions = photochemical reactions (photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik). Membrane mein chlorophyll roshni sokhta, ATP aur NADPH ke moti bante. Aakhir mein membrane par electron ke chhote safar par '?'.
  - NCERT: p006-i10 The former set of reactions, since they are directly light driven are called lig
  - kitaab se bahar: Roshni band hote hi light reactions ka turant rukna; electron ka safar aage.
**11.3-k10** · samjhana · Ab doosra kaam dekho, stroma wala: kya ise bhi seedhe roshni chahiye? Stroma ke kaam ko seedhe roshni ki zaroorat nahi, kyunki uske enzymes roshni pakadte hi nahi. Par use light reactions mein bane ATP aur NADPH chahiye. Inke bina sugar ban hi nahi sakti. Matlab yeh kaam roshni par seedhe nahi tika, balki ATP aur NADPH ke zariye tika hai. Seedhe kisi par tikna direct dependence hai, aur kisi doosre ke zariye tikna indirect dependence. Isi farq ko dikhane ke liye in reactions ko dark reactions kehte hain. Inka doosra naam carbon reactions hai, kyunki inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai. Ab cheeni mill yaad karo. Floor ki machines dhoop nahi dekhti, par bijlighar ki bijli se hi chalti hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: shaam ko jab bijlighar band ho jaaye, toh floor kitni der chalega? Aapne socha hoga ki bas thodi der, jab tak bachi hui bijli khatam na ho; bilkul theek. Waise hi andhera hote hi ATP aur NADPH banna ruk jaata hai. Phir thodi der mein dark reactions bhi ruk jaati hain. Aksar log naam sunkar sochte hain ki dark reactions andhere mein hoti hain, par aisa nahi hai. Samjho, dark reactions ko andhere ki koi zaroorat nahi. Yeh zyadatar din mein, light reactions ke saath-saath chalti hain. Yaad karo, light-dependent yaani jo roshni ke bina na chale. Dark reactions bhi light-dependent hain, bas ATP aur NADPH ke zariye. Toh phir aisa bhatkane wala naam kyun rakha gaya? Aisa naam isliye rakha gaya, kyunki yeh by convention chala aa raha hai. By convention yaani bahut pehle se sabka maana hua naam, chahe woh poori baat na bataye. Toh 'dark' ka matlab andhera nahi, sirf itna ki roshni inhe seedhe nahi chalati. Exam ka yeh sabse bada jaal hai: koi kahe ki dark reactions raat mein hi hoti hain, toh woh galat hai. ATP aur NADPH se stroma mein sugar kaise banti hai, yeh bhi hum aage dekhenge.
  - terms: dark reactions = Stroma ki sugar banane wali reactions, jo seedhe roshni se nahi chalti par light reactions ke ATP aur NADPH par tiki hain; andhere mein nahi hoti.; by convention = Bahut pehle se chala aa raha, sabka maana hua naam ya tareeka.; carbon reactions = Dark reactions ka doosra naam; inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai.
  - screen: Stroma mein enzymes, jin par roshni ka koi teer nahi; membranes se ATP aur NADPH ke moti stroma mein aate, unse sugar banti. Do teer: roshni → membranes (seedha, label 'direct') aur roshni → ATP + NADPH → stroma (ghuma kar, label 'indirect'). Label: dark reactions = carbon reactions; CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta. Cheeni mill: shaam ko bijlighar band, floor ki machines thodi der chal kar ruk jaati; sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir chloroplast par din ka sooraj: light reactions aur dark reactions saath-saath chalti; 'dark = andhera?' par kaata hua nishaan, aur neeche chhota label 'by convention: purana maana hua naam'.
  - NCERT: p006-i11 The latter are not directly light driven but are dependent on the products of li
  - kitaab se bahar: Direct aur indirect dependence ka matlab; carbon reactions naam ki wajah; andhere mein dark reactions ka thodi der mein ruk jaana; cheeni mill ki shaam.
**11.3-k11** · asli duniya · Yeh baat aap roz ki sabzi mandi mein bhi pehchaan sakte ho. Aapne dekha hoga, kachcha tamatar hara hota hai, hari mirch hari hoti hai, aur matar ki phali bhi hari hoti hai. In sab ki cells mein chloroplast hote hain, isliye dhoop mein yeh bhi thoda khana bana lete hain. Toh pakne par tamatar laal kyun ho jaata hai? Pakne par tamatar isliye laal hota hai, kyunki uske chloroplast badal kar laal rang wale plastid ban jaate hain. Inhe chromoplast kehte hain. Saath mein uska chlorophyll toot jaata hai, isliye paka laal tamatar khana banana chhod deta hai. Ghar ke money plant ki hari bel bhi, pattiyon ke saath-saath, apne hare tane se thoda khana banati hai.
  - screen: Sabzi mandi ki tokriyan: kachche hare tamatar, hari mirch, matar ki hari phaliyan; har ek ke andar chhote hare chloroplast aur upar dhoop. Phir ek tamatar hara se naarangi se laal hota, andar hare chloroplast laal chromoplast mein badalte. Aakhir mein khidki par money plant ki hari bel, tane par bhi chloroplast ke daane.
  - kitaab se bahar: Kachche hare phal, pakne par chloroplast ka chromoplast banna; money plant ka hara tana.
**11.3-k12** · poori kahani dobara · Ab aaj ka poora safar ek saath dohra lo. Humne dekha ki paudhe mein yeh kaam patti mein sabse zyada hota hai, aur hare tane, sepals aur kachche phal mein bhi. Patti ke andar yeh kaam mesophyll ki cells karti hain, jinme dheron chloroplast deewaron se lage rehte hain. Aapne dekha ki kam roshni mein chloroplast apna chehra roshni ki taraf karte hain, aur tez roshni mein apna kinaara. Chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes roshni pakad kar ATP aur NADPH banati hain. Yeh light reactions hain. Stroma ke enzymes unhi se sugar aur phir starch banate hain. Yeh dark reactions hain, jinhe andhere ki zaroorat nahi. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: yeh kaam kin hisson mein, aur unke andar kahan hota hai? Yeh kaam un sab hare hisson mein hota hai, jahan chloroplast hon aur roshni pahunche. Sabse zyada yeh patti ke mesophyll mein hota hai. Aur un hisson ke andar theek jagah hai chloroplast: roshni wala kaam uski membranes par, aur sugar wala kaam stroma mein. Bilkul us cheeni mill ki tarah, jahan bijlighar aur floor ne kaam aapas mein baant rakha hai. Par ek baat abhi baaki hai: membranes roshni pakadti kis cheez se hain, aur patti ka hara rang aakhir kis cheez ka hai? Patti ka hara rang kis cheez se banta hai, aur membranes roshni kaise pakadti hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Ek line mein aaj ka safar: poora paudha (patti, hara tana, sepals, kachcha phal chamakte) → patti ka cross-section (mesophyll) → cell ki deewaron se lage chloroplast (kam roshni mein chehra, tez roshni mein kinaara) → chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae (roshni → ATP + NADPH, label light reactions) aur stroma (sugar → starch, label dark reactions). Shuru ka sawaal phir aata hai, saath mein jawab: hare hisse, mesophyll, chloroplast, membranes aur stroma; kone mein cheeni mill ka bijlighar aur floor. Aakhir mein hari patti par sawaal ka nishaan, 'hara rang kis cheez ka?', jo khula rehta hai.

## 11.4 HOW MANY TYPES OF PIGMENTS ARE INVOLVED IN PHOTOSYNTHESIS?

_Maqsad: Agar patti ka hara rang sirf chlorophyll se aata, toh ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein hare ke itne alag-alag shade kyun hote?_

_Misaal: Ganga aur uski sahayak nadiyaan: Ganga = chlorophyll a (mukhya nadi, apne ilaake ki baarish khud batorti hai, jaise chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai); Yamuna, Ghaghra, Kosi jaisi sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids (accessory = sahayak); baarish ke alag-alag ilaake = roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein milna = pigments ki pakdi energy chlorophyll a tak pahunchna; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga ka kul paani = poora photosynthesis (action spectrum), Ganga ke apne ilaake ka paani = chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum_

Kitab ki baatein (84):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · shades · Ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein bhi hare rang ke kai shades dikhte hain; sawaal hai ki aisa kyun aur kaise hota hai.
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · paper chromatography · Is sawaal ka jawab paane ke liye kisi bhi hare paudhe ki patti ke pigments ko paper chromatography se alag kiya ja sakta hai.
- f3 · kitab kehti hai · Chromatography se alag karne par dikhta hai ki patti ka rang ek pigment se nahi, chaar pigments se banta hai.
- f4 · shabd ka matlab · paper chromatography · Paper chromatography: kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko alag karne ka tareeka; chromato = rang, graphy = likhna; solvent kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai aur har pigment ko alag oonchai tak le jaata hai.
- f5 · pehle ki zaroori baat · mixture · Mixture: kai cheezon ka mel jisme har cheez apni pehchaan rakhti hai aur use alag kiya ja sakta hai; patti ke chloroplast mein kai pigments ka mel hota hai (leaves contain chloroplasts).
- f6 · pehle ki zaroori baat · chlorophyll · Chlorophyll: patti ka hara pigment jo roshni pakadta hai; chloro = hara, phyll = patti; yeh chloroplast ki thylakoid membranes mein hota hai.
- f7 · teacher ki extra baat · Chromatography ka naam aur tareeka 1906 mein roosi vaigyanik Mikhail Tsvet ne patti ke pigments alag karte hue diya tha.
- f8 · pehle ki zaroori baat · solvent · Solvent: woh dravya jisme cheezein ghul jaati hain; chromatography mein acetone jaisa solvent patti ke pigments ghol leta hai aur kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai.
- f9 · teacher ki extra baat · Palak ki patti ka ras solvent mein; kaagaz ke lambe tukde par neeche ek gaadha hara bindu; kaagaz ka sira solvent mein aur bindu solvent se upar; solvent upar chadhte hue pigments ko alag-alag oonchai tak le jaata hai: jo pigment zyada ghulta aur kaagaz se kam chipakta hai, woh upar; yeh wazan se tay nahi hota.
- f10 · kitab kehti hai · Chlorophyll a · Chlorophyll a chromatogram mein chamakdaar ya neela-hara (bright or blue green) dikhta hai.
- f11 · kitab kehti hai · chlorophyll b · Chlorophyll b peela-hara (yellow green) dikhta hai.
- f12 · kitab kehti hai · xanthophylls · Xanthophylls peele (yellow) dikhte hain.
- f13 · kitab kehti hai · carotenoids · Carotenoids peele se peela-naarangi (yellow to yellow-orange) dikhte hain.
- f14 · kitab kehti hai · Ab dekhna hai ki photosynthesis mein alag-alag pigments kya kaam karte hain.
- f15 · shabd ka matlab · chromatogram · Chromatogram: alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wala kaagaz, chromatography ka nateeja.
- f16 · shabd ka matlab · chlorophyll a; chlorophyll b · Chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b: dono hare chlorophyll, par molecule thoda alag, isliye unka hara aur unki sokhi roshni thodi alag.
- f17 · shabd ka matlab · xanthophylls · Xanthophylls: xantho = peela, phyll = patti; patti ke peele pigments.
- f18 · shabd ka matlab · carotenoids · Carotenoids: naam carrot (gaajar) se; gaajar ka naarangi rang inhi ka; peele se naarangi pigments.
- f19 · teacher ki extra baat · Chromatogram par neeche se upar: chlorophyll b, chlorophyll a, xanthophylls, aur sabse upar carotenoids.
- f20 · teacher ki extra baat · Xanthophylls bhi carotenoids ke parivaar ke hain, par kaagaz par apni alag lakeer banate hain.
- f21 · teacher ki extra baat · shades · Har patti mein yeh chaar pigments alag-alag matra mein hote hain: chlorophyll zyada to gehra hara, peele pigments ka hissa zyada to halka, peela-sa hara; isliye ek hi paudhe mein hare ke kai shades.
- f22 · teacher ki extra baat · Patjhad mein chlorophyll toot jaata hai aur pehle se maujood peele-naarangi carotenoids dikhne lagte hain.
- f23 · teacher ki extra baat · stable · Andhere mein rakhi patti peeli ya halki hari: chlorophyll lagaataar toot-ta hai aur naya chlorophyll banne ke liye roshni chahiye; peele carotenoids zyada tikau (stable) hain, isliye bache rehte hain.
- f24 · teacher ki extra baat · Chhaanv wali pattiyan aksar zyada gehri hari: kam roshni pakadne ke liye unme chlorophyll zyada (chlorophyll b ka hissa bhi zyada); dhoop wali pattiyon mein chlorophyll kam aur bachaav wale peele pigments ka hissa zyada.
- f25 · teacher ki extra baat · Andhere mein ugi aaloo ki konpal peeli-safed hoti hai aur roshni milte hi hari ho jaati hai.
- f26 · kitab kehti hai · specific wavelengths · Pigments woh padarth hain jinme roshni ko sokhne ki kshamta hai, par sirf kuch khaas wavelengths (specific wavelengths) par.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · pigments · Pigments: rang wale padarth jo roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths sokhte hain aur baaki lauta dete hain; jo rang lautate hain, wahi unka dikhne wala rang.
- f28 · shabd ka matlab · absorb · Absorb: sokhna; roshni ki energy ko apne andar le lena.
- f29 · shabd ka matlab · wavelength · Wavelength: roshni ki lehar mein ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori; har rang ki apni wavelength, neele ki chhoti, laal ki lambi.
- f30 · pehle ki zaroori baat · colours of light · Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai (light contains different colours); prism use indradhanush jaise rangon mein tod deta hai; har rang ki apni wavelength hai (colour corresponds to wavelength).
- f31 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Koi cheez jis rang ki dikhti hai, woh rang woh lautati hai aur baaki sokh leti hai; kaali cheez lagbhag sab rang sokhti hai, safed sab lautati hai.
- f32 · teacher ki extra baat · Patti hari isliye dikhti hai kyunki chlorophyll hari roshni sabse kam sokhta hai aur use lauta deta hai; hari roshni photosynthesis mein sabse kam kaam aati hai. Galat soch: hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai.
- f33 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: duniya mein sabse zyada paya jaane wala plant pigment kaunsa hai? Jawab: chlorophyll a.
- f34 · teacher ki extra baat · Chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur har hari alga mein hota hai; jahan bhi photosynthesis mein oxygen nikalti hai, wahan chlorophyll a hota hai.
- f35 · kitab kehti hai · Graph (Figure 11.3a) dikhata hai ki chlorophyll a alag-alag wavelengths ki roshni kitni sokhta hai.
- f36 · kitab kehti hai · VIBGYOR · Dikhne wali roshni (visible spectrum) ki wavelengths aur VIBGYOR ka kram aap jaante hain.
- f37 · shabd ka matlab · VIBGYOR · VIBGYOR: dikhne wali roshni ke rangon ka kram: violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange, red (baingni, jamuni, neela, hara, peela, naarangi, laal); violet ki wavelength sabse chhoti, red ki sabse lambi.
- f38 · shabd ka matlab · visible spectrum · Visible spectrum: roshni ka woh hissa jo aankh dekh sakti hai, lagbhag 400 se 700 nm.
- f39 · shabd ka matlab · nanometre · Nanometre (nm): lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai; 1 nm = 10⁻⁹ metre, yaani metre ka ek arabvaan hissa.
- f40 · shabd ka matlab · absorption spectrum · Absorption spectrum: ek pigment har wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska graph; absorption spectra iska bahuvachan.
- f41 · pehle ki zaroori baat · graph · Graph: neeche ki leti line aur khadi line; line jitni oonchi, utna zyada; sabse oonchi jagah choti (peak, maximum), sabse neechi jagah minimum; har line (curve) ke paas uska naam (label).
- f42 · pehle ki zaroori baat · violet; indigo; blue; green; yellow; orange; red · Visible light ke saat rang, chhoti se lambi wavelength ke kram mein (visible light has different wavelengths).
- f43 · pehle ki zaroori baat · horizontal axis; vertical axis · Horizontal axis: neeche ki leti line; vertical axis: khadi line (horizontal and vertical graph axes).
- f44 · kitab kehti hai · Sawaal: Figure 11.3a mein chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni kis wavelength (kis rang) par sokhta hai, aur kya kisi aur wavelength par bhi choti hai? Jawab: sabse zyada neele hisse mein, lagbhag 430 nm par; doosri choti laal hisse mein, lagbhag 660 nm par.
- f45 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.3a mein teen absorption curves; chlorophyll a ki badi choti neele hisse mein lagbhag 420–430 nm par aur doosri laal hisse mein lagbhag 660–670 nm par; beech ke zyadatar hisse mein absorption kam.
- f46 · kitab kehti hai · Chlorophyll b ki oonchi choti lagbhag 460 nm par aur chhoti laal choti lagbhag 645 nm par.
- f47 · kitab kehti hai · Carotenoids zyadatar neele hisse mein sokhte hain, choudi chotiyan lagbhag 450–480 nm par, aur lagbhag 520 nm ke baad lagbhag zero.
- f48 · figure ka nishaan · Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments · Khadi line ka label: chloroplast ke pigments roshni kitni sokhte hain; is par koi number nahi.
- f49 · figure ka nishaan · Chlorophyll a · Chlorophyll a ka teer us line par jiski chotiyan neele aur laal mein hain.
- f50 · figure ka nishaan · Chlorophyll b · Chlorophyll b ka teer us line par jiski oonchi choti neele mein aur chhoti choti laal mein hai.
- f51 · figure ka nishaan · Carotenoids · Carotenoids ka teer us line par jo sirf neele hisse mein oonchi hai, laal mein lagbhag zero.
- f52 · shabd ka matlab · absorbance · Absorbance: koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap; absorb = sokhna.
- f53 · teacher ki extra baat · Graph ki line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi; pigment ko naam wale teer se pehchaante hain. Pigment ka dikhne wala rang uska sokha hua rang nahi: carotenoids peele-naarangi dikhte hain par neeli roshni sokhte hain (pigment colour differs from absorbed colour).
- f54 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.3b dikhata hai ki kin wavelengths par paudhe mein photosynthesis sabse zyada hota hai.
- f55 · kitab kehti hai · Jin wavelengths par chlorophyll a sabse zyada sokhta hai, yaani neele aur laal hisse mein, wahin photosynthesis ki rate bhi zyada hai.
- f56 · shabd ka matlab · action spectrum · Action spectrum: har wavelength par photosynthesis kitna hua, uska graph; action = kaam.
- f57 · pehle ki zaroori baat · O₂ release · Photosynthesis mein oxygen (O₂) bahar nikalti hai (photosynthesis releases oxygen); jitna zyada photosynthesis, utni zyada O₂; Engelmann ke bacteria isi O₂ ke paas jama hue the.
- f58 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.3b ki kaali line photosynthesis ki rate dikhati hai, jo O₂ nikalne se naapi gayi: neele hisse mein oonchi, hare se peela-hara hisse mein lagbhag 550–570 nm par sabse neechi, laal hisse mein lagbhag 680 nm par sabse oonchi, aur lagbhag 700 nm ke paas tezi se neeche.
- f59 · kitab kehti hai · Beech ke hisse mein bhi photosynthesis ki rate zero nahi hoti.

- f62 · shabd ka matlab · rate of photosynthesis · Rate of photosynthesis: ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua; rate = tay samay mein kitna kaam.
- f63 · kitab kehti hai · chief pigment · Isliye chlorophyll a photosynthesis se juda chief pigment, yaani mukhya pigment, hai.
- f64 · shabd ka matlab · chief pigment · Chief pigment: mukhya pigment, sabse bada kaam karne wala; chief ka matlab akela (sole) nahi.

- f67 · kitab kehti hai · Neeche ki leti line par 400, 500, 600 aur 700 ke nishaan; dono lines alag-alag cheezein naapti hain, isliye unki oonchai seedhe nahi milaate.
- f68 · figure ka nishaan · Rate of photosynthesis · Kaali line ka naam Rate of photosynthesis: yahi action spectrum.
- f69 · figure ka nishaan · Absorption · Doosri line ka naam Absorption: sirf chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum, saare pigments ka nahi.
- f70 · figure ka nishaan · Light absorbed · Khadi line par likha label: Light absorbed.
- f71 · figure ka nishaan · Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm) · Neeche ki leti line ka label: Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm), nishaan 400, 500, 600, 700.
- f72 · shabd ka matlab · superimposed · Superimposed: tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhna; super = upar.
- f73 · kitab kehti hai · one-to-one overlap · Sawaal: Figure 11.3c dekh kar kya chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur photosynthesis ke action spectrum mein poora one-to-one overlap hai? Jawab: nahi.
- f74 · shabd ka matlab · one-to-one overlap · One-to-one overlap: har wavelength par dono lines ka ekdum saath-saath chalna, ek line doosri par poori chadh jaaye.
- f75 · kitab kehti hai · Teeno graph milkar dikhate hain ki zyadatar photosynthesis spectrum ke neele aur laal hisse mein hota hai; kuch photosynthesis dikhne wali roshni ki baaki wavelengths par bhi hota hai.
- f76 · kitab kehti hai · accessory pigments · Roshni pakadne ka mukhya pigment chlorophyll hai, par thylakoid ke doosre pigments, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids, bhi roshni sokhte hain aur energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain; inhe accessory pigments kehte hain.
- f77 · kitab kehti hai · wider range of wavelength · Accessory pigments aane wali roshni ki zyada choudi range (wider range of wavelength) ko photosynthesis ke kaam laate hain.
- f78 · kitab kehti hai · photo-oxidation · Accessory pigments chlorophyll a ko photo-oxidation se bachate bhi hain.
- f79 · shabd ka matlab · accessory pigments · Accessory pigments: sahayak pigments; accessory = saath dene wala, madad karne wala.
- f80 · shabd ka matlab · photo-oxidation · Photo-oxidation: tez roshni ki zyada energy se pigment ka kharaab hokar toot jaana; photo = roshni; oxidation = molecule ka apne electron ya hydrogen kho dena.
- f81 · teacher ki extra baat · Carotenoids zyada energy ko garmi bana kar surakshit nikaal dete hain, isliye tez dhoop mein chlorophyll a bacha rehta hai.
- f82 · pehle ki zaroori baat · thylakoid · Thylakoid: chloroplast ki chapti band thailiyan; inki membranes mein saare pigments baithe hain (chloroplast thylakoids).
- f83 · teacher ki extra baat · Jis paudhe mein chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho, woh photosynthesis nahi kar payega: baaki pigments ki pakdi energy bhi chlorophyll a tak hi jaati hai aur aage ka kaam wahi shuru karta hai; chlorophyll b aur doosre pigments choudi range aur bachaav ke liye hain.
- f84 · figure ka nishaan · VIBGYOR · Graph ki leti line par rang VIBGYOR ke kram mein baayein se daayein: violet sabse chhoti wavelength par, red sabse lambi par; isiliye neeli choti baayein aur laal choti daayein dikhti hai.

**11.4-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli baar humne photosynthesis ki jagah dhoondhi: paudhe ke hare hisse, sabse zyada patti ka mesophyll, aur uski cells mein chloroplast. Chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes roshni pakadti hain, aur stroma mein sugar banti hai. Yaad rakhein: roshni pakadne ka kaam chloroplast ki membranes par, yaani thylakoid ki thailiyon par, hota hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki in membranes mein aisa kya baitha hai jo roshni ko pakad leta hai. Pichhli film ke aakhir mein humne do baatein khuli chhodi thi. Pehli, patti ka hara rang kis cheez ka hai; doosri, membranes roshni kaise pakadti hain. Pehli baat par aap turant kahoge: hara rang chlorophyll ka hai, kyunki chloro yaani hara aur phyll yaani patti. Par ek baat par gaur karo: ek hi paudhe ki nayi pattiyan aksar halki hari hoti hain, purani gehri hari, aur kuch peeli-si. Agar patti ka hara rang sirf chlorophyll se aata, toh ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein hare ke itne alag-alag shade kyun hote? Hare ke itne shade kyun hote hain, yahi aaj ka pehla sawaal hai. Uske saath judega doosra sawaal: patti roshni pakadti kaise hai. Rang ka raaz kholne ka ek seedha tareeka hai: patti ke rang ko uske hisson mein tod kar dekho. Aapne shayad kaali sketch pen ki line geele kaagaz par dekhi ho. Paani upar chadhte hue usme se neela, baingni aur peela rang alag kar deta hai. Kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko aise alag karne ke tareeke ko paper chromatography kehte hain. Naam todo toh kaam samajh aata hai: chromato yaani rang, aur graphy yaani likhna. Kaagaz par har rang apni jagah khud likh deta hai. Yahi tareeka kisi bhi hare paudhe ki patti par lagao. Tab patti ke pigments ka mel khul jaata hai, aur har pigment alag dikhta hai. Dilchasp baat yeh hai ki chromatography bani hi patti ke pigments alag karne ke liye thi. 1906 mein roosi vaigyanik Mikhail Tsvet ne yeh tareeka diya. Toh aaj hum teen kadam chalenge. Pehle patti ke rang alag karenge, phir dekhenge ki kaun-sa rang kaun-si roshni pakadta hai, aur aakhir mein ki mukhya kaam kaun karta hai.
  - terms: paper chromatography = Kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko alag karne ka tareeka; chromato = rang, graphy = likhna.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: hare paudhe se patti, patti ke mesophyll se cell, cell ke chloroplast; chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes par roshni ke teer, stroma mein sugar; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: roshni pakadne ka kaam thylakoid ki membranes par'. Phir ek hi paudhe ki teen pattiyan: nayi halki hari, purani gehri hari, ek peeli-si; sawaal ka dabba 'Itne shade kyun?'. Kaali sketch pen ki line wala geela kaagaz, paani chadhte hi neela, baingni aur peela rang alag hote. Label: paper chromatography (chromato = rang, graphy = likhna); kone mein '1906, Mikhail Tsvet'. Aakhir mein teen kadam ki patti: rang alag, kaun kaun-si roshni, mukhya kaun.
  - NCERT: p007-i2 Looking at plants have you ever wondered why and how there are so many shades of
  - kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.3) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; nayi aur purani pattiyon ke shade; kaali syaahi ki chromatography; chromato + graphy; 1906 mein Mikhail Tsvet.
**11.4-k2** · example · Chalo, palak ki ek patti lekar yeh karke dekhte hain. Pehle hum patti ko acetone jaise solvent mein peeste hain; solvent yaani woh dravya jisme cheezein ghul jaati hain. Patti ke saare rang usme ghul jaate hain, aur gehra hara ras ban jaata hai. Ab kaagaz ka ek lamba tukda lo, aur uske neeche wale sire se thoda upar is ras ka ek gaadha hara bindu lagao. Kaagaz ka sira solvent mein dubao, par dhyan rahe ki bindu solvent se upar rahe. Ab dekho, solvent dheere-dheere kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai, jaise tissue paper ka kona paani mein dubane par paani upar chadh jaata hai. Chadhta hua solvent bindu ke rangon ko bhi apne saath le chalta hai. Par har rang ek jitna upar nahi jaata; aisa kyun? Har rang alag oonchai tak isliye jaata hai, kyunki har pigment ka ghulna alag hai. Jo solvent mein zyada ghulta hai aur kaagaz se kam chipakta hai, woh tez bhaagta hai. Jo pigment kam ghulta hai aur kaagaz se zyada chipakta hai, woh peechhe reh jaata hai. Dhyan do, yeh alag hona pigment ke wazan se nahi hota, ghulne aur chipakne se hota hai. Ab thodi der baad kaagaz ko dekho. Us ek hare bindu ki jagah wahan chaar alag rangon ki lakeerein ban gayi hain. Alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wale is kaagaz ko chromatogram kehte hain. Ab ungli rakh kar neeche se upar chalo: sabse neeche peela-hara rang hai, yeh chlorophyll b hai. Uske upar chamakdaar, neela-hara rang hai; yeh chlorophyll a hai. Phir peele rang ki lakeer aati hai; yeh xanthophylls hain. Aur sabse upar peele se naarangi rang ki lakeer hai; yeh carotenoids hain. Matlab jo ek hara bindu dikh raha tha, woh asal mein chaar pigments ka mel tha. Chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b, dono hare chlorophyll hain; bas unka molecule thoda alag hai, isliye unka hara bhi thoda alag hai. Xanthophylls ka naam todo: xantho yaani peela, aur phyll yaani patti; matlab patti ka peela pigment. Carotenoids ka naam carrot, yaani gaajar, se aaya hai; gaajar ka naarangi rang inhi pigments ka hai. Xanthophylls bhi asal mein carotenoids ke hi parivaar ke hain, par kaagaz par apni alag lakeer banate hain.
  - terms: chromatogram = Alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wala kaagaz, chromatography ka nateeja.; chlorophyll a = Chamakdaar ya neela-hara chlorophyll; photosynthesis ka mukhya pigment.; chlorophyll b = Peela-hara chlorophyll.; xanthophylls = Patti ke peele pigments; xantho = peela, phyll = patti.; carotenoids = Peele se naarangi pigments; naam carrot (gaajar) se.
  - screen: Lab ki mez: palak ki patti acetone mein peesi jaati, gehra hara ras banta; kaagaz ke lambe tukde par neeche ek gaadha hara bindu; tukde ka sira solvent mein, bindu solvent se upar. Solvent kaagaz mein upar chadhta (kone mein paani mein dooba tissue paper), saath mein rang upar khisakte; chhota label 'wazan se nahi: ghulna + chipakna'. Thodi der baad chaar lakeerein, neeche se upar ungli ke saath: peela-hara (chlorophyll b), chamakdaar neela-hara (chlorophyll a), peela (xanthophylls), peela-naarangi (carotenoids); poore kaagaz par label chromatogram. Naam ke hisse: xantho = peela, phyll = patti; carotenoids ke paas ek gaajar; xanthophylls aur carotenoids ek parivaar ke ghere mein.
  - NCERT: p007-i3 pigments: Chlorophyll a (bright or blue green in the chromatogram), chlorophyll 
  - kitaab se bahar: Palak ki chromatography ke kadam (acetone jaisa solvent, bindu, solvent ka chadhna, tissue paper); ghulne aur chipakne se alag hona, wazan se nahi; chromatogram par lakeeron ka kram; naam ke hisse (xantho, carrot); xanthophylls carotenoid parivaar mein.
**11.4-k3** · samjhana · Ab aaj ke pehle sawaal par lautte hain. Aksar hum sochte hain ki patti ka hara rang sirf ek pigment, chlorophyll, ka hai, par aisa nahi hai. Patti ka rang chaar pigments ke mel se banta hai: do hare, aur do peele-naarangi. Aur har patti mein yeh chaaron alag-alag matra mein hote hain. Socho, hare rang mein thoda peela milao, toh hara halka aur peela-sa ho jaata hai. Aur hara zyada daalo, toh rang gehra ho jaata hai. Patti mein bhi yahi hota hai. Chlorophyll zyada ho toh patti gehri hari dikhti hai, aur peele pigments ka hissa zyada ho toh halki, peeli-si hari. Hare ke itne shade isliye hote hain, kyunki har patti mein in chaar pigments ka mel alag hai. Nayi aur purani patti, dhoop aur chhaanv ki patti, sab mein yeh mel badal jaata hai. Par sirf rang gin lena kaafi nahi hai. Asli baat yeh hai ki photosynthesis mein in alag-alag pigments ka kaam kya hai. Aur iske liye pehle hum samjhenge ki koi pigment asal mein karta kya hai.
  - screen: Ek taraf ek hara bindu, doosri taraf usse nikle chaar rang; 'sirf chlorophyll?' par kaata hua nishaan. Phir rang ki pyali mein hare mein peela milte hi hara halka hota, hara badhte hi gehra. Teen pattiyan (nayi, purani, chhaanv wali), har ek ke saath chaar pigments ki chhoti pattiyon ka alag-alag hissa: gehri hari patti mein chlorophyll ki patti lambi, halki hari mein peele pigments ki patti badi. Aakhir mein sawaal: 'In pigments ka kaam kya?'
  - kitaab se bahar: Shades ka kaaran: chaar pigments ki alag-alag matra; rang milaane ki misaal.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i4
**11.4-k4** · samjhana · Yaad karo, pigment ko humne rang dene wala padarth kaha tha, jo roshni ka kuch hissa sokh leta hai. Ab isi kuch hisse wali baat ko thoda khol kar dekhte hain. Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai; prism se bani indradhanush jaisi rangon ki patti aapne Engelmann ke prayog mein dekhi thi. Jab roshni kisi cheez par padti hai, toh woh cheez kuch rangon ko apne andar le leti hai. Baaki rangon ko woh lauta deti hai. Roshni ko apne andar le lene ko absorb karna kehte hain; absorb yaani sokhna. Jo rang lautta hai, wahi hamari aankh tak pahunchta hai, aur cheez hamein usi rang ki dikhti hai. Isliye kaali shirt dhoop mein jaldi garam hoti hai: woh lagbhag saare rang sokh leti hai. Safed shirt saare rang lauta deti hai, isliye kam garam hoti hai. Ab apni patti ko dekho: woh hari dikhti hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: toh chlorophyll kaun-si roshni sabse kam sokhta hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki hari roshni; bilkul sahi. Chlorophyll hari roshni sabse kam sokhta hai aur use lauta deta hai, isliye patti hari dikhti hai. Aksar log ulta sochte hain ki hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta hoga. Par asal mein yahi roshni sabse kam kaam aati hai. Yaad karo, Engelmann ke prayog mein bhi hari roshni ke paas sabse kam bacteria jama hue the. Matlab koi pigment har rang ki roshni barabar nahi sokhta; woh kuch khaas rangon ko hi chunta hai. Yeh rang ek-doosre se alag kaise hote hain, yeh samajhne ke liye roshni ki lehar socho. Talaab mein patthar phenko, toh paani par lehrein uthti hain: ek chot, phir neechai, phir agli chot. Roshni bhi lehron mein chalti hai. Ek lehar ki chot se agli lehar ki chot tak ki doori ko wavelength kehte hain. Har rang ki apni wavelength hoti hai: neeli roshni ki lehar chhoti, aur laal roshni ki lehar lambi. Ab pigments ki poori pehchaan suno: pigments woh padarth hain jo roshni ko sokh sakte hain, par sirf kuch khaas wavelengths par. Kuch khaas yaani specific; har pigment ki apni pasand ki wavelengths hoti hain, aur wahi uski pehchaan hai.
  - terms: absorb = Sokhna; roshni ki energy apne andar le lena.; wavelength = Roshni ki lehar mein ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori.; pigments = Roshni ko kuch khaas (specific) wavelengths par sokhne wale padarth.
  - screen: Safed roshni prism se indradhanush jaisi patti mein bant-ti. Ek laal gend par roshni: laal teer wapas aankh tak, baaki rang gend mein samaate (label: absorb = sokhna). Dhoop mein kaali aur safed shirt: kaali par saare teer andar, safed se sab wapas. Hari patti par roshni: neele aur laal teer andar, hara teer wapas; sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). 'Hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta?' par kaata hua nishaan; kone mein Engelmann ki alga, hare hisse par sabse kam bacteria. Talaab mein lehrein, chot se agli chot tak teer (label: wavelength); neeli roshni ki chhoti lehar, laal ki lambi. Neeche: pigments = kuch khaas (specific) wavelengths sokhne wale padarth.
  - NCERT: p007-i4 Pigments are substances that have an ability to absorb light, at specific wavele
  - kitaab se bahar: Cheez ka rang = lautaaya hua rang; kaali aur safed shirt; patti hari kyun aur hari roshni sabse kam kaam ki kyun; talaab ki lehron se wavelength; neeli lehar chhoti, laal lambi.
**11.4-k5** · sawaal · Ab ek mazedaar sawaal: aap batao, poori duniya mein sabse zyada kaun-sa plant pigment milta hoga? Poori duniya mein sabse zyada milne wala plant pigment chlorophyll a hai. Kyunki chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur har hari alga mein hota hai, chahe woh khet mein ho ya samudra mein. Jahan bhi photosynthesis mein oxygen nikalti hai, wahan chlorophyll a zaroor milta hai. Isliye ab hum graphs mein sabse pehle chlorophyll a ko hi parkhenge.
  - screen: Duniya ka globe: hare khet, jungle aur samudra ki hari alga chamakte; sawaal ka dabba 'sabse zyada kaun-sa plant pigment?'; jawab ka label chlorophyll a, aur har jagah se oxygen ke bulbule.
  - NCERT: p007-i5 Can you guess which is the most abundant plant pigment in the world?
  - kitaab se bahar: Chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur alga mein; oxygen chhodne wale photosynthesis mein hamesha.
**11.4-k6** · samjhana · Kaun-sa pigment kis wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, yeh ek graph par saaf dikhta hai. Engelmann wale prayog mein humne absorption spectra ka naam suna tha: pigments kis rang ko kitna sokhte hain, uske naqshe. Ek akele pigment ke aise graph ko absorption spectrum kehte hain; spectra iska bahuvachan hai. Ab is graph ki do seedhi lines ko pehchaano. Neeche ki leti line, yaani horizontal axis, par roshni ki wavelength hai. Is line par rang baayein se daayein ek kram mein chalte hain, jise aap VIBGYOR naam se jaante ho. VIBGYOR yaani violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange aur red: baingni, jamuni, neela, hara, peela, naarangi aur laal. Violet ki taraf wavelength sabse chhoti hai, aur red ki taraf sabse lambi. Aankh ko dikhne wali yeh roshni, yaani visible spectrum, lagbhag 400 se 700 nanometre tak ki hai. Nanometre, yaani nm, lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai hai: ek nm metre ka ek arabvaan hissa hai, yaani 10⁻⁹ metre. Socho, aapke ek baal ki motai mein bhi roshni ki sau se zyada lehrein samaa jaayein. Ab khadi line, yaani vertical axis, par dikhaya jaata hai ki pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai. Line jitni oonchi, sokhna utna zyada; aur jahan line sabse oonchi ho, us jagah ko choti, yaani peak, kehte hain. Yahi graph Figure 11.3a hai; chalo, ab ise saath-saath padhte hain.
  - terms: absorption spectrum = Ek pigment har wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska graph.; VIBGYOR = Dikhne wali roshni ke rangon ka kram: violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange, red.; visible spectrum = Aankh ko dikhne wali roshni, lagbhag 400 se 700 nm.; nanometre (nm) = Lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai; 1 nm = 10⁻⁹ metre.
  - screen: Khaali graph banta hai: neeche leti line (horizontal axis) par baayein se daayein VIBGYOR ki rangeen patti, violet se red tak, neeche 400 se 700 nm; label visible spectrum. Kone mein ek baal ki motai, uske aar-paar bahut saari chhoti lehrein (nm = metre ka arabvaan hissa, 10⁻⁹ metre). Phir khadi line (vertical axis) uthti, label 'kitna sokha'; ek namoone ki line jiski sabse oonchi jagah par 'choti (peak)'. Upar label: absorption spectrum; aakhir mein 'Figure 11.3a'.
  - NCERT: p007-i6 Let us study the graph showing the ability of chlorophyll a pigment to absorb li
  - kitaab se bahar: VIBGYOR ke Hindi naam; visible spectrum lagbhag 400–700 nm; nm = 10⁻⁹ metre aur baal ki motai; graph ki do lineon ka padhna, choti (peak).
**11.4-k7** · figure · Figure 11.3a par ungli rakh kar chalo. Khadi line par likha hai: Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments. Absorbance yaani koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap; is line par koi number nahi diya gaya. Graph mein teen lines hain, aur har line par ek teer se pigment ka naam likha hai. Dhyan do, line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi hai; pigment ko sirf naam wale teer se pehchaano. Pehle Chlorophyll a naam wali line dekho. Ab aap batao: chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni kis wavelength par sokhta hai? Chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni neele hisse mein, lagbhag 420 se 430 nm par, sokhta hai. Kya iski koi doosri choti bhi hai? Haan, iski doosri choti laal hisse mein hai, lagbhag 660 se 670 nm par. Beech mein, hare aur peele hisse mein, yeh line kaafi neeche rehti hai; yaani wahan sokhna kam hai. Ab Chlorophyll b naam wali line dekho: iski oonchi choti neele mein, lagbhag 460 nm par hai. Iski laal choti chhoti hai, lagbhag 645 nm par; dono chlorophyll ki chotiyan paas-paas hain, par ek jagah nahi. Aur Carotenoids naam wali line sirf neele hisse mein oonchi hai, lagbhag 450 se 480 nm par. Lagbhag 520 nm ke baad yeh line zameen se lag jaati hai; matlab carotenoids laal roshni lagbhag nahi sokhte. Yahan ek baareek baat chhupi hai: carotenoids dikhte peele-naarangi hain, par sokhte neeli roshni hain. Kyunki jo rang woh lautate hain, wahi hamein dikhta hai; sokha hua rang toh aankh tak pahunchta hi nahi.
  - terms: absorbance = Koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap.
  - screen: Figure 11.3a ka saaf, bada chitra; ungli ka nishaan har label par baari-baari: khadi line ka label 'Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments' (koi number nahi) → teen lines aur unke naam ke teer, chhota label 'line ka rang ≠ pigment ka rang' → Chlorophyll a ki line: neele mein lagbhag 420–430 nm par badi choti, laal mein lagbhag 660–670 nm par doosri, beech mein neechi → Chlorophyll b: lagbhag 460 nm par oonchi choti, lagbhag 645 nm par chhoti → Carotenoids: lagbhag 450–480 nm par chotiyan, lagbhag 520 nm ke baad zameen se lagi. Kone mein ek naarangi gaajar: neela teer andar, naarangi teer wapas.
  - NCERT: p007-i11 Figure 11.3a: three absorption curves against wavelength. The cyan chlorophyll a; p007-i7 From Figure 11.3a can you determine the wavelength (colour of light) at which ch
  - kitaab se bahar: Graph ki line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi; carotenoids naarangi dikhte hain par neeli roshni sokhte hain.
**11.4-k8** · samjhana · Sokhna ek baat hai; par kya sokhi hui har roshni se photosynthesis bhi utna hi hota hai? Sokhi hui roshni se photosynthesis kitna hota hai, yeh dekhne ke liye ek doosra graph banaya gaya, Figure 11.3b. Yaad karo, Engelmann ne alag-alag rangon mein photosynthesis naapa tha, aur us naqshe ko action spectrum kaha tha. Figure 11.3b bhi ek action spectrum hai; bas yeh aaj ke naapne ke tareekon se bana hai. Iski khadi line par likha hai: Rate of photosynthesis (measured by O₂ release). Rate yaani ek tay samay mein kitna kaam hua; toh yeh line batati hai ki ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua. Aur photosynthesis kitna hua, yeh nikalti hui O₂ se naapa gaya: jitni zyada oxygen nikli, utna zyada photosynthesis hua. O₂ yaani oxygen ka molecule; neeche likha chhota 2 batata hai ki isme oxygen ke do atom hain. Ab is line par ungli chalao, aur dekho: neele hisse mein yeh oonchi hai. Hare se peela-hara hisse mein, lagbhag 550 se 570 nm par, yeh sabse neeche aa jaati hai. Phir laal hisse mein, lagbhag 680 nm par, yeh phir sabse oonchi ho jaati hai. Lagbhag 700 nm ke paas yeh tezi se gir jaati hai. Par dhyan do, beech ke hisse mein bhi yeh line kabhi zero tak nahi girti. Ab aap dono graph milao; kya dikhta hai? Dono graph milane par dikhta hai ki jahan chlorophyll a sabse zyada sokhta hai, wahin photosynthesis ki rate bhi zyada hai. Yeh do jagah hain: neela hissa aur laal hissa. Matlab chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur action spectrum, dono ki chotiyan neele aur laal mein milti hain.
  - terms: rate of photosynthesis = Ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua.
  - screen: Figure 11.3a halka padta, uske theek neeche Figure 11.3b (wahi wavelength ki line): khadi line ka label 'Rate of photosynthesis (measured by O₂ release)', kone mein ghadi aur patti se nikalte O₂ ke bulbule; O₂ = do oxygen atom. Kaali line par ungli: neele mein oonchi, lagbhag 550–570 nm par neechi (par zero se upar), lagbhag 680 nm par sabse oonchi, lagbhag 700 nm par tez girti. Phir dono graph upar-neeche: neele aur laal ki chotiyan seedhi lakeeron se judti, label 'absorption spectrum ↔ action spectrum'; kone mein Engelmann ka pehla action spectrum.
  - NCERT: p007-i17 Figure 11.3b: black action-spectrum curve shows photosynthetic rate measured by ; p007-i8 Now look at Figure 11.3b showing the wavelengths at which maximum photosynthesis
  - kitaab se bahar: Rate ka matlab; O₂ ke chhote 2 ka matlab; Engelmann ke action spectrum se jod.
**11.4-k9** · samjhana · Ab socho: jo pigment sabse zyada roshni pakadta hai, aur jiske saath-saath photosynthesis bhi chalta hai, woh kaun hua? Sabse zyada roshni pakadne wala yeh pigment chlorophyll a hi hai. Kyunki dono graphs ki chotiyan usi ki chotiyon ke saath chalti hain. Isliye kehte hain ki chlorophyll a photosynthesis se juda chief pigment hai. Chief yaani mukhya, sabse bada kaam karne wala. Par dhyan rakhna, chief ka matlab akela nahi hota. Toh agar chlorophyll a mukhya hai, toh baaki teen pigment kya kar rahe hain?
  - terms: chief pigment = Mukhya pigment, jiske saath photosynthesis sabse zyada chalta hai; akela nahi.
  - screen: Dono graphs ki neeli aur laal chotiyan chlorophyll a ke naam par aakar milti; label 'chief pigment = mukhya pigment'; neeche 'chief ≠ akela'. Chlorophyll a ke paas teen aur pigment (chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids), har ek par sawaal ka nishaan.
  - NCERT: p007-i9 Hence, we can conclude that chlorophyll a is the chief pigment associated with p
  - kitaab se bahar: Chief ka matlab akela nahi.
**11.4-k10** · analogy · Is baat ko Ganga aur uski sahayak nadiyon se samjho. Ganga Himalaya se nikalti hai, aur raaste bhar apne ilaake ki baarish ka paani batorti hai. Par Ganga har ilaake tak nahi pahunchti. Door ke ilaakon ki baarish ka paani Yamuna, Ghaghra aur Kosi jaisi nadiyaan batorti hain. Phir woh yeh paani Ganga mein la kar mila deti hain. Isliye inhe Ganga ki sahayak nadiyaan kehte hain; sahayak yaani madad karne wali. Ab jod kar dekho: baarish ke alag-alag ilaake roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths hain. Ganga chlorophyll a hai, jo apni pasand ki neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai. Aur chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids sahayak nadiyon jaise hain. Yeh doosri wavelengths ki roshni pakadte hain, aur uski energy chlorophyll a tak pahuncha dete hain. Isliye Bihar aur Bengal tak pahunchte-pahunchte Ganga mein bahut saara paani sahayak nadiyon ka hota hai.
  - screen: Bharat ka naksha: Himalaya se nikalti Ganga, us par baarish; door ke ilaakon mein Yamuna, Ghaghra aur Kosi apne ilaake ki baarish batorti aur Ganga mein milti; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga chaudi hoti jaati. Saath mein jod: baarish ke ilaake = alag wavelengths; Ganga = chlorophyll a (neeli aur laal roshni); sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein = energy chlorophyll a tak.
  - kitaab se bahar: Ganga aur sahayak nadiyon ki misaal (Himalaya, Yamuna, Ghaghra, Kosi, Bihar-Bengal).
  - kya kis jaisa: Ganga = chlorophyll a (mukhya pigment, neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai); sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids (accessory = sahayak); baarish ke alag-alag ilaake = roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein milna = pigments ki pakdi energy chlorophyll a tak jaana; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga ka kul paani = poora photosynthesis (action spectrum).
  - kahan tootti hai: Nadiyaan paani jaisa padarth dhoti hain, jabki pigments sirf energy aage dete hain, koi padarth nahi; pigments ek hi thylakoid membrane mein paas-paas baithe hote hain, door-door nahi; aur nadi wala chitra photo-oxidation se bachaav nahi dikhata.
**11.4-k11** · figure · Ab Figure 11.3c dekho; isme do graph ek hi jagah, ek ke upar ek rakhe gaye hain. Tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhne ko superimposed kehte hain; super yaani upar. Neeche ki leti line par likha hai: Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm). Is par 400, 500, 600 aur 700 ke nishaan hain, jo upar ke graphs ki seedh mein hi hain. Khadi line par likha hai: Light absorbed. Ek kone mein dono lines ke naam likhe hain. Kaali line ke saamne likha hai Rate of photosynthesis; yeh wahi action spectrum hai. Doosri line ke saamne likha hai Absorption; yeh sirf chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum hai, saare pigments ka nahi. Dhyan do, dono lines alag-alag cheezein naapti hain, isliye unki oonchai ko seedhe mat milao; sirf unka aakaar milao. Ab dekho, dono lines ki chotiyan neele aur laal hisse mein saath-saath hain.
  - terms: superimposed = Tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhna.
  - screen: Figure 11.3c: do lines ek ke upar ek aakar baithti (label superimposed, super = upar). Ungli neeche ki line par: 'Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm)', nishaan 400, 500, 600, 700, upar ke graphs ki seedh mein → khadi line 'Light absorbed' → kone ke naam: kaali line 'Rate of photosynthesis' (action spectrum), doosri line 'Absorption' (sirf chlorophyll a). 'Oonchai mat milao, aakaar milao' ka label; neele aur laal par dono chotiyan saath chamakti.
  - NCERT: p007-i20 Figure 11.3c superimposes a black rate-of-photosynthesis curve and a cyan chloro
  - kitaab se bahar: Dono lines ki oonchai alag-alag naap hai; sirf aakaar milaana.
**11.4-k12** · ruko, socho · Abhi humne dekha ki dono lines neele aur laal mein saath-saath chotiyan banati hain. Agar do lines har wavelength par ekdum saath-saath chalein, toh kaha jaata hai ki unka one-to-one overlap hai. Socho, phir aage badhenge: kya Figure 11.3c mein chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur action spectrum ka one-to-one overlap hai? Nahi, Figure 11.3c mein in dono ka one-to-one overlap nahi hai. Hare aur peele hisse mein, lagbhag 500 se 600 nm ke beech, chlorophyll a bahut kam sokhta hai. Phir bhi wahan photosynthesis ki kaali line kaafi upar rehti hai. Matlab wahan bhi photosynthesis ho raha hai, jabki chlorophyll a wahan ki roshni zyada pakad hi nahi raha. Toh wahan ki roshni pakad kaun raha hai? Wahan ki roshni pakad rahe hain baaki pigments, jo apni pakdi energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain. Bilkul Ganga ki tarah: jis ilaake tak Ganga khud nahi pahunchti, wahan ka paani sahayak nadiyaan le aati hain.
  - terms: one-to-one overlap = Har wavelength par do lines ka ekdum saath-saath chalna.
  - screen: Figure 11.3c par sawaal 'one-to-one overlap?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi. Phir lagbhag 500–600 nm wala hissa roshan: chlorophyll a ki line neeche, kaali line upar; beech ki khaali jagah par sawaal 'yahan roshni kaun pakad raha?'. Phir doosre pigments ki chhoti chamak, unse chlorophyll a ki taraf teer; kone mein Ganga mein milti sahayak nadi.
  - NCERT: p007-i10 But by looking at Figure 11.3c can you say that there is a complete one-to-one o
  - kitaab se bahar: Lagbhag 500–600 nm par chlorophyll a ka kam sokhna aur wahan bhi photosynthesis.
  - jawab: Nahi. Hare-peele hisse (lagbhag 500–600 nm) mein chlorophyll a bahut kam sokhta hai, phir bhi photosynthesis hota hai; yaani wahan ki roshni doosre pigments pakad kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain. Neele aur laal mein mel batata hai ki chlorophyll a mukhya hai, aur adhoora mel batata hai ki woh akela nahi.
  - aam galti: Galat soch 1: mel poora nahi, toh chlorophyll a zaroori nahi; galat, kyunki neele-laal ka mel use mukhya banata hai. Galat soch 2: hare hisse mein photosynthesis zero hai; galat, rate kam hai par zero nahi. Aur dono lines ki oonchai alag-alag cheezon ki naap hai, unhe seedhe nahi milaate.
**11.4-k13** · samjhana · Teeno graph milkar batate hain ki zyadatar photosynthesis spectrum ke neele aur laal hisse mein hota hai. Par dikhne wali roshni ki baaki wavelengths par bhi thoda photosynthesis zaroor hota hai. Ab dekhte hain ki aisa hota kaise hai. Roshni pakadne wala mukhya pigment chlorophyll hai, yeh hum jaan chuke hain. Par thylakoid ki membranes mein uske saath teen aur pigments baithe hain: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Yeh bhi roshni sokhte hain, par us roshni ki energy apne paas nahi rakhte. Yeh woh energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain. Isi madad ki wajah se inhe accessory pigments kehte hain; accessory yaani sahayak, saath dene wala. Bilkul Ganga ki sahayak nadiyon ki tarah, jo apna paani Ganga mein mila deti hain. Inka pehla fayda: aane wali roshni ki zyada chaudi range photosynthesis ke kaam aa jaati hai. Jo wavelengths chlorophyll a khud nahi pakad paata, woh bhi bekaar nahi jaati. Inka doosra fayda hai bachaav: yeh chlorophyll a ko photo-oxidation se bachate hain. Socho, dhoop mein kai din sookhte rangeen kapde ka rang dheere-dheere ud jaata hai. Tez roshni ki zyada energy rang ke molecule ko hi tod deti hai. Tez dhoop mein chlorophyll ke saath bhi aisa ho sakta hai: zyada energy se woh kharaab hokar toot sakta hai. Photo yaani roshni, aur oxidation yaani molecule ka apne electron ya hydrogen kho dena. Roshni se hone wale is nuksaan ko photo-oxidation kehte hain. Carotenoids yeh faltu energy apne upar le lete hain, aur use garmi bana kar surakshit nikaal dete hain. Isliye tez dhoop mein bhi chlorophyll a bacha rehta hai. Ab ek sawaal socho: kisi paudhe mein chlorophyll b khoob ho, par chlorophyll a bilkul na ho, toh kya woh photosynthesis karega? Aise paudhe mein photosynthesis nahi ho payega, kyunki baaki pigments ki pakdi energy bhi aakhir chlorophyll a tak hi jaani hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki roshni ka asli kaam chlorophyll a se hi shuru hota hai; abhi ke liye bas itna jaan lo. Toh paudhe ko chlorophyll b aur baaki pigments kyun chahiye? Paudhe ko baaki pigments isliye chahiye, kyunki woh zyada wavelengths ki roshni pakadte hain. Saath hi woh chlorophyll a ko tez roshni se bachate hain.
  - terms: accessory pigments = Chlorophyll a ke sahayak pigments (chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids), jo roshni pakad kar energy use dete hain aur use bachate hain.; photo-oxidation = Tez roshni ki zyada energy se pigment ka kharaab hokar toot jaana.
  - screen: Teeno graph saath, neele aur laal hisse roshan, beech ka hissa halka par zero nahi. Phir thylakoid membrane ka paas ka chitra: chlorophyll a ke chaaron taraf chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids; har ek apni alag rang ki roshni pakadta aur energy ki chamak chlorophyll a tak jaati; label 'accessory = sahayak', kone mein sahayak nadi Ganga mein milti. Phir dhoop mein sookhta rangeen kapda, rang feeka hota; tez dhoop mein chlorophyll a par 'photo-oxidation' ka khatra, carotenoids faltu energy ko garmi ki lehron mein badal kar bahar karte. Aakhir mein do paudhe: 'sirf chlorophyll b' wala paudha bina photosynthesis ke (kaata hua nishaan), aur saare pigments wala paudha.
  - NCERT: p008-i1 These graphs, together, show that most of the photosynthesis takes place in the 
  - kitaab se bahar: Rangeen kapde ka rang udna; oxidation ka matlab; carotenoids faltu energy ko garmi bana kar nikaalte hain; chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho toh photosynthesis nahi (exercise 5 ke liye).
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i2
**11.4-k14** · asli duniya · Yeh baatein aapke aas-paas roz dikhti hain. Patjhad mein pattiyan peeli aur naarangi kyun ho jaati hain? Patjhad mein pattiyan peeli isliye hoti hain, kyunki unka chlorophyll toot jaata hai. Tab pehle se chhupe peele-naarangi carotenoids dikhne lagte hain. Yeh peela rang naya nahi bana; woh hare rang ke peeche pehle se maujood tha. Waise hi aap koi gamla kai din andhere mein rakh do, toh uski pattiyan peeli ya halki hari pad jaati hain. Kyunki chlorophyll lagaataar toot-ta rehta hai, aur naya chlorophyll banne ke liye roshni chahiye. Peele carotenoids zyada tikau, yaani stable, hote hain; isliye woh bache rehte hain aur patti peeli dikhti hai. Andhere mein ugi aaloo ki konpal bhi isi wajah se peeli-safed hoti hai, aur roshni milte hi hari ho jaati hai. Ab aap ek hi paudhe ki chhaanv wali aur dhoop wali pattiyon ko milao. Chhaanv wali pattiyan aksar zyada gehri hari hoti hain. Kyunki kam roshni mein zyada se zyada roshni pakadne ke liye unme chlorophyll zyada banta hai. Chlorophyll b ka hissa bhi badh jaata hai. Dhoop wali pattiyon ko roshni ki kami nahi hoti, isliye unme chlorophyll kam aur bachaav wale peele pigments ka hissa zyada hota hai.
  - screen: Patjhad ke ped: hari patti peeli-naarangi hoti, andar chlorophyll tootkar ghat-ta aur pehle se maujood carotenoids dikhte. Andhere kamre mein rakha gamla, pattiyan dheere-dheere peeli; andhere mein ugi aaloo ki peeli-safed konpal, dhoop mein hari hoti. Ek hi paudha: chhaanv wali patti gehri hari (chlorophyll zyada, chlorophyll b badha hua), dhoop wali halki hari (peele pigments ka hissa zyada).
  - kitaab se bahar: Patjhad, andhere mein peeli patti aur aaloo ki konpal, chhaanv aur dhoop ki pattiyan: inki baatein body nahi padhati; exercise 6 aur 7 ke liye.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i3, p022-i4
**11.4-k15** · poori kahani dobara · Ab aaj ki poori baat hum ek saath dohra lete hain. Paper chromatography ne dikhaya ki patti ka rang chaar pigments ka mel hai: chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Har pigment roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths hi sokhta hai, aur jo rang lautata hai, wahi uska rang dikhta hai. Chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai, aur photosynthesis bhi wahin sabse tez hota hai. Isliye woh chief pigment hai. Baaki teen accessory pigments doosri wavelengths pakad kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain, aur use tez roshni se bachate hain. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se dekho: ek hi paudhe mein hare ke itne rang kyun dikhte hain? Ek hi paudhe mein hare ke alag-alag rang isliye dikhte hain, kyunki har patti mein chaaron pigments alag matra mein hain. Aur patti roshni aise pakadti hai: kai pigment milkar alag-alag roshni pakadte hain, aur saari energy chlorophyll a tak pahunchti hai. Bilkul Ganga ki tarah, jisme door-door ki sahayak nadiyon ka paani aakar milta hai. Par ek baat abhi khuli hai: yeh saare pigments membrane mein kaise jame hote hain, aur chlorophyll a tak pahunchi energy se aage kya hota hai? Pigments membrane mein kaise jame hain aur energy aage kya karti hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Ek line mein aaj ka safar: chromatogram ki chaar lakeerein → hari patti par roshni (hara teer wapas) → Figure 11.3a aur 11.3b ki neeli-laal chotiyan chlorophyll a par milti (chief pigment) → teen accessory pigments se chlorophyll a tak energy ke teer aur bachaav. Shuru ka sawaal phir aata hai, saath mein jawab: teen pattiyan aur unme chaar pigments ka alag mel; kone mein Ganga aur sahayak nadiyaan. Aakhir mein thylakoid membrane par pigments ke dher par sawaal ka nishaan 'kaise jame hain, energy aage kya karti hai?', jo khula rehta hai.

## 11.5 WHAT IS LIGHT REACTION?

_Maqsad: Ek akela pigment molecule ek second mein bas das-ek baar roshni pakad paata hai; toh is thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam chalta kaise hai?_

_Misaal: aatishi sheesha (magnifying lens): sheeshe ka bada chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain; chamakta bindu = reaction centre, woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan saari energy milti hai; kaagaz ka sulagna = reaction centre se electron ka uchhal kar primary acceptor tak jaana, yaani rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; sheeshe ke bina har jagah thodi dhoop = akele pigment ki thodi-si pakad_

Kitab ki baatein (55):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Photochemical · Light reactions ka doosra naam 'Photochemical' phase hai; isme chaar kaam aate hain: light absorption (roshni sokhna), water splitting (paani ka tootna), oxygen release (oxygen ka nikalna), aur high-energy chemical intermediates ATP aur NADPH ka banna.
- f2 · shabd ka matlab · high-energy chemical intermediates · High-energy chemical intermediates: energy se bhare rasayanik molecule jo beech ki kadi hain; roshni ki energy inme bhar kar sugar banane tak pahunchti hai; yahan ATP aur NADPH.
- f3 · shabd ka matlab · phase · Phase: kisi kaam ka ek daur; photosynthesis ka roshni wala daur Photochemical phase.
- f4 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Light absorption by pigments: pigments roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths sokhte hain; chlorophyll a mukhya hai aur sahayak pigments apni energy use dete hain (pichhli film).
- f5 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Water as a molecule: paani (H₂O) ek molecule hai, hydrogen ke do atom aur oxygen ka ek atom; water splitting mein yahi molecule toot-ta hai.
- f6 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chemical energy: molecule ke bandhanon mein jama energy, jo reaction mein kaam aati hai; jaise khaane ki energy.
- f7 · pehle ki zaroori baat · ATP energy lekar chalne wala molecule aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule; dono roshni wale hisse mein bante hain aur stroma mein sugar banane ke kaam aate hain.
- f8 · teacher ki extra baat · Roshni ko na jama kiya ja sakta hai, na stroma tak bheja ja sakta hai; isliye uski energy pehle ATP aur NADPH mein bhari jaati hai.
- f9 · kitab kehti hai · protein complexes · Light reaction mein several protein complexes kaam karte hain.
- f10 · kitab kehti hai · Pigments do alag (discrete) photochemical light harvesting complexes (LHC) mein jame hote hain, jo Photosystem I (PS I) aur Photosystem II (PS II) ke andar hain.
- f11 · kitab kehti hai · PS I aur PS II ke naam unki khoj ke kram se rakhe gaye, light reaction mein kaam karne ke kram se nahi.
- f12 · shabd ka matlab · protein complexes · Protein complexes: kai protein molecule judkar bana bada jhund jo milkar ek kaam karta hai; complex = kai hisson ka juda hua samooh.
- f13 · shabd ka matlab · light harvesting complexes · Light harvesting complexes (LHC): roshni batorne wale pigments aur proteins ke jhund; harvest = fasal batorna.
- f14 · shabd ka matlab · LHC · LHC: light harvesting complex ka chhota roop.
- f15 · shabd ka matlab · discrete · Discrete: alag-alag, apni seema wale; dono LHC ek-doosre se alag hain.
- f16 · shabd ka matlab · Photosystem · Photosystem: photo = roshni, system = kai hisson ka mila-jula intezaam; pigments, proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ka poora jhund jo roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai.
- f17 · shabd ka matlab · PS I; PS II · PS I: Photosystem I ka chhota roop; PS II: Photosystem II ka chhota roop.
- f18 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Proteins can form molecular complexes: proteins cell ke bade molecule hain jo aapas mein judkar complexes bana sakte hain.
- f19 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Chloroplast thylakoids: chloroplast ki chapti thailiyan, jinki membrane mein pigments baithe hain aur roshni pakadne ka kaam hota hai.
- f20 · teacher ki extra baat · Kaam ke kram mein pehle PS II aata hai, phir PS I (aage electron ke raaste mein); PS I pehle khoja gaya, isliye I.
- f21 · kitab kehti hai · LHC hundreds (sainkdon) pigment molecules se bane hain, jo proteins se bound (kas kar jude) hote hain.
- f22 · kitab kehti hai · Har photosystem mein ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigments light harvesting system banate hain, jise antennae bhi kehte hain (Figure 11.4).
- f23 · kitab kehti hai · Antenna ke pigments alag-alag wavelengths ki roshni sokh kar photosynthesis ko zyada efficient banate hain.
- f24 · shabd ka matlab · antennae · Antennae: antenna ka bahuvachan; jaise TV ya mobile ka antenna hawa ki tarangein pakadta hai, waise yeh pigments roshni pakadte hain; light harvesting system ka doosra naam.
- f25 · shabd ka matlab · light harvesting system · Light harvesting system: roshni batorne wala intezaam; har photosystem ke saare pigment, ek reaction centre chlorophyll a ko chhod kar.
- f26 · shabd ka matlab · efficient · Efficient: kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam; yahan aane wali roshni ka zyada hissa kaam aana.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · bound · Bound: kas kar jude hue; pigment proteins par apni jagah jude rehte hain.
- f28 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Photosystem organisation aur pigment–protein binding: ek photosystem mein bahut saare pigment proteins par apni jagah jame hote hain.
- f29 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Accessory-pigment energy transfer: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids doosri wavelengths sokh kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain (pichhli film).
- f30 · teacher ki extra baat · Ek akela chlorophyll molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein bas kuch hi, lagbhag das, baar roshni pakadta hai; sainkdon pigment apni energy ek hi jagah bhejte hain, taaki woh jagah lagaataar kaam kare.
- f31 · teacher ki extra baat · Proteins pigments ko sahi doori aur disha mein bithaate hain, taaki energy ek molecule se paas ke molecule tak jaldi pahunche.
- f32 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.4 (The light harvesting complex): hare pigment molecules ka jhund; ek photon kinare ke pigment par padta hai; laal zigzag raasta jhund se reaction centre tak; reaction centre se chauda neela teer upar Primary acceptor tak; leader lines reaction centre aur several pigment molecules dikhati hain.
- f33 · figure ka nishaan · Photon · Photon: baayein, laal nishaan jo kinare ke ek hare pigment par aata hai; roshni ki energy ka ek packet.
- f34 · figure ka nishaan · Pigment molecules · Pigment molecules: daayein, kai leader lines hare ovals ko dikhati hain; antenna ke pigment, sab ek rang mein bane par asal mein kai tarah ke.
- f35 · figure ka nishaan · Reaction centre · Reaction centre: jhund ke beech, neele teer ki jad mein, jahan laal zigzag khatam hota hai; ek khaas chlorophyll a.
- f36 · figure ka nishaan · Primary acceptor · Primary acceptor: upar ka peela oval, jahan reaction centre se upar jaata neela teer pahunchta hai.
- f37 · shabd ka matlab · photon · Photon: roshni ki energy ka sabse chhota packet; roshni aise packeton mein aati hai.
- f38 · shabd ka matlab · reaction centre · Reaction centre: reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech ki jagah; photosystem ka woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan pahunchi energy se electron nikalta hai.
- f39 · shabd ka matlab · primary acceptor · Primary acceptor: primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala; reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle lene wala molecule.
- f40 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Antenna pigments aur reaction-centre chlorophyll: antenna energy batorta hai, reaction centre ka chlorophyll a electron chhodta hai.
- f41 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Light excites electrons: energy milne par electron oonchi, zyada energy wali haalat mein uchhalta hai (excite hona); bahut energy mile toh molecule chhod sakta hai.
- f42 · teacher ki extra baat · Zigzag aur neela teer alag cheezein: zigzag mein energy pigment se pigment jaati hai, koi electron nahi chalta; neele teer mein ek electron reaction centre se primary acceptor tak jaata hai; yahin roshni ki energy rasayanik badlaav banti hai.
- f43 · teacher ki extra baat · Figure mein sab pigment ek hare rang mein bane hain, par antenna mein kai tarah ke pigment hote hain.
- f44 · kitab kehti hai · Ek akela chlorophyll a molecule reaction centre banata hai; dono photosystems mein reaction centre alag hai.
- f45 · kitab kehti hai · PS I ke reaction centre chlorophyll a ka absorption peak 700 nm par hai, isliye P700; PS II mein absorption maxima 680 nm par hai, isliye P680.
- f46 · shabd ka matlab · absorption peak · Absorption peak: absorption spectrum ki sabse oonchi choti; woh wavelength jahan pigment sabse zyada roshni sokhta hai.
- f47 · shabd ka matlab · absorption maxima · Absorption maxima: maximum ka bahuvachan; sabse zyada sokhne ki jagah, peak ka hi doosra naam.
- f48 · shabd ka matlab · P700 · P700: P = pigment, 700 = choti 700 nm par; PS I ka reaction centre.
- f49 · shabd ka matlab · P680 · P680: PS II ka reaction centre; choti 680 nm par.
- f50 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Reaction centre versus antenna; wavelength: lehar ki ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori; absorption peak: absorption spectrum ke graph ki sabse oonchi choti (pichhli film).
- f51 · teacher ki extra baat · P700 aur P680 dono chlorophyll a hi hain; akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm par hoti hai, aur reaction centre mein proteins ka maahaul choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiskaata hai.
- f52 · teacher ki extra baat · 680, 700 se kam hai: PS II ka reaction centre thodi chhoti wavelength chahta hai, PS I thodi lambi.
- f53 · teacher ki extra baat · Ek photon ka safar: lagbhag 450 nm ki neeli roshni PS II ke antenna ke kinare carotenoid par; energy chlorophyll b, phir chlorophyll a molecules se P680 tak, second ke arabvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein; P680 ka electron PS II ke primary acceptor tak.
- f54 · teacher ki extra baat · Har kood mein energy ka thoda hissa garmi ban jaata hai, isliye energy zyadatar reaction centre ki taraf hi badhti hai.
- f55 · teacher ki extra baat · Reaction centre na ho toh antenna ki pakdi energy garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaati hai, koi electron nahi chhootta; isliye chlorophyll a ke bina photosynthesis nahi hota.

**11.5-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli film mein humne patti ke hare rang ko tod kar dekha. Usme chaar pigments mile: chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai, aur baaki teen sahayak pigments apni pakdi energy usi ko saunp dete hain. Yaad rakhein: patti mein jitni bhi roshni pakdi jaaye, uski energy aakhir chlorophyll a tak hi pahunchti hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki pigments membrane mein kaise jame hain, aur chlorophyll a tak pahunch kar energy karti kya hai. Yahi do baatein pichhli film ne khuli chhodi thi. Par isse pehle ek ajeeb baat suno. Ek akela pigment molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein bas das-ek baar roshni pakad paata hai. Samjho, molecule ki duniya mein das baar bahut dheema hai. Wahan kai kaam second ke laakhvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein ho jaate hain. Toh jab ek akela pigment itni kam roshni pakadta hai, toh is thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam chalta kaise hai? Thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam kaise chalta hai, yahi aaj ka sawaal hai. Iske jawab mein woh dono khuli baatein bhi khul jaayengi. Par jawab se pehle dekh lo ki roshni wale is hisse mein kul kitne kaam hote hain. Yaad karo, roshni se seedhe chalne wali reactions ko humne light reactions kaha tha, aur unka doosra naam photochemical reactions. Isi hisse ko Photochemical phase bhi kehte hain; phase yaani kisi kaam ka ek daur. Dekho, is daur mein kul chaar kaam hote hain. Pehla hai light absorption, yaani pigments ka roshni sokhna, jo hum dekh chuke hain. Doosra hai water splitting, yaani paani ke molecule ka tootna. Teesra hai oxygen release, yaani usi tootne se oxygen ka bahar nikalna. Aur chautha hai do khaas molecule ka banna: ATP aur NADPH. Yaad karo, ATP energy lekar chalta hai, aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar chalta hai. In dono ko high-energy chemical intermediates kehte hain. High-energy yaani energy se bhare, chemical yaani rasayanik, aur intermediates yaani beech ki kadi. Par roshni ki energy ko seedhe sugar banane mein kyun nahi lagaya jaata, beech mein yeh kadi kyun? Roshni ki energy seedhe sugar mein isliye nahi lagti, kyunki roshni ko jama nahi kiya ja sakta. Use stroma tak bheja bhi nahi ja sakta. Isliye uski energy pehle ATP aur NADPH mein bhar di jaati hai, aur yahi dono stroma tak jaakar sugar banwate hain. Paani kaise toot-ta hai aur ATP-NADPH kaise bante hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge. Aaj hum shuruaat par rukenge: roshni pakadne ka intezaam kaisa hai.
  - terms: phase = Kisi kaam ka ek daur; roshni wala daur Photochemical phase kehlata hai.; high-energy chemical intermediates = Energy se bhare rasayanik molecule jo roshni aur sugar ke beech ki kadi hain: ATP aur NADPH.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: chromatogram ki chaar lakeerein (chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids); teen sahayak pigments se energy ke teer chlorophyll a tak; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: energy aakhir chlorophyll a tak'. Thylakoid membrane par pigments ke dher par do sawaal ke nishaan: 'kaise jame?', 'energy aage kya karti?'. Ek akela pigment molecule dhoop mein, saath mein ek second ki ghadi aur bas das chamak gini jaati; sawaal ka dabba 'Itni thodi pakad se kaam kaise chalta hai?'. Phir roshni wale daur ka naksha: label 'light reactions = photochemical reactions = Photochemical phase (phase = daur)'; chaar khaane baari-baari roshan: 1 light absorption (pigment par roshni), 2 water splitting (paani ka molecule toot-ta), 3 oxygen release (oxygen ke bulbule), 4 ATP aur NADPH ke moti; label 'high-energy chemical intermediates'. Aakhir mein roshni thylakoid par hi rukti, aur ATP aur NADPH ke moti stroma mein sugar ki taraf jaate.
  - NCERT: p008-i3 Light reactions or the ‘Photochemical’ phase include light absorption, water spl
  - kitaab se bahar: Ek akela pigment molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein lagbhag das baar hi roshni pakadta hai, aur molecule ki duniya mein yeh dheema hai; roshni jama ya stroma tak bheji nahi ja sakti, isliye ATP aur NADPH beech ki kadi hain.
**11.5-k2** · samjhana · Toh pehli baat: pigments membrane mein jame kaise hain, bikhre hue ya kisi tarteeb mein? Pigments membrane mein bikhre hue nahi, ek pakki tarteeb mein jame hain. Unhe is tarteeb mein proteins pakad kar rakhte hain. Proteins ko aap khaane mein daal aur doodh se jaante ho. Par cell ke andar proteins kaam karne wale bade molecule bhi hain. Kai protein molecule aapas mein judkar ek bada jhund bana sakte hain, jo milkar ek hi kaam karta hai. Aise jhund ko protein complex kehte hain. Complex yaani kai hisson ka juda hua samooh, jaise kai purzon se bani ek machine. Dekho, light reaction ka kaam koi akela molecule nahi karta. Ismein several, yaani kai, protein complexes milkar kaam karte hain. Yeh sab chloroplast ki thylakoid membrane mein baithe hain, wahi chapti thailiyan jinhe hum pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Ab pigments ki baat karte hain. Woh proteins se jud kar do alag jhund banate hain, jo roshni ko batorte hain. Jaise kisan khet mein ghoom-ghoom kar fasal batorta hai, waise hi yeh jhund aane wali roshni ko batorte hain. Isliye inhe light harvesting complexes kehte hain, chhote mein LHC; harvest yaani fasal batorna. Samjho, yeh do LHC ek-doosre se bilkul alag hain, jise discrete kehte hain. Aur dono photochemical hain, yaani roshni se rasayanik kaam shuru karte hain. Har LHC apne proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ke saath milkar ek poora intezaam banata hai. Yeh intezaam roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai. Is poore intezaam ko Photosystem kehte hain: photo yaani roshni, aur system yaani kai hisson ka mila-jula intezaam. Aise do photosystem hain: Photosystem I, chhote mein PS I, aur Photosystem II, chhote mein PS II. Dono LHC inhi ke andar baithe hain. Ab naam dekh kar batao: PS I pehle kaam karta hoga ya PS II? PS I pehle kaam karta hai ya PS II, iska jawab naam se nahi milta. Naam kaam ke kram se rakhe hi nahi gaye. Aksar hum sochte hain ki PS I pehle kaam karta hoga, kyunki ek pehle aata hai do se; par aisa nahi hai. Dono ke naam unki khoj ke kram se rakhe gaye hain. Jo photosystem pehle khoja gaya, use I mila, aur baad wale ko II. Light reaction mein toh pehle PS II kaam karta hai, phir PS I. Yeh aage hum electron ke raaste mein dekhenge. Aisa isliye hua, kyunki vaigyanikon ne PS I ko pehle pehchaana, aur dono ke kaam ka asli kram baad mein samajh aaya. Bilkul class ke roll number ki tarah: roll number admission ke kram se milta hai. Roll number ek wala bachcha line mein sabse aage khada ho, yeh zaroori nahi. Exam mein yeh baat aksar poochhi jaati hai, isliye pakki kar lo. PS I aur PS II ke number khoj ka kram batate hain, kaam ka nahi.
  - terms: protein complexes = Kai protein molecule judkar bana bada jhund jo milkar ek kaam karta hai; complex = kai hisson ka juda hua samooh.; light harvesting complexes (LHC) = Proteins se jude pigments ke jhund jo roshni batorte hain; harvest = fasal batorna.; LHC = Light harvesting complex ka chhota roop.; discrete = Alag-alag, apni seema wale.; Photosystem = Pigments, proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ka poora intezaam jo roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai; photo = roshni, system = mila-jula intezaam.; PS I = Photosystem I ka chhota roop; pehle khoja gaya photosystem.; PS II = Photosystem II ka chhota roop; baad mein khoja gaya, par kaam mein pehle aata hai.
  - screen: Thylakoid membrane ka paas ka chitra: pigments bikhre nahi, proteins par apni jagah jame. Kone mein daal aur doodh, phir cell ke andar protein ke bade molecule; kai protein judkar ek jhund (label: protein complex, kai purzon wali machine). Membrane mein kai protein complexes ek line mein (label: several = kai). Pigments proteins se jud kar do alag jhund banate; kone mein khet mein fasal batorta kisan; label 'light harvesting complexes (LHC), harvest = fasal batorna', 'discrete = alag-alag'. Har LHC ek bade ghere ke andar: label Photosystem (photo = roshni, system = mila-jula intezaam); do ghere: Photosystem I (PS I) aur Photosystem II (PS II). Sawaal 'PS I pehle kaam karta hai?' par kaata hua nishaan; ek patti par 'khoj: pehle PS I, phir PS II', neeche 'kaam: pehle PS II, phir PS I'. Kone mein class ka register: roll number admission ke kram se, line mein bachche alag kram mein.
  - NCERT: p008-i4 Several protein complexes are involved in the process. The pigments are organise
  - kitaab se bahar: Proteins khaane se jaane-pehchaane, cell mein kaam karne wale molecule; protein complex = kai purzon wali machine; harvest = fasal batorna; PS I pehle pehchaana gaya aur kaam ka kram baad mein samajh aaya; roll number ki misaal; exam ki baareek baat.
**11.5-k3** · samjhana · Ab shuru wale sawaal ka jawab isi intezaam mein chhupa hai. Ek LHC mein ek-do nahi, sainkdon pigment molecule hote hain. Sab proteins se kas kar jude, yaani bound, hote hain. Proteins inhe sahi doori aur sahi disha mein bithaate hain. Isse energy ek molecule se paas wale molecule tak jhat se pahunch jaati hai. Ab ek photosystem ke andar jhaanko. Usme ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigments ek hi kaam karte hain. Woh roshni pakadte hain aur uski energy aage badhaate hain. Roshni pakadne wale is poore jhund ko light harvesting system kehte hain, aur iska doosra naam antennae hai. Aapne ghar ki chhat par TV ka antenna ya mobile tower ka antenna dekha hoga; woh hawa mein aati tarangein pakadta hai. Hamare pigments bhi waise hi roshni pakadte hain. Antennae, antenna ka bahuvachan hai, kyunki yahan sainkdon pigment milkar pakadte hain. Toh is antenna mein kaun-kaun se pigment baithe hain? Is antenna mein woh sab pigment baithe hain jo humne kaagaz par alag kiye the. Chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids, aur bahut saare chlorophyll a bhi. Yaad karo, har pigment apni pasand ki wavelengths sokhta hai. Chlorophyll a aur b neeli aur laal ke alag-alag hisse sokhte hain, aur carotenoids neeli roshni. Isliye jab itne alag pigment ek saath baithe hon, toh roshni ke zyada rang pakde jaate hain, aur kam roshni bekaar jaati hai. Kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam ko efficient hona kehte hain. Antenna ke pigments isi tarah photosynthesis ko zyada efficient banate hain. Ab doosra fayda dekho, jo shuru wale sawaal ka seedha jawab hai. Yaad karo, akela pigment ek second mein bas das-ek baar hi roshni pakad paata tha. Par jab sainkdon pigment apni-apni pakdi energy ek hi jagah bhejte hain, toh kya hota hai? Us ek jagah tak har second sainkdon baar energy pahunchti hai. Isliye woh jagah kabhi khaali nahi baithti, aur roshni ka kaam lagaataar chalta rehta hai. Toh thodi-si pakad se bhi kaam isliye chal jaata hai, kyunki pigments akele nahi, sainkdon ki toli mein kaam karte hain. Woh ek jagah kaun-si hai, aur wahan kya hota hai, yeh pehle ek misaal se samjho.
  - terms: bound = Kas kar jude hue; pigment proteins par apni jagah jude rehte hain.; light harvesting system = Har photosystem ke woh saare pigment jo roshni pakad kar energy aage badhaate hain (ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar).; antennae = Light harvesting system ka doosra naam; antenna ka bahuvachan, kyunki sainkdon pigment milkar roshni pakadte hain.; efficient = Kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam; aane wali roshni ka zyada hissa kaam aana.
  - screen: Ek LHC ka paas ka chitra: protein ke dhaanche par sainkdon pigment molecule apni-apni jagah kase hue (label: bound = kas kar jude), paas-paas aur sahi disha mein. Ek photosystem ke andar: beech mein ek alag chamakta chlorophyll a, baaki saare pigment uske chaaron taraf; poore ghere par label 'light harvesting system = antennae'. Kone mein chhat ka TV antenna aur mobile tower, hawa ki tarangein pakadte. Antenna ke pigments alag rangon mein: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids aur bahut saare chlorophyll a; har pigment apne rang ki roshni pakadta, kam roshni bekaar; label 'efficient = kam barbaadi, zyada kaam'. Phir ek akele pigment par ek second mein das chamak, aur sainkdon pigments se energy ke teer ek jagah aakar har second sainkdon chamak; neeche: 'thodi pakad + badi toli = lagaataar kaam'.
  - NCERT: p008-i5 The LHC are made up of hundreds of pigment molecules bound to proteins. Each pho
  - kitaab se bahar: Proteins pigments ko sahi doori aur disha mein bithaate hain; TV aur mobile antenna ki misaal; sainkdon pigment ek hi jagah energy bhejte hain, isliye woh jagah lagaataar kaam karti hai (efficiency ka doosra kaaran, akele pigment ki lagbhag das baar pakad ke saamne).
**11.5-k4** · analogy · Socho, aap dhoop mein ek aatishi sheesha, yaani bada lens, kaagaz ke upar pakad kar khade ho. Sheeshe ka bada chehra apne poore hisse par padi dhoop ko ek chhote-se chamakte bindu par le aata hai. Thodi hi der mein usi bindu par kaagaz sulagne lagta hai, aur patla-sa dhuaan uthta hai. Par sheeshe ke bina bhi wahi dhoop kaagaz par padti hai; tab kaagaz kyun nahi sulagta? Sheeshe ke bina kaagaz isliye nahi sulagta, kyunki tab har jagah thodi-thodi dhoop padti hai. Kisi ek jagah par itni energy nahi jamti. Ab jodo: aatishi sheeshe ka bada chehra antenna ke sainkdon pigment hain, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain. Chamakta bindu woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule hai, jahan saari energy aakar milti hai. Aur kaagaz ka sulagna woh pal hai, jab us bindu par pahunchi energy se asli rasayanik kaam shuru hota hai. Is khaas bindu ka naam kya hai, aur wahan asal mein kya sulagta hai, yeh hum ab figure mein dekhenge.
  - screen: Dhoop mein aatishi sheesha (bada lens) kaagaz ke upar: sheeshe ke poore chehre se roshni ke teer ek chamakte bindu par milte, bindu par kaagaz sulagta aur patla dhuaan uthta. Phir bina sheeshe ke wahi kaagaz, har jagah halki dhoop, kuch nahi sulagta. Phir jod: sheeshe ka chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment; chamakta bindu = ek khaas chlorophyll a; kaagaz ka sulagna = rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; bindu par sawaal ka nishaan 'iska naam?'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Aatishi sheeshe (magnifying lens) ki misaal.
