  - kya kis jaisa: Aatishi sheeshe ka bada chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain; chamakta bindu = reaction centre, woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan saari energy milti hai; kaagaz ka sulagna = wahan electron ka uchhal kar primary acceptor tak jaana, yaani rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; sheeshe ke bina har jagah thodi dhoop = akele pigment ki thodi-si pakad, jisse kuch shuru nahi hota.
  - kahan tootti hai: Sheesha roshni ko mod kar ek jagah laata hai, jabki pigments roshni nahi, uski energy ek molecule se paas ke molecule ko aage dete hain; sheesha saare rang ek saath modta hai, jabki antenna mein alag pigment alag rang pakadte hain; aur reaction centre jalta nahi, uska ek electron nikal kar doosre molecule ke paas jaata hai.
**11.5-k5** · figure · Ab Figure 11.4 dekho; iske neeche likha hai The light harvesting complex, yaani wahi roshni batorne wala jhund. Dekho, beech mein hare-hare ovals ka ek jhund hai. Uske paas likha hai Pigment molecules, aur kai patli lines alag-alag ovals ki taraf ishaara karti hain. Yahi antenna ke pigment hain. Figure mein sab ek hi hare rang mein bane hain, par asal mein inme kai tarah ke pigment hote hain. Baayein taraf likha hai Photon, aur ek laal nishaan kinare ke ek hare pigment ki taraf aata dikhta hai. Roshni chhote-chhote packet mein aati hai, aur roshni ki energy ke ek packet ko photon kehte hain. Toh yeh laal nishaan roshni ka ek packet hai, jo antenna ke kinare wale pigment par pada. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo. Us pigment se ek laal zigzag raasta, pigment se pigment hota hua, jhund ke beech ki taraf jaata hai. Raasta zigzag isliye hai, kyunki energy ek pigment se paas wale pigment tak kood-kood kar jaati hai. Koi pigment apni jagah se nahi hilta. Zigzag jahan khatam hota hai, wahan likha hai Reaction centre; yeh woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule hai. Yahi hamare aatishi sheeshe ka chamakta bindu hai, jahan antenna ki saari energy aakar milti hai. Reaction yaani rasayanik badlaav, aur centre yaani beech ki jagah. Jis jagah pahunchi energy se rasayanik badlaav shuru hota hai, use reaction centre kehte hain. Ab dekho, reaction centre se ek chauda neela teer upar jaata hai, aur ek peele oval tak pahunchta hai. Yeh neela teer ek electron ka safar dikhata hai. Iska raaz samajhne ke liye pehle ek clue suno. Yaad karo, jab kisi atom ko energy milti hai, toh uska electron oonchi, zyada energy wali haalat mein uchhal jaata hai. Ise electron ka excite hona kehte hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: agar reaction centre ke electron ko itni energy mil jaaye ki woh molecule mein tik hi na paaye, toh kya hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki woh electron molecule chhod kar bahar nikal jaayega; aapne bilkul sahi socha. Dekho, reaction centre ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur paas baitha ek molecule use turant pakad leta hai. Us peele oval par yahi likha hai: Primary acceptor. Primary yaani pehla, aur acceptor yaani lene wala. Toh primary acceptor woh molecule hai jo uchhla electron sabse pehle leta hai. Isliye figure mein zigzag aur neela teer alag rang ke hain. Zigzag mein sirf energy aage badhti hai, koi electron nahi chalta. Neele teer mein ek electron sach mein ek molecule se doosre tak jaata hai. Yahi woh pal hai jab roshni ki energy rasayanik kaam ban jaati hai: ek electron apni jagah chhod kar doosre molecule ke paas chala gaya. Reaction centre ka khoya electron kahan se bharta hai, aur primary acceptor use aage kahan deta hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - terms: photon = Roshni ki energy ka sabse chhota packet; roshni aise packeton mein aati hai.; reaction centre = Photosystem ka woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan pahunchi energy se electron nikal kar rasayanik badlaav shuru hota hai; reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech ki jagah.; primary acceptor = Reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle lene wala molecule; primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala.
  - screen: Figure 11.4 ka saaf, bada chitra; neeche caption 'The light harvesting complex'. Ungli baari-baari: hare ovals ka jhund aur 'Pigment molecules' ki kai lines (chhota label: sab ek rang mein bane, asal mein kai pigment) → baayein 'Photon' aur laal nishaan kinare ke pigment par (label: photon = roshni ki energy ka packet) → laal zigzag pigment se pigment hota beech tak, energy ki chamak koodti aur pigment apni jagah → zigzag ke ant par 'Reaction centre', kone mein aatishi sheeshe ka chamakta bindu; label reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech → reaction centre se chauda neela teer upar peele oval tak. Phir clue: atom mein electron energy lekar oonchi haalat mein uchhalta; sawaal 'tik na paaye toh?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Electron molecule chhod kar uchhalta, peela oval use pakadta: label 'Primary acceptor (primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala)'. Aakhir mein zigzag (sirf energy) aur neela teer (ek electron) ke alag label; reaction centre par electron ki khaali jagah par chhota '?'.
  - NCERT: p008-i6 The light harvesting complex is drawn as a cluster of green pigment molecules. A
  - kitaab se bahar: Photon ka matlab; zigzag kyun; figure mein sab pigment ek rang mein par asal mein kai tarah ke; electron ka excite hona aur bahut energy par molecule chhod dena; zigzag (energy) aur neela teer (electron) ka farq; khoye electron ki baat aage.
**11.5-k6** · samjhana · Toh har photosystem mein reaction centre kitne molecule se banta hai? Har photosystem mein reaction centre sirf ek akele chlorophyll a molecule se banta hai. Antenna ke sainkdon pigment isi ek ke liye energy batorte hain. Par dono photosystems ka reaction centre ek jaisa nahi hai. Dono ki roshni ki pasand thodi alag hai. Yaad karo, absorption spectrum mein sabse oonchi jagah ko humne choti, yaani peak, kaha tha. Jis wavelength par kisi pigment ki sabse oonchi choti ho, use uska absorption peak kehte hain. Isi ko absorption maxima bhi kehte hain; maxima, maximum ka bahuvachan hai, yaani sabse zyada sokhne wali jagah. PS I ke reaction centre wale chlorophyll a ka absorption peak 700 nm par hai. Isliye use P700 kehte hain. PS II ke reaction centre ki absorption maxima 680 nm par hai, aur use P680 kehte hain. Aap naam aise padho: P yaani pigment, aur saath ka number batata hai ki uski choti kitne nm par hai. Toh kya P700 aur P680 do naye pigment hain, jo kaagaz par alag dikhe hi nahi? P700 aur P680 koi naye pigment nahi hain; dono chlorophyll a hi hain, bas ek khaas jagah par baithe hue. Pichhli film mein akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660 se 670 nm par thi. Reaction centre ka chlorophyll a apne proteins ke beech baitha hai. Yahi maahaul uski choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiska deta hai. Ek baat aur dhyan se dekho: 680, 700 se kam hai. Yaani PS II ka reaction centre thodi chhoti wavelength ki roshni chahta hai, aur PS I thodi lambi. Exam ke liye yeh jodi pakki rakho: PS I ke saath P700 aur 700 nm; PS II ke saath P680 aur 680 nm. Do photosystem, do alag reaction centre, aur dono milkar kaise kaam karte hain, yeh aage hum electron ke raaste mein dekhenge.
  - terms: absorption peak = Woh wavelength jahan kisi pigment ka sokhna sabse zyada ho; graph ki sabse oonchi choti.; absorption maxima = Absorption peak ka hi doosra naam; maxima = maximum ka bahuvachan.; P700 = PS I ka reaction centre, ek chlorophyll a jiski choti 700 nm par hai; P = pigment.; P680 = PS II ka reaction centre, ek chlorophyll a jiski choti 680 nm par hai.
  - screen: Ek photosystem: beech mein ek akela chlorophyll a (reaction centre), chaaron taraf antenna. Do photosystem saath-saath, dono ke reaction centre alag rang ki chamak mein. Absorption spectrum ka graph, sabse oonchi jagah par 'choti (peak)'; label 'absorption peak = absorption maxima (maxima = maximum ka bahuvachan)'. PS I ke reaction centre par 700 nm ki choti aur naam P700; PS II par 680 nm ki choti aur naam P680; naam ke hisse: P = pigment, number = choti ka nm. 'Naya pigment?' par kaata hua nishaan, dono par 'chlorophyll a'. Pichhli film ka chlorophyll a graph (laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm) aur reaction centre ki choti thoda daayein khiski, proteins ke ghere ke saath. 680 aur 700 ki tulna: 680 thoda baayein (chhoti wavelength). Aakhir mein exam ki jodi ka card: PS I – P700 – 700 nm; PS II – P680 – 680 nm.
  - NCERT: p008-i12 The single chlorophyll a molecule forms the reaction centre. The reaction centre
  - kitaab se bahar: P = pigment; akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm (Figure 11.3a), aur reaction centre mein proteins ka maahaul choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiskaata hai; 680 aur 700 ki tulna; exam ki jodi.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.5-k7** · example · Chalo, ab ek photon ke saath shuru se aakhir tak poora safar karke dekhte hain. Socho, neeli roshni ka ek photon, lagbhag 450 nm wala, PS II ke antenna ke kinare baithe ek carotenoid molecule par padta hai. Dekho, carotenoid us photon ko sokh leta hai, par uski energy apne paas nahi rakhta. Yeh energy paas ke chlorophyll b tak jaati hai. Phir ek chlorophyll a se doosre chlorophyll a tak koodti jaati hai. Har kood mein energy ka thoda-sa hissa garmi ban jaata hai, isliye woh zyadatar aage, reaction centre ki taraf hi badhti hai. Socho, second ke ek arabvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein yeh energy P680 tak pahunch jaati hai. Tab P680 ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur PS II ka primary acceptor use pakad leta hai. Bilkul aatishi sheeshe ki tarah: dhoop kinare par padi thi, par kaagaz beech ke bindu par sulga. Ab batao, is safar mein electron kisne diya: carotenoid ne ya P680 ne? Is safar mein electron P680 ne diya; carotenoid ne sirf roshni pakad kar uski energy aage badhaayi. Matlab photon sainkdon pigments mein se kisi ek ne pakda, par rasayanik kaam ek hi jagah shuru hua: reaction centre par.
  - screen: PS II ka antenna, kinare par ek naarangi carotenoid; neeli roshni ka ek photon (lagbhag 450 nm) us par girta. Energy ki chamak carotenoid se chlorophyll b, phir ek chlorophyll a se doosre par koodti, har kood par halki garmi ki lehar, aur chamak P680 ki taraf hi badhti; kone mein ghadi 'second ke arabvein hisse se kam'. P680 par pahunch kar uska electron uchhalta, PS II ka primary acceptor use pakadta. Kone mein aatishi sheesha: dhoop kinare par, kaagaz beech ke bindu par sulagta. Sawaal ka dabba 'electron kisne diya: carotenoid ya P680?', jawab P680 par sahi ka nishaan.
  - kitaab se bahar: Ek photon ka safar: lagbhag 450 nm ki neeli roshni PS II ke antenna ke carotenoid par, chlorophyll b aur chlorophyll a se P680 tak, second ke arabvein hisse se kam samay mein; har kood mein thodi energy garmi banti hai, isliye energy reaction centre ki taraf badhti hai.
**11.5-k8** · ruko, socho · Ab ek line ko dhyan se parkho, jisme bahut se bachche phisal jaate hain. Humne kaha tha ki har photosystem mein ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigment antenna banate hain. Socho, phir aage badhenge: toh kya antenna mein chlorophyll a hota hi nahi? Antenna mein chlorophyll a hota hai, aur woh bhi sainkdon molecule. Aksar hum sochte hain ki chlorophyll a sirf reaction centre mein hota hai, par aisa nahi hai. Ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar ka matlab hai ki sirf ek khaas molecule alag hai. Baaki sainkdon chlorophyll a antenna mein hi roshni pakadte hain. Samjho, wahi ek khaas chlorophyll a reaction centre hai, aur baaki saare pigment uske liye energy batorte hain. Ab ulta socho: agar kisi photosystem ke antenna mein pigment bharpoor hon, par uska reaction centre na ho, toh kya hoga? Reaction centre na ho, toh antenna roshni pakadta rahega, par koi electron nahi uchhlega. Kyunki energy ko electron ke uchhalne mein badalne ka kaam sirf reaction centre karta hai. Pakdi hui energy bas garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaayegi. Isi wajah se jis paudhe mein chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a bilkul na ho, usme photosynthesis nahi ho sakta. Usme reaction centre banane wala molecule hi nahi hai.
  - screen: Line ka card: 'ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar baaki saare pigment antenna'; sawaal ka dabba 'antenna mein chlorophyll a nahi?'. Antenna ke andar sainkdon chlorophyll a chamakte, beech mein sirf ek khaas chlorophyll a alag rang mein (reaction centre); 'chlorophyll a sirf reaction centre mein' par kaata hua nishaan. Phir ek photosystem jiska reaction centre gayab: antenna roshni pakadta, energy beech tak aakar garmi aur halki laal chamak ban kar nikal jaati, koi electron nahi uchhalta. Aakhir mein do paudhe: sirf chlorophyll b wala (photosynthesis nahi, kaata hua nishaan) aur chlorophyll a wala.
  - kitaab se bahar: Reaction centre ke bina pakdi energy garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaati hai; chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho toh photosynthesis nahi (exercise 5 ke liye).
  - jawab: Antenna mein bhi sainkdon chlorophyll a hote hain; sirf ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule reaction centre banta hai. Reaction centre ke bina antenna roshni pakad leta hai par koi electron nahi chhootta, isliye light reaction shuru nahi hoti; isi wajah se chlorophyll a ke bina, sirf chlorophyll b se, photosynthesis nahi hota.
  - aam galti: Galat soch 1: 'ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar' ka matlab antenna mein chlorophyll a nahi; galat, antenna mein bahut chlorophyll a hai. Galat soch 2: pigment bahut hon toh reaction centre ki zaroorat nahi; galat, roshni pakadna aur electron chhodna alag kaam hain. Galat soch 3: P700 aur P680 alag pigment hain; galat, dono chlorophyll a hain.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i2
**11.5-k9** · poori kahani dobara · Ab aaj ka poora safar ek saath dohra lete hain. Light reactions, yaani Photochemical phase, mein chaar kaam hote hain. Roshni sokhna, paani ka tootna, oxygen ka nikalna, aur ATP aur NADPH ka banna. Pigments proteins se jud kar do alag LHC banate hain, jo PS I aur PS II ke andar hain. Inke number khoj ke kram se hain, kaam ke kram se nahi. Humne dekha ki har photosystem mein sainkdon pigment antenna banate hain, aur alag-alag rang ki roshni pakad kar uski energy beech tak bhejte hain. Beech mein ek akela chlorophyll a reaction centre hai; wahan electron uchhal kar primary acceptor ke paas jaata hai. PS I ka reaction centre P700 hai, jiski choti 700 nm par hai. PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, jiski choti 680 nm par hai. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: akele pigment ki pakad itni dheemi hai, phir bhi patti roshni ka kaam lagaataar kaise kar leti hai? Patti roshni ka kaam lagaataar isliye kar leti hai, kyunki sainkdon pigment milkar roshni batorte hain. Woh saari energy ek reaction centre ko dete hain, jo kabhi khaali nahi baithta. Bilkul aatishi sheeshe ki tarah, jo poore chehre ki dhoop ek bindu par la kar kaagaz sulga deta hai. Par ab ek nayi baat khadi ho gayi hai. Reaction centre ne apna electron de diya, toh woh electron aage kahan jaata hai, aur do photosystem aapas mein kaise jude hain? Electron aage kahan jaata hai aur dono photosystem aapas mein kaise jude hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Aaj ka safar ek line mein: Photochemical phase ke chaar khaane (roshni sokhna, paani tootna, oxygen nikalna, ATP aur NADPH) → proteins par jame pigments ke do LHC, PS I aur PS II ke ghere (khoj ka kram, kaam ka nahi) → antenna ke sainkdon pigment se energy beech ke reaction centre tak → electron uchhal kar primary acceptor tak → P700 (700 nm) aur P680 (680 nm) ke card. Shuru ka sawaal laut kar aata: akela pigment das chamak, poori toli se reaction centre par lagaataar chamak; kone mein aatishi sheesha kaagaz sulgaata. Aakhir mein primary acceptor se aage ek khaali raasta aur do photosystem ke beech sawaal ka nishaan, jo khula rehta hai.

## 11.6 THE ELECTRON TRANSPORT

_Maqsad: Har baar roshni padne par dono photosystem apna ek electron chhod dete hain; toh yeh electron jaate kahan hain, aur photosystem khaali kyun nahi ho jaate?_

_Misaal: roller coaster: gaadi = electron; do motor wali chain = roshni ke do dhakke (pehla PS II par, doosra PS I par); har choti = electron acceptor; pehli lambi utaar = PS II se PS I tak cytochromes ki kadi (downhill); doosri, aur bhi oonchi choti = PS I ka acceptor (greater redox potential); aakhri utaar = NADP⁺ tak; oonche platform wala station = NADP⁺, jahan electron rukta hai; poore raaste gaadi wahi rehti hai = electron kharch nahi hota; oonchai = energy_

Kitab ki baatein (68):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · PS II ke reaction centre ka chlorophyll a 680 nm wavelength ki laal roshni sokhta hai.
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · Isse electron excited hote hain aur atom ke nucleus se door wale orbit mein kood jaate hain.
- f3 · kitab kehti hai · In electrons ko ek electron acceptor pakadta hai, jo inhe aage electron transport system ko de deta hai.
- f4 · shabd ka matlab · excited · Excited: energy paakar electron ka zyada energy wali, uchhli hui haalat mein pahunchna; excited = uttejit.
- f5 · shabd ka matlab · orbit · Orbit: nucleus ke chaaron taraf electron ke ghoomne ka raasta; nucleus se jitna door ka orbit, wahan tikne ke liye utni zyada energy.
- f6 · shabd ka matlab · electron acceptor · Electron acceptor: doosre molecule se electron lene wala molecule; accept = lena; PS II ka pehla acceptor wahi primary acceptor hai.
- f7 · pehle ki zaroori baat · PS II and P680: PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, ek chlorophyll a jiski absorption choti 680 nm par; kaam mein PS II pehle aata hai, number khoj ke kram se pade (pichhli film).
- f8 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electron and atomic nucleus: atom ke beech mein nucleus (proton aur neutron), aur uske chaaron taraf chhote, negative charge wale electron ghoomte hain (Class 9; chapter ki shuruaat).
- f9 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Photon absorption: roshni photon naam ke packeton mein aati hai; antenna ke pigment photon sokh kar uski energy reaction centre tak bhejte hain (pichhli film).
- f10 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electron acceptor (pichhli film): reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle primary acceptor pakadta hai.
- f11 · teacher ki extra baat · Excited electron apne molecule mein der tak nahi tikta; paas baitha acceptor use turant le leta hai.
- f12 · kitab kehti hai · Electron transport system cytochromes se bana hai (Figure 11.5).
- f13 · kitab kehti hai · Electrons ka yeh chalna oxidation-reduction, yaani redox potential, scale par downhill hai.
- f14 · kitab kehti hai · Electron transport chain se guzarte hue electron kharch nahi hote; woh aage PS I ke pigments tak de diye jaate hain.
- f15 · kitab kehti hai · Saath-saath PS I ke reaction centre ke electron 700 nm wavelength ki laal roshni paakar excite hote hain, aur ek doosre acceptor molecule ko de diye jaate hain, jiska redox potential greater hai.
- f16 · shabd ka matlab · cytochromes · Cytochromes: electron lekar aage dene wale rangeen proteins; cyto = cell, chrome = rang; inke beech ka loha (iron) electron leta aur deta hai.
- f17 · shabd ka matlab · redox potential · Redox potential: koi molecule electron ko kitne zor se pakadta hai ya kitni aasaani se chhodta hai, iska maap; redox = reduction + oxidation; is maap se molecules ko upar-neeche rakhne par redox potential scale banti hai.
- f18 · shabd ka matlab · electron transport system · Electron transport system: electron ko aage pahunchane wala intezaam; transport = ek jagah se doosri jagah pahunchana, system = kai hisson ka milkar kaam karne wala intezaam.
- f19 · shabd ka matlab · downhill · Downhill: down = neeche, hill = pahadi; redox potential scale par electron ka apne aap upar se neeche utarna, jismein uski kuch energy nikalti hai.
- f20 · shabd ka matlab · electron transport chain · Electron transport chain: electron ko aage dene wale molecules ki kadi; chain = zanjeer, jo kadiyon se banti hai.
- f21 · shabd ka matlab · greater redox potential · Greater redox potential wala acceptor: PS I ka doosra acceptor, jo scale par PS II wale acceptor se bhi oonchi jagah baitha hai; wahan pahunch kar electron sabse zyada energy wali haalat mein hota hai.
- f22 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Light absorption by reaction-centre pigments: reaction centre ka chlorophyll a roshni ki energy paakar apna electron chhodta hai (pichhli film).
- f23 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electrons and oxidation-reduction: molecule se electron (ya hydrogen) nikalna oxidation hai, aur milna reduction (11.2 aur 11.4 mein dekha).
- f24 · pehle ki zaroori baat · PS II and PS I: do photosystem; PS II ka reaction centre P680, PS I ka P700; kaam mein pehle PS II, phir PS I (pichhli film).
- f25 · teacher ki extra baat · PS I ka electron chale jaane se uski jagah khaali hoti hai; kadi se aaya PS II ka electron yahi khaali jagah bharta hai, isliye dono photosystem ek line mein jude hain aur PS I khaali nahi hota.
- f26 · teacher ki extra baat · Redox potential scale par upar woh molecule jo electron aasaani se chhodte hain, neeche woh jo use kas kar pakadte hain; isliye electron apne aap upar se neeche jaata hai, kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf.
- f27 · kitab kehti hai · Phir yeh electron dobara downhill jaate hain, is baar energy-rich NADP⁺ ke ek molecule tak.
- f28 · kitab kehti hai · In electrons ke judne se NADP⁺ reduce hota hai.
- f29 · shabd ka matlab · NADP⁺ · NADP⁺: electron lene ko taiyaar, khaali carrier molecule (khaali thaila); electron paakar yahi bhara hua NADPH banta hai.
- f30 · shabd ka matlab · energy-rich · Energy-rich: energy se bhara; electron paakar bana NADPH energy se bhara hota hai.
- f31 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electron gain as reduction: kisi molecule ko electron milna hi uska reduction hai.
- f32 · pehle ki zaroori baat · PS I excitation: PS I ka P700 700 nm ki roshni paakar excite hota hai aur apna electron doosre acceptor ko deta hai (abhi dekha).
- f33 · teacher ki extra baat · Ek NADP⁺ ko NADPH banne ke liye do electron chahiye; exam ke liye: khaali NADP⁺, bhara NADPH, dono ke naam aapas mein nahi badalte.
- f34 · kitab kehti hai · NADP⁺ reduce hokar NADPH + H⁺ banta hai.
- f35 · kitab kehti hai · Electron ka poora raasta, PS II se uphill acceptor tak, electron transport chain se neeche PS I tak, electron ka excite hona, doosre acceptor tak jaana, aur aakhir mein downhill NADP⁺ tak jo reduce hokar NADPH + H⁺ banta hai, apne khaas aakaar ki wajah se Z scheme kehlata hai (Figure 11.5).
- f36 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh aakaar tab banta hai jab saare carriers redox potential scale par ek kram mein rakhe jaayein.
- f37 · shabd ka matlab · Z scheme · Z scheme: do baar roshni se upar uthte electron ka poora raasta, jo carriers ko scale par rakhne par karvat lete Z jaisa dikhta hai; scheme = khaaka.
- f38 · shabd ka matlab · carrier · Carrier: electron lekar agle molecule ko dene wala molecule; carry = dhona, jaise cycle ka carrier saamaan dhota hai.
- f39 · shabd ka matlab · uphill · Uphill: up = upar; scale par electron ka upar chadhna, jo apne aap nahi hota; iske liye roshni ki energy lagti hai.
- f40 · shabd ka matlab · H⁺ · H⁺: hydrogen atom jiska ek electron nikal gaya; bacha sirf ek proton, aur plus ka nishaan uska positive charge.
- f41 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electron transport between photosystems: PS II ka electron cytochromes ki kadi se utar kar PS I tak jaata hai (abhi dekha).
- f42 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Light-driven electron excitation: roshni electron ko oonchi energy tak uthaati hai, PS II par 680 nm aur PS I par 700 nm (abhi dekha).
- f43 · pehle ki zaroori baat · NADP⁺ reduction: NADP⁺ electron paakar reduce hota hai aur NADPH banta hai (abhi dekha).
- f44 · teacher ki extra baat · Membrane mein electron Z ke aakaar mein nahi chalta; Z energy ka chitra hai, membrane ka raasta nahi (exam ki aam galatfahmi).
- f45 · teacher ki extra baat · Ek NADP⁺ do electron aur H⁺ lekar NADPH banta hai; NADPH + H⁺ mein saath likha H⁺ hisaab poora rakhta hai.
- f46 · teacher ki extra baat · Exam mein Z scheme aksar adhoora dekar poochha jaata hai; kram: PS II, uska acceptor, kadi, PS I, uska acceptor, aur NADP⁺.
- f47 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.5 (Z scheme of light reaction): baayein Photosystem II, daayein Photosystem I; Light se peele dashed teer dono LHC tak; PS II ke LHC se laal teer upar uske e⁻ acceptor tak; phir laal teer Electron transport system se neeche PS I ke LHC tak; is system ke upar ADP + iP se ATP; PS I ke LHC se doosra laal teer upar uske e⁻ acceptor tak, phir NADP⁺ se NADPH; PS II ke neeche H₂O se 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O], aur ek teer electron PS II ke LHC tak.
- f48 · figure ka nishaan · Photosystem II · Photosystem II: upar baayein bracket, jo PS II aur uske electron acceptor ko gherta hai.
- f49 · figure ka nishaan · Photosystem I · Photosystem I: daayein bracket, jo PS I aur uske electron acceptor ko gherta hai.
- f50 · figure ka nishaan · Light · Light: baayein peela gola; usse peele dashed teer dono LHC tak, yaani roshni dono photosystem par padti hai.
- f51 · figure ka nishaan · LHC · LHC (baayein, hara oval): PS II ka roshni batorne wala jhund; roshni aur paani se aaya electron yahin pahunchte hain, aur yahin se teer upar acceptor tak.
- f52 · figure ka nishaan · LHC · LHC (daayein, hara oval): PS I ka roshni batorne wala jhund; roshni aur kadi se aaya electron yahin pahunchte hain, aur yahin se doosra teer upar.
- f53 · figure ka nishaan · e⁻ acceptor · e⁻ acceptor (baayein, gulabi oval): PS II ke LHC se upar jaata laal teer yahin pahunchta hai; yahan se electron kadi ki taraf.
- f54 · figure ka nishaan · e⁻ acceptor · e⁻ acceptor (daayein, gulabi oval): PS I ke LHC se doosra upar jaata teer yahin pahunchta hai; yahan se electron NADP⁺ ki taraf.
- f55 · figure ka nishaan · Electron transport system · Electron transport system: beech ka baingani dabba, PS II ke acceptor aur PS I ke LHC ke beech neeche utarte laal teeron par; cytochromes wali kadi.
- f56 · figure ka nishaan · ADP + iP · ADP + iP: dabbe ke upar mudi teer ki shuruaat; ATP banne ki shuruaati cheezein, electron ke utaar ke saath.
- f57 · figure ka nishaan · ATP · ATP: usi mudi teer ka ant; electron ke utaar ke saath ATP banta hai (kaise, yeh aage).
- f58 · figure ka nishaan · NADP⁺ · NADP⁺: daayein aakhri mudi teer par, khaali acceptor jahan electron ka safar khatam hota hai.
- f59 · figure ka nishaan · NADPH · NADPH: usi aakhri teer par NADP⁺ ke upar likha bhara hua product.
- f60 · figure ka nishaan · H₂O · H₂O: PS II ke neeche paani, jahan se electron dene wala teer shuru hota hai.
- f61 · figure ka nishaan · 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] · 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]: ek paani ke molecule ka hisaab, do electron, do H⁺ aur oxygen ka ek atom; ek teer electron PS II ke LHC tak le jaata hai.
- f62 · shabd ka matlab · e⁻ · e⁻: electron ka nishaan; e = electron, upar ka chhota minus = uska negative charge.
- f63 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Photosystems: do photosystem, har ek mein roshni batorne wala LHC aur ek reaction centre (pichhli film).
- f64 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Electron transfer: electron ek molecule se doosre molecule tak jaata hai; dene wala oxidise, lene wala reduce hota hai (abhi dekha).
- f65 · pehle ki zaroori baat · ATP and NADPH as light-reaction products: ATP energy lekar aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar stroma tak jaate hain, jahan sugar banti hai (11.3, 11.5).
- f66 · teacher ki extra baat · [O] yaani oxygen ka ek atom; aise do atom milkar O₂ gas banti hai, isliye figure ka [O] O₂ nahi hai.
- f67 · teacher ki extra baat · Ek NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon lagte hain: do electron, aur har electron ke peeche ek photon PS II par aur ek PS I par.
- f68 · teacher ki extra baat · Kuch kharpatwar-naashak dawaiyaan (weed-killer) PS II ke aage ki kadi mein electron ka raasta rok deti hain; tab PS I ki khaali jagah nahi bharti, NADPH nahi banta aur paudha bhookha mar jaata hai.

**11.6-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli film mein humne dekha ki har photosystem ke sainkdon pigment milkar roshni batorte hain. Unki saari energy ek hi jagah pahunchti hai: reaction centre par. Wahan ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur primary acceptor use pakad leta hai. Yeh kaam PS II mein P680 karta hai, aur PS I mein P700. Yaad rakhein: roshni ka asli kaam yahi hai, reaction centre ke ek electron ko uthakar doosre molecule tak pahunchana. Aage hum dekhenge ki primary acceptor ke baad electron kis raaste jaata hai, aur do photosystem ek-doosre se kaise bandhe hain. Par pehle ek ajeeb baat par gaur karo. Socho, patti par din bhar roshni padti hai, aur har baar dono photosystem apna ek electron chhod dete hain. Toh yeh saare electron jaate kahan hain, aur itne electron dekar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi ho jaate? Electron kahan jaate hain aur photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote, yahi aaj ka sawaal hai. Iska jawab ek safar mein chhupa hai, jo electron teen padaav mein poora karta hai. Pehle padaav par PS II hai. Wahan roshni electron ko upar uchhaalti hai, aur ek acceptor use pakad leta hai. Doosre padaav mein electron molecules ki ek kadi se neeche utar kar PS I tak aata hai. Phir wahan roshni use doosri baar uchhaalti hai. Aur teesre padaav mein electron phir neeche utarta hai. Woh ek khaas molecule tak pahunch kar ruk jaata hai. Aur ek hi electron ko roshni ka dhakka do baar kyun chahiye, yeh bhi aaj saaf ho jaayega.
  - screen: Pichhli film ki yaad: ek photosystem ke sainkdon pigment se energy ke teer beech ke reaction centre tak; P680 (PS II) aur P700 (PS I) ke reaction centre se electron uchhal kar primary acceptor tak; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: roshni ek electron ko uthakar doosre molecule tak pahunchati hai'. Phir thylakoid membrane par PS II aur PS I saath-saath, din bhar dhoop; dono par roshni padti, dono se ek-ek electron uchhalta; sawaal ka dabba 'electron kahan jaate hain? photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote?'. Phir safar ka naksha teen khaano mein: 1 PS II par roshni ka dhakka aur acceptor; 2 molecules ki kadi se neeche utar kar PS I tak, wahan doosra dhakka; 3 phir neeche utar kar ek khaas molecule par ruk jaana (naam ki jagah '?'); kone mein chhota sawaal '2 dhakke kyun?'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Opening recap of 11.5 (sainkdon pigment, reaction centre, primary acceptor, P680 aur P700) aur uske do khule sawaal; aaj ka sawaal: din bhar electron chhod kar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote.
**11.6-k2** · samjhana · Ab pehle padaav par chalte hain, yaani PS II par. Yaad karo, kaam mein PS II pehle aata hai, chahe uska number do ho. Number toh khoj ke kram se pade the. PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, yaani ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule. Yeh 680 nm wavelength wali laal roshni sokhta hai. Ab sochiye, roshni ki yeh energy jaati kahan hai? Roshni ki yeh energy seedhe reaction centre ke ek electron ko milti hai. Yaad karo, har atom ke beech mein nucleus hota hai, aur electron uske chaaron taraf ghoomte rehte hain. Electron nucleus ke chaaron taraf jis raaste par ghoomta hai, use orbit kehte hain. Nucleus se jitna door ka orbit ho, wahan tikne ke liye electron ko utni hi zyada energy chahiye. Isliye roshni ki energy milte hi electron nucleus se door wale orbit mein kood jaata hai. Bilkul waise, jaise aap koi achhi khabar sunkar khushi se uchhal padte ho. Energy paakar electron ki is uchhli hui, zyada energy wali haalat ko excited kehte hain; excited yaani uttejit. Ab samjho, excited electron apne molecule mein zyada der tik nahi paata. Paas baitha ek molecule use turant pakad leta hai. Jo molecule doosre molecule se electron le leta hai, use electron acceptor kehte hain; accept yaani lena. Humne pichhli film mein isi pehle acceptor ko primary acceptor kaha tha, yaani sabse pehle lene wala. Toh kya acceptor yeh electron apne paas rakh leta hai? Acceptor yeh electron apne paas nahi rakhta. Woh use turant aage, ek electron transport system ko de deta hai. Transport ka matlab aap jaante ho: ek jagah se doosri jagah pahunchana. System yaani kai hisson ka milkar kaam karne wala intezaam. Matlab electron transport system woh intezaam hai, jo electron ko aage pahunchata hai. Yeh intezaam kin molecules se bana hai, aur electron ko kahan le jaata hai, yeh hum agle padaav mein dekhenge.
  - terms: orbit = Nucleus ke chaaron taraf electron ke ghoomne ka raasta; door ka orbit = zyada energy.; excited = Energy paakar electron ka zyada energy wali, uchhli hui haalat mein pahunchna; uttejit.; electron acceptor = Doosre molecule se electron lene wala molecule; accept = lena.; electron transport system = Electron ko aage pahunchane wala intezaam; transport = pahunchana, system = mila-jula intezaam.
  - screen: Thylakoid membrane par PS II ka ghera; kone mein patti 'kaam ka kram: pehle PS II (number khoj se)'. Reaction centre P680 (ek chlorophyll a) par 680 nm ki laal roshni ka teer; sawaal ka chhota dabba 'energy kahan jaati?'. Phir paas se ek atom: beech mein nucleus, chaaron taraf orbit ke ghere, ek electron andar wale ghere par (label: orbit; door ka orbit = zyada energy); roshni ki energy aate hi electron door wale ghere mein koodta. Kone mein khushkhabri sunkar uchhalta insaan; label 'excited = uttejit'. Phir excited electron molecule se nikalta aur paas ka molecule use pakad leta (label: electron acceptor, accept = lena; wahi primary acceptor). Acceptor ke paas sawaal 'rakh lega?' par kaata nishaan; electron aage ek dhundhle intezaam ki taraf, label 'electron transport system: transport = pahunchana, system = intezaam', uspar '?' 'kin molecules se bana?'.
  - NCERT: p008-i14 In photosystem II the reaction centre chlorophyll a absorbs 680 nm wavelength of
  - kitaab se bahar: Orbit ka matlab aur 'door ka orbit = zyada energy' (Class 9 ka atom); khushi se uchhalne ki misaal; excited electron ka der tak na tikna; PS II ka pehla acceptor hi pichhli film ka primary acceptor; transport aur system ke matlab.
**11.6-k3** · analogy · Is poore safar ko ek mele ke roller coaster se samjho. Roller coaster ki gaadi pehle dheere-dheere ek oonchi chadhai par chadhti hai. Use ek motor wali chain upar kheenchti hai; gaadi apne aap upar nahi ja sakti. Choti par pahunch kar gaadi apne aap neeche utarti hai. Utaar par woh ek ke baad ek mod paar karti jaati hai. Neeche pahunchte hi ek doosri chain use phir upar kheenchti hai, aur is baar pehle se bhi oonchi choti tak. Wahan se gaadi doosri utaar par neeche aati hai. Aakhir mein woh ek oonche platform wale station par ruk jaati hai. Ab batao, station toh oonchai par hai; pehli utaar ke baad gaadi wahan apne aap kyun nahi pahunch sakti? Pehli utaar ke baad gaadi station tak apne aap isliye nahi pahunch sakti, kyunki woh neeche aa chuki hoti hai. Oonchai par chadhne ke liye use phir zor chahiye, aur yahi zor doosri chain deti hai. Ab jodo: roller coaster ki gaadi hai electron. Dono motor wali chain hain roshni ke do dhakke, pehla PS II par aur doosra PS I par. Har choti ek electron acceptor hai, jo upar pahunche electron ko pakad leta hai. Pehli lambi utaar woh kadi hai, jisse electron PS II se PS I tak neeche utarta hai. Aur oonche platform wala station woh khaas molecule hai, jahan electron ka safar khatam ho jaata hai. Gaur karo, poore raaste mein gaadi wahi rehti hai; badalti hai toh bas uski oonchai. Electron ke liye yahi oonchai uski energy hai: upar matlab zyada energy, aur neeche matlab kam.
  - screen: Mele ka roller coaster, bagal se: ek gaadi motor wali chain par dheere-dheere pehli oonchi chadhai chadhti (chain ki tak-tak); choti se gaadi apne aap ghumaavdaar utaar par neeche; neeche doosri chain use aur bhi oonchi choti tak kheenchti; wahan se doosri utaar, aur aakhir mein oonche platform wale station par gaadi rukti. Sawaal ka dabba 'pehli utaar ke baad station tak kyun nahi?': gaadi platform ke neeche ruki, upar chadhne ke liye chain. Phir jod ke label, ek-ek karke: gaadi = electron; do chain = roshni ke do dhakke (PS II, PS I); choti = electron acceptor; pehli utaar = PS II se PS I tak ki kadi; station = khaas molecule (naam '?'). Aakhir mein gaadi par nishaan 'wahi gaadi', aur bagal mein ek khadi patti 'oonchai = energy'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Mele ke roller coaster ki misaal: do motor wali chadhai, beech ki utaar, oonche platform wala station.
  - kya kis jaisa: Roller coaster ki gaadi = electron; dono motor wali chain = roshni ke do dhakke, pehla PS II par (680 nm) aur doosra PS I par (700 nm); har choti = electron acceptor; pehli lambi utaar = PS II se PS I tak cytochromes ki kadi, jahan electron apne aap downhill utarta hai; doosri, aur oonchi choti = PS I ka acceptor (greater redox potential); aakhri utaar = NADP⁺ tak; oonche platform wala station = NADP⁺, jahan electron ka safar khatam hota hai; poore raaste gaadi wahi rehti hai = electron kharch nahi hota; oonchai = energy, isliye pehli utaar ke baad doosra dhakka chahiye.
  - kahan tootti hai: Electron sach mein upar-neeche nahi jaata: oonchai yahan energy hai, membrane mein jagah nahi. Gaadi ek patri par khud chalti hai, par electron ek molecule se doosre molecule ke paas jaata hai, jo use lete aur aage dete hain. Asli roller coaster ghoom kar shuru wale station par laut aata hai, par is raaste mein electron aakhri molecule (NADP⁺) par ruk jaata hai, wapas PS II par nahi aata. Motor aur chain bijli se chalte hain, yahan dhakka roshni deti hai.
**11.6-k4** · samjhana · Ab doosra padaav: PS II ke acceptor ke baad electron jaata kahan hai? Acceptor ke baad electron molecules ki ek kadi mein jaata hai, jismein kai khaas proteins baithe hain. Yeh proteins electron ko ek pal ke liye lete hain, aur phir agle ko de dete hain. Inke beech mein loha, yaani iron, ka ek atom hota hai; wahi electron ko leta aur aage deta hai. In proteins ko cytochromes kehte hain: cyto yaani cell, aur chrome yaani rang, kyunki yeh rangeen proteins hain. Isliye electron ko aage le jaane wala yeh intezaam cytochromes wala electron transport system kehlata hai. Ab dekho ki electron is kadi mein kis disha mein chalta hai. Yaad karo, molecule se electron nikal jaaye toh oxidation hota hai, aur electron mil jaaye toh reduction. Har molecule electron ko alag zor se pakadta hai. Koi use kas kar pakadta hai, koi aasaani se chhod deta hai. Pakad ke is zor ke maap ko redox potential kehte hain; redox yaani reduction aur oxidation, dono milkar. Ab saare molecules ko is maap ke hisaab se upar-neeche ek line mein khada karo; yahi redox potential scale hai. Upar woh molecule aate hain jo electron aasaani se chhod dete hain. Neeche woh, jo use kas kar pakadte hain. Isliye electron apne aap upar se neeche jaata hai, kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf. Is utaar mein uski kuch energy nikalti hai. Electron ke is apne aap neeche utarne ko downhill jaana kehte hain; down yaani neeche, aur hill yaani pahadi. Bilkul roller coaster ki pehli utaar ki tarah, jahan gaadi bina motor ke neeche aati hai. Aksar hum sochte hain ki energy dete-dete electron raaste mein kharch ho jaata hai; par aisa nahi hai. Electron transport chain se guzarte hue electron kharch nahi hote. Wahi electron ek molecule se agle molecule tak badhta jaata hai. Chain yaani zanjeer: jaise zanjeer kadiyon se banti hai, waise hi yeh kadi bhi kai molecules se bani hai. Kharch hoti hai toh sirf uski thodi energy. Bilkul waise, jaise roller coaster ki gaadi utaar par apni oonchai khoti hai. Aur yeh kadi electron ko le kahan jaati hai? Yeh kadi electron ko seedhe PS I ke pigments tak pahuncha deti hai.
  - terms: cytochromes = Electron lekar aage dene wale rangeen proteins; cyto = cell, chrome = rang; inme loha (iron) electron leta-deta hai.; redox potential = Molecule electron ko kitne zor se pakadta ya kitni aasaani se chhodta hai, iska maap; redox = reduction + oxidation.; downhill = Redox potential scale par electron ka apne aap upar se neeche utarna, jismein energy nikalti hai; down = neeche, hill = pahadi.
  - screen: PS II ke acceptor se aage molecules ki ek kadi (zanjeer ki kadiyon jaisi); kadi mein kuch proteins chamakte, electron ek pal unke paas rukta aur aage jaata; ek protein ke beech loha (Fe) ka atom electron leta-deta; label 'cytochromes: cyto = cell, chrome = rang', proteins halke rangeen. Label 'cytochromes wala electron transport system'. Phir ek khadi seedhi jaisi patti: molecules upar-neeche khade, upar 'electron aasaani se chhodte', neeche 'kas kar pakadte'; kone mein 'oxidation = electron nikalna, reduction = electron milna'; label 'redox potential = pakad ka maap; redox = reduction + oxidation'; patti par likha 'redox potential scale'. Electron upar se neeche ek-ek molecule par utarta, har kadam par halki chamak (energy nikalti); label 'downhill: down = neeche, hill = pahadi'; kone mein roller coaster ki pehli utaar. 'Electron kharch?' par kaata nishaan: wahi electron har kadam par chamakta rehta, sirf oonchai ghatti. Aakhir mein kadi ka sira PS I ke pigments tak pahunchta.
  - NCERT: p009-i1 system consisting of cytochromes (Figure 11.5). This movement of electrons is do
  - kitaab se bahar: Cytochromes mein heme ka loha (iron) electron leta-deta hai aur inka rang inhi se hai (cyto + chrome); redox potential ko 'pakad ka zor' aur scale ko upar-neeche ki line ki tarah samjhaya; downhill = down + hill; chain = zanjeer; electron kharch nahi hota, sirf uski energy ghatti hai (roller coaster ki utaar).
**11.6-k5** · samjhana · Ab kadi ke doosre sire par chalo, jahan PS I baitha hai. PS II ka electron abhi kadi mein neeche utar hi raha hota hai. Theek usi waqt PS I par bhi roshni padti hai. PS I ke reaction centre, yaani P700, ke electron 700 nm wavelength wali laal roshni paakar excite ho jaate hain. Yeh excited electron ek doosre acceptor molecule ke paas chala jaata hai. Yeh doosra acceptor redox potential scale par PS II wale acceptor se bhi oonchi jagah baitha hai. Isliye ise greater redox potential wala acceptor kaha jaata hai. Wahan pahunch kar electron sabse zyada energy wali haalat mein hota hai. Yeh doosra dhakka isliye zaroori hai, kyunki kadi se utar kar electron neeche aa chuka hota hai. Roller coaster mein yahi doosri chain hai, jo gaadi ko pehle se bhi oonchi choti tak le jaati hai. Ab socho, PS I ka apna electron toh chala gaya, toh uski jagah khaali ho gayi. Ek clue par dhyan do: PS II ka electron kadi par neeche aa raha hai. Aur yeh kadi seedhe PS I tak jaati hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: PS I ki yeh khaali jagah kaun bharta hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki PS II se aaya electron hi use bharta hai; aapne bilkul sahi socha. Kadi PS II wale electron ko PS I ke pigments tak pahunchati hai. Wahi electron P700 ki khaali jagah bhar deta hai. Isliye PS I baar-baar electron dekar bhi khaali nahi hota: uski bharpaai PS II se aate electron karte rehte hain. Yahi woh jod hai jo pichhli film ne khula chhoda tha: dono photosystem electron ke ek hi safar se jude hain. Pehle PS II, phir kadi, phir PS I; dono ek line mein, ek ke baad ek kaam karte hain.
  - screen: Kadi ka doosra sira aur wahan PS I ka ghera; kadi par neeche utarta PS II ka electron, aur usi waqt PS I par 700 nm ki laal roshni (dono saath-saath, label 'usi waqt'). P700 ka electron uchhal kar ek doosre acceptor tak, jo scale ki patti par PS II wale acceptor se bhi upar bana; label 'greater redox potential wala acceptor: sabse zyada energy'. Kone mein roller coaster ki doosri chain, oonchi choti tak. Phir P700 par electron ki khaali jagah ka chamakta ghera; clue ka chhota teer kadi ke sire se PS I ki taraf. Sawaal 'Ruko, pehle khud socho: khaali jagah kaun bharega?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir kadi se aaya PS II ka electron P700 ki khaali jagah mein baith jaata; label 'bharpaai'. Aakhir mein ek line: PS II → kadi → PS I, aur upar 'dono photosystem jude'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Book ka 'greater redox potential' (PS I ka doosra acceptor) figure ki tarah scale par oonchi jagah aur zyada energy ke roop mein padhaya; electrochemistry ki bhasha mein is acceptor ka reduction potential zyada negative hota hai, yeh baat sirf yahan record mein. PS II ka electron PS I ki khaali jagah bharta hai, yeh 'passed on to the pigments of PS I' ka matlab; ruko, pehle khud socho isi mod par.
**11.6-k6** · samjhana · Ab teesra aur aakhri padaav: PS I ke doosre acceptor se electron aage kidhar badhta hai? PS I ke acceptor ke baad electron phir downhill jaata hai, yaani apne aap neeche utarta hai. Bilkul roller coaster ki doosri utaar ki tarah, jo gaadi ko aakhri station tak le jaati hai. Yeh station ek khaas molecule hai, jo electron lene ke liye khaali baitha rehta hai. Socho, bazaar jaate waqt haath mein ek khaali thaila, jo saamaan bharne ka intezaar kar raha ho. Is khaali molecule ka naam NADP⁺ hai, aur electron aate hi yeh bharne lagta hai. Yaad karo, kisi molecule ko electron milna hi uska reduction hai. Isliye electron judte hi NADP⁺ reduce ho jaata hai; yahi is padaav ka asli kaam hai. Ek NADP⁺ ko poora bharne ke liye do electron chahiye. Bhar kar yahi molecule energy-rich, yaani energy se bhara, NADPH ban jaata hai. NADPH ko aap pehle se jaante ho: yeh electron aur hydrogen lekar stroma tak jaata hai, jahan sugar banti hai. Exam ke liye yeh pakki kar lo: khaali thaila NADP⁺ hai, aur bhara thaila NADPH. Dono ke naam aapas mein mat badalna. Toh electron ka safar yahin khatam hota hai; roller coaster ki gaadi station par ruk gayi.
  - terms: NADP⁺ = Electron lene ko taiyaar khaali carrier molecule (khaali thaila); electron paakar NADPH banta hai.
  - screen: PS I ke acceptor se electron ek doosri utaar par neeche; kone mein roller coaster ki doosri utaar aur oonche platform wala station. Station ki jagah ek molecule, uske paas khaali thaile ki chhoti tasveer (bazaar jaata haath); label 'NADP⁺ = khaali thaila'. Do electron aakar judte, thaila bharta; label 'electron milna = reduction', 'NADP⁺ reduce hua'; ginti '2 electron'. Bhara thaila: label 'NADPH = energy-rich, energy se bhara'; NADPH stroma ki taraf jaata, wahan sugar ke moti. Exam ka card: 'khaali NADP⁺, bhara NADPH, naam mat badlo'. Aakhir mein roller coaster ki gaadi station par rukti.
  - NCERT: p009-i2 These electrons then are moved downhill again, this time to a molecule of energy
  - kitaab se bahar: Khaali aur bhare thaile ki misaal; ek NADP⁺ ko do electron chahiye; book ka 'energy-rich NADP⁺' yahan bhare hue NADPH par lagaya, kyunki energy se bhara asal mein NADPH hai; exam ke liye NADP⁺ aur NADPH ka farq.
**11.6-k7** · samjhana · Yaad karo, hydrogen sabse chhota atom hai: beech mein ek proton, aur bahar ek electron. Woh electron nikal jaaye, toh jo bachta hai use H⁺ likhte hain; plus ka nishaan uske positive charge ka hai. Ab NADP⁺ ke bharne ko aksar NADPH + H⁺ likha jaata hai. Ek NADP⁺ do electron aur H⁺ lekar NADPH banta hai. Hisaab poora rakhne ke liye saath mein ek H⁺ likha jaata hai. Ab is poore safar ko shuru se aakhir tak ek saath dekho. Roshni ke bina electron kabhi upar nahi chadhta; upar chadhne ke liye use energy chahiye. PS II par roshni ne electron ko acceptor tak upar chadhaya; is chadhai ko uphill jaana kehte hain, up yaani upar. Phir electron transport chain se neeche PS I tak aaya, wahan dobara excite hua, aur doosre acceptor tak chadha. Aakhir mein downhill utar kar woh NADP⁺ tak pahuncha, aur use NADPH + H⁺ bana diya. Is raaste mein jo bhi molecule electron lekar agle ko deta hai, use carrier kehte hain; carry yaani dhona. Jaise cycle ke peeche ka carrier saamaan dhota hai, waise yeh molecule electron dhote hain. Ab saare carriers ko redox potential scale par unki jagah rakho, aur electron ke raaste ki lakeer kheencho. Lakeer pehle upar jaati hai, phir neeche, phir dobara upar, aur aakhir mein neeche. Is lakeer ko ek karvat lita do, toh angrezi ka akshar Z dikhta hai. Isi khaas aakaar ki wajah se is poore raaste ko Z scheme kehte hain; scheme yaani khaaka. Z ka aakaar isliye banta hai, kyunki electron do baar roshni se upar uthta hai. Aur dono baar uske baad woh neeche utarta hai. Bilkul roller coaster ki patri jaisa: do chadhai, beech mein lambi utaar, aur aakhri utaar station tak. Kai bachche sochte hain ki electron membrane mein sach mein Z ke aakaar mein chalta hai. Par asal mein Z sirf energy ka chitra hai, membrane ka raasta nahi. Exam mein Z scheme aksar adhoora dekar poochha jaata hai. Isliye iska kram pakka yaad rakho: PS II, acceptor, kadi, PS I, acceptor, aur NADP⁺.
  - terms: H⁺ = Hydrogen atom jiska electron nikal gaya; bacha sirf ek proton, plus = positive charge.; uphill = Scale par electron ka upar chadhna, jo roshni ki energy se hi hota hai; up = upar.; carrier = Electron lekar agle molecule ko dene wala molecule; carry = dhona.; Z scheme = Do baar roshni se upar uthte electron ka poora raasta, jo carriers ko redox potential scale par rakhne par karvat lete Z jaisa dikhta hai; scheme = khaaka.
  - screen: Hydrogen ka chhota atom: beech mein ek proton, bahar ek electron; electron udkar chala jaata, bacha proton, label 'H⁺: plus = positive charge'. Phir NADP⁺ ke bhare thaile ke paas likha 'NADPH + H⁺'; chhota hisaab: NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH, saath mein ek H⁺. Phir poora safar ek lakeer ki tarah: PS II par roshni, electron upar acceptor tak (label 'uphill: up = upar'), kadi se neeche PS I tak, phir roshni se upar doosre acceptor tak, aakhir mein neeche NADP⁺ tak. Label 'carrier: carry = dhona', kone mein cycle ke peeche ka carrier jis par saamaan bandha. Saare carriers redox potential scale par apni jagah, unhe jodti lakeer: upar, neeche, upar, neeche. Lakeer karvat leti hai aur akshar Z ban jaata hai; label 'Z scheme (scheme = khaaka)'. Kone mein roller coaster ki patri usi aakaar mein. 'Membrane mein Z raasta?' par kaata nishaan: membrane mein carriers baithe, Z sirf energy ke graph par. Aakhir mein exam ka kram card: PS II → acceptor → kadi → PS I → acceptor → NADP⁺.
  - NCERT: p009-i3 to NADPH + H⁺. This whole scheme of transfer of electrons, starting from the PS 
  - kitaab se bahar: H⁺ ka matlab (proton); NADPH + H⁺ ke likhne ka hisaab (NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ → NADPH + H⁺); carrier ki cycle wali misaal; lakeer ko karvat lita kar Z dekhna; Z membrane ka raasta nahi, energy ka chitra hai (exam ki galatfahmi); adhoore Z scheme wale exam sawaal ke liye kram.
**11.6-k8** · figure · Ab Figure 11.5 dekho; iske neeche likha hai Z scheme of light reaction, yaani light reaction ka Z jaisa khaaka. Upar baayein ek bracket par likha hai Photosystem II, aur daayein doosre bracket par Photosystem I. Har bracket apne photosystem ke saath uske acceptor ko bhi gherta hai. Baayein ek peela gola hai, jis par likha hai Light, yaani roshni. Usse peele dashed teer dono taraf ke hare ovals tak jaate hain, aur dono ovals par likha hai LHC. LHC wahi roshni batorne wale jhund hain, jo humne pichhli film mein dekhe. Peele teer batate hain ki roshni dono photosystem par padti hai. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo, baayein PS II ke LHC se. Yahan se ek laal teer seedha upar ek gulabi oval tak jaata hai, jis par likha hai e⁻ acceptor. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai: e yaani electron, aur upar ka chhota minus uska negative charge. Yeh upar jaata teer pehli chadhai hai: roshni ne electron ko uthaya, aur acceptor ne use pakda. Ab ungli neeche le chalo: laal teer ek baingani dabbe se hote hue neeche utarte hain. Dabbe par likha hai Electron transport system. Yahi cytochromes wali kadi hai, aur teer ka neeche jaana electron ka downhill utaar hai. Is dabbe ke upar ek mudi hui teer dekho: ADP + iP se ATP. Matlab electron ke is utaar ke saath ATP banne ka kaam bhi juda hai. ADP aur iP kya hain, aur utaar se ATP banta kaise hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge. Laal teer utaar ke baad daayein PS I ke LHC tak pahunchte hain. Wahan se doosra laal teer phir upar jaata hai, aur doosre gulabi oval par bhi likha hai e⁻ acceptor. Is acceptor se electron aakhir mein ek mudi hui teer par NADP⁺ tak jaata hai. Usi teer par likha hai NADPH. Dekho, yahi hamara station hai: khaali NADP⁺ electron paakar bhara hua NADPH ban gaya. Ab PS II ke neeche dekho: wahan likha hai H₂O, aur usse ek teer 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] tak jaata hai. Wahan se ek teer electron ko PS II ke LHC tak pahunchata hai. Matlab PS II ki khaali jagah ka rishta paani se hai. Bracket wala [O] yaani oxygen ka ek atom; aise do atom milkar O₂ gas banti hai. Yeh hissa abhi bas pehchaan lo; iski poori kahani aage hum dekhenge.
  - terms: e⁻ = Electron ka nishaan; e = electron, minus = negative charge.
  - screen: Figure 11.5 ka saaf, bada chitra; neeche caption 'Z scheme of light reaction'. Ungli baari-baari: upar baayein bracket 'Photosystem II' aur daayein bracket 'Photosystem I' (dono apne acceptor samet) → baayein peela gola 'Light', usse peele dashed teer dono hare ovals tak, dono par 'LHC' → baayein LHC se laal teer upar gulabi oval 'e⁻ acceptor' tak (chhota label: e⁻ = electron, minus = negative charge; kone mein roller coaster ki pehli chadhai) → laal teer neeche baingani dabbe 'Electron transport system' se hote hue (label: cytochromes wali kadi, downhill) → dabbe ke upar mudi teer 'ADP + iP → ATP' halki chamak mein, uspar 'aage' ka chhota nishaan → teer daayein LHC tak → wahan se doosra laal teer upar doosre gulabi oval 'e⁻ acceptor' tak → mudi teer par 'NADP⁺' aur uske upar 'NADPH' (kone mein station par ruki gaadi) → PS II ke neeche 'H₂O' aur teer '2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]' tak, ek teer electron ko PS II ke LHC tak; label '[O] = oxygen ka ek atom; 2 [O] = O₂'; is hisse par 'aage' ka nishaan.
  - NCERT: p009-i4 Z scheme of light reaction. Photosystem II is shown on the left and Photosystem 
  - kitaab se bahar: e⁻ ka matlab; peele dashed teer roshni ke, laal teer electron ke; ADP + iP → ATP electron ke utaar par bana hai, iska tareeka aage; [O] oxygen ka ek atom hai, O₂ nahi (exam ki baareek baat); paani wala hissa sirf pehchaan, poori baat aage.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6-k9** · example · Chalo, ab ginti karke dekhte hain ki ek NADPH banane mein kitni roshni lagti hai. Humne dekha ki ek NADP⁺ ko poora bharne ke liye do electron chahiye. Ab pehle electron ka safar dekho. PS II par ek photon padta hai, aur P680 ka electron uchhal kar PS II ke acceptor tak jaata hai. Usi waqt roshni ka ek aur packet P700 ko jagaata hai, aur uska electron PS I ke apne acceptor par pahunch jaata hai. PS II wala electron cytochromes ki kadi se utar kar P700 ki khaali jagah bhar deta hai. Aur P700 wala electron downhill utar kar NADP⁺ tak pahunch jaata hai. Toh NADP⁺ tak ek electron pahunchane ke peeche do photon lage: ek PS II par, aur ek PS I par. Ab batao, ek poore NADPH ke liye kul kitne photon lagenge? Ek poore NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon lagenge. Do electron hain, aur har electron ke peeche do photon. Inme se do photon PS II par padte hain, aur do PS I par. Aur dhyan do, poore raaste mein dono electron kahin kharch nahi hue; bas unki energy upar-neeche hoti rahi. Roller coaster mein socho toh do gaadiyaan, har ek ne do chadhai chadhi, aur dono station tak pahunchi.
  - screen: Ginti ka board: 'ek NADPH = ?'. Pehle '2 electron' likha. Phir Z ka raasta: PS II par ek photon (roshni ka packet) girta, P680 ka electron uchhal kar acceptor tak; udhar PS I par ek photon, P700 ka electron doosre acceptor tak; PS II wala electron kadi se utar kar P700 ki khaali jagah mein; P700 wala electron neeche NADP⁺ tak. Board par '1 electron = 2 photon (1 PS II + 1 PS I)'. Sawaal 'ek NADPH = kitne photon?', phir '2 × 2 = 4': do photon PS II par, do PS I par. Dono electron ke paas 'kharch nahi' ka nishaan. Kone mein do roller coaster gaadiyaan, har ek do chadhai chadh kar station par.
  - kitaab se bahar: Ek NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon (do electron, har electron ke peeche PS II aur PS I par ek-ek); asli patti mein kuch roshni bekaar jaane se thodi zyada lagti hai, yeh sirf record mein.
**11.6-k10** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: agar beech ki kadi kahin ruk jaaye, aur PS II ka electron PS I tak na pahunche, toh kya hoga? Agar kadi ruk jaaye, toh PS I ki khaali jagah bharne wala koi nahi rahega. PS I ek-do baar electron dekar khaali baitha rahega, aur NADP⁺ tak electron pahunchna band ho jaayega. Isliye NADPH banna ruk jaayega, aur stroma mein sugar banne ka kaam bhi thap pad jaayega. Kuch kharpatwar-naashak dawaiyaan, yaani weed-killer, theek yahi karti hain: woh is kadi mein electron ka raasta rok deti hain. Isliye un dawaiyon se kharpatwar ka paudha roshni mein khada rehkar bhi bhookha mar jaata hai. Matlab NADPH banane ke liye dono photosystem ek-doosre par tike hain. Beech ki kadi hi un dono ko jodti hai.
  - screen: Z ka raasta; beech ki kadi par laal 'ruka' ka nishaan; sawaal ka dabba 'kadi ruk jaaye toh?'. PS II ka electron kadi par atka; P700 ki khaali jagah khaali hi chamakti; NADP⁺ tak jaata teer dhundhla aur band; NADPH ki jagah '0', stroma mein sugar ki machine thami. Phir khet mein kharpatwar par dawai ka chhidkaav (label: weed-killer), kadi par 'rok'; dhoop mein khada kharpatwar murjha jaata. Aakhir mein do photosystem aur beech ki kadi, upar 'ek-doosre par tike'.
  - kitaab se bahar: Kuch weed-killer (jaise DCMU aur atrazine wali dawaiyaan) PS II ke aage electron ka raasta rok deti hain; kadi rukne par NADPH banna ruk jaata hai.
  - jawab: Kadi ruk jaaye toh PS II ka electron PS I tak nahi pahunchta, isliye P700 ki khaali jagah nahi bharti; PS I ek-do baar electron dekar ruk jaata hai, NADP⁺ tak electron jaana band hota hai aur NADPH nahi banta, to stroma mein sugar banna bhi rukta hai; kuch weed-killer is kadi ko rok kar yahi karte hain. Isliye NADPH ke liye dono photosystem ek-doosre par tike hain.
  - aam galti: Galat soch 1: PS I ko roshni mil rahi hai, toh woh akele hi NADPH banata rahega; galat, uski khaali jagah PS II ke electron se hi bharti hai. Galat soch 2: kadi bas ek raasta hai, uske rukne se kuch nahi bigadta; galat, wahi dono photosystem ko jodti hai. Galat soch 3: electron raaste mein kharch ho jaate hain, isliye kami hoti hai; galat, electron kharch nahi hote, unka aage jaana rukta hai.
**11.6-k11** · poori kahani dobara · Chalo, aaj ke safar ko shuru se aakhir tak ek baar phir chalein. PS II ka P680, 680 nm ki laal roshni se excite hota hai, aur uska electron acceptor tak chadhta hai. Phir electron cytochromes ki kadi mein downhill utarta hai, kharch hue bina, aur PS I tak pahunchta hai. PS I ka P700, 700 nm ki roshni se excite hota hai. Uska electron doosre, aur bhi oonche acceptor tak jaata hai. Wahan se electron downhill utar kar NADP⁺ ko reduce karta hai, aur NADPH + H⁺ banta hai. Saare carriers ko redox potential scale par rakhne se yeh raasta Z jaisa dikhta hai, isliye iska naam Z scheme hai. Ab wahi sawaal jisse shuru kiya tha: itne electron chhod kar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi padte, aur electron pahunchte kahan hain? Photosystem isliye khaali nahi padte, kyunki ek ka chhoda electron doosre ki kami poori karta hai. PS II ka electron PS I ko milta hai. PS I ka electron NADP⁺ tak pahunch kar ruk jaata hai, aur NADPH ban kar stroma ke kaam aata hai. Aur PS II ki khaali jagah ka rishta, jaisa figure ne dikhaya, paani se hai. Roller coaster mein bhi yahi tha: gaadi wahi rahi, bas do baar upar kheenchi gayi aur do baar neeche utri. Par ek baat abhi bhi khuli hai: paani jaisa seedha-saadha molecule apne electron chhodta kaise hai, aur PS II ko lagaataar electron kaise milte rehte hain? Paani apne electron kaise chhodta hai aur PS II ko lagaataar electron kaise milte hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Aaj ka safar Z ki ek lakeer par: PS II (P680, 680 nm) se electron upar acceptor tak → cytochromes ki kadi par neeche (kharch nahi) → PS I (P700, 700 nm) se aur bhi oonche acceptor tak → neeche NADP⁺, jo NADPH + H⁺ banta; carriers scale par aur Z ka aakaar. Shuru ka sawaal laut kar aata: 'electron kahan? photosystem khaali kyun nahi?'; jawab ke teer: PS II ka electron PS I ki khaali jagah mein, PS I ka electron NADP⁺ par ruk kar NADPH, NADPH stroma ki taraf; PS II ki khaali jagah ke neeche paani ki boond. Kone mein roller coaster ki wahi gaadi, do chadhai aur do utaar. Aakhir mein paani ki boond par bada sawaal ka nishaan 'paani electron kaise chhodta hai?', jo khula rehta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11

## 11.6.1 Splitting of Water

_Maqsad: Ab PS II ki replacement supply ko water splitting se jodenge, aur electrons, oxygen aur protons ka hisaab unki sahi location par rakhenge._

**11.6.1-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab PS II ki replacement supply ko water splitting se jodenge, aur electrons, oxygen aur protons ka hisaab unki sahi location par rakhenge. Humne dekha tha ki PS II se electron transport chain PS I ko replacement electrons pahunchati hai. Lekin PS II khud continuously electrons kaise supply karega? Dekho, PS II ke donated electrons ki replacement water splitting se milti hai, isliye uski supply sirf pehle se maujood electrons par nahi tikti. Yahaan teen baatein follow karenge: replacement ka route, products ka balanced hisaab, aur membrane par unki release location.
  - screen: Realistic thylakoid membrane cutaway mein PS II se carriers aur PS I tak pehle se jaana electron route faint rahe; PS II ke paas water input highlight ho.
  - NCERT: p009-i21 You would then ask, How does PS II supply electrons continuously? The
**11.6.1-k2** · samjhana · Splitting of water woh process hai jisme water se electrons, H⁺ aur oxygen milte hain; yeh PS II se associated hai. Is process ko photolysis bhi kehte hain: photo light ko aur lysis splitting ko batata hai. Water ke electrons PS II ki electron kami poori karte hain, aur PS II transport chain ke through PS I ki kami poori karta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki water splitting PS I ko directly refill karti hai, par asal mein yahaan replacement route pehle PS II se guzarta hai. Oxygen photosynthesis ke net products mein hai; net products poori process ka overall hisaab karne ke baad milne wale substances hain. Pehle dekha [O] shorthand kya actual released gas tha? Dekho, [O] oxygen-atom equivalent ka accounting symbol tha, jabki released oxygen gas O₂ hoti hai.
  - terms: splitting of water = PS II-associated process jisme water se electrons, H⁺ aur oxygen milte hain.; net products = Poore process ke overall input–output hisaab mein milne wale substances.; photolysis = Light-driven machinery se judi water splitting; photo light aur lysis splitting hai.
  - screen: Ussi membrane picture par water se PS II ki replacement arrow judti hai; PS II → carriers → PS I route highlight hota hai aur oxygen output alag nikalta hai.
  - NCERT: p009-i22 electrons that were moved from photosystem II must be replaced. This is achieved
  - kitaab se bahar: Water splitting ka alternative naam photolysis aur uske word-parts jode gaye hain.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.1-k3** · analogy · Socho, ek chalti packing line par pehla worker token doosre worker ko deta hai, jo use aage dispatch kar deta hai. Pehla worker har handover ke baad apne supply box se naya token leta hai. Supply box band ho jaaye, toh continuous handover kyun rukega? Dekho, pehle worker ka khaali token-slot refill nahi hoga, isliye downstream line ko regular supply nahi mil paayegi. Yahaan supply box water hai, pehla worker PS II hai, beech ki line electron carriers hain, aur doosra worker PS I hai. Tokens electrons hain; water se PS II ki replacement downstream electron supply ko chalne deti hai.
  - screen: Supply box → worker PS II → carrier line → worker PS I ka token-flow model; khaali slot aur refill ek-ek karke dikhte hain.
  - kitaab se bahar: Electron replacement ke liye packing-line analogy.
  - kya kis jaisa: Supply box water, tokens electrons, first worker PS II, connecting line electron carriers aur second worker PS I hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Analogy replacement route samjhati hai; light excitation ki energy aur water ki chemical oxidation ko token handover represent nahi karta.
**11.6.1-k4** · example · Ab do water molecules ka batch lo: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. H⁺ aur electron ke charges hum pehle khol chuke hain; 4e⁻ yahaan chaar electrons ki count dikhata hai. Do H₂O mein total chaar hydrogen atoms aur do oxygen atoms hain. Products mein chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ molecule wahi atom-count sambhalte hain; chaar electrons electron-supply ledger mein aate hain. Charge ka balance bhi milta hai? Dekho, reactant water ka total charge zero hai, aur products ke +4 proton charges tatha −4 electron charges milkar zero dete hain. Isliye har ek O₂ ke saath do waters ki oxidation se chaar replacement electrons aur chaar H⁺ milte hain. Water electrons deta hai, isliye yeh water ki oxidation hai; light-driven machinery is electron extraction ko possible banati hai.
  - terms: 4e⁻ = Chaar electrons; har electron par ek negative elementary charge hai.
  - screen: Ussi PS II picture ke paas do water molecular models expand hon; 4 H⁺, 1 O₂ aur 4 e⁻ counted groups banen, saath atom aur charge ledger ho.
  - NCERT: p009-i23 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻
  - kitaab se bahar: Equation ka explicit charge balance aur water oxidation se sambandh samjhaya gaya hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.1-k5** · samjhana · Ab products ki location samajhne ke liye usi thylakoid membrane ko cross-section mein dekho. Thylakoid sacs ko hum grana ke building units pehchaan chuke hain; membrane se ghiri unki andar ki jagah lumen hai. Bahar surrounding fluid stroma hai, jise hum pehle locate kar chuke hain. Water splitting complex water oxidise karne wali PS II-associated machinery hai, jo membrane ke lumen-facing side par hoti hai. Inner side ka matlab yahaan thylakoid lumen ki taraf wala face hai, poore chloroplast ki koi bhi andar wali jagah nahi. Kya poora PS II lumen mein freely tairta hai? Dekho, PS II membrane mein embedded complex hai, aur uska water-splitting apparatus lumen ki taraf face karta hai.
  - terms: water splitting complex = PS II se associated water oxidise karke electrons, H⁺ aur oxygen dene wali machinery.; inner side = Thylakoid membrane ka lumen ki taraf wala face.; lumen = Thylakoid membrane se ghiri andar ki jagah.
  - screen: Ussi realistic membrane cutaway mein lumen andar aur stroma bahar label hon; membrane-embedded PS II ka water-splitting complex lumen-facing surface par highlight ho.
  - NCERT: p009-i24 We need to emphasise here that the water splitting complex is associated with th
  - kitaab se bahar: Location prerequisite ke liye lumen ab khola gaya hai; PS II membrane-embedded aur water-splitting apparatus lumen-facing hone ka distinction spasht kiya gaya hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.1-k6** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: water-splitting machinery lumen-facing hai, toh bane H⁺ aur O₂ pehle lumen mein release honge ya stroma mein? Apne jawab ko machinery ke face se jodo; sirf compartment ka naam yaad mat karo. Dekho, reaction lumen-facing side par hoti hai, isliye H⁺ aur O₂ lumen side par release hote hain, jabki electrons PS II ki replacement supply mein jaate hain. Kya oxygen wahin permanently band rahegi? Dekho, O₂ baad mein diffuse karke bahar ja sakti hai; formation ki location aur final destination alag baatein hain. Lumen mein mile H⁺ ka ATP formation se sambandh hum aage dekhenge.
  - screen: Ussi membrane picture mein 4 H⁺ lumen mein appear hon, O₂ lumen side se outward diffusion path le, aur 4 e⁻ PS II ko route hon.
  - NCERT: p009-i25 Then, where are the protons and O₂ formed likely to be released – in the lumen? 
  - kitaab se bahar: O₂ ki initial release location aur subsequent diffusion ka distinction joda gaya hai.
  - jawab: Lumen-facing water-splitting apparatus H⁺ aur O₂ lumen side par release karta hai; electrons PS II ko milte hain, aur O₂ baad mein diffuse kar sakti hai.
  - aam galti: Stroma ko release site na chuno: woh thylakoid ke bahar hai. PS I ko directly water-splitting electrons dena bhi replacement route ko galat karta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.1-k7** · asli duniya · Sunlit crop field se atmosphere mein pahunchne wali photosynthetic oxygen ka origin isi water oxidation mein hai. Released oxygen ke water-source ka isotope evidence hum pehle establish kar chuke hain. Ab us evidence ka mechanism kya mila? Dekho, PS II-associated water splitting oxygen banati hai, isliye air ki oxygen supply ka sambandh leaf ke water input se judta hai, CO₂ ki oxygen nikaalne se nahi.
  - screen: Sunlit crop leaf se chloroplast aur lumen-facing water splitting tak zoom; water ki oxygen ko O₂ ban kar stomatal route se atmosphere tak trace kiya jaaye.
**11.6.1-k8** · poori kahani dobara · Poora connection jodo: PS II electron donate karta hai, aur lumen-facing water-splitting complex uski replacement supply deta hai. Do waters se chaar electrons, chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ milte hain; atoms aur total charge conserved rehte hain. Electrons PS II ko milte hain, phir transport chain PS I ki replacement karti hai. H⁺ aur O₂ lumen side par release hote hain; O₂ baad mein bahar diffuse kar sakti hai. Water yahaan electron source hai, jabki photosystems ki excitation ko energy light deti hai.
  - screen: Ussi completed local membrane picture mein teen quiet routes: electrons → PS II → PS I; H⁺ → lumen; O₂ → outward diffusion. Neeche balanced equation rahe.

_→ Ab replacement electrons ka source clear hai; agle heading mein dekhenge ki electrons aage nikalne ya PS I par lautne se non-cyclic aur cyclic photophosphorylation kaise alag hoti hain._

## 11.6.2 Cyclic and Non-cyclic Photo-phosphorylation

_Maqsad: Agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega, aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?_

_Misaal: relay daud: electron baton, carriers khiladi; non-cyclic mein baton NADP⁺ ke haath tak, cyclic mein track ka ek chakkar_

Kitab ki baatein (45):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Jeev oxidisable substances se energy nikalte hain aur use bond energy ke roop mein rakhte hain.
- f2 · kitab kehti hai · ATP · ATP jaise khaas molecules yeh energy apne chemical bonds mein lekar chalte hain.
- f3 · kitab kehti hai · phosphorylation · Cell mitochondria aur chloroplast mein ATP banata hai; is kaam ko phosphorylation kehte hain.
- f4 · kitab kehti hai · Photo-phosphorylation · Roshni mein ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banna photo-phosphorylation hai.
- f5 · kitab kehti hai · non-cyclic · Do photosystem series mein kaam karein, pehle PS II phir PS I, toh non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation hoti hai.
- f6 · kitab kehti hai · electron transport chain · Dono photosystem electron transport chain se jude hain, jaise Z scheme mein dekha.
- f7 · kitab kehti hai · Is electron flow se ATP aur NADPH + H⁺ dono bante hain (Figure 11.5).
- f8 · kitab kehti hai · cyclic · Figure 11.6: PS I ka electron acceptor aur electron transport system se hokar P 700 par laut aata hai; saath mein ADP + Pi se ATP banta hai.
- f9 · kitab kehti hai · Jab sirf PS I kaam kare, electron photosystem ke andar ghoomta hai aur cyclic flow se phosphorylation hoti hai.
- f10 · kitab kehti hai · stroma lamellae · Iski ek sambhav jagah stroma lamellae hai.
- f11 · kitab kehti hai · Grana ki lamellae mein PS I aur PS II dono hain; stroma lamellae mein PS II aur NADP reductase nahi hote.
- f12 · kitab kehti hai · Excited electron NADP⁺ ko nahi jaata; electron transport chain se PS I complex par laut aata hai.
- f13 · kitab kehti hai · Cyclic flow se sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH + H⁺ nahi.
- f14 · kitab kehti hai · Jab excitation ke liye sirf 680 nm se aage ki wavelength mile, tab bhi cyclic photophosphorylation hoti hai.
- f15 · shabd ka matlab · oxidisable substances · Jo cheezein oxidation mein electron de sakein, jaise glucose.

- f20 · shabd ka matlab · phosphorylation · ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banana.

- f25 · shabd ka matlab · NADP reductase · Reductase yaani reduction karaane wala enzyme; NADP⁺ se NADPH banwata hai.
