**11.6.2-k1** · recap · Pichhli film mein humne dekha ki PS II ka khoya hua electron paani se aata hai. Do paani ke molecule tootte hain, aur chaar electron, chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ milte hain. Usse pehle Z scheme mein electron ka poora safar dekha tha: paani se PS II, phir carriers ki kadi, phir PS I, aur aakhir mein NADP⁺. Yaad rakhein: is safar mein electron ek hi disha mein chalta hai, paani se shuru hokar NADP⁺ par khatam. Aage hum dekhenge ki yeh safar hamesha aise hi chalta hai ya nahi. Ab socho: agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega? Aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?
  - screen: Z scheme ka raasta ek chalti line ki tarah: paani, PS II, carriers ki kadi, PS I, NADP⁺; paani ke do molecule toot kar 4 e⁻, 4 H⁺ aur ek O₂; yaad rakhein: electron ek hi disha mein, paani se NADP⁺ tak (ek teer); aakhri line par PS I ke aage NADP⁺ wala raasta band.
**11.6.2-k2** · samjhana · Is sawaal ka jawab isi film mein milega. Par usse pehle ek zaroori cheez pakki kar lo: ATP. Har jeev ko kaam ke liye energy chahiye: saans lene ke liye, chalne ke liye, ek naya molecule banane ke liye. Yeh energy jeev kuch cheezon se nikaalte hain, jaise khaane ka glucose. Yaad karo, oxidation ka matlab hai electron dena, aur reduction ka matlab hai electron lena. Jo cheezein oxidation mein electron de sakti hain, unhe oxidisable substances kehte hain. Glucose aisi hi ek cheez hai. Glucose se energy kaise nikalti hai, yeh aap aage respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki aisi cheezon se energy nikalti hai. Nikli hui energy jeev turant kharch nahi karte. Use molecules ke andar band karke rakh lete hain. Band karke isliye rakhte hain kyunki energy ki zaroorat har jagah aur har pal hoti hai, aur band energy ko jahan chaaho wahan le ja sakte ho. Socho ek gulel, jiski rubber ko kheench kar pakad liya. Energy us khinchi hui rubber mein band hai; chhodoge toh kaam karegi. Molecule mein atom aapas mein bandhanon se jude hote hain, jinhe chemical bonds kehte hain. In bonds mein bhi aise hi energy band rehti hai; ise bond energy kehte hain. ATP ek aisa hi khaas molecule hai. Yeh apne chemical bonds mein energy lekar chalta hai, aur cell ke har kaam tak pahunchata hai. ATP ka poora naam hai adenosine triphosphate. Tri yaani teen, toh isme teen phosphate jude hain. ADP yaani adenosine diphosphate. Di yaani do, toh isme do hi phosphate hain. ATP ko ek bhari hui battery samjho, aur ADP ko ek khaali hoti battery. Khaali battery ko phir se bharna hai, toh ADP mein ek phosphate aur jodna padta hai. Aur is jodne mein energy lagti hai. Jo phosphate akela hai, kisi molecule se juda nahi, use inorganic phosphate kehte hain. Chhote mein ise Pᵢ likhte hain; i yaani inorganic. ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banane ko phosphorylation kehte hain. Phosphate jodna, isliye naam phosphorylation. Cell yeh kaam do jagah karta hai: mitochondria mein aur chloroplast mein. Mitochondria wala tareeka bhi aap respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Hamari kahani chloroplast ki hai. Chloroplast mein yeh charging roshni se hoti hai, bilkul waise jaise solar charger dhoop se phone charge karta hai. Roshni ki madad se ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banane ko photo-phosphorylation kehte hain, kyunki photo ka matlab hi roshni hai.
  - terms: oxidisable substances = jo oxidation mein electron de sakein; bond energy = chemical bonds mein band energy; inorganic phosphate = akela phosphate, Pᵢ; phosphorylation = ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banana; Photo-phosphorylation = roshni ki madad se phosphorylation
  - screen: Energy ki zaroorat ke chhote drishya (saans, chalna, naya molecule); glucose se energy; oxidation = electron dena, reduction = electron lena; gulel ki khinchi rubber (apni tasveer); atomon ke beech chemical bond mein band energy; ATP: adenosine ke saath teen phosphate, ADP: do phosphate; bhari aur khaali battery (apni tasveer); ADP + Pᵢ → ATP, jodne mein energy; mitochondria aur chloroplast; dhoop se phone bharta solar charger (apni tasveer); roshni ke saath ADP + Pᵢ → ATP.
  - NCERT: p009-i27 Living organisms have the capability of extracting energy from oxidisable substa; p010-i1 ATP is synthesised by cells (in mitochondria and chloroplasts) is named phosphor
**11.6.2-k3** · samjhana · Ab Figure 11.5 wali Z scheme yaad karo, jo hum pehle padh chuke hain. Usme do photosystem the: pehle PS II, phir PS I. Dono par roshni padti thi. Dono ke beech carriers ki ek kadi thi, jo electron ko ek se doosre tak aage deti hai. Is kadi ko electron transport chain kehte hain: electron ka transport, yaani le jaana, ek chain mein. Ise ek relay daud ki tarah socho. Har khiladi baton ko agle khiladi ke haath mein deta hai, aur baton aage badhta jaata hai. Yahan baton hai electron, aur khiladi hain carriers. Roshni do jagah zor ka dhakka deti hai: PS II par aur PS I par. Is daud mein baton paani se chalta hai. PS II aur PS I se hokar, aakhir mein NADP⁺ ke haath mein pahunchta hai. NADP⁺ electron le leta hai aur NADPH ban jaata hai. Baton wahin ruk gaya; woh wapas shuru par nahi aata. Toh is raaste mein do cheezein banti hain: ATP aur NADPH. Saath mein paani tootne se O₂ bhi nikalti hai. Aksar ise NADPH + H⁺ likhte hain. Iska matlab bas itna hai ki NADPH ke saath ek H⁺ bhi likha jaata hai. Dhyan do, electron ek seedhe raaste par aage gaya, aur jahan se chala tha wahan wapas nahi lauta. Raasta gol nahi hai. Isliye ise non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation kehte hain. Cyclic yaani gol chakkar wala, aur non-cyclic yaani jo gol chakkar na ho. Yaad rakho: non-cyclic mein dono photosystem ek ke baad ek kaam karte hain, pehle PS II, phir PS I. Aur ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai. Yahan exam ki ek baareek baat hai: naam PS I hai, par kaam mein woh doosre number par aata hai. Number kaam ke kram se nahi pade; PS I pehle khoja gaya tha, isliye uska naam I pada.
  - terms: electron transport chain = carriers ki kadi jo electron aage deti hai; non-cyclic = jo gol chakkar na ho
  - screen: Z scheme: PS II aur PS I, dono par roshni, beech mein carriers ki kadi; relay daud (apni tasveer): baton electron, khiladi carriers; electron paani se NADP⁺ tak pahunch kar NADPH; ATP, NADPH aur O₂; seedha raasta jo shuru par wapas nahi aata; kaam ka kram PS II pehle, PS I baad mein, aur naam ka kram khoj se (PS I pehle khoja gaya).
  - NCERT: p010-i2 When the two photosystems work in a series, first PS II and then the PS I, a pro
  - kitaab se bahar: PS I aur PS II ke naam khoj ke kram se pade, yeh kitaab mein nahi; exam ki baareek baat ke roop mein joda.
  - kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki PS I pehle kaam karta hai kyunki uska number pehla hai.
**11.6.2-k4** · figure · Ab Figure 11.6 dekho. Yahan ek bilkul alag raasta hai. Sabse upar bracket mein likha hai Photosystem I. Matlab is figure mein sirf PS I ka kaam dikhaya gaya hai; ise kahin-kahin PSI bhi likha milta hai. Neeche likha hai Chlorophyll P 700. Yeh PS I ka reaction centre hai, jo lagbhag 700 nm ki roshni pakadta hai. Peeli dashed teer par Light likha hai. Roshni seedhe P 700 par padti hai. Roshni padte hi P 700 ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai. Ungli rakho: gulabi teer seedhe upar e⁻ acceptor tak jaata hai. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai. Aur e⁻ acceptor woh molecule hai jo uchhla hua electron sabse pehle pakadta hai. Ab acceptor se ek mudi hui gulabi teer neeche Electron transport system tak aati hai. Yeh carriers ki wahi kadi hai. Ab ungli aage le chalo. Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer kahan jaati hai? Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer wapas P 700 par hi pahunchti hai, wahin jahan se electron chala tha. Electron ne ek gol chakkar poora kiya: P 700 se acceptor, phir kadi, phir wapas P 700. Isi gol chakkar ki wajah se ise cyclic kehte hain. Relay wali daud yaad hai? Yahan baton kisi ko diya hi nahi gaya. Khiladi track ka ek chakkar lagakar wahin laut aaya jahan se chala tha. Ab kadi ke paas ek kaali mudi teer dekho: ADP + Pi se ATP. Yaani is chakkar ke saath ATP banta hai. Isliye ise cyclic photophosphorylation kehte hain: roshni se, ek gol chakkar ke saath, ATP banana. Aur yeh bhi dekho ki figure mein kya nahi hai: na PS II hai, na NADP⁺ tak jaane wala raasta.
  - terms: cyclic = gol chakkar wala
  - screen: Cyclic photophosphorylation ka figure jaisa drishya, har label tabhi jab line uska naam le: Photosystem I bracket, Chlorophyll P 700, Light ki peeli dashed teer, upar e⁻ acceptor tak gulabi teer, Electron transport system, wapas P 700 tak teer, ADP + Pi → ATP; relay mein khiladi track ka chakkar lagakar lautta (apni tasveer); aakhir mein dikhe ki PS II aur NADP⁺ ka raasta nahi hai.
  - NCERT: p010-i3 Cyclic photophosphorylation: a dashed yellow arrow carries Light to Chlorophyll 
**11.6.2-k5** · samjhana · Toh shuruaat ka sawaal: jab NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta na ho, toh electron kahan jaata hai? Jab NADP⁺ tak raasta na ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se hokar wapas PS I par laut aata hai. Aisa tab hota hai jab sirf PS I kaam kar raha ho. Electron PS I ke andar hi chakkar lagata rehta hai. Is gol bahav ke saath jo phosphorylation hoti hai, wahi cyclic photo-phosphorylation hai. Par chloroplast mein aisa kahan ho sakta hai, yeh samajhne ke liye pehle uske andar ka naksha yaad karo. Chloroplast mein thylakoid ki chapti thailiyan sikkon ki tarah ek ke upar ek rakhi hoti hain. Inke dher grana kehlate hain. Do dheron ko jodne wali patli, bina dher wali membrane bhi hoti hai, jaise do imaraton ke beech ka galiyara. Inhe hum pehle stroma lamellae ke naam se dekh chuke hain; lamella yaani patli parat. Grana ki membrane mein dono photosystem hote hain, PS I bhi aur PS II bhi. Par stroma lamellae ki membrane mein PS II nahi hota. Aur NADP reductase enzyme bhi nahi hota. NADP reductase woh enzyme hai jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banne ka kaam karata hai. Reductase yaani reduction karaane wala. Toh socho, stroma lamellae mein uchhle electron ko NADP⁺ tak pahunchane wala enzyme hi nahi hai. Isliye electron ke paas wapas lautne ke siva koi raasta nahi. Isliye stroma lamellae ko cyclic photo-phosphorylation ki ek sambhav jagah maana jaata hai. Nateeja pakka yaad rakho: gol raaste mein sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH + H⁺ nahi banta. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge. Par aisa nahi hai: gol raaste mein electron NADP⁺ tak jaata hi nahi, aur PS II ke bina paani bhi nahi tootta, isliye sirf ATP banta hai. Ek aur haalat hai. Yaad karo, roshni lehron mein chalti hai, aur ek lehar ki chot se agli chot tak ki doori wavelength hai. PS II ka centre P680 hai, jo lagbhag 680 nm ki roshni chahta hai. PS I ka P700, 700 nm tak ki roshni bhi pakad leta hai. Jab sirf 680 nm se aage ki roshni mile, toh PS II theek se nahi jaagta. Sirf PS I chalta hai, aur cyclic photo-phosphorylation hoti hai. Aksar log ise ulta samajh lete hain, par 680 nm se aage ka matlab hai 680 se zyada, yaani lambi wavelength, kam wavelength nahi. Paudhe ko yeh gol raasta kyon chahiye, iska ek bada kaaran hum aage Calvin cycle mein dekhenge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki kabhi paudhe ko NADPH se zyada ATP chahiye hota hai.
  - terms: NADP reductase = reduction karaane wala enzyme jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banwata hai
  - screen: Electron wapas PS I par; chloroplast ka naksha: grana sikkon ke dher jaise, unhe jodti stroma lamellae galiyare jaisi; grana ki membrane mein PS I aur PS II, stroma lamellae mein sirf PS I (PS II aur NADP reductase nahi); lautta electron; sirf ATP; 680 nm aur 700 nm ki lehrein, 680 se lambi lehar.
  - NCERT: p010-i12 When only PS I is functional, the electron is circulated within the photosystem 
**11.6.2-k6** · example · Ab ek electron ko pakad kar stroma lamellae mein uska poora safar chalate hain. Roshni P 700 par padi, aur uska ek electron energy lekar uchhal gaya. e⁻ acceptor ne use pakda, aur woh carriers ki kadi mein aage badha. Kadi ke aage use NADP⁺ tak le jaane wala NADP reductase mila hi nahi, kyunki yahan woh enzyme hai hi nahi. Toh electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa gaya, aur agli roshni ka intezaar karne laga. Is ek chakkar ke saath ATP bana. NADPH ek bhi nahi bana, aur O₂ bhi nahi nikli.
  - screen: Stroma lamellae mein ek electron ka safar, kadam-kadam ek hi tasveer par: P 700 par roshni, electron uchhla, e⁻ acceptor, kadi, NADP reductase ki khaali jagah, wapas P 700; ATP bana, NADPH aur O₂ nahi.
**11.6.2-k7** · ruko, socho · Ruko, pehle khud socho: agar kisi patti par sirf 700 nm wali roshni pade, toh usme NADPH banega ya nahi? Aapne socha hoga ki NADPH nahi banega, aur yahi sahi hai. Aisi roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, aur sirf ATP banega. Ab dono raaste ek saath rakh kar dekho. Seedhe raaste, yaani non-cyclic, mein electron paani se chalta hai: pehle PS II, phir PS I, aur aakhir NADP⁺. ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno bante hain. Gol raaste, yaani cyclic, mein sirf PS I kaam karta hai, aur electron wapas P 700 par aata hai. Sirf ATP banta hai; na NADPH, na O₂. Ab shuruaat ka sawaal phir se: NADP⁺ ka raasta band ho, toh electron kahan jaayega, aur kya kuch banega? Raasta band ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa jaata hai. Aur tab bhi kuch banta hai: ATP, bas NADPH nahi. Ab ek ajeeb baat par gaur karo, jo hamari agli film ka sawaal hai: dono raaston mein ATP bana, par electron ne kahin bhi ADP ko chhua tak nahi. Toh ATP bana kaise? ATP bana kaise, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Sawaal screen par; dono raaste saath-saath: non-cyclic (paani, PS II, PS I, NADP⁺; ATP, NADPH, O₂) aur cyclic (PS I se wapas P 700; sirf ATP); shuruaat ka sawaal phir screen par, NADP⁺ ka band raasta; electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700, ATP bana, NADPH nahi; aakhir mein electron aur ADP ke beech khaali jagah, sawaal khula.
  - jawab: Sirf 700 nm ki roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, sirf ATP banega, NADPH nahi.
  - aam galti: Yeh sochna ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge.

## 11.6.3 Chemiosmotic Hypothesis

_Maqsad: Electron transport ki energy seedhe ADP ko nahi milti, toh ATP banane tak kaise pahunchti hai?_

_Misaal: Baandh aur turbine: paani ke levels ka fark proton gradient hai, baandh thylakoid membrane hai, aur bahav se kaam karne wali turbine ATP synthase hai._

**11.6.3-k1** · samjhana · Humne dekha ki cyclic aur non-cyclic electron flow, dono se ATP ban sakta hai. Electron transport ki energy seedhe ADP ko nahi milti, toh ATP banane tak kaise pahunchti hai? Electron transport ki energy ATP banane tak H⁺ ka fark banaakar pahunchti hai; unka lautna ATP synthesis chalaata hai. Thylakoid ki band thaili yaad karo: andar lumen hai aur bahar stroma hai. H⁺ positive hydrogen ion hai; ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banane mein energy lagti hai. Ab ek dhalaan socho: ek taraf H⁺ zyada hain, doosri taraf kam hain. Isi zyada-se-kam ki dhalaan ko gradient kehte hain; iske saath particles apne-aap kam wali taraf jaa sakte hain. Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki concentration aur electrical haalat ke fark ko proton gradient kehte hain. Is fark ko electrochemical kehte hain: electro yaani charge ka fark; chemical yaani concentration ka fark. Ab ATP banne ko samjhaane ke liye ek vichaar rakho: H⁺ jama honge, phir unka lautna ATP banwaayega. Is tarah pehle rakhi gayi explanation ko hypothesis kehte hain; use experiments se jaanchte hain, sirf maan nahi lete. Hamaari is hypothesis mein membrane paar H⁺ ka jaana chemical kaam, yaani ATP banana, chalaata hai. Isi jod ka naam chemiosmotic hai: chemi yahaan chemical kaam batata hai, aur osmotic membrane ke paar movement. Isliye ATP banne ki is explanation ko chemiosmotic hypothesis kehte hain. Photosynthesis mein H⁺ thylakoid lumen mein jama hote hain, stroma mein nahi. Respiration mein bhi membrane ke aar-paar proton gradient ATP synthesis se juda hai. Respiration ka poora kaam aage dekhenge; abhi mitochondrion ki outer aur inner membrane socho. Inke beech ki jagah intermembrane space hai; inter yahaan beech ka matlab deta hai. Respiration ki electron transport system, ya ETS, chalne par H⁺ isi space mein jama hote hain. Yaad rakho, mitochondrial accumulation intermembrane space mein hai, matrix mein nahi.
  - terms: gradient = Dhalaan jaisa fark; ek taraf zyada aur doosri taraf kam.; proton gradient = Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki concentration aur electrical haalat ka fark.; electrochemical = Electro charge aur chemical concentration ke fark se juda.; chemiosmotic hypothesis = Proton gradient aur proton return se ATP synthesis samjhaane wala vichaar.; hypothesis = Pehle rakha explanation jise experiments se parkhte hain.; chemiosmotic = Chemi chemical kaam aur osmotic membrane ke paar movement se jodta hai.; intermembrane space = Mitochondrion ki outer aur inner membrane ke beech ki jagah.
  - screen: Chloroplast cutaway mein lumen aur stroma; mitochondrial cutaway mein outer aur inner membranes ke beech H⁺. Proton concentration aur charge ke fark alag dikhte hain.
  - NCERT: p010-i14 Let us now try and understand how actually ATP is synthesised in the chloroplast
  - kitaab se bahar: Mitochondrial double-membrane arrangement ka zaroori base yahaan diya hai; naam ke chemi aur osmotic hisse samajhne ke liye khole hain.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k2** · analogy · Aap ek baandh socho: ek taraf paani jama hai, doosri taraf level neecha hai. Paani upar jama karne mein energy lagi; ab use turbine ke raaste neeche behne do. Us bahav se turbine kaam kar sakti hai; jama energy ab kaam mein aa rahi hai. Yahaan baandh thylakoid membrane hai, paani ke levels ka fark proton gradient hai. Turbine ki jagah ATP banane wala enzyme socho; iska naam ATP synthase hai. Synthase ka matlab kisi cheez ko banaane wala enzyme hai; yahaan banne wali cheez ATP hai. Ab hum pehle H⁺ jama hona, phir unke lautne se ATP banna samjhenge.
  - terms: synthase = Kisi substance ko banane wala enzyme.
  - screen: Baandh aur turbine ke saath thylakoid membrane, proton gradient aur ATP synthase ki tulna.
  - kitaab se bahar: Baandh aur turbine ki analogy energy jama hone aur usse kaam lene ko jodti hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: Baandh membrane, water-level difference gradient aur turbine ATP synthase hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: H⁺ gravity se nahi, electrochemical gradient ki disha mein behte hain.
**11.6.3-k3** · pehle poori jhalak · Yaad karo, light electron ko excited karti hai; carriers use aage le jaate hain. Isi excited hone ko yahaan activation of electrons kehte hain. Ab hum isi safar mein gradient banane wale teen kaam dhoondhenge. Pehla, water splitting lumen mein H⁺ deti hai. Doosra, carrier stroma se H⁺ lumen mein le jaata hai. Teesra, NADPH banne mein stroma ke H⁺ istemal hote hain. Phir dekhenge ki H⁺ ka ATP synthase se lautna kya kaam karta hai.
  - terms: activation of electrons = Light ki energy se electron ka excited hona.
  - screen: Wahi thylakoid cutaway; teen numbered jagah par water splitting, carrier transport aur stromal NADP⁺ reduction.
  - NCERT: p010-i15 Let us understand what causes the proton gradient across the membrane. We need t
**11.6.3-k4** · samjhana · Water-splitting complex ki jagah yaad karo: PS II se juda, lumen ki taraf khulta hai. Do waters ka hisaab tha: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. Protons or hydrogen ions, dono yahaan H⁺ ke naam hain. Aap machinery ka face dekho; kya in H⁺ ko pehle stroma se membrane paar karni padegi? In H⁺ ko stroma se membrane paar nahi karni padegi, kyunki water splitting lumen-facing side par hoti hai. Reaction inner side par hoti hai, isliye H⁺ seedhe lumen mein milte hain. Baandh wali tasveer mein pehla kaam mila: jama hone wali taraf H⁺ badh gaye.
  - screen: Lumen-facing water splitting par do H₂O se chaar H⁺ lumen mein judte hain; electron aur oxygen arrows shaant rehte hain.
  - NCERT: p010-i16 (a) Since splitting of the water molecule takes place on the inner side of the m
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k5** · samjhana · Reaction centre ka electron pehle primary acceptor leta hai, phir carriers use aage dete hain. Ab aisa carrier socho jo electron ke saath hydrogen ka hissa bhi le jaaye. Isi ko H carrier kehte hain; H hydrogen ka sanket hai. Outer-side primary acceptor se electron is H carrier tak pahunchta hai. Electron lete waqt carrier stroma se H⁺ bhi leta hai. Ab aap inner side dekho: electron agle carrier ko mila, H⁺ lumen mein chhoot gaya. Water splitting ne H⁺ andar diye the; yahaan stroma ke H⁺ bhi kaise ghat gaye? Yahaan stroma ke H⁺ isliye ghate kyunki carrier unhe lekar lumen mein chhod aaya. Isi stroma-se-lumen movement ke kaaran bahar H⁺ kam aur andar zyada hote hain. Electron aur H⁺ ke safar jude hain, lekin dono ka raasta ek nahi hai.
  - terms: H carrier = Electron ke saath hydrogen ka hissa le jaane wala carrier.
  - screen: Wahi membrane picture mein carrier stromal H⁺ leta aur lumen mein chhodta hai; electron aur proton arrows alag rang mein.
  - NCERT: p011-i20 (b) As electrons move through the photosystems, protons are transported across t
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k6** · samjhana · Yaad karo, PS I ke acceptor se aaye electrons aakhir NADP⁺ ko milte hain. NADP⁺ electrons leta hai; isi electron lene ko reduction kehte hain. NADP reductase yeh badlav karaata hai; woh electrons ka mool source nahi hai. Yeh enzyme stroma ki taraf kaam karta hai; isi face ko stroma side kehte hain. Aap pehle ka hisaab dekho: NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH. NADPH banne mein yeh H⁺ kahaan se aata hai? NADPH banne mein yeh H⁺ stroma se aata hai, isliye wahaan bache H⁺ aur ghatte hain. Aksar lagta hai ki teesra kaam bhi H⁺ ko lumen bhejta hai, par aisa nahi hai. Yahaan H⁺ stromal reaction mein istemal hua; isi se dono taraf ka fark badha.
  - terms: stroma side = Thylakoid membrane ka stroma ki taraf wala face.
  - screen: PS I se stromal NADP reductase tak electrons; NADPH formation mein stromal H⁺ istemal hota hai, lumen ki taraf proton arrow nahi.
  - NCERT: p011-i21 (c) The NADP reductase enzyme is located on the stroma side of the membrane. Alo
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k7** · example · Ab aap do water molecules wala example teenon kaamon se jodo. Water splitting ke chaar H⁺ lumen mein mile; carrier ne aur H⁺ andar pahunchaye. Saath hi NADPH banne mein stroma ke H⁺ istemal hue. Isliye lumen mein H⁺ zyada aur stroma mein kam hote gaye. Yeh fark thylakoid membrane ke aar-paar hai, chloroplast ki baahari boundary par nahi. Ab zyada H⁺ wale ghol ko socho: uski acidity badh jaati hai. Acidity bataane wale scale ko pH kehte hain; H⁺ ki concentration badhe toh pH ghatta hai. Isliye lumen ka pH ghatta hai, aur is kami ko naapa ja sakta hai.
  - terms: pH = Acidity ka scale; H⁺ badhne par pH ghatta hai.
  - screen: Lumen mein water splitting se +4 H⁺ aur transported H⁺; stroma mein transport aur NADPH formation se H⁺ kam. Lumen pH neeche jaata hai.
  - NCERT: p011-i22 Hence, within the chloroplast, protons in the stroma decrease in number, while i
  - kahan tootti hai: High H⁺ ka matlab high pH nahi; lumen ki acidity badhti aur pH ghatta hai.
**11.6.3-k8** · ruko, socho · Ab lumen mein H⁺ jama hain, lekin ADP mein phosphate judna abhi samjhaana baaki hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: proton gradient mein hamaari itni dilchaspi kyun hai? Proton gradient mein dilchaspi isliye hai kyunki H⁺ ka sahi raaste lautna ATP synthesis ko energy deta hai. Aap baandh se turbine ki taraf bahav yaad karo; sirf paani jama karna kaafi nahi tha. Ab hum proton return ka woh protein raasta kholenge.
  - screen: High-lumen aur low-stroma H⁺; ATP synthase ki jagah lumen-to-stroma return ka adhura raasta.
  - NCERT: p011-i23 Why are we so interested in the proton gradient?
  - jawab: H⁺ ka ATP synthase se lautna gradient ki energy ko ATP formation se jodta hai.
  - aam galti: Sirf H⁺ jama hone se ATP nahi banta; ATP synthase aur proton return bhi chahiye.
**11.6.3-k9** · samjhana · Ab aap membrane mein laga proton return ka raasta dekho. ADP aur inorganic phosphate jodkar ATP banane wala enzyme ATP synthase hai. Synthase ka banaane wala matlab hum turbine ki tasveer mein khol chuke hain. Is enzyme ke do hisse hain: membrane mein dhansa CF₀ aur stroma ki taraf nikla CF₁. CF₀ proton channel hai; CF₁ membrane ki outer stromal surface par nikla head hai. Channel membrane ke aar-paar hai; aise aar-paar faile hone ko transmembrane kehte hain. Ab particles ka apne-aap zyada concentration se kam ki taraf phailna socho. Isi phailne ko diffusion kehte hain; yahaan H⁺ electrochemical gradient ki disha mein chalte hain. Protein ka raasta milne par aise paar jaane ko facilitated diffusion kehte hain. Facilitated ka matlab yahaan raasta milne se aasaan hua hai. H⁺ CF₀ se lumen se stroma lautte hain, kyunki unka electrochemical gradient isi disha mein hai. Lautne se dono taraf ka fark ghatta hai; isi ko breakdown of this gradient kehte hain. Aksar lagta hai ki protons toot gaye, par asal mein unki dono taraf ki asamaanta ghati hai. Is fark mein jama energy proton return par milti hai, isliye ATP synthesis chal sakti hai.
  - terms: ATP synthase = Proton flow ki energy se ADP aur phosphate jodkar ATP banane wala enzyme.; CF₀ = Membrane mein dhansa proton channel.; CF₁ = ATP synthase ka stroma ki taraf nikla head.; transmembrane = Membrane ke aar-paar faila hua.; diffusion = Particles ka apne-aap zyada concentration se kam ki taraf phailna.; facilitated diffusion = Protein ke raaste apne gradient ki disha mein paar jaana.; breakdown of this gradient = Proton return se dono taraf ka fark kam hona.
  - screen: Ussi thylakoid ka enlargement: membrane mein CF₀, stroma mein CF₁; lumen → CF₀ → stroma proton arrow aur ghatta gradient.
  - NCERT: p011-i24 This gradient is important because it is the breakdown of this gradient that lea; p012-i1 channel of the CF₀ of the ATP synthase. The ATP synthase enzyme consists of two 
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k10** · samjhana · Aapne CF₀ ka raasta aur CF₁ ka stromal head dekh liya. Par H⁺ ka bahav ADP aur phosphate jodne ka kaam kaise chalaata hai? H⁺ ka bahav yeh kaam protein ki banawat badalne ki energy dekar chalaata hai. Protein ki teen dishaon mein phaili banawat uski conformation hai. Conformational ka matlab isi banawat se juda hai; iske badalne ko conformational change kehte hain. Gradient breakdown ki energy CF₁ mein yahi badlav karaati hai. Isse enzyme ATP ke kai molecules banaata hai; kai ke liye yahaan several kaha gaya hai. In ATP molecules mein kaam ke liye energy hoti hai, isliye inhe energy-packed keh rahe hain. Aap yahaan ek H⁺ se ek ATP ka fixed anupaat mat nikaalna.
  - terms: conformational = Protein ki banawat, ya conformation, se juda.; conformational change = Protein ki teen-dimensional banawat mein badlav.
  - screen: Ussi CF₀–CF₁ enlargement mein proton return ke saath CF₁ ki banawat badalti hai; ADP + Pᵢ se kai ATP bante hain.
  - NCERT: p012-i2 The break down of the gradient provides enough energy to cause a conformational 
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k11** · poori kahani dobara · Ab hum baandh aur turbine ka poora sambandh biological naam se jodte hain. Pehle proton gradient banao, phir ATP synthase se H⁺ lautaakar ATP banao; yahi chemiosmosis hai. Chemiosmosis ko chaar cheezein chahiye: membrane, proton pump, proton gradient aur ATP synthase. Energy lagaakar H⁺ membrane ke paar jama karne wali machinery proton pump hai. Thylakoid lumen wahi andar ki jagah hai, jahaan humne high H⁺ concentration dekhi. Pehle pumping mein energy lagti hai; waapas gradient ki disha mein diffusion se energy milti hai. ATP synthase channel waapasi ka raasta deta hai; enzyme mili energy se ATP formation karaata hai. Aap order jodo: gradient bana, CF₀ se H⁺ laute, CF₁ ki banawat badli, ATP bana. Electron seedhe ADP se judkar ATP nahi bana raha.
  - terms: chemiosmosis = Proton gradient aur ATP synthase se proton return ko ATP formation se jodna.; proton pump = Energy lagaakar H⁺ membrane ke paar jama karne wali machinery.
  - screen: Wahi thylakoid picture par chaar requirements; pehle inward pumping, phir CF₀ se outward return aur CF₁ par ATP formation.
  - NCERT: p012-i3 Chemiosmosis requires a membrane, a proton pump, a proton gradient and ATP synth
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k12** · figure · Ab aap Figure 11.7 par ungli rakhkar poori kahani jodo. Thylakoid membrane band boundary hai; ismein photosystems, carriers aur ATP synthase lage hain. Bahar Stroma (low H⁺), ya Stroma, hai; andar Lumen (high H⁺) mein H⁺ zyada hain. Pehle electron route dekho: Light ke arrows P680, PS II aur P700, PS I tak pahunchte hain. PS ka matlab photosystem hai; P680 aur P700 unke reaction centres hain. PS II ke paas H₂O se Oxidation of water hoti hai; ½O₂ + H⁺ uske products dikhata hai. ½O₂ ek water ka proportional oxygen hisaab hai; alag aadha oxygen molecule nahi tair raha. Water ke electrons PS II ko milte hain; ab aap arrow PQ tak dekho. PQ ka naam Plastoquinone hai; electrons aur do H⁺ lekar iska reduced form PQH₂ banta hai. Agla Cytochrome B6f membrane complex hai; B6f iski pehchaan, aur proton arrow lumen ki taraf hai. PC, ya Plastocyanin, electron ko B6f se P700, PS I tak pahunchata hai. Yahaan light doosri excitation deti hai; phir stromal side par Fd electron leta hai. Fd ferredoxin hai; isse electron FNR, ya ferredoxin–NADP⁺ reductase, tak pahunchta hai. FNR enzyme NADP⁺ reduction karaata hai; NADP⁺ + H⁺ aur NADPH labels starting substances aur product jodte hain. Ab aap H⁺ arrows alag padho: carrier stroma se leta, lumen mein chhodta hai. Water oxidation bhi lumen H⁺ badhaati hai; NADPH formation stromal H⁺ ghataati hai. Repeated H⁺ labels protons hain; green return arrow unhe ATP synthase se stroma laata hai. High Electrochemical Potential Gradient se Low ki wedge lumen-to-stroma disha dikhati hai. Is electrochemical fark mein charge aur concentration, dono ke asar yaad rakho. Return arrow membrane ke CF₀ channel se guzarta hai; CF₁, ATP synthase ka head, stroma mein hai. Head ke paas ADP + Pᵢ → ATP hai; ADP adenosine diphosphate, Pᵢ inorganic phosphate hai. ATP adenosine triphosphate hai; aap electron route NADPH tak aur proton return stroma tak alag rakho.
  - terms: PQ / Plastoquinone = Membrane carrier jo electrons aur stromal H⁺ leta hai.; PQH₂ = Plastoquinone ka electrons aur do H⁺ liya hua reduced form.; Cytochrome B6f = PQ aur PC ke beech electron transfer aur proton movement se juda membrane complex.; PC / Plastocyanin = B6f se PS I tak electrons pahunchane wala carrier.; Fd / Ferredoxin = PS I se FNR tak electrons pahunchane wala carrier.; FNR = Ferredoxin–NADP⁺ reductase; NADP⁺ reduction karaane wala enzyme.
  - screen: Pehle bana wahi thylakoid cutaway ab Figure 11.7 ke labels ke saath; electron route, proton additions aur CF₀ return alag-alag ubharte hain.
  - NCERT: p011-i1 A closed thylakoid membrane encloses a lumen with high H⁺; the surrounding strom
  - kitaab se bahar: Fd aur FNR ke full names aur PQH₂ ka reduced form hona khola gaya hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: ½O₂ proportional notation hai, released aadha molecule nahi.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.6.3-k13** · samjhana · Ab aap membrane par bana ATP aur electron movement se bana NADPH ek saath dekho. ATP energy deta hai; NADPH naye organic compounds banane ke liye electrons deta hai. Dono taiyaar hain, lekin ab inka kaam chloroplast ke kis hisse mein hoga? Inka kaam surrounding stroma mein hoga, jahaan sugar banane ki reactions hoti hain. Sugar banane ko energy aur electrons chahiye, isliye ATP aur NADPH dono turant in reactions mein lagte hain. Naye jeev-padarth banane ke is kaam ko biosynthetic kehte hain; aage hum iske steps kholenge. In reactions mein CO₂ ka carbon organic molecule mein judta hai; isi ko fixing CO₂ kehte hain. Is CO₂ fixation ka poora raasta aage dekhenge; abhi ise stroma mein sugar banne se jodo. Shuruaat ka jod mil gaya: electron transport H⁺ ka fark banaata hai, ATP synthase uski energy kaam mein leta hai.
  - terms: biosynthetic = Naye organic jeev-padarth banane se juda.; fixing CO₂ = CO₂ ke carbon ko organic molecule ka hissa banana.
  - screen: Wahi thylakoid se ATP aur NADPH surrounding stroma ki sugar-forming reactions tak; CO₂ carbon organic molecule mein judta hai.
  - NCERT: p012-i4 Along with the NADPH produced by the movement of electrons, the ATP will be used

_→ Ab ATP ki energy aur NADPH ke electrons taiyaar hain; agle heading mein dekhenge ki stroma inhe sugar banane mein kahaan lagaata hai._

## 11.7 WHERE ARE THE ATP AND NADPH USED?

_Maqsad: Ab ATP aur NADPH ki taiyaar supply ko sugar synthesis se jodenge, aur jaanenge ki fixed carbon ka pehla pehchaana product kya hai._

**11.7-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab ATP aur NADPH ki taiyaar supply ko sugar synthesis se jodenge, aur jaanenge ki fixed carbon ka pehla pehchaana product kya hai. Stromal reactions ko ATP ki energy aur NADPH ki reducing power milti hai, jaisa hum abhi jod chuke hain. Light reaction ke teen products ki destinations alag karo: O₂ chloroplast se diffuse karke bahar jaati hai, jabki ATP aur NADPH sugars banane ke kaam aate hain. Kya released oxygen bhi sugar synthesis ki energy supply hai? Dekho, energy aur reducing-power supplies ATP aur NADPH hain; O₂ ka outward diffusion unse alag destination hai. Yahaan do sawaal follow karenge: sugar synthesis light ki supply par kaise depend karti hai, aur CO₂ ka carbon sabse pehle kis product mein milta hai?
  - screen: Realistic chloroplast cutaway: thylakoids se ATP aur NADPH ki arrows surrounding stroma mein; O₂ ki separate arrow chloroplast boundary ke bahar. Saath do question labels: light dependence aur first fixation product.
  - NCERT: p012-i6 We learnt that the products of light reaction are ATP, NADPH and O₂. Of these O₂
**11.7-k2** · example · Sugar banane wala yeh hissa biosynthetic phase hai; bio jeevan se aur synthesis naye substances banane se juda hai. Is phase ko ATP aur NADPH ke alawa CO₂ aur H₂O chahiye; water ka formula hum pehle samajh chuke hain. Indirect light dependence ko light-on → light-off → light-on comparison se samjho. Light band karte hi sugar synthesis turant kyun nahi rukti? Dekho, pehle se available ATP aur NADPH kuch der reactions support karte hain, lekin unki renewed supply na milne par synthesis ruk jaati hai. Light wapas milne par light reactions supplies dobara banati hain aur sugar synthesis resume hoti hai. Yeh sequence dikhata hai ki biosynthetic phase ko light seedhe nahi chalati, lekin light-reaction products ki availability usse jodkar rakhti hai.
  - terms: biosynthetic phase = Photosynthesis ka organic substances, khaaskar sugars, banane wala hissa.
  - screen: Usi chloroplast ke neeche three-part timeline: light on par supplies aur sugar formation; light off par remaining ATP/NADPH ke saath brief continuation, phir stop; light return par renewed supplies aur restart.
  - NCERT: p012-i7 This is the biosynthetic phase of photosynthesis. This process does not directly
**11.7-k3** · analogy · Socho, bakery ko roz ingredients aur charged battery packs ki delivery milti hai. Delivery van ruk jaaye, toh counter par rakhe ingredients aur battery packs se kuch aur batches ban sakte hain. Kya van rukte hi ovens ka kaam usi second band hona zaroori hai? Dekho, bacha stock kuch der kaam chalata hai, lekin stock khatam hone par fresh delivery ke bina production rukega. Yahaan bakery stromal biosynthesis hai, delivery light reactions ki supply hai, aur bacha stock available ATP aur NADPH hai. Light lautne se supply renew hona van ki delivery dobara shuru hone jaisa hai.
  - screen: Bakery mein limited ingredient trays aur charged packs se do remaining batches; neeche matching chloroplast timeline mein available ATP/NADPH ghatte hue, phir supply restart.
  - kitaab se bahar: Limited-stock bakery analogy indirect dependence samjhane ke liye added hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: Bakery = stromal biosynthesis; delivery = light-reaction supply; remaining stock = available ATP aur NADPH; delivery restart = illumination se renewed supply.
  - kahan tootti hai: ATP aur NADPH ko long-term stored reserves ya bakery ingredients ke chemically identical equivalents na samjhein.
**11.7-k4** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: kya biosynthetic phase ko dark reaction kehna misleading naam, ya misnomer, ho sakta hai? Light-off timeline ko reason banao; kya darkness ne synthesis ko start kiya tha? Dekho, darkness is phase ki requirement nahi hai, aur light-reaction supplies khatam hone par yeh rukta hai, isliye dark naam night-only process ka galat impression deta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reactions din mein aur dark reactions sirf raat mein chalti hain, par asal mein yeh directly aur indirectly light-dependent kaamon ka distinction hai.
  - screen: Light-off timeline par brief continuation aur later stop highlighted; uske saamne label: directly light-driven nahi, light-reaction products par dependent.
  - NCERT: p012-i8 Can we, hence, say that calling the biosynthetic phase as the dark reaction is a
  - jawab: Dark naam misleading hai kyunki darkness required nahi; ATP aur NADPH ki renewed supply ke liye biosynthesis indirectly light reactions par depend karti hai.
  - aam galti: Brief continuation ko unlimited darkness mein synthesis ka proof na samjhein; remaining supplies limited hain.
**11.7-k5** · samjhana · Ab ATP aur NADPH ka detailed use samajhne ke liye overall sugar formation ke andar carbon ka raasta dhoondhenge. Humne CO₂ aur H₂O se carbohydrate formation ka overall account dekha tha; ab wahi carbohydrate composition (CH₂O)ₙ se likh sakte hain. Is notation mein n batata hai ki CH₂O ka composition ratio kitni baar liya gaya; glucose ke liye n six hai. Kya overall equation bata deti hai ki incoming carbon sabse pehle kis compound mein gaya? Dekho, overall equation manzil batati hai, isliye scientists ne first product, yaani CO₂ fix hone ke baad sabse pehle pehchaane jaane wale compound, ki khoj ki. Fixation ka pehle khola meaning yaad rakho: CO₂ ke carbon ko organic molecule ka hissa banana.
  - terms: (CH₂O)ₙ = Carbohydrate composition ka general notation; n CH₂O ratio ki repetitions batata hai.; first product = CO₂ fixation ke baad shuru mein pehchaana gaya reaction product.
  - screen: Stroma mein CO₂ + H₂O se (CH₂O)ₙ ki overall arrow; arrow ke beech pehla unknown product-box khulta hai. Side par (CH₂O)₆ → C₆H₁₂O₆.
  - NCERT: p012-i9 Let us now see how the ATP and NADPH are used in the biosynthetic phase. We saw 
**11.7-k6** · kahani · Calvin aur Bassham ki labelled carbon wali khoj hum pehle mil chuke hain; ab us investigation ka specific result dekho. World War II ke baad radioactive isotopes, ya radioisotopes, ka beneficial use karte hue Calvin ne algae mein photosynthesis investigate ki. Pehle khola radioactive ¹⁴C yahaan carbon ki pehchaan tha; early products mein uska signal incoming carbon ka destination batata tha. Un studies mein pehla identified CO₂ fixation product three-carbon organic acid, 3-phosphoglyceric acid, mila; iska short name PGA hai. Final sugar se pehle acid milna important kyun tha? Dekho, isse pata chala ki fixed carbon seedha final glucose ke roop mein identify nahi hua, balki beech ke product PGA mein mila. Calvin aur co-workers ne complete biosynthetic pathway bhi work out kiya; unke naam par ise Calvin cycle kaha gaya.
  - terms: radioisotopes = Elements ke radioactive isotopes, jinhe detectable tracing labels ki tarah use kar sakte hain.; ¹⁴C = Carbon ka radioactive isotope jiska mass number 14 hai; pehle C¹⁴ notation mein pehchaana tha.; PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid; teen carbon wala organic acid aur identified early fixation product.; Calvin cycle = Calvin ke naam par jaana jaane wala photosynthetic carbon-assimilation ka biosynthetic pathway.
  - screen: Algal culture ko ¹⁴CO₂ input; early separated product mein labelled PGA highlighted. PGA ke teen carbon positions aur neeche pathway name Calvin cycle; abhi detailed cycle steps nahi.
  - NCERT: p012-i10 Just after world war II, among the several efforts to put radioisotopes to benef
**11.7-k7** · sawaal · Ab batao, PGA ke ek molecule mein kitne carbon atoms hain? Dekho, PGA mein teen carbon atoms hain; tracer ka 14 carbon isotope ka mass number hai, PGA ke carbon atoms ki count nahi. Ek PGA molecule mein labelled carbon milne se uska poora skeleton fourteen-carbon wala nahi ban jaata.
  - screen: Ek PGA carbon skeleton par teen numbered carbon positions; ek position par ¹⁴C tag. Separate labels: molecule carbon count = 3; tracer mass number = 14.
  - NCERT: p012-i11 How many carbon atoms does it have?
  - kitaab se bahar: Tracer mass number aur product carbon-count ka explicit distinction added hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: PGA ke ek molecule mein teen carbon atoms hote hain.
  - kahan tootti hai: ¹⁴C ka 14 isotope mass number hai, molecule mein carbon atoms ki sankhya nahi.
**11.7-k8** · samjhana · Agla sawaal tha: kya har plant mein initial CO₂ fixation ka pehla stable product PGA hi hota hai? Wide plant surveys mein doosra group mila, jiska first stable product four-carbon organic acid oxaloacetic acid tha. First stable product fixation ke baad pehla stable compound hai, sirf overall final sugar nahi. CO₂ fixation ka meaning wahi organic molecule mein carbon jodna hai; CO₂ assimilation ka broader meaning us carbon ko plant ke organic substances mein incorporate karna hai. Jin plants ka initial stable product three-carbon PGA hai, unka initial route C₃ pathway kehlaata hai. Jinmein four-carbon oxaloacetic acid pehla stable product hai, unka initial route C₄ pathway kehlaata hai. In names ke three aur four kya ginte hain? Dekho, yeh first stable product ke carbon atoms ginte hain, incoming CO₂ molecules ya carbon lene wale starting molecule ke carbons nahi; associated differences aage dekhenge.
  - terms: CO₂ fixation = CO₂ ke carbon ko organic molecule mein jodna; pehle fixing CO₂ ke roop mein samjha tha.; CO₂ assimilation = CO₂ ke carbon ko plant ke organic substances ka hissa banana.; oxaloacetic acid or OAA = Four-carbon organic acid; C₄ route ka initial first stable fixation product.; first stable product = Initial fixation ke baad milne wala pehla stable compound.
  - screen: Survey ka simple two-branch comparison: C₃ → PGA → 3 carbon positions; C₄ → oxaloacetic acid → 4 carbon positions. Column heading: initial first stable fixation product.
  - NCERT: p013-i1 Scientists also tried to know whether all plants have PGA as the first product o
  - kahan tootti hai: First stable product ko carbon receive karne wale starting molecule se confuse na karein.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.7-k9** · poori kahani dobara · Ab poora connection jodo: light reactions ki O₂ bahar diffuse hoti hai, aur ATP tatha NADPH stromal sugar synthesis ko support karte hain. Light band hone par remaining supplies se brief continuation milti hai, phir stop; light lautne par renewed supply se synthesis resume hoti hai. Carbon ka raasta dhoondhne par algal tracer studies mein three-carbon PGA pehla identified product mila. Wider surveys ne four-carbon oxaloacetic acid wala initial route bhi dikhaya, isliye first stable product se C₃ aur C₄ classification bani. Lekin incoming CO₂ ko sabse pehle receive kaun karta hai? Dekho, product ki identity mil gayi hai; use banane wale primary CO₂ acceptor ki identity agle heading mein dekhenge.
  - screen: Teen connected compact panels: light supplies → stromal synthesis; on/off/on response; PGA–3C versus oxaloacetic acid–4C. Last panel se agle sawaal ki taraf arrow: CO₂ ko receive kaun karta hai?

_→ Pehla product PGA pehchaan liya; ab primary CO₂ acceptor khojkar samjhenge ki incoming carbon us product tak kaise pahunchta hai._

## 11.7.1 The Primary Acceptor of CO₂

_Maqsad: Ab PGA ki pehchaan se aage badhkar CO₂ lene wala starting molecule khojenge, aur uske carbon count ko product ke carbon count se jodenge._

**11.7.1-k1** · sawaal · Ab PGA ki pehchaan se aage badhkar CO₂ lene wala starting molecule khojenge, aur uske carbon count ko product ke carbon count se jodenge. Yahaan incoming CO₂ jis starting molecule se judta hai, use khojna hai; electron receive karne wala primary acceptor isse alag hai. PGA mein teen carbon atoms hain, jaisa hum abhi pehchaan chuke hain. Scientists ka sawaal bhi yahi tha: ek-carbon CO₂ judne ke baad three-carbon PGA mile, toh starting molecule mein kitne carbons honge? Teen mein se ek ghataakar two-carbon starting molecule sochna natural hai, lekin kya product ke sirf ek molecule ko ginana kaafi hai? Dekho, starting molecule ka carbon count nikaalne ke liye product molecules ki total count bhi chahiye, isliye sirf subtraction se identity decide nahi hogi.
  - screen: Chloroplast ke stromal region par CO₂ ka one-carbon model aur ek three-carbon PGA model; starting molecule ki jagah question mark, saath product molecules ki count ke liye khaali boxes.
  - NCERT: p013-i3 Let us now ask ourselves a question that was asked by the scientists who were st
  - kya kis jaisa: Ek PGA ke three carbons se starting molecule ka size decide nahi hota; total product molecules bhi ginne honge.
  - kahan tootti hai: 3 − 1 = 2 tabhi valid inference hota jab ek starting molecule aur ek CO₂ se sirf ek PGA banta.
**11.7.1-k2** · samjhana · Experiments ka unexpected answer five-carbon ketose sugar ribulose bisphosphate, ya RuBP, tha. Incoming CO₂ isi starting sugar molecule se judta hai. RuBP mein five-carbon skeleton aur do alag positions par phosphate groups hote hain; bisphosphate naam in do phosphate groups ko batata hai. Ketose sugar ka matlab aisa sugar hai jiski open-chain form mein ketone group hota hai. Ketone group mein ek carbon oxygen se double bond banata hai aur dono taraf carbon se juda hota hai. Five-carbon starting molecule se three-carbon product kaise milega? Dekho, carbon conservation sahi rehti hai; humein reaction ke saare product molecules ka hisaab jodna hoga.
  - terms: ribulose bisphosphate (RuBP) = Five-carbon sugar jiske do alag positions par phosphate groups lage hote hain; incoming CO₂ isse judta hai.; ketose sugar = Sugar jiski open-chain form mein ketone group hota hai.
  - screen: Usi stromal picture mein RuBP ka five-carbon molecular model; terminal positions par do phosphate tags aur open-chain structure mein internal C=O highlight.
  - NCERT: p013-i4 The studies very unexpectedly showed that the acceptor molecule was a 5-carbon k
  - kitaab se bahar: Ketone group ki basic bonding aur bisphosphate naam ke parts ki explanation clarity ke liye jodi gayi hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.7.1-k3** · kahani · Agar tumne two-carbon acceptor guess kiya, toh ghabraao mat; scientists ne bhi pehle yahi possibility sochi thi. Unhone many years ek 2-carbon compound, yaani do carbon atoms wale compound, ko acceptor ke roop mein dhoondhne mein lagaaye. Bahut experiments ke baad five-carbon RuBP ki identity mili. Galat guess itna convincing kyun laga? Aksar log sochte hain ki three-carbon PGA milne par starting acceptor two-carbon hi hoga, par asal mein ek product molecule ka size poore reaction ka carbon total nahi hota. Dekho, saare product molecules ginne par five-carbon RuBP ka hisaab milta hai.
  - terms: 2-carbon compound = Aisa compound jiske ek molecule mein do carbon atoms hon.
  - screen: Research timeline: three-carbon PGA se two-carbon acceptor ka initial hypothesis; multiple experimental observations ke baad five-carbon RuBP ki confirmed identity.
  - NCERT: p013-i5 Did any of you think of this possibility? Do not worry; the scientists also took
**11.7.1-k4** · example · Ab ek RuBP molecule ke five carbon counters lo, aur incoming CO₂ ka ek carbon counter unke saath jodo. Balanced equations mein atoms conserve hote hain, jaisa hum overall photosynthesis equation mein dekh chuke hain. Total six carbons ko do three-carbon groups mein baanto; har group ek PGA molecule ka carbon skeleton dikhata hai. Carbon ledger hua: 5 + 1 = 3 + 3. Kya six-carbon total ka matlab ek stable six-carbon product hi milega? Dekho, total carbon count six hai, lekin stable products do PGA molecules hain; ek molecule ka size aur saare products ka total alag quantities hain.
  - screen: Pehle wale RuBP model ke five carbon counters mein ek labelled CO₂ carbon judta hai; wahi six counters do PGA groups mein bantte hain, neeche 5 + 1 = 3 + 3.
  - kitaab se bahar: Do PGA molecules ka carbon ledger agle heading ki reaction ko samajhne ke liye yahaan briefly khola gaya hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.7.1-k5** · analogy · Socho, packing table par five toy blocks pehle se rakhe hain aur courier ek aur block laata hai. Worker six blocks ko do parcels mein pack karta hai, har parcel mein three blocks. Tum sirf ek parcel kholkar bolo ki three blocks mein se one naya tha, isliye table par pehle sirf two blocks honge. Yeh conclusion kyun galat hoga? Dekho, doosre parcel ke three blocks bhi same starting collection se aaye hain, isliye poori shipment ginne par starting five blocks ka hisaab milta hai. Yahaan starting five blocks RuBP ke carbons hain, naya block CO₂ ka carbon hai, aur two parcels do PGA molecules hain.
  - screen: Packing table ke five toy blocks, ek arriving block, phir do three-block parcels; unke neeche corresponding RuBP, CO₂ aur two PGA carbon groups.
  - kitaab se bahar: Parcel analogy carbon conservation aur product count ka distinction samjhaati hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: Five starting blocks = RuBP ke five carbons; incoming block = CO₂ ka carbon; two three-block parcels = do PGA molecules.
  - kahan tootti hai: Toy blocks unchanged objects hain; actual reaction mein atoms ke chemical bonds rearrange hote hain.
**11.7.1-k6** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: two RuBP molecules two CO₂ molecules accept karein, toh kitne PGA molecules ke liye carbon available hoga? Pehle starting aur incoming carbons ka total nikaalo, phir three-carbon groups gino. Dekho, two RuBP ke ten carbons aur two CO₂ ke two carbons milkar twelve hote hain, isliye four three-carbon PGA molecules ka carbon account banta hai. Isse acceptor aur first stable product ka farq clear rakho: acceptor RuBP five-carbon hai, jabki product PGA three-carbon hai.
  - screen: Do RuBP carbon groups, do incoming CO₂ carbons, aur learner ke liye khaali PGA boxes; phir twelve counters four three-carbon groups mein.
  - jawab: 2 × 5 + 2 × 1 = 12 carbons; 12 ÷ 3 = 4 PGA molecules.
  - aam galti: Two CO₂ se sirf two PGA ginne par six carbons missing ho jaate hain; har RuBP acceptance se do PGA ka account banta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10

_→ Ab acceptor aur products ka carbon hisaab mil gaya; Calvin cycle mein dekhenge ki reaction kaise chalti hai aur RuBP dobara kaise taiyaar hota hai._

## 11.7.2 The Calvin Cycle

_Maqsad: Har CO₂ ko pakadne mein ek RuBP kharch ho jaata hai; toh patti din bhar CO₂ kaise pakadti rehti hai, uska RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?_

_Misaal: dukaan ki poonji: RuBP poonji ki thaili (paanch carbon = paanch rupaye), har CO₂ ka carbon ek naya rupaya, triose phosphate teen rupaye ka hissa, ATP aur NADPH bijli-mazdoori ka kharcha, bahar nikli sugar kamaai_

Kitab ki baatein (57):
- f1 · kitab kehti hai · Calvin aur unke saathiyon ne poora raasta khoja aur dikhaya ki yeh raasta cycle ki tarah, yaani gol chakkar mein, chalta hai, aur RuBP dobara banta hai (regenerated).
- f2 · shabd ka matlab · regenerated · Regenerated: use hone ke baad phir se banaya gaya; re = phir se, generate = banana.
- f3 · pehle ki zaroori baat · RuBP paanch carbon wala sugar hai jo CO₂ ko pakadta hai, yaani CO₂ acceptor; CO₂ mein ek hi carbon atom hai; RuBP + CO₂ se teen-teen carbon ke do PGA bante hain (5 + 1 = 3 + 3).
- f4 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Cycle: aisa raasta jo ghoom kar apne starting component par return karta hai; ek chakkar ke baad shuruaat wali cheez wapas mil jaati hai.
- f5 · teacher ki extra baat · Melvin Calvin ko paudhon mein CO₂ assimilation ki is khoj ke liye 1961 mein Chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar mila.
- f6 · kitab kehti hai · Ab dekhna hai ki Calvin pathway kaise chalta hai aur sugar kahan banti hai; Calvin pathway sabhi photosynthetic paudhon mein hota hai, chahe unka raasta C₃ ho, C₄ ho ya koi aur.
- f7 · shabd ka matlab · Calvin pathway · Calvin pathway: Calvin cycle ka hi naam; pathway = raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta.
- f8 · pehle ki zaroori baat · C₃ aur C₄ classification pehle stable product ke carbon ginkar: C₃ mein PGA (teen carbon), C₄ mein oxaloacetic acid (chaar carbon).
- f9 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Stroma: chloroplast mein thylakoid ki thailiyon ke chaaron taraf ka gaadha ras; thylakoid mein bane ATP aur NADPH yahin aate hain, aur sugar banane wale enzymes yahin hain.
- f10 · teacher ki extra baat · C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle ek alag tareeke se pakadte hain par aakhir mein Calvin cycle ko hi dete hain; CO₂ se sugar banane ka kaam sirf Calvin cycle karta hai.
- f11 · kitab kehti hai · Samajhne mein aasaani ke liye Calvin cycle ko teen stages mein baanta jaata hai: carboxylation, reduction aur regeneration.
- f12 · kitab kehti hai · Kadam 1, carboxylation: CO₂ ko ek stable organic intermediate mein fix karna; yeh Calvin cycle ka sabse crucial kadam hai, jahan CO₂ se RuBP ka carboxylation hota hai.
- f13 · kitab kehti hai · Yeh reaction enzyme RuBP carboxylase catalyse karta hai, aur isse 3-PGA ke do molecule bante hain.
- f14 · kitab kehti hai · Is enzyme mein oxygenation activity bhi hai, isliye ise RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase ya RuBisCO kehna zyada sahi hai.
- f15 · shabd ka matlab · Carboxylation · Carboxylation: molecule par carboxyl group (COOH) jodna, jo CO₂ se banta hai; yahan RuBP par CO₂ judna.
- f16 · shabd ka matlab · stable organic intermediate · Stable organic intermediate: stable = tikau, turant na tootne wala; organic = carbon wala molecule; intermediate = beech ka padaav, aakhri product nahi.
- f17 · shabd ka matlab · 3-PGA · 3-PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: teen carbon ka glyceric acid, jiske teesre carbon par phosphate laga hai; COOH group ise acid banata hai.
- f18 · shabd ka matlab · catalysed · Catalysed: enzyme ne reaction tez karaya, aur khud badla nahi.
- f19 · shabd ka matlab · oxygenation activity · Oxygenation activity: CO₂ ki jagah O₂ ko RuBP par jodne ki kaabiliyat.
- f20 · shabd ka matlab · RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase · RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase (RuBisCO): RuBP par carboxylation bhi aur oxygenation bhi karne wala enzyme; -ase = enzyme.
- f21 · teacher ki extra baat · RuBP aur CO₂ pehle chhe carbon ka ek molecule banate hain jo turant do 3-PGA mein toot jaata hai; isliye pehla stable molecule 3-PGA hai.
- f22 · teacher ki extra baat · RuBisCO dharti par sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein hai.
- f23 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Enzyme: protein jo reaction tez karta hai aur khud nahi badalta; naam aksar -ase par khatam hota hai.
- f24 · kitab kehti hai · Kadam 2, reduction: reactions ki series jo glucose banne ki taraf le jaati hai; per CO₂ molecule fixed 2 ATP phosphorylation ke liye aur 2 NADPH reduction ke liye lagte hain.
- f25 · kitab kehti hai · Six CO₂ ka fixation aur cycle ke 6 turns se ek glucose molecule banta hai.
- f26 · shabd ka matlab · per CO₂ molecule fixed · Per CO₂ molecule fixed: har ek pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se.
- f27 · shabd ka matlab · turn · Turn: cycle ka ek poora ghumaav, jisme ek CO₂ fix hota hai; turns = aise kai ghumaav.
- f28 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Phosphorylation = phosphate jodna (ATP apna phosphate deta hai); reduction = electron aur hydrogen milna (NADPH deta hai).
- f29 · teacher ki extra baat · Reduction mein 3-PGA (acid) ATP se phosphate aur NADPH se electron lekar triose phosphate (teen carbon ki sugar) banta hai; tri = teen, ose = sugar.
- f30 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Glucose mein chhe carbon hote hain; CO₂ mein ek.
- f31 · kitab kehti hai · Kadam 3, regeneration: CO₂ acceptor RuBP ka dobara banna crucial hai taaki cycle uninterrupted chale; RuBP banane ki phosphorylation mein ek ATP lagta hai.
- f32 · shabd ka matlab · CO₂ acceptor · CO₂ acceptor: CO₂ ko lene wala molecule (accept = lena), yahan RuBP; light reaction ke electron lene wale primary acceptor se alag.
- f33 · shabd ka matlab · uninterrupted · Uninterrupted: bina ruke, lagaataar.
- f34 · teacher ki extra baat · Paanch triose phosphate (15 carbon) jod-tod se teen RuBP (15 carbon) bante hain; RuBP ka doosra phosphate ATP jodta hai, isliye RuBP mein do phosphate (bis).
- f35 · teacher ki extra baat · Teen CO₂ par: 3 RuBP (15 C) + 3 CO₂ (3 C) = 18 C, phir 6 3-PGA, phir 6 triose phosphate; 5 wapas (15 C se 3 RuBP), 1 bahar (3 C); chhe CO₂ par 2 bahar = 6 C = ek glucose.
- f36 · kitab kehti hai · Har CO₂ jo Calvin cycle mein aata hai, uske liye 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH lagte hain.
- f37 · kitab kehti hai · ATP aur NADPH ki sankhya ka yeh farq poora karne ke liye shayad cyclic phosphorylation hota hai.
- f38 · shabd ka matlab · cyclic phosphorylation · Cyclic phosphorylation: cyclic photophosphorylation, jisme electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi.
- f39 · pehle ki zaroori baat · Non-cyclic raasta ATP aur NADPH dono banata hai; cyclic sirf ATP.
- f40 · kitab kehti hai · Ek glucose molecule banane ke liye cycle ke 6 turns chahiye.
- f41 · kitab kehti hai · Ek glucose ke liye kitne ATP aur NADPH lagenge, yeh khud nikaalna hai: chhe turns × 3 ATP = 18 ATP, chhe × 2 NADPH = 12 NADPH.
- f42 · kitab kehti hai · Calvin cycle mein kya andar jaata hai aur kya bahar aata hai, yeh dekhna madad karta hai: In: six CO₂, 18 ATP, 12 NADPH; Out: one glucose, 18 ADP, 12 NADP.
- f43 · kitab kehti hai · Figure 11.8: Calvin cycle teen stages mein: (1) carboxylation, CO₂ ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate se judta hai; (2) reduction, photochemically made ATP aur NADPH ke kharche par carbohydrate banta hai; (3) regeneration, CO₂ acceptor ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate phir banta hai taaki cycle chalta rahe.
- f44 · shabd ka matlab · photochemically made · Photochemically made: roshni se chalne wali reactions mein bana; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
- f45 · figure ka nishaan · Atmosphere · Upar Atmosphere: CO₂ hawa se aata hai.
