- f48 · figure ka nishaan · Carboxylation · 1 Carboxylation ka dabba, pehla stage.
- f49 · figure ka nishaan · 3-phosphoglycerate · Carboxylation ke baad bana compound; 3-PGA ka doosra naam.
- f50 · figure ka nishaan · Reduction · 2 Reduction ka dabba, doosra stage.
- f51 · figure ka nishaan · ATP + NADPH · Reduction mein andar aate hain.
- f52 · figure ka nishaan · ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺ · Reduction se bahar jaate hain: kharch hue ATP aur NADPH ke bache roop.
- f53 · figure ka nishaan · Triose phosphate · Reduction ke baad bana; yahan se raasta do mein bantta hai.
- f54 · figure ka nishaan · Sucrose, starch · Triose phosphate se neeche ka teer: sucrose aur starch.
- f55 · figure ka nishaan · Regeneration · 3 Regeneration ka dabba; ATP andar, ADP bahar, aur raasta wapas RuBP par.
- f56 · teacher ki extra baat · Sucrose ganne aur cheeni wali sugar hai jo paudhe mein ek jagah se doosri jagah jaati hai; starch jama ki gayi sugar hai, jaise aaloo, chawal, gehun mein.
- f57 · teacher ki extra baat · Carboxylation mein ATP ya NADPH nahi lagta; RuBP mein regeneration wale ATP ki energy pehle se hoti hai, isliye CO₂ ka judna apne aap aage badhne wali reaction hai.

**11.7.2-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Pichhli baar humne woh molecule pehchaana jo CO₂ ko pakadta hai: paanch carbon wala sugar, RuBP. RuBP ke paanch carbon mein CO₂ ka ek carbon juda, kul chhe hue. Inse teen-teen carbon ke do PGA bane. Yaad rakhein: CO₂ pakadte hi woh RuBP, RuBP nahi rehta; woh toot kar PGA ban jaata hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki yahi ek baat ek badi paheli khadi kar deti hai. Ab socho: har CO₂ ko pakadne mein ek RuBP kharch ho jaata hai. Aur patti din bhar laakhon CO₂ pakadti rehti hai. Toh patti CO₂ kaise pakadti rehti hai, uska RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota? RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota, isi sawaal ka jawab Melvin Calvin aur unke saathiyon ne dhoondha. Yeh wahi Calvin hain jinke radioactive carbon wale prayog se humne pehla product PGA pehchaana tha. Ab unhone kadam-dar-kadam poora raasta khoj nikala. Is kaam ke liye Calvin ko 1961 mein chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar mila. Dekho, unhone dikhaya ki yeh raasta seedhi line nahi, ek gol chakkar hai: jahan se shuru hota hai, ghoom kar wahin lautta hai. Isliye ise cycle kehte hain: chakkar ki shuruaat mein RuBP hai, aur chakkar ke aakhir mein RuBP phir se ban jaata hai. Use hone ke baad phir se banna, isi ko regenerated hona kehte hain: re yaani phir se, generate yaani banana. Toh RuBP kharch to hota hai, par chakkar use dobara bana deta hai. Isliye woh kabhi khatam nahi hota. Bilkul ek achhi dukaan ki tarah: dukaandar roz apni poonji lagata hai, par shaam ko poonji wapas thaili mein rakh leta hai. Ghar sirf uski kamaai jaati hai. Poonji roz lagti hai, phir bhi khatam nahi hoti, kyunki kamaai mein se hi wapas bhar jaati hai. Toh samjho, is chakkar mein RuBP wahi poonji hai, aur jo sugar bahar nikalti hai, woh kamaai. Par RuBP dobara banta kis cheez se hai, aur is kaam mein kharcha kitna lagta hai? RuBP dobara kaise banta hai aur kharcha kitna lagta hai, yeh hum chakkar ke kadam khol kar dekhenge.
  - terms: regenerated = Use hone ke baad phir se banaya gaya; re = phir se, generate = banana.
  - screen: Pichhla milan: paanch moti ki RuBP maala mein ek CO₂ moti judta hai, chhe moti teen-teen ke do PGA mein bantte hain, aur RuBP ki maala wahan nahi bachti; neeche 'Yaad rakhein: CO₂ pakadte hi RuBP toot kar PGA'. Phir ek hari patti par din bhar aate CO₂ ke teer, aur sawaal 'RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?'. Melvin Calvin ka naam aur '1961 Nobel'. Ek gol chakkar ka teer jo RuBP se chalkar wapas RuBP par lautta hai, us par 'regenerated'. Phir dukaan: dukaandar ki thaili se poonji nikalti, shaam ko wapas thaili mein, aur alag se 'kamaai' ghar jaati; neeche jod: RuBP = poonji, sugar = kamaai.
  - NCERT: p013-i7 Calvin and his co-workers then worked out the whole pathway and showed that the 
  - kitaab se bahar: Melvin Calvin ko 1961 mein chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar (paudhon mein CO₂ assimilation ki khoj); dukaan ki poonji ki misaal.
  - kahan tootti hai: Dukaan ki poonji sirf picture hai: RuBP ke carbon sikkon ki tarah jyon ke tyon nahi lautte; woh nayi molecules mein jod-tod hokar RuBP banate hain.
**11.7.2-k2** · samjhana · Yeh chakkar chalta kahan hai? Yeh chakkar chalta hai chloroplast ke stroma mein, yaani thylakoid ki thailiyon ke chaaron taraf bhare gaadhe ras mein. Yaad karo, thylakoid mein roshni se ATP aur NADPH bante hain, aur dono stroma mein hi chhode jaate hain. Isliye sugar bhi yahin stroma mein banti hai: CO₂, ATP aur NADPH, teeno ek hi jagah milte hain. Ab C₃ aur C₄ ko yaad karo: yeh naam pehle stable product ke carbon ginkar diye gaye the. C₃ paudhon mein pehla product teen carbon wala PGA hai, aur C₄ paudhon mein chaar carbon wala oxaloacetic acid. Aksar log sochte hain ki Calvin cycle sirf C₃ paudhon mein chalta hai, kyunki PGA wahi banate hain. Par aisa nahi hai. Dekho, har photosynthesis karne wale paudhe mein yeh chakkar chalta hai, chahe woh C₃ ho, C₄ ho, ya koi aur raasta rakhta ho. Kyun? Kyunki CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banane ka kaam sirf yahi chakkar karta hai. C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle ek alag tareeke se pakadte hain. Par aakhir mein use isi chakkar ko saunp dete hain; yeh hum aage C₄ wale hisse mein dekhenge. Isliye is raaste ko Calvin pathway bhi kehte hain: pathway yaani raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta. Ab yeh chakkar ek saath kai kaam karta hai, isliye samajhne mein aasaani ke liye ise teen kadamon mein baanta jaata hai. Yaad rakho: pehla kadam carboxylation hai, doosra reduction, aur teesra regeneration. Seedhe shabdon mein: pehle kadam mein CO₂ andar aata hai, doosre mein usse sugar banti hai, aur teesre mein RuBP phir banta hai. Dukaan ki zubaan mein: pehle naya paisa aata hai, phir kharcha karke maal taiyaar hota hai. Aakhir mein poonji wapas thaili mein jaati hai.
  - terms: Calvin pathway = Calvin cycle ka hi doosra naam; pathway = raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta, jo har photosynthetic paudhe mein chalta hai.
  - screen: Chloroplast ka cutaway: hari thylakoid thailiyon ke dher, unke chaaron taraf stroma ka gaadha ras roshan; thylakoid se ATP aur NADPH ke chhote moti stroma mein aate, saath bahar se CO₂ andar aata. Phir do paudhe: C₃ (PGA, teen moti) aur C₄ (oxaloacetic acid, chaar moti); 'sirf C₃ mein?' par kaata hua nishaan, aur dono paudhon se teer ek hi gol chakkar 'Calvin pathway' tak. Phir gol chakkar teen hisson mein: 1 carboxylation (CO₂ andar), 2 reduction (sugar banti), 3 regeneration (RuBP wapas); kone mein dukaan ke teen chhote chitra: naya paisa, maal taiyaar, poonji thaili mein.
  - NCERT: p013-i8 Let us now see how the Calvin pathway operates and where the sugar is synthesise; p013-i9 For ease of understanding, the Calvin cycle can be described under three stages:
  - kitaab se bahar: C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle alag tareeke se pakad kar aakhir mein Calvin cycle ko hi dete hain (aage 11.8).
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.7.2-k3** · samjhana · Pehla kadam wahi milan hai jo humne pichhli baar gina tha: RuBP aur CO₂ ka judna. CO₂ ek gas hai jo hawa mein udti phirti hai. Is kadam mein uska carbon ek molecule mein pakka bandh jaata hai. Gas ke carbon ko aise pakka baandh dena hi CO₂ fixation hai, jise hum pehle mil chuke hain. Ab is kadam ka naam samjho: carboxyl ek chhota group hai, COOH, jo CO₂ se banta hai. Molecule par CO₂ jodkar yeh carboxyl group banana, isi ko carboxylation kehte hain. Toh carboxylation mein CO₂ ka carbon ek stable organic intermediate mein fix ho jaata hai. Stable yaani tikau, jo turant na toote; organic yaani carbon wala; intermediate yaani beech ka padaav, aakhri manzil nahi. Ise tikau kyun kaha? Ise tikau isliye kaha, kyunki RuBP aur CO₂ milkar pehle chhe carbon ka ek molecule banate hain. Woh molecule pal bhar bhi nahi tikta, aur turant teen-teen carbon ke do hisson mein toot jaata hai. Yahi tikau hissa 3-PGA hai, aur ek RuBP aur ek CO₂ se iske do molecule bante hain. Ab 3-PGA ka naam todo: poora naam 3-phosphoglyceric acid hai, aur glyceric acid teen carbon ka ek acid hai. Phospho yaani us par ek phosphate laga hai, aur shuru ka 3 batata hai ki phosphate teesre carbon par baitha hai. Aur ise acid isliye kehte hain, kyunki CO₂ se aaya COOH group hi ise acid banata hai. Yeh poore chakkar ka sabse zaroori, yaani most crucial, kadam hai. Kyunki yahin hawa ka carbon pehli baar jeevit duniya mein aata hai. Par yeh milan apne aap bahut dheere hota hai, isliye ek enzyme ise tez karta hai. Enzyme ek protein hai jo reaction ko tez karata hai aur khud nahi badalta; aise naam aksar -ase par khatam hote hain. Enzyme ke is tez karaane ko kehte hain ki reaction catalysed hua; yahan reaction RuBP carboxylase se catalysed hota hai. Naam hi kaam batata hai: RuBP par carboxylation karne wala, aur aakhir ka -ase yaani enzyme. Par iska poora naam isse bhi lamba hai; aisa kyun? Poora naam isliye lamba hai, kyunki yahi enzyme RuBP par CO₂ ki jagah O₂ bhi jod sakta hai. O₂ jodne ki is kaabiliyat ko oxygenation activity kehte hain. Isliye ise RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase kehna zyada sahi hai, aur chhote mein RuBisCO. Dekho, RuBisCO dharti par sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein hai; har hari patti mein yeh bhara hota hai. O₂ judne se kya nuksaan hota hai, yeh aage photorespiration mein dekhenge. Dukaan mein yeh naya paisa aane ka kadam hai: har CO₂ ek naya rupaya hai jo poonji ke saath judta hai.
  - terms: Carboxylation = Molecule par CO₂ se bana carboxyl group (COOH) jodna; yahan RuBP par CO₂ ka judna, Calvin cycle ka pehla kadam.; stable organic intermediate = Tikau (turant na tootne wala), carbon wala, beech ka molecule jo aakhri product nahi; yahan 3-PGA.; 3-PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: teen carbon ka acid jiske teesre carbon par phosphate hai; figure mein 3-phosphoglycerate.; catalysed = Enzyme dwara tez karayi gayi (reaction), jisme enzyme khud nahi badalta.; oxygenation activity = RuBP par CO₂ ki jagah O₂ jodne ki enzyme ki kaabiliyat.; RuBisCO = RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase: RuBP par carboxylation bhi aur oxygenation bhi karne wala enzyme.
  - screen: Stroma mein paas se: paanch moti wala RuBP aur ek moti wala CO₂ ek-doosre ki taraf; beech mein RuBisCO ka bada protein. Mile to chhe moti ka molecule pal bhar chamakta hai aur turant teen-teen moti ke do 3-PGA mein toot jaata hai; ek sire par 'COOH' aur teesre moti par phosphate ka tag. Naam ke hisse alag-alag chamakte: carboxyl + ation; stable, organic, intermediate; 3 + phospho + glyceric acid. Enzyme par label 'RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase = RuBisCO', aur ek chhota O₂ bhi enzyme ki taraf aata dikhe. Kone mein dukaan ki thaili mein naya rupaya judta hua.
  - NCERT: p013-i10 1. Carboxylation – Carboxylation is the fixation of CO₂ into a stable organic in
  - kitaab se bahar: RuBP aur CO₂ ka chhe carbon wala pal bhar ka molecule jo turant do 3-PGA mein tootta hai; carboxyl = COOH; RuBisCO dharti ka sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i1
**11.7.2-k4** · samjhana · Ab 3-PGA ko dhyan se dekho: carbon to isme aa gaya, par yeh abhi sugar nahi, ek acid hai. Acid ko sugar banane ke liye do cheezein chahiye: thodi energy, aur electron. Yaad karo, phosphate jodne ko phosphorylation kehte hain, aur ATP apna ek phosphate dekar energy deta hai. Aur electron, saath mein hydrogen, milne ko reduction kehte hain; NADPH yahi electron aur hydrogen dene wala molecule hai. Pehle har 3-PGA par ATP ek aur phosphate jod deta hai, aur molecule mein energy bhar jaati hai. Phir NADPH use electron aur hydrogen deta hai, aur acid wala sira sugar wala sira ban jaata hai. Ab aap samajh gaye honge ki is kadam ka naam reduction kyun hai: yahan electron milne se hi badlaav hota hai. Jo banta hai, use triose phosphate kehte hain: tri yaani teen, ose yaani sugar, yaani teen carbon ki sugar jis par phosphate laga hai. Yahi is chakkar ki asli sugar hai. Ise banane mein kai reactions ek ke baad ek chalti hain, aur yahi series aage glucose banne tak le jaati hai. Ab kharcha gino: ek CO₂ jodne se do 3-PGA bane the, aur har 3-PGA ko ek ATP aur ek NADPH chahiye. Toh har pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se is kadam mein 2 ATP aur 2 NADPH lagte hain. Isi 'har pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se' ko per CO₂ molecule fixed kehte hain. Ab ek sawaal: CO₂ mein sirf ek carbon hai, aur glucose mein chhe. Toh ek glucose ke liye kitne CO₂ pakadne padenge? Ek glucose ke liye chhe CO₂ pakadne padenge, kyunki har CO₂ sirf ek carbon laata hai. Aur chakkar ke ek poore ghumaav mein ek hi CO₂ pakda jaata hai; is ek ghumaav ko turn kehte hain. Isliye yaad rakho: chhe CO₂ ka fixation, yaani cycle ke 6 turns, milkar ek glucose molecule banate hain. Dukaan ki tarah socho: maal taiyaar karne mein bijli aur mazdoori lagti hai. Chakkar mein yeh kharcha ATP aur NADPH se chukaya jaata hai.
  - terms: triose phosphate = Teen carbon ki sugar jis par phosphate laga hai (tri = teen, ose = sugar); reduction ka product.; per CO₂ molecule fixed = Har ek pakde (fix kiye) gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se.; turn = Cycle ka ek poora ghumaav, jisme ek CO₂ fix hota hai; kai ghumaav = turns.
  - screen: Ek 3-PGA (teen moti, ek sire par COOH, teesre moti par phosphate): pehle ATP aata hai aur ek aur phosphate tag judta hai, ATP se ADP bachta hai; phir NADPH electron aur H deta hai, COOH wala sira sugar ke sire mein badalta hai, NADPH se NADP⁺ bachta hai; ab naam 'triose phosphate' (tri = 3, ose = sugar). Neeche ginti: 1 CO₂ → 2 3-PGA → 2 ATP + 2 NADPH. Phir glucose ke chhe moti, chhe alag CO₂ ke ek-ek moti, aur chakkar ke 6 ghumaav ke gol nishaan. Kone mein dukaan ka bijli-mazdoori ka bill.
  - NCERT: p014-i16 2. Reduction – These are a series of reactions that lead to the formation of glu
  - kitaab se bahar: 3-PGA par pehle ATP se phosphate judta hai, phir NADPH se electron aur hydrogen; acid wala sira sugar wala sira banta hai (triose phosphate).
**11.7.2-k5** · samjhana · Ab lautte hain us sawaal par jo shuruaat mein chhoda tha: RuBP dobara banta kis cheez se hai? RuBP kis cheez se banega, iska ek clue hai: chakkar mein bahar se sirf CO₂ aata hai, aur reduction ke baad triose phosphate bante hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: RuBP ke paanch carbon aakhir aayenge kahan se? Aapne socha hoga ki triose phosphate se hi; bilkul sahi. Dekho, reduction ke baad bane triose phosphate mein se zyadatar wapas chakkar mein lautte hain. Unke carbon jod-tod kar phir se paanch carbon wale RuBP ban jaate hain. Isliye is kadam ka naam regeneration hai: wahi regenerated wala shabd, yaani dobara banana. Jo molecule CO₂ ko pakadta hai, use CO₂ acceptor kehte hain: accept yaani lena, acceptor yaani lene wala. Yaad karo, light reaction mein primary acceptor electron leta tha; yeh CO₂ acceptor CO₂ leta hai, aur yahan woh RuBP hai. Is kadam ka kharcha bhi hum gin lete hain: har CO₂ par ek ATP. Kyunki RuBP mein do phosphate hote hain, bis yaani do, aur doosra phosphate ATP hi jodta hai; yeh bhi phosphorylation hai. Par yeh kadam itna zaroori kyun hai? Yeh kadam zaroori isliye hai, kyunki RuBP dobara na bane to agla CO₂ pakadne wala koi nahi bachega. Tab ATP aur NADPH bhare pade rahenge, phir bhi chakkar ruk jaayega. RuBP banta rahe to chakkar bina ruke chalta rehta hai; bina ruke chalne ko uninterrupted kehte hain. Yahi dukaan ka niyam hai: jo dukaandar poonji kha jaaye, uski dukaan agle din nahi khulti.
  - terms: CO₂ acceptor = CO₂ ko lene (accept karne) wala molecule; Calvin cycle mein RuBP. Light reaction ke electron lene wale primary acceptor se alag.; uninterrupted = Bina ruke, lagaataar chalne wala.
  - screen: Chakkar ka teesra hissa: sawaal 'RuBP ke paanch carbon kahan se?' ke saath bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir reduction ke baad bane chhe triose phosphate (teen-teen moti); paanch wapas chakkar ki taraf mudte hain, unke moti khulkar teen paanch-paanch moti ki RuBP maala ban jaate hain; har maala ke doosre sire par ATP se phosphate tag judta, ATP → ADP. Label 'CO₂ acceptor = RuBP', aur alag chhote dabbe mein light reaction ka 'primary acceptor: electron'. Phir RuBP na bane to aate CO₂ ke teer khaali laut jaate aur chakkar ka teer ruk jaata; RuBP bane to teer lagaataar ghoomta. Kone mein dukaan ka band shutter.
  - NCERT: p014-i17 3. Regeneration – Regeneration of the CO₂ acceptor molecule RuBP is crucial if t
  - kitaab se bahar: Paanch triose phosphate ke 15 carbon jod-tod se teen RuBP ke 15 carbon bante hain; RuBP ka doosra phosphate ATP jodta hai (bis = do).
**11.7.2-k6** · example · Chalo, ab gin kar dekhte hain ki chakkar sach mein poora ghoom kar band hota hai ya nahi. Hisaab aasaan rahe, isliye teen CO₂ lekar chalte hain. Socho, teen RuBP hain, har ek mein paanch carbon: kul pandrah carbon. Teen CO₂ aaye, har ek mein ek carbon, toh ab kul athaarah carbon ho gaye. Carboxylation ke baad yeh athaarah carbon chhe 3-PGA mein bante hain, har ek mein teen. Reduction ke baad yahi chhe triose phosphate ban jaate hain; carbon abhi bhi athaarah hi hain. Ab batao, inme se kitne triose phosphate wapas jaayenge, aur kitne bahar? Wapas paanch triose phosphate jaayenge, aur bahar sirf ek. Paanch triose phosphate ke pandrah carbon jod-tod kar teen RuBP bana dete hain, yaani jitne RuBP the, utne hi wapas. Bacha ek triose phosphate, teen carbon wala, is chakkar ki asli kamaai hai; yahi sugar banne ke liye bahar nikalta hai. Dhyan do, yeh teen carbon wahi hain jo teen CO₂ lekar aaye the. Chakkar ne jitna carbon pakda, utna hi bahar diya. Chhe CO₂ par yahi hisaab do baar chalta hai: do triose phosphate bahar, yaani chhe carbon, ek glucose jitne. Dukaan mein bhi yahi hota: pandrah rupaye ki poonji, teen naye rupaye, kul athaarah; pandrah wapas thaili mein, aur teen rupaye kamaai.
  - screen: Moti gin kar: teen RuBP maala (5 + 5 + 5 = 15 moti) aur teen CO₂ moti (+3), kul 18. Phir chhe 3-PGA (6 × 3) aur chhe triose phosphate (6 × 3 = 18). Paanch triose phosphate ke 15 moti mud kar teen RuBP maala banate; ek triose phosphate (3 moti) bahar 'kamaai' ki taraf. Neeche ginti: 15 + 3 = 18 → 15 wapas + 3 bahar. Phir chhe CO₂ wala chitra: do triose phosphate bahar = 6 moti = ek glucose. Saath mein dukaan ke sikke: ₹15 poonji + ₹3 = ₹18; ₹15 thaili mein, ₹3 kamaai.
  - kitaab se bahar: Teen CO₂ par carbon ki ginti: 15 + 3 = 18 carbon, chhe triose phosphate, paanch se teen RuBP, ek bahar; chhe CO₂ par do bahar = ek glucose.
**11.7.2-k7** · figure · Ab Figure 11.8 dekho; isme yahi poora chakkar ek gol raaste ki tarah bana hai. Upar dekho, likha hai Atmosphere, yaani hawa; wahan se teer isliye aata hai, kyunki CO₂ hawa se hi patti mein aata hai. Us teer par likha hai CO₂ + H₂O, jo pehle dabbe, 1 Carboxylation, mein jaate hain. Upar ke dabbe mein likha hai Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate; yeh RuBP ka poora naam hai. Naam mein 1,5 isliye hai, kyunki paanch carbon mein se pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate lage hain; bis yaani do phosphate. Carboxylation se teer ghadi ki sui ki disha mein 3-phosphoglycerate tak jaata hai; yeh wahi 3-PGA hai. Ab ungli aage le chalo: dabba hai 2 Reduction, aur isme andar aate teer par likha hai ATP + NADPH. Bahar jaate teer par likha hai ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺; kharch hone ke baad ATP aur NADPH ke yahi bache roop hain. Reduction ke baad aata hai Triose phosphate, aur yahan se raasta do mein bant jaata hai. Neeche ki taraf ka teer kehta hai Sucrose, starch; kamaai wali sugar isi taraf jaati hai. Doosra raasta 3 Regeneration se hokar lautta hai; yahan ATP andar jaata hai aur ADP bahar aata hai. Aur teer phir se Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate par pahunch kar chakkar band kar deta hai. Figure ke neeche likha hai ki reduction mein carbohydrate, photochemically made ATP aur NADPH ke kharche par banta hai. Photochemically made yaani roshni wali reactions mein bane hue: photo yaani roshni, chemical yaani rasayanik. Isliye yeh chakkar roshni ke bina zyada der nahi chal sakta; iska kharcha roshni ki kamaai se hi chalta hai.
  - terms: photochemically made = Roshni se chalne wali reactions (light reaction) mein bana hua; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
  - screen: Figure 11.8 ka saaf, bada chitra; ungli ka nishaan har label par baari-baari: upar Atmosphere aur CO₂ + H₂O ka teer → 1 Carboxylation ke paas Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate (pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate chamakte) → ghadi ki disha mein 3-phosphoglycerate → 2 Reduction (andar ATP + NADPH, bahar ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺) → Triose phosphate → neeche Sucrose, starch, aur doosra teer 3 Regeneration (ATP andar, ADP bahar) → wapas Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate. Aakhir mein figure ke neeche ki line 'photochemically made ATP and NADPH' roshan, saath thylakoid mein roshni se ATP aur NADPH bante dikhane wala chhota chitra.
  - NCERT: p014-i1 Calvin cycle: atmospheric CO₂ + H₂O enters stage 1, Carboxylation, with ribulose
  - kitaab se bahar: Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate mein 1,5 = pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate.
**11.7.2-k8** · asli duniya · Yeh kamaai wali sugar aapko roz ki zindagi mein milti hai. Patti triose phosphate se sucrose banati hai, jo poore paudhe mein bheji ja sakti hai. Aapke ghar ki cheeni aur ganne ka meetha ras, dono yahi sucrose hain. Aur jo sugar turant kaam nahi aati, use paudha starch banakar jama karta hai. Aaloo, chawal aur gehun ka aata zyadatar starch hi hai. Toh aaloo ka starch aaloo mein bana kaise, jabki aaloo mitti ke neeche andhere mein hai? Aaloo ka starch patti mein bani sugar se bana: sucrose patti se aaloo tak pahunchi, aur wahan starch banakar jama ho gayi. Yaad karo, chapter ki shuruaat wale prayog mein patti ke jis hisse ko CO₂ nahi mili, wahan iodine se starch nahi dikha. Kyunki CO₂ ke bina yeh chakkar chal hi nahi sakta, aur chakkar ke bina na sugar banti hai, na starch.
  - terms: sucrose = Ganne aur cheeni wali sugar; paudha ise patti se baaki hisson tak bhejta hai.
  - screen: Patti se tane aur jad ki taraf behti sugar ki dhaara (sucrose); ek taraf ganna aur cheeni ki katori, doosri taraf mitti ke neeche aaloo ke andar starch ke daane, saath chawal aur aata. Phir chapter ki shuruaat wala prayog: tube ke andar wala patti ka hissa iodine ke baad bhoora, bahar wala hissa neela-kaala.
  - kitaab se bahar: Ganne ka ras aur cheeni sucrose hain; aaloo, chawal aur gehun mein starch; aaloo ka starch patti se aayi sucrose se banta hai.
**11.7.2-k9** · samjhana · Ab poore chakkar ka kharcha jodne se pehle ek baat socho: kya CO₂ ko pakadne mein bhi energy lagti hai? CO₂ ko pakadne mein koi energy nahi lagti; carboxylation mein na ATP kharch hota hai, na NADPH. RuBP mein regeneration wale ATP ki energy pehle se bhari hoti hai. Isliye CO₂ se judna uske liye dhalaan se neeche ludhakne jaisa aasaan hai; RuBisCO bas ise tez karta hai. Matlab poora kharcha baaki do kadamon mein hai: sugar banane wale reduction mein, aur RuBP ko phir taiyaar karne wale regeneration mein. Dukaan mein bhi naya paisa aane ka koi kharcha nahi; kharcha maal banane aur poonji sambhalne mein hai.
  - screen: Chakkar ke teen hisse ek line mein: 1 carboxylation par '0 ATP, 0 NADPH', RuBP ki maala ek dhalaan ke upar se CO₂ ki taraf aasaani se ludhakti; 2 reduction par ATP aur NADPH ke moti; 3 regeneration par ATP ka moti, jo RuBP ki maala mein energy bhar kar use dhalaan ke upar pahunchata. Kone mein dukaan: paisa aane par koi bill nahi, maal banane aur thaili sambhalne par bill.
  - kitaab se bahar: Carboxylation mein ATP ya NADPH nahi lagta; regeneration ke ATP ki energy RuBP mein rehti hai, isliye CO₂ ka judna apne aap aage badhne wali reaction hai.
**11.7.2-k10** · samjhana · Ab bill jodo: reduction ke 2 ATP aur regeneration ka 1 ATP milkar har CO₂ par 3 ATP hue, aur NADPH sirf 2. Yaad karo, seedhe raaste, yaani non-cyclic, mein ATP aur NADPH dono saath-saath bante hain. Aur gol raaste, yaani cyclic, mein electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai, aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi. Ab socho, chakkar ki ATP ki yeh zyada maang kaun poori karta hoga? ATP ki zyada maang poori karne ke liye hi shayad cyclic phosphorylation hota hai, jo NADPH badhaaye bina sirf ATP deta hai. Kyunki agar sirf seedha raasta chale, to har ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banega, aur ATP kam pad jaayega. Yahan cyclic phosphorylation wahi gol raaste wala cyclic photophosphorylation hai, jo humne light reaction mein dekha tha.
  - terms: cyclic phosphorylation = Cyclic photophosphorylation: electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi.
  - screen: Bill ki parchi: reduction 2 ATP + 2 NADPH, regeneration 1 ATP; neeche total 3 ATP + 2 NADPH har CO₂ par, tarazu mein ATP ka palda bhaari. Phir thylakoid par do raaste: seedha raasta (PS II → PS I → NADP⁺) ATP aur NADPH dono deta; gol raasta (PS I se ghoom kar wapas PS I) sirf ATP deta. Gol raaste se aata ATP tarazu ko barabar karta.
  - NCERT: p015-i1 Hence for every CO₂ molecule entering the Calvin cycle, 3 molecules of ATP and 2
  - kitaab se bahar: Sirf seedhe raaste se ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai, isliye 3 : 2 ki maang ke liye ATP kam padta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**11.7.2-k11** · ruko, socho · Hum gin chuke hain ki ek glucose ke chhe carbon ke liye cycle ke 6 turns chahiye. Ab khud nikaalo: ek glucose banane mein kitne ATP aur kitne NADPH lagenge? Kitne ATP lagenge, iska clue yeh hai ki har turn ka kharcha aapko pata hai; bas use chhe se guna karo. Har turn mein 3 ATP, toh chhe turns mein 6 × 3 = 18 ATP. Aur har turn mein 2 NADPH, toh 6 × 2 = 12 NADPH. Aksar bachche sochte hain ki 12 ATP lagenge, kyunki woh sirf reduction gin lete hain; par asal mein regeneration ke 6 ATP bhi judte hain. Aur yaad rakho, chhe turns se chhe glucose nahi, milkar ek hi glucose banta hai.
  - screen: Sawaal ka dabba 'Ek glucose = kitne ATP, kitne NADPH?' aur chhoti ghadi. Phir chhe gol chakkar ek line mein, har chakkar ke neeche 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH ke moti; jod: 18 ATP, 12 NADPH. Ek galat jawab '12 ATP' par kaata hua nishaan, aur regeneration ke 6 ATP judkar 18. Aakhir mein chhe chakkar → ek glucose.
  - NCERT: p015-i2 To make one molecule of glucose 6 turns of the cycle are required.; p015-i3 Work out how many ATP and NADPH molecules will be required to make one molecule 
  - jawab: Ek glucose ke liye 6 turns; har turn mein 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH, toh 6 × 3 = 18 ATP aur 6 × 2 = 12 NADPH (reduction ke 12 ATP + regeneration ke 6 ATP = 18).
  - aam galti: Sirf reduction ginne par 12 ATP aata hai, regeneration ke 6 ATP chhoot jaate hain; aur 6 turns se 1 glucose banta hai, 6 glucose nahi.
**11.7.2-k12** · activity · Yeh sab ek nazar mein dekhna ho, to dekho ki Calvin cycle mein kya andar jaata hai aur kya bahar aata hai. Andar jaate hain chhe CO₂, aur bahar aata hai ek glucose. Andar jaate hain 18 ATP, aur bahar aate hain 18 ADP. Andar jaate hain 12 NADPH, aur bahar aate hain 12 NADP⁺. ATP aur NADPH kharch hokar ADP aur NADP⁺ ban jaate hain. Yeh thylakoid mein laut kar roshni se phir chaarj ho jaate hain. Par is hisaab mein RuBP kahin nahi dikhta; kyun? RuBP hisaab mein isliye nahi dikhta, kyunki jitna lagta hai, utna hi chakkar mein wapas ban jaata hai. Dukaan ke hisaab mein bhi poonji nahi likhi jaati; sirf kamaai aur kharcha likhe jaate hain.
  - screen: Do khaane ki table: In | Out; Six CO₂ | One glucose; 18 ATP | 18 ADP; 12 NADPH | 12 NADP⁺; har row baari-baari roshan. Side mein ADP aur NADP⁺ ke moti thylakoid ki taraf laut kar roshni mein phir ATP aur NADPH bante. Table ke bahar RuBP ka band gol chakkar jo khud mein ghoomta rehta hai.
  - NCERT: p015-i4 It might help you to understand all of this if we look at what goes in and what 
  - kitaab se bahar: Table mein NADP ko NADP⁺ kaha; RuBP table mein isliye nahi kyunki woh regenerate hota hai.
**11.7.2-k13** · poori kahani dobara · Ab poora chakkar ek saath dohra lo. Pehle kadam, carboxylation, mein RuBisCO RuBP par CO₂ jodta hai, aur do 3-PGA bante hain. Doosre kadam, reduction, mein ATP aur NADPH ke kharche se 3-PGA triose phosphate ban jaate hain. Teesre kadam, regeneration, mein zyadatar triose phosphate ek ATP aur lagakar wapas RuBP ban jaate hain. Har teen CO₂ par ek triose phosphate kamaai bankar bahar jaata hai, aur usse sucrose aur starch bante hain. Har CO₂ ka kharcha 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH hai, aur ek glucose ka 18 ATP aur 12 NADPH. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: har CO₂ par ek RuBP kharch hota hai, toh patti ka RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota? RuBP khatam isliye nahi hota, kyunki jitna RuBP pehle kadam mein lagta hai, utna hi teesre kadam mein wapas ban jaata hai. Bilkul wahi dukaan jo shuru mein dekhi: poonji roz lagti hai, shaam ko wapas thaili mein aa jaati hai, aur ghar sirf kamaai jaati hai. Aur yeh chakkar har hare paudhe mein chalta hai, chahe woh C₃ ho ya C₄. Toh aap poochhoge, agar chakkar sab mein ek hai, to C₄ paudhe alag kis baat mein hain? C₄ paudhe alag kis baat mein hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
  - screen: Figure 11.8 jaisa poora gol chakkar, teeno kadam baari-baari roshan: RuBisCO par RuBP + CO₂ → do 3-PGA; ATP + NADPH se triose phosphate; paanch wapas RuBP, ek bahar sucrose aur starch ki taraf. Neeche parchi: 1 CO₂ = 3 ATP + 2 NADPH; 1 glucose = 18 ATP + 12 NADPH. Phir shuru ka sawaal 'RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?' laut kar aata hai: pehle kadam mein ek RuBP lagta, teesre kadam mein utna hi RuBP wapas banta; kone mein dukaan ki thaili mein poonji wapas jaati aur kamaai ghar jaati. Aakhir mein do paudhe, C₃ aur C₄, dono ka teer isi chakkar tak, aur C₄ par sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10

_→ Calvin cycle har hare paudhe mein ek hi hai; agle heading mein dekhenge ki C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko is chakkar tak pahunchane ka kaun sa khaas tareeka rakhte hain._

## 11.8 THE C₄ PATHWAY

_Maqsad: Ab shared Calvin cycle tak C₄ plants ki carbon delivery follow karenge, aur samjhenge ki leaf ki special anatomy is delivery ko kaise support karti hai._

**11.8-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab shared Calvin cycle tak C₄ plants ki carbon delivery follow karenge, aur samjhenge ki leaf ki special anatomy is delivery ko kaise support karti hai. Anatomy pehchaanne ke baad process ke chaar steps follow karenge: initial carbon fixation, four-carbon compound ki delivery aur CO₂ release, three-carbon remainder ki return, aur acceptor regeneration. C₄ oxaloacetic acid wahi pehle pehchaana four-carbon first stable product hai, aur dono groups ka shared Calvin cycle bhi hum jaan chuke hain. C₄ plants dry tropical regions, yaani garam ilaakon ki paani-kam conditions, ke liye adapted ho sakte hain. Inka main biosynthetic pathway, yaani organic substances banane wala connected reaction route, Calvin cycle hi hai. Phir C₃ plants se inka farq kya hai? Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants Calvin cycle use nahi karte, par asal mein unki initial carbon capture aur delivery alag hai, sugar-making Calvin cycle shared hai.
  - terms: C₄ oxaloacetic acid = Pehle pehchaana four-carbon oxaloacetic acid; C₄ initial fixation ka first stable product.; dry tropical regions = Garam tropical ilaake jahaan paani ki availability kam ho sakti hai.; biosynthetic pathway = Organic substances banane wali connected reactions ka route.
  - screen: Realistic maize leaf se internal tissue tak zoom; four-carbon oxaloacetic acid entry route ke aage shared Calvin-cycle symbol. Side par chaar numbered steps: fixation, delivery/release, return, regeneration.
  - NCERT: p015-i10 Plants that are adapted to dry tropical regions have the C₄ pathway mentioned ea; p015-i11 as the main biosynthetic pathway. Then, in what way are they different from C₃ p
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k2** · samjhana · C₄ plants ki paanch khas baatein jodo: special leaf anatomy, higher-temperature tolerance, high light intensities par achchha response, photorespiration ka practically absent hona, aur greater biomass productivity. Leaf anatomy leaf ke andar tissues aur cells ka arrangement hai. Photorespiration RuBisCO ki O₂ wali activity se shuru hone wala route hai, jisme fixed carbon aur energy ka loss hota hai; iska detail agle heading mein kholenge. RuBisCO ki CO₂ aur O₂, dono wali activities hum pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Productivity of biomass ka matlab fixed time mein naya plant material banne ki rate hai. Photorespiration ko C₄ plants mein absent kyun maana jaata hai? Dekho, inka carbon-delivery system RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhata hai, jisse photorespiration bahut strongly suppress hoti hai. Pehle anatomy samjhenge, phir delivery route se in features ka connection jodenge.
  - terms: leaf anatomy = Leaf ke andar tissues aur cells ki banawat aur arrangement.; photorespiration = RuBisCO ki O₂ wali activity se shuru carbon aur energy kharch karne wala route.; productivity of biomass = Ek fixed time mein naye plant material ke production ki rate.
  - screen: Maize leaf ke paas paanch features: internal tissue ring, warmth, strong light, suppressed photorespiration aur biomass gain.
  - NCERT: p015-i12 C₄ plants are special: They have a special type of leaf anatomy, they tolerate h
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16
**11.8-k3** · activity · Teacher-prepared vertical sections mein ek C₃ leaf aur ek C₄ maize leaf ko microscope se compare karo. Vertical section leaf ki upper se lower surface tak thickness ko expose karne wala thin cut hai. Mesophyll wahi internal photosynthetic tissue hai, jise hum pehle locate kar chuke hain. Kya dono leaves mein mesophyll ka arrangement aur veins ke aas-paas ke cells ek jaise hain? Dekho, maize mein vein ke around prominent large-cell ring dikhti hai, aur uske baahar mesophyll arranged hota hai. C₃ reference section mein yahi characteristic C₄ ring organisation nahi milti. Mesophyll ke layers bhi compare karo, lekin har C₃ leaf mein ek jaisi layer differentiation maan lena sahi nahi hai.
  - terms: vertical sections = Leaf ki upper se lower surface tak thickness dikhane wale thin cuts.
  - screen: Side-by-side realistic microscopic C₃ reference aur maize vertical sections; vein-surrounding cells aur outer mesophyll highlighted.
  - NCERT: p015-i13 Study vertical sections of leaves, one of a C₃ plant and the other of a C₄ plant
  - kitaab se bahar: C₃ leaves mein mesophyll differentiation ki variation ko clarify kiya.
  - kya kis jaisa: Maize section mein characteristic prominent sheath ring aur surrounding mesophyll organisation milti hai; har C₃ leaf ka mesophyll identical nahi hota.
  - kahan tootti hai: Ek reference C₃ section ki layering ko sabhi C₃ species ka compulsory pattern na maano.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i12
**11.8-k4** · analogy · Socho, ek central stand ke chaaron taraf flowers ki wreath, yaani gol maala, lagi hai. Har flower central stand ko gherne wale ring ka hissa hai; isi surrounding arrangement ko leaf mein pehchaano. Vascular bundles leaf ki veins ke andar water aur food transport karne wale xylem–phloem groups hain. In bundles ko gherne wale cells bundle sheath cells kehlaate hain. Typical C₄ leaf mein particularly large sheath cells ka wreath-like arrangement Kranz anatomy kehlaata hai; Kranz ka meaning wreath hai. Yahaan central stand vascular bundle hai, aur flower-ring large bundle sheath cells hain. Kranz naam leaf ke baahari shape ko kyun nahi batata? Dekho, naam internal cells ki bundle ko gherne wali arrangement se aaya hai, poori leaf ki outline se nahi.
  - terms: vascular bundles = Xylem aur phloem ke transport groups jo leaf veins ke andar hote hain.; bundle sheath cells = Vascular bundle ko chaaron taraf gherne wale cells.; Kranz anatomy = Typical C₄ leaf mein vascular bundle ke around large sheath cells ki wreath-like organisation.
  - screen: Circular flower wreath ke saath realistic maize vein cross-section; centre vascular bundle aur surrounding large bundle sheath cells matched.
  - NCERT: p015-i14 The particularly large cells around the vascular bundles of the C₄ plants are ca
  - kitaab se bahar: Xylem–phloem ka basic transport role prerequisite ke roop mein khola.
  - kya kis jaisa: Central stand vascular bundle hai; flower wreath surrounding large bundle sheath cells ka spatial arrangement hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Wreath sirf arrangement samjhati hai; bundle sheath cells flowers nahi hain aur unke biochemical functions analogy se explain nahi hote.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i15, p022-i12
**11.8-k5** · samjhana · Bundle sheath ki ring mein several layers ho sakti hain, lekin multiple layers compulsory nahi hain. In cells mein numerous chloroplasts, thick walls aur cells ke beech intercellular spaces ki absence khas features hain. Intercellular spaces neighbouring cells ke beech ki khaali jagahen hain. Walls ko impervious to gaseous exchange kehte hain, yaani gases ka freely aar-paar jaana strongly restricted hota hai. Gas escape restrict karna useful kyun hoga? Dekho, sheath ke andar delivered CO₂ retain hogi, aur numerous chloroplasts ke stromal reactions us carbon ko use kar sakenge. Chloroplast stroma ka sugar-making role hum pehle samajh chuke hain.
  - terms: intercellular spaces = Paas ke cells ke beech ki khaali jagahen.; impervious to gaseous exchange = Gases ke freely cross karne ko strongly restrict karne wala.
  - screen: Wahi realistic maize section enlarged: tightly packed sheath cells, many chloroplasts, thick walls aur restricted outward CO₂ arrows.
  - NCERT: p015-i15 The bundle sheath cells may form several layers around the vascular bundles; the
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i15, p022-i12
**11.8-k6** · example · Ab maize aur sorghum ke teacher-prepared thin leaf sections mein Kranz anatomy identify karo. Pehle vascular bundle locate karo, phir uske around large chloroplast-rich sheath aur baahar mesophyll cells trace karo. Mesophyll cells wahi leaf ke internal photosynthetic tissue ke cells hain, jinhe hum pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Sorghum ke section mein bhi kya isi organisation ko dhoondhoge? Dekho, dono named C₄ examples mein bundle, surrounding specialised sheath aur outer mesophyll ka relationship match karna hai. Result sirf green cells dekhna nahi, balki unki organised positions pehchaanana hai.
  - screen: Maize aur sorghum prepared sections; har sample mein vascular bundle → bundle sheath → mesophyll ke nested highlights.

  - exercise ke liye: p021-i15, p022-i12
**11.8-k7** · activity · Ab aas-paas ki diverse plant species ki leaves collect karke labels lagao, aur teacher se unke thin vertical sections prepare karwao. Microscope mein har section ka vascular bundle, sheath ke cells aur mesophyll distribution record karo. Observation sheet mein maize aur sorghum ko characteristic Kranz organisation wale samples ke roop mein rakho. Kya kisi bhi bundle sheath ka milna C₄ hone ka proof hai? Dekho, C₃ leaves mein bhi bundle sheath ho sakti hai; characteristic large, chloroplast-rich Kranz organisation ko pehchaanana zaroori hai. Sirf external leaf shape ya plant ka garam jagah ugna pathway establish nahi karta; internal organisation zyada informative evidence deti hai.
  - screen: Filled observation sheet: maize—Kranz; sorghum—Kranz; reference C₃ section—characteristic Kranz absent; saath corresponding tissue micrographs.
  - NCERT: p015-i17 It would be interesting for you to collect leaves of diverse species of plants a
  - kitaab se bahar: C₃ plants mein bhi bundle sheath ho sakne ki clarification di.
  - kya kis jaisa: Sections mein specialised Kranz arrangement compare karna hai; sheath ki simple presence ya external morphology decisive nahi hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Har vein-surrounding sheath ko Kranz kehne se C₃ leaves ko bhi galti se C₄ classify kar doge.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i14, p021-i15, p022-i12
**11.8-k8** · asli duniya · Bazaar ka bhutta maize crop se aata hai, aur jowar ki roti sorghum ke grain se banti hai. In dono food crops ki leaves mein yahi microscopic organisation carbon delivery support karti hai. Higher-temperature tolerance aur strong-light performance inki farming se jude physiological features hain. Kya in crops ko har environment mein sabse zyada yield milegi? Dekho, actual harvest paani, light aur doosri growing conditions par bhi depend karta hai, isliye C₄ adaptation universal yield guarantee nahi hai.
  - screen: Bhutta aur jowar ki roti ke saath maize aur sorghum fields; crop leaves se Kranz tissue ka connection.
  - kitaab se bahar: Food connections aur C₄ advantage ko universal yield guarantee se alag kiya.
**11.8-k9** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab Figure 11.9 ka Hatch and Slack Pathway chaar steps mein follow karenge: initial fixation, four-carbon compound ki delivery aur CO₂ release, three-carbon return, aur acceptor renewal. Hatch and Slack Pathway C₄ carbon-capture aur delivery route ka doosra naam hai. Yeh cyclic process hai, kyunki starting acceptor dobara available hota hai; acceptor renewal ka logic Calvin cycle mein bhi dekha tha. Dono cycles ka acceptor kya same hoga? Dekho, Calvin cycle ka acceptor RuBP tha, lekin is initial delivery cycle ka acceptor ab alag pehchaanenge.
  - terms: Hatch and Slack Pathway = C₄ plants ka initial carbon-capture, delivery aur acceptor-renewal route.; cyclic process = Aisa connected reaction route jisme starting working component dobara milta hai.
  - screen: Realistic adjacent mesophyll aur bundle sheath cell cutaways; chaar numbered steps: fixation, delivery/release, return, renewal.
  - NCERT: p016-i1 Now study the pathway shown in Figure 11.9. This pathway that has been named the
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k10** · samjhana · Mesophyll mein primary acceptor phosphoenol pyruvate, ya PEP, three-carbon molecule hai. PEP carboxylase, ya PEPcase, initial carbon fixation catalyse karta hai, aur four-carbon OAA banta hai. OAA wahi pehle khola oxaloacetic acid hai; carbon ledger three plus one equals four hai. Figure mein incoming inorganic carbon HCO₃⁻, bicarbonate, ke roop mein dikhta hai; PEPcase isi form ko use karta hai. Is typical C₄ arrangement ke mesophyll cells mein PEPcase hai, lekin RuBisCO nahi hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ ka primary acceptor four-carbon hoga, par asal mein PEP three-carbon acceptor aur OAA four-carbon product hai. Acceptor aur enzyme ko alag kyun rakhna hai? Dekho, PEP carbon skeleton receive karta hai, jabki PEPcase reaction catalyse karta hai; enzyme ko three-carbon acceptor kehna galat hoga.
  - terms: phosphoenol pyruvate (PEP) = C₄ initial fixation ka three-carbon acceptor molecule.; PEP carboxylase or PEPcase = PEP aur bicarbonate se initial four-carbon product banane wali reaction ka enzyme.; OAA = Pehle pehchaane oxaloacetic acid ka short name; four-carbon first stable product.; HCO₃⁻ = Bicarbonate ion; PEPcase ko milne wala inorganic carbon ka form.
  - screen: Wahi mesophyll cutaway: atmospheric CO₂ se HCO₃⁻, phir PEP ke three carbon beads mein one carbon add hokar OAA four beads; PEPcase label.
  - NCERT: p016-i2 The primary CO₂ acceptor is a 3-carbon molecule phosphoenol pyruvate (PEP) and i
  - kitaab se bahar: PEPcase ke actual bicarbonate substrate ko figure ke HCO₃⁻ label se clarify kiya.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k11** · samjhana · Mesophyll mein OAA se malic acid ya aspartic acid jaise other four-carbon compounds bante hain. In four-carbon organic acids ko yahaan C₄ acids keh rahe hain; yeh captured carbon neighbouring bundle sheath cells tak transport karte hain. Sheath mein inka breakdown CO₂ release karta hai aur three-carbon remainder deta hai; CO₂ nikalne ko decarboxylation kehte hain. Four carbons ka balance kahaan gaya? Dekho, four-carbon carrier se one-carbon CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder milta hai, isliye four equals one plus three rehta hai.
  - terms: malic acid or aspartic acid = Four-carbon compounds jo captured carbon mesophyll se bundle sheath tak pahunchate hain.; C₄ acid = Yahaan four-carbon organic acid jo carbon delivery mein kaam aata hai.; decarboxylation = Kisi compound se CO₂ release hona.
  - screen: Same cells par OAA se malic/aspartic acid; four-carbon cargo sheath mein enters, phir one-carbon CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder mein splits.
  - NCERT: p016-i3 It then forms other 4-carbon compounds like malic acid or aspartic acid in the m
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i1, p021-i16, p022-i10
**11.8-k12** · samjhana · Ab three-carbon remainder bundle sheath se mesophyll mein return hota hai, jahaan usse PEP dobara banta hai. Regeneration ka meaning wahi starting working molecule ka renewal hai; yahaan renew hone wala acceptor PEP hai, RuBP nahi. Kya return route ko free carbon recycling samajh sakte hain? Dekho, PEP renewal energy maangta hai, isliye carbon-concentrating system ka extra energy bill bhi hota hai. Typical C₄ accounting mein har delivered CO₂ ke liye two additional ATP equivalents lagte hain.
  - screen: Same cell cutaways par three-carbon return arrow aur mesophyll mein PEP regeneration; energy badge: +2 ATP equivalents per CO₂.
  - NCERT: p016-i4 The 3-carbon molecule is transported back to the mesophyll where it is converted
  - kitaab se bahar: PEP regeneration ka extra cost: two ATP equivalents per CO₂; commonly ATP-to-AMP conversion ki energy accounting.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k13** · samjhana · Bundle sheath mein released CO₂ ab C₃ ya Calvin pathway ko feed karti hai. RuBisCO ke through RuBP carboxylation ka reaction hum pehle follow kar chuke hain. Yahaan CO₂ preliminary four-carbon compound ki decarboxylation se milti hai; Calvin cycle CO₂ release nahi karti. Calvin cycle mein fixed carbon ka net output triose phosphate sugar ke roop mein nikalta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki initial C₄ fixation hi sugar-making Calvin cycle ko replace karti hai, par asal mein yeh usse concentrated CO₂ supply karti hai. Do fixation events ko kyun alag pehchaanenge? Dekho, pehla event carbon delivery ke liye capture karta hai, aur doosra shared Calvin pathway mein carbohydrate formation ko feed karta hai.
  - screen: Same bundle sheath cutaway par four-carbon compound se released CO₂ ka arrow chloroplast stroma ke Calvin-cycle inset tak; net triose-phosphate output alag highlighted.
  - NCERT: p016-i5 The CO₂ released in the bundle sheath cells enters the C₃ or the Calvin pathway,
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p022-i1, p021-i16
**11.8-k14** · figure · Figure 11.9 mein upper Mesophyll cell ki outer Cell wall aur uske andar Plasma membrane, cell ki selective boundary, identify karo. Atmospheric CO₂ ka arrow HCO₃⁻ tak, phir Phosphoenol-pyruvate ke saath Fixation box tak follow karo. Fixation se C₄ acid aur downward Transport arrow bundle sheath ki taraf jaate hain; Plasmodesmata neighbouring cells ko jodne wale channels hain. Lower Bundle sheath cell mein incoming C₄ acid Decarboxylation box tak pahunchta hai. Ek branch CO₂ se Fixation by Calvin cycle tak, aur doosri C₃ acid se upward Transport tak jaati hai. Returned C₃ acid mesophyll mein Regeneration se Phosphoenol-pyruvate ko renew karta hai. Dono Transport labels opposite directions kyun dikhate hain? Dekho, downward route four-carbon delivery hai, jabki upward route three-carbon skeleton ki return hai.
  - terms: plasma membrane = Cell ki selective boundary jo substances ke passage ko regulate karti hai.; plasmodesmata = Neighbouring plant cells ke interiors ko jodne wale fine channels.
  - screen: Ab tak built realistic two-cell pathway par Figure 11.9 ke teaching labels sequentially highlighted; active branch bright, earlier arrows subdued.
  - NCERT: p016-i6 Two connected cells show the Hatch and Slack pathway. The upper mesophyll cell r
  - kitaab se bahar: Plasma membrane aur plasmodesmata ke basic meanings prerequisite ke roop mein khole.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p022-i12
**11.8-k15** · samjhana · Ab enzyme-location map complete karo: C₄ mesophyll mein PEPcase aur bundle sheath mein abundant RuBisCO hota hai. Is arrangement ke bundle sheath cells mein PEPcase nahi hota; initial capture aur Calvin fixation ki jobs spatially separated hain. C₃ leaves mein Calvin pathway mesophyll cells mein chalta hai, jabki typical C₄ leaf mein woh bundle sheath cells tak restricted hai. Same Calvin pathway hone par location ka difference important kyun hai? Dekho, four-carbon compounds CO₂ sheath mein release karte hain, isliye RuBisCO aur Calvin cycle wahin rakhne se delivered CO₂ seedhe use ho sakti hai. Dono groups sugars ke precursors shared Calvin pathway se banate hain; difference uski identity mein nahi, carbon supply aur cellular location mein hai.
  - screen: Two-column enzyme-location matrix: C₃ mesophyll—RuBisCO/Calvin; C₄ mesophyll—PEPcase, bundle sheath—RuBisCO/Calvin; corresponding tissue cells highlighted.
  - NCERT: p017-i1 rich in an enzyme Ribulose bisphosphate carboxylase-oxygenase (RuBisCO), but lac; p017-i2 Did you note that the Calvin pathway occurs in all the mesophyll cells of the C₃
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p022-i12, p021-i16, p022-i1
**11.8-k16** · example · Ab maize ke ek incoming carbon ko follow karo: bicarbonate ka carbon three-carbon PEP ke saath four-carbon OAA ke account mein judta hai. Malic-acid delivery ke baad sheath mein one-carbon CO₂ release hoti hai aur three-carbon skeleton mesophyll lautkar PEP renew karta hai. Starting three-carbon acceptor pool wapas mila; net delivered carbon kitna raha? Dekho, ek incoming carbon Calvin cycle ko mila, jabki delivery skeleton regenerate hua; delivery loop khud extra carbon create nahi karta. Six delivered CO₂ ke liye Calvin cycle ka pehle joda bill eighteen ATP aur twelve NADPH hai. Typical C₄ delivery ke twelve extra ATP equivalents jodne par glucose-equivalent ka total thirty ATP equivalents aur twelve NADPH hota hai. Yeh energy investment CO₂ concentration ke liye hai; C₄ naam ka matlab four CO₂ molecules ek saath fix hona nahi.
  - screen: Same maize two-cell picture par one marked carbon ka route; side ledger: 3C pool restored, 1C delivered; six-CO₂ energy ledger 18 + 12 = 30 ATP equivalents, 12 NADPH.
  - kitaab se bahar: Typical C₄ extra ATP cost ko Calvin budget se jodkar glucose-equivalent energy ledger derive kiya.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k17** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: four-carbon compound sheath mein CO₂ chhod de, lekin three-carbon remainder mesophyll lautna band ho jaaye, toh continuous delivery ka kya hoga? Kya sheath mein Calvin cycle hone se missing return route apne-aap theek ho jaayega? Dekho, return rukne par PEP acceptor pool renew nahi hoga, isliye initial capture aur sustained CO₂ delivery ghatengi, chahe sheath ki Calvin machinery present ho. Delivery loop aur Calvin cycle alag acceptors renew karte hain; ek ki renewal doosre ki missing regeneration ko replace nahi karti.
  - screen: Same two-cell pathway mein return arrow blocked; mesophyll PEP pool dwindles, sheath Calvin-cycle inset still present.
  - jawab: Three-carbon return ke bina PEP renewal fail hogi; finite initial acceptor pool use hone ke baad sustained capture aur delivery nahi chal sakegi.
  - aam galti: Calvin cycle RuBP renew karta hai, PEP nahi; dono acceptor pools ko ek samajhna galat hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.8-k18** · poori kahani dobara · Poora route phir jodo: Kranz leaf mein mesophyll large chloroplast-rich bundle sheath ko surround karta hai, aur sheath vascular bundle ko gherati hai. Mesophyll ka PEPcase three-carbon PEP ke saath incoming inorganic carbon fix karke four-carbon OAA banata hai. Malic ya aspartic acid carbon sheath tak laata hai; decarboxylation one CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder deti hai. CO₂ sheath ke RuBisCO aur Calvin cycle ko feed karti hai, jabki remainder mesophyll lautkar energy-dependent PEP renewal karta hai. Is arrangement se growth ko fayda kyun mil sakta hai? Dekho, CO₂ RuBisCO ke paas concentrate hoti hai aur photorespiratory loss suppress hota hai, isliye comparatively few Calvin-performing sheath cells bhi productive carbon assimilation support kar sakte hain. Ab O₂ wali competing RuBisCO activity ka poora carbon aur energy consequence samjhenge.
  - screen: Same completed realistic tissue-to-two-cell picture, ek waqt mein capture, delivery, Calvin use aur return branches highlighted; final net carbon-gain badge.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10, p022-i12

_→ C₄ delivery RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhati hai; ab photorespiration mein dekhenge ki O₂ wali reaction ka loss kya hai aur concentrated CO₂ usse kaise rokti hai._

## 11.9 PHOTORESPIRATION

_Maqsad: Ab RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ka carbon aur energy loss samjhenge, aur dekhenge ki C₄ ki concentrated CO₂ supply is loss ko kaise dabaati hai._

**11.9-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ka carbon aur energy loss samjhenge, aur dekhenge ki C₄ ki concentrated CO₂ supply is loss ko kaise dabaati hai. Yahaan teen links follow karenge: gases ki competition, oxygenation ka carbon–energy account, aur C₄ plants ka protective CO₂ concentration. Normal carboxylation ka pehle joda reference yaad karo: RuBP + CO₂ se RuBisCO ki madad se two 3PGA molecules bante hain. RuBP wahi five-carbon acceptor aur 3PGA wahi three-carbon PGA hai; 2 × 3PGA ka matlab do molecules, har ek mein three carbons. Comparison ka baseline kya hai? Dekho, five plus one se six carbons, yaani three plus three, milte hain; ab gas badalne par isi account ko compare karenge.
  - terms: 2 × 3PGA = 3PGA ke do molecules; har molecule mein teen carbon atoms.
  - screen: Realistic chloroplast-stroma close-up: RuBisCO ke paas familiar carboxylation arrow, RuBP 5C + CO₂ 1C → 2 × 3PGA, total 6C; oxygenation branch abhi nahi.
  - NCERT: p017-i4 Let us try and understand one more process that creates an important difference ; p017-i5 RuBP + CO₂ ⟶[RuBisCo] 2 × 3PGA
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.9-k2** · samjhana · RuBisCO duniya ka most abundant enzyme hai; photosynthetic carbon fixation ki enormous demand ke liye plants mein iski bahut badi total quantity milti hai. Itna enzyme kyun chahiye? Dekho, bahut bade scale par carbon fixation hoti hai aur RuBisCO comparatively slow catalyst hai, isliye considerable enzyme investment chahiye. Active site enzyme ka reaction karane wala region hai; RuBisCO ke isi catalytic region par CO₂ aur O₂ alternative reactions mein participate kar sakte hain. Ek enzyme dono gases ke saath kaise kaam kar sakta hai? Dekho, enzyme specificity hamesha absolute nahi hoti; RuBisCO ki catalytic chemistry RuBP ke saath dono alternative gas reactions allow karti hai. Carboxylase-oxygenase naam wahi pehle kholi dual activity batata hai; affinity kisi molecule se judne ka jhukaav hai.
  - terms: active site = Enzyme ka region jahaan substrate ki catalysed reaction hoti hai.; affinity = Kisi molecule ke saath judne ka jhukaav.
  - screen: Ussi stromal RuBisCO ka enlarged active site; RuBP present, CO₂ aur O₂ ke alternative entry arrows, dono ek saath reaction karte hue nahi.
  - NCERT: p017-i6 RuBisCO that is the most abundant enzyme in the world (Do you wonder why?) is ch
  - kitaab se bahar: RuBisCO ki abundance samjhaane ke liye comparatively slow catalysis aur enzyme specificity ka non-absolute hona joda.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i1
**11.9-k3** · samjhana · CO₂ aur O₂ ki concentrations comparable hon, toh RuBisCO ka jhukaav CO₂ ki taraf kaafi zyada hota hai. Agar yeh preference na hoti, toh kya hota? Dekho, comparable concentrations par oxygenation ko zyada opportunity milti aur productive carbon fixation ka advantage kam hota. Binding competitive hai, yaani dono gases alternative reactions ke liye compete karti hain. Relative concentration ka matlab ek gas ki concentration doosri ke mukaable kitni hai; CO₂:O₂ isi concentration ratio ko likhta hai. Aksar lagta hai ki greater CO₂ affinity O₂ binding ko impossible bana degi, par asal mein reaction balance local gas concentrations par bhi depend karta hai. CO₂ kam hone par O₂ wali reaction ka relative chance badh sakta hai.
  - terms: competitive = CO₂ aur O₂ ka alternative RuBisCO reactions ke liye muqaabla.; relative concentration = Ek substance ki concentration doosre ke mukaable kitni hai.; CO₂:O₂ = CO₂ concentration ka O₂ concentration se ratio.
  - screen: Same active-site view: pehle comparable gas concentrations, phir CO₂ particles kam; carboxylation aur oxygenation arrows ki relative widths badalti hain.
  - NCERT: p017-i7 is nearly equal. Imagine what would happen if this were not so! This binding is 
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i1
**11.9-k4** · analogy · Socho, ek service counter par do queues se candidates aa rahe hain, aur clerk ek waqt par ek candidate process karta hai. Clerk ko pehli queue ko preference dene ki aadat hai, lekin us queue se log aana bahut kam ho jaayein. Kya preference hone se doosri queue ki service bilkul band rahegi? Dekho, available candidates badalne par doosri queue ko bhi zyada turns mil sakte hain. Ab doosri queue ki arrival same rakho aur pehli queue se arrivals badha do; preferred queue ko phir zyada service opportunities milengi. Yahaan counter RuBisCO ka active site hai, preferred queue CO₂ hai, doosri queue O₂ hai, aur arrivals unki local concentrations hain.
  - screen: Ek counter, CO₂ aur O₂ labelled do queues; O₂ arrivals constant rehte hue CO₂ arrivals badhti hain.
  - kya kis jaisa: Counter active site hai; queues CO₂ aur O₂ hain; preference affinity aur arrivals local concentrations ko map karte hain.
  - kahan tootti hai: Enzyme conscious choice nahi karta; molecular reaction probabilities affinity aur conditions se badalti hain.
**11.9-k5** · example · Ab C₃ leaf mein ek five-carbon RuBP molecule ka O₂ wala reaction follow karo. Oxygenation se one three-carbon phosphoglycerate, wahi PGA, aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate banta hai. Phosphoglycolate oxygenation ka two-carbon product hai, jiska processing photorespiratory route mein hota hai. Carbon ledger yahaan five equals three plus two hai; O₂ koi naya carbon nahi laati. Normal route se difference kya mila? Dekho, carboxylation one external carbon jodkar two PGA deti hai, jabki oxygenation external carbon jode bina one PGA aur one phosphoglycolate deti hai. Isliye O₂ reaction ki opportunity badhne se productive CO₂ fixation ghatati hai; isi diversion se pehle pehchaana photorespiration route shuru hota hai.
  - terms: phosphoglycolate (2 Carbon) = RuBP oxygenation se banne wala two-carbon compound.
  - screen: Ussi stromal picture mein normal branch shaant rehti hai; nayi branch RuBP 5C + O₂ → PGA 3C + phosphoglycolate 2C, carbon dots counted.
  - NCERT: p017-i8 In C₃ plants some O₂ does bind to RuBisCO, and hence CO₂ fixation is decreased. 
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.9-k6** · samjhana · Photorespiratory processing mein ATP utilise hota hai aur CO₂ release hoti hai; utilisation of ATP ka matlab energy kharch hona hai. Is route se net sugar synthesis, ATP synthesis ya NADPH synthesis nahi milti. Kya oxygenation ke turant first step mein hi CO₂ nikal gayi thi? Dekho, first split mein saare five carbons three plus two products mein the; CO₂ baad ke photorespiratory processing mein release hoti hai. Isliye carbon gain par double pressure hai: initial CO₂ fixation ki opportunity ghati aur processing mein pehle fixed carbon ka kuch hissa bhi chhoota. Photorespiration ko sirf useless kehna bhi poori biological picture nahi hai; yeh phosphoglycolate se carbon recover karne ka salvage route hai. Iske protective roles bhi samjhe gaye hain, isliye biological function bilkul unknown kehna sahi nahi; yahaan focus photosynthetic carbon aur energy cost hai.
  - terms: utilisation of ATP = ATP ki energy ka use hona; ATP ka production nahi.
  - screen: Oxygenation ke 2C product se processing arrow aage badhe: ATP → ADP expense aur outward CO₂ arrow; separate net-output ledger mein sugar, ATP, NADPH production zero.
  - NCERT: p017-i9 In the photorespiratory pathway, there is neither synthesis of sugars, nor of AT
  - kitaab se bahar: Outdated function-unknown statement ki jagah carbon-salvage aur protective roles ki modern clarification; first oxygenation aur later CO₂ release ko alag kiya.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i10
**11.9-k7** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: two RuBP molecules oxygenation karein, toh immediate products mein kitna carbon aur kitna naya atmospheric carbon hoga? Kya photorespiration naam dekhkar ise ATP banane wali ordinary respiration maan sakte hain? Dekho, ten starting carbons two PGA ke six aur two phosphoglycolate ke four carbons mein milenge, aur koi naya atmospheric carbon add nahi hoga. Baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karke CO₂ release karta hai, isliye is route ko ATP-producing ordinary respiration samajhna galat hoga.
  - screen: Two RuBP ke 10 carbon dots → two PGA, 6C total + two phosphoglycolate, 4C total; incoming carbon zero, later ATP-expense marker.
  - jawab: Immediate oxygenation carbon conserve karti hai: 10 = 6 + 4; external CO₂ carbon zero add hota hai. Later processing ATP consume aur CO₂ release karti hai.
  - aam galti: Immediate oxygenation mein carbon missing nahi hota; CO₂ loss later processing ka hai. Photorespiration ATP-producing respiration nahi hai.
**11.9-k8** · samjhana · Ab C₄ ka cure jodo: mesophyll ke C₄ acids bundle sheath mein breakdown hokar CO₂ release karte hain, jaisa hum delivery route mein follow kar chuke hain. Isse intracellular concentration, yaani cell ke andar per volume CO₂ ki amount, RuBisCO ke paas badhti hai. O₂ ko hataye bina iska fayda kaise milega? Dekho, local CO₂:O₂ ratio badhne se RuBisCO ki carboxylase activity favour hoti hai aur oxygenase activity minimum ki taraf jaati hai. Minimising oxygenase activity ka matlab O₂ wali reaction ko strongly reduce karna hai, enzyme ki ability mita dena nahi. Isi strong suppression ke kaaran C₄ plants ko practically photorespiration-absent kehte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants mein RuBisCO hi nahi hota, par asal mein sheath ka RuBisCO concentrated CO₂ ke saath kaam karta hai.
  - terms: intracellular concentration = Cell ke andar kisi substance ki amount per unit volume.; minimising the oxygenase activity = RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ko bahut kam karna.
  - screen: Realistic C₄ mesophyll–bundle-sheath pair: familiar C₄-acid delivery, sheath mein CO₂ release; same O₂ density par CO₂ density badhe aur carboxylation arrow widen ho.
  - NCERT: p017-i10 In C₄ plants photorespiration does not occur. This is because they have a mechan
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10
**11.9-k9** · asli duniya · Warm, bright conditions mein maize aur sorghum ki C₄ carbon concentration crop productivity ko support kar sakti hai. Productivity wahi naya organic material banne ki rate hai; yield crop se milne wali useful harvested upaj hai. Tolerance to higher temperatures ka matlab comparatively warmer conditions mein bhi functioning maintain karne ki capacity hai. Extra ATP kharch karne ke bawajood C₄ system faydemand kyun ho sakta hai? Dekho, delivery ka energy bill photorespiratory carbon aur energy losses ko bacha sakta hai, isliye suitable conditions mein net growth aur yield better ho sakte hain. Higher-temperature tolerance bhi C₄ plants ki khas feature hai, lekin har environment mein higher harvest ki guarantee nahi hai.
  - terms: yield = Crop se milne wali useful harvested upaj.; tolerance to higher temperatures = Comparatively warmer conditions mein functioning maintain karne ki capacity.
  - screen: Maize aur sorghum field ke saath two budgets: C₃ photorespiratory carbon loss aur C₄ delivery-energy expense ke badle retained growth carbon; harvest grain alag output.
  - NCERT: p017-i11 Now that you know that the C₄ plants lack photorespiration, you probably can und
  - kitaab se bahar: Favourable warm, bright conditions aur environment-dependent yield ka qualification.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i10
**11.9-k10** · activity · Ab C₃–C₄ comparison table bharo; pehle locations aur carbon identities ki ten rows complete karo. Calvin cycle C₃ mein mesophyll aur C₄ mein bundle sheath mein hota hai, lekin initial carboxylation dono mein mesophyll se shuru hoti hai. CO₂-fixing cell-type categories C₃ mein one, mesophyll, aur C₄ mein two, mesophyll plus bundle sheath, hain; palisade aur spongy dono mesophyll categories ke andar aate hain. Primary acceptors C₃ mein five-carbon RuBP aur C₄ mein three-carbon PEP hain; first products respectively three-carbon PGA aur four-carbon OAA hain. Dono plants mein RuBisCO hai, lekin photosynthetic location C₃ mein mesophyll aur C₄ mein bundle sheath hai. PEPcase wali row mein kya likhoge? Dekho, initial photosynthetic fixation ke context mein C₃ ke liye No aur C₄ ke liye Yes likhenge; iska matlab C₃ plant mein kisi doosre metabolic role ke liye PEPcase kabhi nahi hota, yeh nahi hai.
  - screen: Table 11.1, rows 1–10, columns Characteristic | C₃ | C₄: Calvin site | Mesophyll | Bundle sheath; Initial carboxylation | Mesophyll | Mesophyll; Fixing cell types | One: mesophyll | Two: mesophyll and bundle sheath; Primary acceptor | RuBP | PEP; Acceptor carbons | 5 | 3; Primary product | PGA | OAA; Product carbons | 3 | 4; RuBisCO present | Yes | Yes; Photosynthetic initial-fixation PEPcase | No | Yes; RuBisCO cells | Mesophyll | Bundle sheath.
  - NCERT: p017-i12 Based on the above discussion can you compare plants showing the C₃ and the C₄ p
  - kitaab se bahar: PEPcase ke C₃ non-photosynthetic metabolic roles ka clarification; table ka No initial photosynthetic fixation ke context mein hai.
  - kya kis jaisa: Screen ki ten completed rows; enzyme presence ko uske photosynthetic role aur cell location se alag pehchaano.
  - kahan tootti hai: C₄ plants RuBisCO rakhte hain; acceptor PEP hai, PEPcase enzyme hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p022-i12
**11.9-k11** · activity · Ab remaining seven rows bharo; environmental comparisons ko comparable conditions mein samjho, universal fixed rates ki tarah nahi. High light par suitable temperature aur gas supply mein relative fixation C₃ ke liye Low aur C₄ ke liye High choose karte hain. Photorespiration ke low-light row mein C₃ ke liye Sometimes aur C₄ ke liye Negligible, jabki high-light row mein C₃ High aur C₄ Negligible rakho. Light akeli oxygenation decide kyun nahi karti? Dekho, local gas ratio aur temperature bhi matter karte hain, isliye C₃ ka low-light Sometimes conditional response hai, guaranteed absence nahi. Low CO₂ par C₃ High aur C₄ Negligible; high CO₂ par dono Negligible choose karo, kyunki available CO₂ oxygenation ke against carboxylation favour karti hai. Typical temperature optimum C₃ ke liye 20–25°C aur C₄ ke liye 30–40°C rakho; optimum best-working temperature hai, aur temperature ka mechanism aage kholenge. Examples mein C₃ wheat aur rice, C₄ maize aur sorghum likho; collected sections ko characteristic Kranz organisation ke comparison se classify karo.
  - screen: Same table, rows 11–17: High-light fixation | Low | High; Low-light photorespiration | Sometimes | Negligible; High-light photorespiration | High | Negligible; Low-CO₂ photorespiration | High | Negligible; High-CO₂ photorespiration | Negligible | Negligible; Typical temperature optimum | 20–25°C | 30–40°C; Examples | Wheat, rice | Maize, sorghum. Side-by-side labelled leaf sections connect example classification to Kranz organisation.
  - kitaab se bahar: Table ke inferred environmental entries ko conditional banaya; wheat aur rice examples aur typical temperature ranges jode. Severe CO₂ deprivation mein C₄ concentration bhi unlimited protection nahi deti.
  - kya kis jaisa: Screen ki remaining seven rows; light rows comparable temperature aur gas conditions ke saath relative trends hain.
  - kahan tootti hai: High light akela high photorespiration guarantee nahi karta; CO₂:O₂ aur temperature bhi influence karte hain. Typical optimum har species ka fixed universal value nahi hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i14, p021-i15, p022-i10, p022-i12
**11.9-k12** · poori kahani dobara · Pehle samjhi productive carboxylation ko reference rakho; ab uske competing diversion aur C₄ ke protection ko ek saath jodo. O₂ compete kare, toh five-carbon RuBP se one three-carbon PGA aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate banta hai; naya carbon add nahi hota. Baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karta aur CO₂ release karta hai, net sugar, ATP ya NADPH production nahi deta. C₄ mein mesophyll se aayi acids bundle sheath mein CO₂ release karti hain, jisse RuBisCO ki carboxylation favour aur oxygenation strongly suppress hoti hai. Few Calvin-performing cells bhi productive kyun ho sakte hain? Dekho, concentrated carbon supply aur reduced photorespiratory loss un cells ko effective assimilation support dete hain; cell-count akela productivity decide nahi karta.
  - screen: Connected final comparison: carboxylation 5+1→3+3; oxygenation 5→3+2 with later ATP expense and CO₂ loss; C₄ delivery raises sheath CO₂ and directs flow toward Calvin assimilation.
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10

_→ Ab carbon retention ka farq clear hai; agle heading mein dekhenge ki light, CO₂, temperature, water aur plant ki internal features photosynthesis ki actual rate ko kaise control karte hain._

## 11.10 FACTORS AFFECTING PHOTOSYNTHESIS

_Maqsad: Ab photosynthesis ki machinery se aage badhkar samjhenge ki plant ki internal features aur external conditions mein se kaunsi cheez uski actual rate ko rok rahi hai._

**11.10-k1** · samjhana · Ab photosynthesis ki machinery se aage badhkar samjhenge ki plant ki internal features aur external conditions mein se kaunsi cheez uski actual rate ko rok rahi hai. Yield ko useful harvested upaj pehchaan chuke hain; photosynthesis ki rate crop ke liye available organic material aur yield ko strongly influence karti hai. Internal plant factors mein leaves ki number, size, age aur orientation, mesophyll cells aur chloroplasts ki features, internal CO₂ concentration aur chlorophyll ki amount aati hain. Orientation leaf ki direction aur angle hai; isse leaf par light pahunchne ka pattern badal sakta hai. Same sunlight mein do plants ka photosynthesis alag kyun ho sakta hai? Dekho, unki light collect karne aur carbon fix karne wali internal machinery ki quantity aur arrangement alag ho sakti hai. Yeh internal factors genetic predisposition, yaani inherited traits se judi tendencies, aur plant ki growth par depend karte hain.
  - terms: orientation = Leaf kis direction aur angle par positioned hai.; genetic predisposition = Inherited traits ki wajah se plant ki structure aur functioning ki tendencies.
  - screen: Crop plant se leaf, mesophyll cell aur chloroplast tak connected zoom; side inventory: leaf number, size, age, orientation; mesophyll and chloroplast features; internal CO₂; chlorophyll amount. Genetics aur growth se inventory tak arrows.
  - NCERT: p019-i2 An understanding of the factors that affect photosynthesis is necessary. The rat
**11.10-k2** · samjhana · Ab external conditions jodo: sunlight ki availability, temperature, external CO₂ concentration aur water photosynthesis ko influence karte hain. Yeh factors ek-ek karke duty par nahi aate; photosynthesising leaf par sabka influence simultaneously hota hai. Phir actual rate kaun tay karta hai? Dekho, usually koi ek factor sub-optimal, yaani process ki need ke liye kam anukool, hota hai aur wahi immediate rate ko restrict karta hai. Us immediate restriction ko limiting factor kehte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki sab factors influence karte hain, toh sab equally limiting honge, par asal mein influence hona aur abhi bottleneck hona alag baatein hain.
  - terms: sub-optimal = Process ki need ke liye sabse suitable condition se kam anukool.; limiting factor = Woh factor jo abhi process ki rate ko directly restrict kar raha hai.
  - screen: Pehle plant picture ke around sunlight, thermometer, atmospheric CO₂ aur water supply ke four inputs; CO₂ input narrow hota hai aur carbon-fixation output restricted dikhta hai.
  - NCERT: p019-i3 The external factors would include the availability of sunlight, temperature, CO
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10-k3** · samjhana · Blackman ne 1905 mein isi relation ko Law of Limiting Factors ke roop mein express kiya. Kai factors se affected chemical process ki rate us factor se determine hoti hai jo apni minimal value ke nearest hai. Minimal value yahaan process ki requirement ke relative sabse restrictive level hai. Kya temperature ke degrees aur CO₂ ke percentage mein jo number chhota ho, use limiting kahenge? Dekho, different units ke raw numbers compare nahi karte; har factor ko process ki apni requirement ke mukaable assess karte hain. Limiting factor ko favourable direction mein badalne se rate directly respond karti hai, jabki already adequate factor badhana us shortage ko replace nahi karta.
  - terms: Law of Limiting Factors = Kai influencing factors mein jo abhi sabse restrictive hai, wahi process ki rate determine karta hai.; minimal value = Process ki requirement ke relative factor ka sabse restrictive level.
  - screen: Blackman — 1905 — Law of Limiting Factors; four separate requirement gauges mein ek restrictive gauge highlighted. Us gauge ko improve karne par photosynthesis-rate indicator rises.
  - NCERT: p019-i4 When several factors affect any [bio] chemical process, Blackman’s (1905) Law of
  - kitaab se bahar: Different units ke raw numbers compare na karne ka clarification.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10-k4** · analogy · Socho, toy-car factory ko har car ke liye four wheels aur one body chahiye. Ek ghante mein forty wheels aur sirf six bodies milti hain, toh maximum six cars assemble hongi. Ab wheels forty se eighty kar do; kya twelve cars ban jaayengi? Dekho, six bodies abhi bhi sirf six cars allow karti hain, isliye extra wheels bottleneck nahi hataate. Bodies six se ten karo, toh forty wheels ke saath ten cars ban sakti hain; ab wheels agla constraint ban sakte hain. Yahaan factory output photosynthesis ki rate hai, parts ki supplies environmental requirements hain, aur body shortage current limiting factor hai.
  - screen: Assembly table: 40 wheels + 6 bodies → 6 toy cars; then 80 wheels + 6 bodies → 6 cars; finally 40 wheels + 10 bodies → 10 cars.
  - kitaab se bahar: Numerical toy-car factory analogy for requirement-relative limitation and shifting bottlenecks.
  - kya kis jaisa: Factory output photosynthetic rate ko, parts supplies influencing factors ko, aur insufficient bodies immediate limiting factor ko map karti hain; shortage relieve hone par constraint shift ho sakta hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Photosynthesis ke environmental factors toy parts ki tarah fixed stoichiometric supplies nahi hain; temperature reaction conditions ko influence karta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10-k5** · example · Ab ek green leaf lo, jise optimal light aur CO₂ mil rahe hain, lekin temperature bahut low hai. Enzymatic reactions ko hum enzyme-assisted chemical changes pehchaan chuke hain; bahut low temperature unki activity strongly slow kar sakta hai. Isliye adequate light aur CO₂ ke bawajood leaf ki photosynthesis itni restricted ho sakti hai ki practically chalti na dikhe. Is setup mein kya change karna useful hoga? Dekho, light aur CO₂ fixed rakhkar temperature ko optimal temperature tak laane se restriction relieve hogi aur leaf photosynthesis shuru kar sakti hai. Optimal temperature woh temperature hai jis par us plant ki photosynthesis ke liye conditions sabse favourable hoti hain.
  - terms: optimal temperature = Woh temperature jis par us plant ki photosynthesis sabse favourably chalti hai.
  - screen: Same realistic green leaf in controlled chamber: light and CO₂ controls fixed at suitable levels. Thermometer very low se optimal range tak moves; photosynthesis indicator low se active hota hai.
  - NCERT: p019-i5 For example, despite the presence of a green leaf and optimal light and CO₂ cond
  - kitaab se bahar: Very low temperature se enzyme-mediated reactions slow hone ka brief mechanistic explanation.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i7
**11.10-k6** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: isi cold leaf ko extra lamp dein, lekin temperature bahut low hi rakhein, toh kya main restriction hat jaayegi? Apna answer leaf ke green hone se nahi, uske current limiting factor se jodo. Dekho, light pehle hi adequate hai aur restriction low temperature ki hai, isliye extra light temperature wali limitation ko remove nahi karegi. Temperature favourable karne ke baad kya wahi factor hamesha limiting rahega? Dekho, pehli restriction hatne par CO₂ ya koi doosra factor next bottleneck ban sakta hai, isliye limiting factor conditions ke saath badal sakta hai.
  - screen: Controlled leaf chamber mein extra lamp turns on while thermometer remains low; rate unchanged. Phir temperature improves aur rate rises, with another input becoming the narrowest constraint.
  - jawab: Adequate light badhane se low-temperature restriction nahi hategi; temperature improve karne par rate respond kar sakti hai aur phir doosra factor limiting ho sakta hai.
  - aam galti: Low photosynthetic rate ka matlab automatically light shortage nahi; limiting factor ko controlled change ke response se identify karte hain.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10-k7** · asli duniya · Thandi subah khet mein bright sunshine ho, phir bhi crop ki carbon assimilation restricted reh sakti hai. Dhoop dikhna high photosynthetic rate ki guarantee kyun nahi hai? Dekho, leaf ka temperature unfavourably low ho sakta hai, isliye plenty of light ke saath bhi biochemical work slow reh sakta hai. Field performance samajhne ke liye visible sunshine ke saath plant ki internal capacity aur baaki conditions bhi dekhni padti hain.
  - screen: Bright cold morning mein crop field; sunlit leaf ke saath low leaf-temperature reading aur restrained carbon-assimilation indicator.
  - kitaab se bahar: Cold, sunny crop-field conditions mein limiting-factor principle ka practical application.

_→ Ab limiting factor pehchaanne ka rule mil gaya; light ko badalkar dekhenge ki rate kab badhti hai aur kab doosri requirement usse rok deti hai._

## 11.10.1 Light

_Maqsad: Ab limiting-factor rule ko light par lagaakar samjhenge ki roshni badhne se photosynthesis kab badhti hai, kab rukti nahi balki constant rehti hai, aur kab ghat sakti hai._

**11.10.1-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab limiting-factor rule ko light par lagaakar samjhenge ki roshni badhne se photosynthesis kab badhti hai, kab rukti nahi balki constant rehti hai, aur kab ghat sakti hai. Blackman ka rule yaad rakho: current bottleneck badalne par hi rate directly respond karti hai. Light ke teen controls alag hain: light quality uska colour ya wavelength, light intensity unit area par per unit time pahunchne wali light, aur duration of exposure light milne ka total samay hai. Lamp ka colour, brightness aur timer badalne mein kya same condition badalti hai? Dekho, colour quality badalta hai, brightness intensity badalti hai, aur timer exposure duration badalta hai; inhe ek-doosre ka naam nahi denge. Ab intensity response ke teen regions follow karenge: low-light rise, transition, aur saturation ka constant-rate region.
  - terms: light quality = Light ka wavelength ya colour composition.; light intensity = Unit area par per unit time pahunchne wali light ki quantity.; duration of exposure = Plant ko light milne ka total samay.
  - screen: Ek leaf ke saamne lamp; alag colour selector, brightness slider aur timer. Saath mein khaali graph axes: Light intensity aur Rate of photosynthesis.
  - NCERT: p019-i7 We need to distinguish between light quality, light intensity and the duration o
**11.10.1-k2** · samjhana · Low light intensity par incident light badhaane se CO₂ fixation ki rate approximately seedhi line ke pattern mein badhti hai. Isse linear relationship kehte hain; graph mein intensity ke equal increments par rate ke approximately equal increments milte hain. Shuru mein light badhaana effective kyun hai? Dekho, light current limiting supply hai, isliye uski availability badhne se photosynthetic work badh sakta hai. Higher intensity par curve dheere-dheere bend karti hai, kyunki CO₂ supply ya biochemical reactions ki capacity jaise doosre factors limiting hone lagte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki light badhaate rehne se photosynthesis hamesha badhegi, par asal mein doosre factors limiting hone par extra light rate ko aur nahi badhaati. Limiting factor conditions ke saath badal sakta hai, jaisa hum pehle samjhe the.
  - terms: linear relationship = Aisa sambandh jiska graph approximately seedhi line ho.
