  - screen: Wahi leaf aur graph; brightness badhne ke saath pehle rising straight segment, phir sirf bend add ho. Colour aur timer fixed dikhte rahein.
  - NCERT: p019-i8 There is a linear relationship between incident light and CO₂ fixation rates at 
  - kitaab se bahar: Biochemical capacity ko finite reaction capacity ke roop mein briefly clarify kiya.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10.1-k3** · analogy · Socho, hall mein hundred seats hain, aur pehle sirf twenty interested guests tak invitation pahunchi hai. Invitations forty aur phir sixty guests tak pahunchao, toh occupied seats badh sakti hain. Ab hundred seats bhar gayi hain; two hundred guests ko invite karne se kya occupied seats two hundred ho jaayengi? Dekho, seats ki capacity hundred hai, isliye extra invitations ab occupied seats nahi badhaati. Yahaan invitations ki supply light intensity hai, occupied seats photosynthetic rate hain, aur hall ki seating capacity doosre factors ki imposed limit hai. Pehle invitation shortage bottleneck thi, lekin baad mein seat capacity bottleneck ban gayi.
  - screen: Hall mein 20, 40, 60 aur 100 occupied seats ke successive views; invitations badhti rahein, full hall ki occupied-seat count 100 par rahe.
  - kitaab se bahar: Numerical audience analogy for changing limitation.
  - kya kis jaisa: Invitations light supply, occupied seats photosynthetic rate, aur seating capacity non-light constraints ko represent karti hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Leaf ki biochemical capacity permanently fixed nahi hoti; CO₂, temperature aur plant condition badalne par saturation rate badal sakti hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6
**11.10.1-k4** · samjhana · Light saturation woh intensity hai jiske baad aur light dene se photosynthetic rate aur nahi badhti. Is general response ke liye saturation ko full sunlight ke lagbhag 10 per cent par yaad rakho; har species aur condition ka exact threshold identical nahi hota. Full sunlight ko 100 relative units lo, toh 10 per cent ka matlab 10 units hoga, one unit nahi. Nature mein light rarely limiting kyun hoti hai? Dekho, available light aksar saturation requirement se kaafi hoti hai, isliye extra light ke bajay doosri conditions rate restrict kar sakti hain. Shade aur dense forests ke plants important exceptions hain, jahaan light ki supply hi kam pad sakti hai.
  - terms: light saturation = Light ka woh level jiske baad extra light photosynthetic rate ko aur nahi badhaati.
  - screen: Full sunlight ka 100-unit bar aur uska 10-unit portion highlighted; side mein exposed leaf aur canopy ke neeche shaded leaf.
  - NCERT: p019-i9 What is interesting to note is that light saturation occurs at 10 per cent of th
  - kitaab se bahar: Saturation threshold ki species aur conditions par dependence clarify ki.
  - kahan tootti hai: 10 per cent ko 1 per cent na samjhein; rarely limiting ka matlab never limiting nahi.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6
**11.10.1-k5** · figure · Figure 11.10 mein horizontal axis Light intensity aur vertical axis Rate of photosynthesis hai; arrowheads increasing directions dikhate hain, aur numerical scale nahi hai. A rising low-light region ko mark karta hai; B woh transition hai jahaan curve bend hokar flatten hone lagti hai. C horizontal plateau region hai; plateau ka matlab intensity badhne par bhi rate constant rehna hai. D tak vertical dashed projection saturation-region ki light intensity ko horizontal axis par locate karti hai. E tak horizontal dashed projection plateau ki photosynthetic rate ko vertical axis par locate karti hai. D aur E mein kya farq hai? Dekho, D light intensity ki reading hai aur E photosynthetic rate ki reading hai, isliye dono alag axes ki quantities hain. D saturation ki intensity dikhata hai, zero gas-exchange balance nahi; is unscaled graph se exact intensity ya rate calculate nahi kar sakte.
  - terms: plateau = Graph ka horizontal region jahaan x badhne par y constant rehta hai.
  - screen: Wahi graph ab complete: rising region A, bend B, horizontal C; curve se x-axis par dashed projection D aur y-axis par dashed projection E. Dono axis arrowheads aur koi numerical ticks nahi.
  - NCERT: p019-i11 Graph of rate of photosynthesis against light intensity. Both axes have arrowhea
  - kahan tootti hai: Actual figure mein A rising region hai, plateau nahi; C plateau hai aur D saturation-region intensity, compensation point nahi.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7, p022-i8
**11.10.1-k6** · example · Ab ek leaf ke intensity-response ka numerical case lo: relative light 10 se 20 karne par rate 2 se 4 units hoti hai. Yeh rising response batata hai ki is range mein extra light effective thi. Higher range mein relative light 40 se 50 karo, lekin rate 6 se 6 units hi rahe. Kya doosri reading mein photosynthesis band ho gayi? Dekho, rate ab bhi 6 units hai, isliye photosynthesis chal rahi hai; bas extra light uski rate aur nahi badha rahi. Yeh difference rising A aur plateau C ke patterns ko samjhata hai, unke exact numerical values ko nahi.
  - screen: Leaf ke paas alag numerical table: Light 10→20, rate 2→4; Light 40→50, rate 6→6. Printed unscaled graph alag rahe.
  - kitaab se bahar: Arbitrary relative-unit worked example; numbers figure ki readings nahi hain.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6
**11.10.1-k7** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: plateau par extra light se rate nahi badhti; kya isse prove hota hai ki leaf ko CO₂ ki hi kami hai? Blackman ka rule lagao, lekin missing factor ka naam bina test ke mat chuno. Dekho, plateau light ke alawa restriction ki taraf ishara karta hai, lekin CO₂ supply, temperature ya doosri capacity mein kaunsa factor limiting hai, yeh additional comparison se jaanenge. Aksar constant rate ko zero photosynthesis samajh lete hain, par plateau par positive rate continue rehti hai.
  - screen: C plateau par fixed light beam; CO₂ aur temperature ke separate controls ke paas question marks, photosynthetic-rate indicator positive aur steady.
  - jawab: Plateau extra light ke prati non-response dikhata hai; specific non-light limiting factor identify karne ke liye controlled changes chahiye.
  - aam galti: Plateau na zero photosynthesis prove karta hai, na akela CO₂ limitation establish karta hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
**11.10.1-k8** · asli duniya · Dense forest mein upper canopy bahut si incoming light intercept kar leti hai, isliye neeche ki leaves tak kam light pahunchti hai. Yahaan healthy leaf mein pigments hone ke bawajood light limiting ho sakti hai. Canopy mein gap se zyada light aaye, toh kya rate badh sakti hai? Dekho, agar light current bottleneck thi aur baaki conditions adequate hain, toh increased illumination photosynthesis badha sakti hai.
  - screen: Dense forest canopy se attenuated light forest-floor leaf tak; canopy gap se usi leaf par zyada light pahunchti dikhe.
  - kitaab se bahar: Forest canopy interception ko shade limitation ke real mechanism se joda.
**11.10.1-k9** · samjhana · Ab saturation se alag excessive-light damage samjho: incident light bahut zyada hone par chlorophyll breakdown aur photosynthesis mein decrease ho sakta hai. Accessory pigments ki photo-oxidation se protection wali role hum pehle samajh chuke hain, lekin protection unlimited nahi hoti. Saturation aur damage ko alag kyun rakhenge? Dekho, saturation mein rate constant rehti hai, jabki damaging excess mein light-capturing machinery ko nuksaan pahunchne se rate ghat sakti hai. Figure 11.10 ka printed curve plateau tak hai; excessive-light decline ko uske explanation se alag response ke roop mein samjho.
  - screen: Printed plateau graph unchanged rahe; alag leaf close-up mein excessive illumination, chlorophyll loss aur photosynthetic-rate indicator ka decline.
  - NCERT: p020-i1 incident light beyond a point causes the breakdown of chlorophyll and a decrease
  - kahan tootti hai: Plateau ko chlorophyll damage ka evidence na maanein; printed graph mein downturn nahi hai.
**11.10.1-k10** · poori kahani dobara · Poora response jodo: quality aur duration se intensity alag rakhi, phir low light par rising fixation, bend aur saturation plateau follow kiya. A rise, B transition aur C plateau hain; D intensity projection aur E plateau-rate projection hai. Light saturate hone par photosynthesis kyun continue rehti hai? Dekho, available light ongoing work support karti hai, lekin doosri restriction extra light se rate badhne nahi deti. Shade mein light kam pad sakti hai, jabki excessive illumination chlorophyll ko damage karke rate ghata sakti hai.
  - screen: Teen quiet panels: rising response, positive plateau, aur separate excessive-light damage; central graph par A–E apni correct positions par.

_→ Ab light adequate hone par carbon supply bottleneck ban sakti hai; CO₂ concentration mein dekhenge ki extra carbon dioxide kab photosynthesis badhaati hai._

## 11.10.2 Carbon dioxide Concentration

_Maqsad: Ab light ki limitation se aage badhkar samjhenge ki extra CO₂ kab photosynthesis badhaati hai aur C₃–C₄ plants ka response kyun alag hota hai._

**11.10.2-k1** · samjhana · Ab light ki limitation se aage badhkar samjhenge ki extra CO₂ kab photosynthesis badhaati hai aur C₃–C₄ plants ka response kyun alag hota hai. Pehle dekha tha ki light plateau par carbon supply bhi bottleneck ho sakti hai; CO₂ photosynthesis ka ek major limiting factor hai. Concentration ka matlab total hawa mein CO₂ ka kitna hissa hai. Reference atmospheric range 0.03–0.04 per cent lo; yeh historical range hai, aaj ki measured concentration ka claim nahi. CO₂ concentration lagbhag 0.05 per cent tak badhaane se fixation ki rate badh sakti hai. Kya isse zyada CO₂ hamesha aur fayda degi? Dekho, higher concentrations ka prolonged exposure damaging ho sakta hai, isliye enrichment ko unlimited benefit ya har crop ke liye fixed prescription nahi samjhenge.
  - terms: concentration = Total hawa mein CO₂ ka proportion ya hissa.
  - screen: Leaf tak pahunchti CO₂ aur carbon-fixation arrow; concentration cards: historical atmospheric reference 0.03–0.04%, stimulation up to 0.05%, prolonged higher exposure se possible damage.
  - NCERT: p020-i3 Carbon dioxide is the major limiting factor for photosynthesis. The concentratio
  - kitaab se bahar: Atmospheric range ko historical reference aur enrichment values ko condition-dependent samajhne ki clarification.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i7
**11.10.2-k2** · analogy · Socho, construction site par har ghante ten wall-panels jitni bricks aa rahi hain, lekin available powered equipment sirf four panels assemble kar sakta hai. Bricks double karne se kya eight panels ban jaayenge? Dekho, equipment ki current capacity four panels hi hai, isliye extra material akela production nahi badhaayega. Ab equipment ten panels handle kare, lekin bricks sirf six panels ki hon, toh material supply badhaana useful hoga. Yahaan bricks CO₂ ka carbon material hain, powered equipment ki capacity light-supported work hai, aur completed panels photosynthetic output hain. Blackman ke rule ki tarah, extra supply tab useful hai jab wahi current restriction ko relieve kare.
  - screen: Construction comparison: material for 10 panels plus powered capacity 4 gives 4; powered capacity 10 plus material for 6 gives 6.
  - kitaab se bahar: Numerical construction analogy se interacting light aur carbon limitations samjhaayi gayi hain.
  - kya kis jaisa: Bricks CO₂ supply hain, powered assembly light-supported capacity hai, aur panel production photosynthetic rate hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Photosynthesis mein literal panels ya fixed brick-like molecular production ratios nahi hote.
**11.10.2-k3** · samjhana · C₃ and C₄ plants wahi pehle pehchaane groups hain, jinke initial first stable products respectively three-carbon PGA aur four-carbon OAA hain; C₄ ki local CO₂-concentrating supply bhi hum samajh chuke hain. Low light mein neither group extra CO₂ se rate badhaata hai, kyunki light ki restriction abhi bani rehti hai. High light mein dono groups CO₂ badhne par photosynthetic rate badha sakte hain, lekin unki saturation alag levels par aati hai. CO₂ saturation ka matlab ab aur CO₂ dene par rate ka appreciably na badhna hai. C₄ plants about 360 µlL⁻¹ par saturate karte hain, jabki C₃ plants ka saturation 450 µlL⁻¹ se beyond dikhta hai. µlL⁻¹ ka matlab har litre hawa mein CO₂ ke microlitres hai. C₄ ka extra external CO₂ response pehle flatten kyun hota hai? Dekho, uska concentrating system RuBisCO ko pehle se effective CO₂ supply deta hai, jabki C₃ plants mein reference atmospheric levels par carbon supply zyada restrictive reh sakti hai.
  - terms: C₃ and C₄ plants = Pehle pehchaane plant groups: initial first stable fixation product C₃ mein three-carbon PGA aur C₄ mein four-carbon OAA hota hai.; saturation = Yahaan CO₂ ka woh level jiske baad extra CO₂ se photosynthetic rate appreciably nahi badhti.; µlL⁻¹ = Har litre hawa mein CO₂ ke microlitres.
  - screen: Pehle low-light C₃ aur C₄ responses extra CO₂ par nearly flat; phir separate high-light qualitative curves, C₄ plateau near 360 µL L⁻¹ aur C₃ plateau beyond 450 µL L⁻¹.
  - NCERT: p020-i4 The C₃ and C₄ plants respond differently to CO₂ concentrations. At low light con
  - kitaab se bahar: Saturation values ko universal sharp thresholds ke bajay stated comparison ke approximate response levels ke roop mein samjhaaya gaya hai.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.10.2-k4** · example · Ab 0.04 per cent CO₂ ko µlL⁻¹ mein convert karke dekho. Ek litre mein one million microlitres hote hain, aur 0.04 per cent ka fraction 0.0004 hai. One million ko 0.0004 se multiply karne par 400 µlL⁻¹ milta hai. High-light comparison mein 400 ko dono saturation levels se kaise jodoge? Dekho, 400 C₄ ke about 360 se upar hai, lekin C₃ ke beyond-450 saturation level se neeche hai, isliye C₃ mein further CO₂ response ki gunjaaish samajh aati hai. Yeh approximate response comparison hai; plant aur growing conditions ke saath exact saturation badal sakti hai.
  - screen: 0.04/100 × 1,000,000 µL per litre = 400 µL L⁻¹; number line par 360, 400, 450 aur C₃ saturation ka beyond-450 region.
  - kitaab se bahar: Percentage se volume concentration ka worked conversion aur saturation ki condition-dependence.
**11.10.2-k5** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: low-light tomato greenhouse mein sirf CO₂ badha di, toh photosynthetic rate zaroor badhegi? Apne answer mein extra carbon ki quantity ke saath current limiting factor bhi pehchaano. Dekho, low light abhi energy-side restriction hai, isliye extra CO₂ akeli rate nahi badhaayegi; pehle light limitation relieve honi chahiye. Light adequate hone par bhi kya CO₂ enrichment har baar useful hogi? Dekho, enrichment tab useful hogi jab CO₂ restrictive ho aur temperature jaise doosre factors us benefit ko block na kar rahe hon.
  - screen: Tomato greenhouse: low-light control fixed, CO₂ dial badhta hai, rate indicator unchanged; adequate-light case mein carbon-limited rate badhti hai.
  - jawab: Low light limiting ho toh extra CO₂ rate nahi badhaati; adequate light aur doosri suitable conditions mein carbon limitation relieve hone se rate badh sakti hai.
  - aam galti: CO₂ raw material hai, lekin zyada raw material light se milne wali energy ki kami replace nahi karta.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i7
**11.10.2-k6** · asli duniya · Isi response ka use tomatoes aur bell pepper jaise C₃ greenhouse crops mein hota hai. Carbon dioxide enriched atmosphere woh hawa hai jisme CO₂ ka proportion badhaaya gaya ho. Suitable light aur doosri growing conditions mein enrichment photosynthesis badhaakar greater productivity aur higher yields support kar sakti hai. Harvest ka carbon extra CO₂ se kaise judta hai? Dekho, increased carbon fixation plant ko zyada organic material banaane deti hai, aur suitable crop management mein iska benefit useful harvested upaj tak pahunch sakta hai. Yield wahi useful crop harvest hai, jise hum productivity ki rate se alag pehchaan chuke hain.
  - terms: carbon dioxide enriched atmosphere = Aisi hawa jisme CO₂ ki concentration badhaayi gayi ho.
  - screen: Tomato aur bell-pepper greenhouse mein regulated CO₂ supply; leaf fixation se growing crop aur harvested fruits tak connected carbon-flow arrows.
  - NCERT: p020-i5 The fact that C₃ plants respond to higher CO₂ concentration by showing increased

_→ CO₂ supply badhne ka fayda functioning reactions par depend karta hai; ab temperature mein dekhenge ki garmi ya thand un reactions ki capacity kaise badalti hai._

## 11.10.3 Temperature

_Maqsad: Ab CO₂ supply se aage badhkar samjhenge ki temperature reactions ki capacity kaise badalta hai, aur alag plants ka best-working temperature kyun alag hota hai._

**11.10.3-k1** · samjhana · Ab CO₂ supply se aage badhkar samjhenge ki temperature reactions ki capacity kaise badalta hai, aur alag plants ka best-working temperature kyun alag hota hai. Enzymatic ka meaning enzyme-assisted hai, jise hum stromal sugar-making reactions mein pehle samajh chuke hain. Temperature badalne se enzymes ki reaction karane ki activity badalti hai, isliye dark reactions strongly temperature-controlled hoti hain. Kya adequate light aur CO₂ is temperature effect ko hata denge? Dekho, supplies available hone par bhi enzymes ki working capacity restrictive ho sakti hai, isliye unfavourable temperature carbon assimilation ko limit kar sakta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reactions temperature-independent hain, par asal mein woh bhi temperature-sensitive hain; unka response carbon reactions ke mukaable much lesser extent ka hota hai.
  - screen: Chloroplast cutaway mein stromal carbon reactions aur thylakoid light reactions ke saath ek thermometer; temperature change par stromal reaction-speed indicator mein bada aur light-reaction indicator mein chhota badlav.
  - NCERT: p020-i7 The dark reactions being enzymatic are temperature controlled. Though the light 
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i7
**11.10.3-k2** · example · Ab comparable light aur CO₂ conditions mein ek C₃ aur ek C₄ leaf ka temperature response compare karo. Temperature optimum wahi best-working temperature hai, jise hum optimal temperature ke naam se pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Temperature ko horizontal aur photosynthetic rate ko vertical axis par rakho; C₃ ka peak cooler side aur C₄ ka peak warmer side par aata hai. C₄ plants comparatively higher temperatures par higher photosynthetic rates maintain kar sakte hain, jabki C₃ plants ka optimum lower hota hai. C₄ ki warmer performance mein carbon delivery ka kya contribution hai? Dekho, RuBisCO ke paas concentrated CO₂ photorespiratory loss suppress karti hai, isliye warmer conditions mein productive carbon assimilation ko support milta hai. Pehle bhari comparison table ke typical ranges C₃ ke liye 20–25°C aur C₄ ke liye 30–40°C the; har individual plant ka exact optimum identical nahi hota.
  - screen: Same light aur CO₂ controls ke neeche qualitative C₃–C₄ temperature-response curves; C₃ peak cooler aur C₄ peak warmer position par, numerical axis ke bina.
  - NCERT: p020-i8 The C₄ plants respond to higher temperatures and show higher rate of photosynthe
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**11.10.3-k3** · analogy · Socho, do musical instruments alag performance conditions ke liye tune kiye gaye hain. Dono same room mein rakhe hon, phir bhi jis condition mein pehla sabse achchha perform kare, doosre ka best performance wahin milna zaroori nahi. Kya ek instrument ka best setting sab par laga denge? Dekho, har instrument ki tuning alag hai, isliye uska best-working range bhi alag ho sakta hai. Yahaan instruments alag plants hain, tuning unki physiological adaptation hai, aur best performance photosynthesis ka temperature optimum hai.
  - screen: Do instruments ke alag best-setting markers se linked do plants ke temperature-response peaks.
  - kitaab se bahar: Alag tuning wale instruments ka everyday comparison temperature optima ka difference samjhane ke liye.
  - kya kis jaisa: Instruments plants hain, tuning physiological adaptation hai, aur best-performance setting temperature optimum hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Plants manually tune nahi hote; unki temperature response biological adaptation aur functioning se banti hai.
**11.10.3-k4** · asli duniya · Pathway ke alawa plant ka habitat bhi matter karta hai; habitat woh jagah aur environmental conditions hain jahaan plant rehta hai. Tropical plants garam tropical regions se adapted plants hain. Temperate climates tropical regions ke mukaable generally cooler aur seasonal climates hain. In habitats ke adapted plants ka photosynthetic optimum same kyun nahi hota? Dekho, alag thermal conditions ke liye adaptation ke kaaran tropical plants ka temperature optimum temperate-adapted plants se generally higher hota hai. Isliye warm tropical habitat aur cooler temperate habitat ke plants ko ek hi universal photosynthetic temperature optimum nahi de sakte. Tropical hona aur C₄ hona same classification nahi hai; habitat aur carbon-fixation pathway ko separate comparisons rakho.
  - terms: habitat = Plant ke rehne ki jagah aur wahaan ki environmental conditions.; Tropical = Earth ke garam tropical regions se sambandhit.; temperate climates = Tropical regions se generally cooler, seasonal climates.
  - screen: Warm tropical habitat aur cooler temperate habitat ke plants ke saath separate qualitative optimum markers; habitat comparison pathway comparison se alag panel mein.
  - NCERT: p020-i9 The temperature optimum for photosynthesis of different plants also depends on t
  - kitaab se bahar: Habitat category ko C₃–C₄ pathway category ke barabar na maanne ki clarification.
**11.10.3-k5** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir aage badhenge: light aur CO₂ adequate hain, toh kya temperature badhaate rehne se har plant ki photosynthesis badhti rahegi? Apne answer mein optimum aur plants ke beech ka difference dono jodo. Dekho, warming tab favourable ho sakti hai jab plant apne optimum ki taraf jaa raha ho, lekin optimum se aage jaana further improvement ki guarantee nahi deta. Kya tropical plant ka optimum dekhkar use C₄ declare kar sakte hain? Dekho, habitat adaptation bhi optimum influence karti hai, isliye temperature response akela carbon-fixation pathway identify nahi karta.
  - screen: Temperature-response curve par cooler position se optimum tak marker aage badhe, phir peak ke beyond lower-rate position; separate habitat aur pathway labels.
  - kitaab se bahar: Optimum ke beyond temperature increase ko unlimited benefit na maanne ki response-curve clarification.
  - jawab: Temperature increase optimum ki taraf favourable ho sakta hai, lekin beyond-optimum warming unlimited rate increase nahi deti; optimum pathway aur habitat adaptation dono se influenced hai.
  - aam galti: Higher C₄ optimum ka matlab unlimited heat tolerance nahi; tropical origin akela C₄ identity establish nahi karta.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10

_→ Temperature reactions ki capacity badalta hai; ab water mein dekhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise restrict karti hai._

## 11.10.4 Water

_Maqsad: Ab temperature se aage badhkar samjhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise ghataati hai._

**11.10.4-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab temperature se aage badhkar samjhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise ghataati hai. Water ko hum light reaction ka reactant aur leaf ke pores ko gas exchange ke raaste pehchaan chuke hain. Water stress ka matlab plant ki zaroorat ke mukaable paani ki availability kam padna hai. Iske do effects follow karenge: stomatal closure se carbon supply ghatna, aur wilting se leaf ki functioning ghatna. Kya problem sirf water splitting ke liye reactant kam hone ki hai? Aksar log sochte hain ki paani ki kami sirf water splitting ka substrate ghataati hai, par asal mein iska major effect plant ki condition ke through indirect hota hai.
  - terms: water stress = Plant ki zaroorat ke mukaable paani ki availability kam padna.
  - screen: Healthy aur water-stressed potted plant ka paired view; stressed plant se do abhi khaali branches, CO₂ entry aur leaf functioning.
**11.10.4-k2** · samjhana · Stomata wahi leaf ke gas-exchange pores hain; opening ke guard cells unhe regulate karte hain, jaisa hum shuru mein dekh chuke hain. Water stress mein stomata close hone se water vapour ka loss kam hota hai, lekin CO₂ ki entry bhi restrict hoti hai. Paani bachaane se photosynthesis kyun ghategi? Dekho, closed pores ke through kam CO₂ available hoti hai, isliye carbon fixation restrict hoti hai. Doosri branch mein leaves wilt karti hain, yaani paani ki kami se dheeli padkar jhuk jaati hain. Cells ke andar water pressure leaf ko firm rakhta hai; yeh support ghatne par leaf droop kar sakti hai. Wilting se light ko effectively exposed leaf area ghat sakta hai, aur metabolic activity, yaani cells ki chemical reactions ki working, bhi kam hoti hai. Isliye water reactant hone ke bawajood uska major limiting effect stomatal CO₂ supply aur leaf functioning ke through samjhenge.
  - terms: stomata = Pehle pehchaane stomatal pores ka plural naam; leaf ke gas-exchange raaste.; wilt = Paani ki kami se leaf ka dheela padkar jhukna.; metabolic activity = Cells mein chalne wali chemical reactions ki working.
  - screen: Ussi stressed plant par pehli branch: closed stomatal pore → kam incoming CO₂ → reduced fixation; doosri branch: drooping leaf → reduced exposed area aur metabolic activity.
  - NCERT: p020-i11 Even though water is one of the reactants in the light reaction, the effect of w
  - kitaab se bahar: Cell water pressure se firmness aur wilting ka sambandh; reduced surface area ko reduced effectively exposed area ke roop mein clarify kiya.
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i7
**11.10.4-k3** · analogy · Socho, ek workshop ke gate se raw material andar aata hai, lekin khule gate se andar ki thandi hawa bhi bahar nikalti hai. Cooling bachaane ke liye gate band karte ho, toh raw material ki delivery bhi restrict ho jaati hai. Ek protective action production ko kyun ghata sakta hai? Dekho, gate dono movements ka shared raasta hai, isliye ek loss rokne par useful incoming supply bhi kam ho jaati hai. Yahaan gate stomatal pore hai, cooling ka loss water-vapour loss hai, aur incoming raw material CO₂ hai. Stomatal closure paani bachaati hai, lekin carbon supply ka trade-off bhi laati hai.
  - screen: Workshop gate par inward raw-material arrow aur outward cooling-loss arrow; saath mein stomatal pore par inward CO₂ aur outward water-vapour arrows.
  - kitaab se bahar: Shared workshop gate ki analogy.
  - kya kis jaisa: Gate stomatal pore hai; cooling loss water-vapour loss aur incoming material CO₂ hai. Gate closure loss ke saath useful entry bhi restrict karti hai.
  - kahan tootti hai: Stomata literal gates nahi hain; analogy sirf shared exchange route aur closure ke trade-off ko samjhati hai.
**11.10.4-k4** · example · Ab balcony ke water-stressed tomato plant ko lo: sunlight available hai, lekin leaves droop kar rahi hain. Pehli branch mein stomatal closure CO₂ entry ghataati hai; doosri mein wilting effective light exposure aur metabolic functioning ko impair karti hai. Sirf extra lamp lagaane se kya dono problems solve hongi? Dekho, light badhaana closed pores ki carbon restriction ya water-stressed leaf ki impaired functioning ko directly theek nahi karta. Limiting-factor rule ke mutaabik current restriction ko relieve karna zaroori hai, na ki already available supply ko aur badhaana.
  - screen: Balcony tomato plant par adequate sunlight; dono causal branches visible, extra lamp ke bawajood closed pore aur drooping leaf unchanged.
  - kitaab se bahar: Tomato plant ka worked application.
**11.10.4-k5** · asli duniya · Khet mein dry spell ke dauraan crop ki carbon gain visible wilting se pehle bhi ghat sakti hai. Leaf abhi seedhi dikhe, toh kya water shortage ka photosynthesis par effect nahi hua hoga? Dekho, stomatal closure visible wilting se pehle CO₂ entry restrict kar sakti hai, isliye sirf drooping dekhna water stress ke impact ka poora measure nahi hai.
  - screen: Dry field ki abhi upright crop leaf ka close-up; narrowed stomatal pore aur reduced incoming CO₂, paas mein baad ki wilting wali leaf.
  - kitaab se bahar: Stomatal restriction visible wilting se pehle bhi carbon gain ghata sakti hai.
**11.10.4-k6** · poori kahani dobara · Ab dono branches ek saath jodo: water stress stomata close karata hai, jisse CO₂ availability aur carbon fixation ghat sakti hai. Saath hi wilting leaf ka effective exposed area aur metabolic activity ghataakar photosynthetic functioning impair karti hai. Water ka direct reactant role apni jagah hai, lekin factor ke roop mein uska major effect poore plant ki condition ke through aata hai.
  - screen: Ussi potted plant ke completed two-branch diagram ko saaf rakho: CO₂ restriction aur wilting-related functional decline, dono se lower photosynthesis.

_→ Ab environmental limits bhi jud gayi hain; recap mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko poore photosynthetic system ki ek tasveer mein jodenge._

## recap Poore chapter ki tasveer (summary)

_Maqsad: Ab poore photosynthetic system mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko jodkar dekhenge ki plant food kaise banata hai._

**recap-k1** · pehle poori jhalak · Ab poore photosynthetic system mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko jodkar dekhenge ki plant food kaise banata hai. Is connected picture mein chaar dhaage follow karenge: energy, electrons, protons aur carbon. Atmospheric CO₂ stomata se leaf mein aati hai, aur uska carbon photosynthesis se carbohydrates, jaise glucose aur starch, ke account mein pahunchta hai. Carbon aur energy ke sources alag kyun rakhenge? Dekho, CO₂ carbon material deti hai aur sunlight energy deti hai; light khud food ke carbon atoms nahi banati.
  - screen: Sunlit leaf ka realistic close-up; stomatal pore se ek marked CO₂ carbon andar aata hai, aur sunlight ka separate energy arrow leaf par padta hai.
  - NCERT: p021-i2 Green plants make their own food by photosynthesis. During this process carbon d
**recap-k2** · saar · Ab isi leaf se mesophyll cell aur uske numerous chloroplasts tak zoom karke dono working regions jodo. Photosynthesis mainly leaves mein, aur doosre chlorophyll-containing green parts mein bhi hoti hai. Chloroplast ki thylakoid membranes light reactions sambhaalti hain, jabki stroma biosynthetic carbon-fixing reactions ka working region hai. Carbon fixing reactions wahi reactions hain jo CO₂ ke carbon ko organic compounds mein incorporate karti hain. Dono stages alag hote hue independent kyun nahi hain? Dekho, membrane reactions ATP aur NADPH supply karti hain, aur stromal reactions in supplies ko carbon assimilation mein use karti hain; dark naam darkness ki requirement nahi batata.
  - screen: Ussi leaf se mesophyll aur realistic chloroplast cutaway tak zoom; internal membranes aur surrounding stroma ke do clear working regions, unke beech ATP–NADPH supply arrows.
  - NCERT: p021-i3 Photosynthesis takes place only in the green parts of the plants, mainly the lea; p021-i4 Photosynthesis has two stages: the light reaction and the carbon fixing reaction
**recap-k3** · saar · Ab membrane ke photosystem mein antenna pigments se absorbed light ki excitation energy reaction-centre chlorophyll a tak pahunchti hai. Reaction centre chlorophylls wahi special chlorophyll a sites hain jo excitation ko electron donation se jodti hain. PS I ka centre P700, 700 nm par, aur PS II ka centre P680, 680 nm red light par characteristic absorption dikhata hai. Kya antenna se centre tak energy transfer aur centre se acceptor tak electron transfer same event hain? Dekho, antenna excitation energy funnel karti hai, jabki reaction centre electron donate karta hai; collection aur photochemical conversion complementary jobs hain. I aur II discovery ke names hain, electron flow ka order nahi.
  - screen: Ussi chloroplast ki thylakoid membrane par photosystem enlarge ho; antenna se energy flashes centre tak converge hon, phir P680–PS II aur P700–PS I ki wavelength pairings dikhein.
  - NCERT: p021-i5 In the light reaction the light energy is absorbed by the pigments present in th; p021-i6 There are two photosystems, PS I and PS II. PS I has a 700 nm absorbing chloroph
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i2
**recap-k4** · saar · Non-cyclic route mein light PS II ka electron excite karti hai; acceptor aur transport chain use PS I tak pahunchate hain. PS I par doosra light boost milta hai, phir acceptor pathway electrons NADP⁺ reduction tak laata hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki yahaan NAD se NADH banta hai, par asal mein photosynthetic route NADP⁺ ko reduce karke NADPH banata hai. Ek NADPH ke liye two electrons aur one stromal H⁺ chahiye. Electron chain mein kharch kyun nahi ho jaata? Dekho, carriers electrons aage transfer karte hain, destroy nahi karte; unki energy ka conversion aur electrons ka matter ledger alag sambhalte hain.
  - screen: Ussi membrane par sirf electron route highlight ho: PS II → carriers → PS I → NADP⁺; two light boosts aur NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH ka small ledger.
  - NCERT: p021-i7 After absorbing light, electrons are excited and transferred through PS II and P
**recap-k5** · saar · Electron transport ke saath lumen mein high H⁺ aur stroma mein relatively low H⁺ ka gradient banta hai. Water splitting lumen mein H⁺ add karti hai, carrier transport stromal H⁺ andar laata hai, aur NADP⁺ reduction stromal H⁺ use karti hai. Ab ATP ke liye energy kahaan se milegi? Dekho, pehle samjha controlled proton return yaad karo: gradient ki energy ATP-making machinery ko chalati hai. F₀ isi membrane-embedded proton-channel part ka short label hai, jise chloroplast mein CF₀ ke naam se pehchaana tha. Gradient breakdown mein difference kam hota hai, protons destroy nahi hote.
  - terms: F₀ = Pehle pehchaane CF₀ proton-channel part ka short label; ATP synthase ka membrane-embedded hissa.
  - screen: Ussi thylakoid cutaway par electron route quiet rahe; lumen H⁺ pool, stromal H⁺ depletion aur CF₀ se outward return highlight ho, stromal CF₁ ke paas ADP + Pi → ATP.
  - NCERT: p021-i8 During this process a proton gradient is created across the membrane of the thyl
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i11
**recap-k6** · example · Ab do water molecules ka batch lekar electron supply aur oxygen output ko isi picture mein fit karo: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. PS II-associated lumen-facing water splitting four replacement electrons, four lumen H⁺ aur one oxygen molecule deti hai. Yeh four electrons aage two NADP⁺ ko two NADPH banane ki supply de sakte hain. Released O₂ ka source CO₂ kyun nahi hai? Dekho, water oxidation se oxygen milti hai, aur isotope evidence bhi uska water-source establish karta hai; CO₂ ka main tracked contribution carbon assimilation hai. O₂ baahar diffuse ho sakti hai, jabki water ke electrons reducing-power pathway aur H⁺ gradient-building se judte hain.
  - screen: Ussi membrane ke PS II par two water models; four electrons electron route mein, four H⁺ lumen mein aur one O₂ outward diffusion ki taraf; NADPH endpoint par two-carrier tally.
  - NCERT: p021-i9 Splitting of water molecules is associated with PS II resulting in the release o
**recap-k7** · saar · Ab stromal carbon ledger kholo: RuBisCO five-carbon RuBP ke saath one-carbon CO₂ fix karke two three-carbon PGA molecules banata hai. Reduction ATP aur NADPH use karke triose-phosphate pool banati hai; uska kuch carbon output mein aur baaki regeneration mein jaata hai. Three CO₂ aur three RuBP ka total eighteen-carbon pool six three-carbon intermediates deta hai. Inmein three carbons net output hain, jabki fifteen carbons three RuBP regenerate karte hain; iska bill nine ATP aur six NADPH hai. RuBP regenerate karna zaroori kyun hai? Dekho, next CO₂ ko acceptor chahiye, isliye carbon output ke saath starting pool ki renewal bhi essential hai. Glucose-equivalent ka pehle joda eighteen ATP–twelve NADPH budget yaad rakho; spent ADP aur NADP⁺ supplies renew hone ke liye membrane reactions se dobara judte hain.
  - screen: Ussi chloroplast ke stroma mein carbon route stepwise: 3 RuBP + 3 CO₂ → 6 PGA → triose pool; three-carbon output alag ho, fifteen-carbon return RuBP tak close ho; spent carriers membranes ki taraf.
  - NCERT: p021-i10 In the carbon fixation cycle, CO₂ is added by the enzyme, RuBisCO, to a 5-carbon
  - exercise ke liye: p022-i10
**recap-k8** · saar · Ab isi productive route ki competing branch yaad karo: RuBisCO RuBP ki oxygenation bhi catalyse kar sakta hai. Oxygenation wahi O₂ wali alternative reaction hai, jisme five-carbon RuBP one three-carbon PGA aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate deta hai. Is branch mein naya carbon gain kyun nahi hua? Dekho, O₂ carbon nahi laayi, aur baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karke CO₂ release karta hai; net sugar, ATP ya NADPH synthesis nahi milti. Photorespiration ordinary ATP-producing respiration nahi hai; productive carbon assimilation ke comparison mein iska carbon aur energy cost hai.
  - screen: Stromal RuBP site par small competing O₂ branch; 5C → 3C + 2C tally, phir downstream ATP expenditure aur CO₂-release arrows.
  - NCERT: p021-i11 RuBisCO also catalyses a wasteful oxygenation reaction in C₃ plants: photorespir
**recap-k9** · saar · Kuch tropical plants ka C₄ pathway isi competition ke against carbon-concentrating delivery system deta hai. Mesophyll mein three-carbon PEP initial carbon capture se four-carbon OAA banata hai; C₄ acids bundle sheath tak carbon le jaati hain. Sheath mein four-carbon cargo one CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder deta hai; remainder mesophyll lautkar energy-dependent PEP renewal karta hai. Delivered CO₂ bundle sheath ke Calvin cycle mein carbohydrates ke formation ko feed karti hai. Is delivery ko carbon ka final destination kyun nahi kahenge? Dekho, sheath tak pahunchna sirf supply step hai; delivered carbon ko carbohydrate account mein laane ka kaam Calvin cycle karta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants Calvin cycle nahi chalaate, par asal mein initial C₄ capture usi cycle ko concentrated CO₂ supply karta hai. High local CO₂ RuBisCO ki oxygenation suppress karke pehle samjha photorespiratory loss ghataati hai.
  - screen: Leaf ke neighbouring mesophyll aur bundle sheath cells ka realistic cutaway; 3C PEP → 4C cargo → 1C CO₂ + 3C return; sheath chloroplast mein Calvin route aur local CO₂ supply highlight ho.
  - NCERT: p021-i12 Some tropical plants show a special type of photosynthesis called C₄ pathway. In
  - exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10
**recap-k10** · ruko, socho · Socho, phir poori picture close karenge: chloroplast mein light aur CO₂ available hain, lekin RuBP regeneration ruk gayi, toh kya sugar production continuously chalti rahegi? Kya ATP aur NADPH ki supply missing acceptor ko replace kar degi? Dekho, available RuBP pool use hone ke baad next fixation restrict hogi, kyunki energy aur reducing power carbon acceptor ki renewal ka replacement nahi hain. Isi tarah poore plant mein adequate light akeli insufficient CO₂, unfavourable temperature ya water-stressed leaf ki restriction nahi hataati.
  - screen: Pehle bana stromal loop; regeneration arrow rukne par RuBP pool ghatta dikhe, jabki ATP, NADPH aur CO₂ supply arrows present rahen.
  - jawab: Dekho, regeneration rukne se RuBP acceptor pool replenish nahi hota, isliye remaining RuBP use hone ke baad sustained CO₂ fixation restrict hoti hai; adequate energy supplies acceptor ko replace nahi karti.
  - aam galti: ATP aur NADPH available hone ko sufficient na maanein; continuous fixation ke liye RuBP regeneration bhi essential hai.
**recap-k11** · poori kahani dobara · Ab end-to-end tasveer jodo: antenna light energy collect karti hai, reaction centres electron transfer shuru karte hain, aur water replacement electrons ke saath O₂ deta hai. Electron flow NADPH supply aur proton-gradient formation se judta hai; H⁺ ka controlled return ATP synthase ko ATP banane ki energy deta hai. Stroma mein CO₂ carbon RuBP se PGA aur carbohydrate intermediates tak jaata hai, jabki carbon ka returning hissa acceptor regenerate karta hai. ATP energy deta hai aur NADPH reducing electrons deta hai; unke spent carrier forms dobara light-reaction supply se jud sakte hain. C₄ delivery RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhaakar photorespiratory loss dabaati hai, lekin actual rate plant ki capacity aur current environmental bottleneck par bhi depend karti hai. Poore chapter ka central sambandh kya raha? Dekho, sunlight ki energy water ke electron flow aur proton gradient ke through usable chemical supplies banti hai, aur woh supplies atmospheric carbon ko plant ke organic food mein badalne ka kaam support karti hain.
  - screen: Sunlit whole plant ke saath four uncluttered linked tracks: sunlight → ATP support, water → electrons → NADPH, lumen H⁺ → ATP synthase, atmosphere CO₂ → carbohydrate; returning carriers aur acceptor loops apne respective regions mein.
